╚═══════════════════════════════════════════════╝
]]
(function()
--[[
    Ye Anti Loadstring / HTTP Spy Guard
    精简高风险版：
    1. 低风险不弹窗、不记录
    2. 只有 LuaClosure + Upvalue 才视为高风险
    3. 函数启动后被替换 ReplacedAfterStartup 视为高风险
    4. 测试 Hook 点“知道了”后自动清理并继续
    5. 真实高风险 Hook 点“知道了”后阻止主脚本加载
    6. 所有检测项和警告输出使用错误代码，不直接输出 Hook 名称

    修改说明：
    - 已取消 620：不再检测 game.__namecall [LuaClosure, Upvalue]
    - 保留 621：仍然通过 isfunctionhooked 判断 __namecall 是否被 Hook
]]
local YE_REAL_ENV = nil

pcall(function()
    if getrenv then
        local env = getrenv()
        if type(env) == "table" then
            YE_REAL_ENV = env
        end
    end
end)

local YE_BOOT_LOADSTRING = loadstring
local YE_ORIGINAL_LOADSTRING = nil

if YE_REAL_ENV and type(YE_REAL_ENV.loadstring) == "function" then
    YE_ORIGINAL_LOADSTRING = YE_REAL_ENV.loadstring
else
    YE_ORIGINAL_LOADSTRING = loadstring
end

local YE_ORIGINAL_HTTPGET = game.HttpGet
local YE_ORIGINAL_HTTPGETASYNC = game.HttpGetAsync

local YE_ORIGINAL_GETFENV = getfenv
local YE_ORIGINAL_RAWGET = rawget
local YE_ORIGINAL_SETMETATABLE = setmetatable

local YE_ANTI_DETECTED = {}
local YE_ANTI_WARNED = false

local YE_AntiConfig = {
    ShowWarning = true,
    WaitConfirmBeforeContinue = true,
    AutoCleanTestHook = true,
    HookOnlyMode = true,
    TryRestoreHookedFunction = true,
}

local YE_ERROR_CODE_MAP = {
    ENV_DEBUG_GETINFO_MISSING = 310,
    ENV_DEBUG_GETINFO_HOOKED = 311,
    ENV_DEBUG_INFO_HOOKED = 312,
    ENV_TRACEBACK_ABNORMAL = 313,
    ENV_GETFENV_HOOKED = 314,
    ENV_RAWGET_HOOKED = 315,
    ENV_SETMETATABLE_HOOKED = 316,

    HOOK_LOADSTRING_LUA_UPVALUE = 401,
    HOOK_LOADSTRING_PRE_REPLACED = 403,

    HOOK_HTTPGET_LUA_UPVALUE = 501,
    HOOK_HTTPGET_REPLACED = 502,

    HOOK_HTTPGETASYNC_LUA_UPVALUE = 601,
    HOOK_HTTPGETASYNC_REPLACED = 602,

    HOOK_NAMECALL_HOOKED = 621,
    HOOK_NAMECALL_RESTORE_FAILED = 622,

    HOOK_UNKNOWN = 699,

    WARN_STAGE_BEFORE_LOAD = 701,
    WARN_STAGE_BEFORE_EXECUTE = 702,
    WARN_STAGE_UNKNOWN = 703,

    WARN_HIGH_RISK_ITEMS = 704,
    WARN_TEST_HOOK_CLEANED = 705,
    WARN_TEST_HOOK_CLEANED_CONTINUE = 706,
    WARN_TEST_HOOK_STILL_ABNORMAL = 707,
    WARN_BLOCK_MAIN_SCRIPT = 708,

    WARN_URL_INVALID = 801,
    WARN_HTTPGET_MISSING = 802,
    WARN_LOADSTRING_MISSING = 803,
    WARN_FETCH_FAILED = 804,
    WARN_SOURCE_TOO_SHORT = 805,
    WARN_SOURCE_LINK_INVALID = 806,
    WARN_COMPILE_FAILED = 807,
    WARN_RUN_FAILED = 808,
}

local YE_DETECTED_CODE_KEY_MAP = {
    ["loadstring [LuaClosure, Upvalue]"] = "HOOK_LOADSTRING_LUA_UPVALUE",
    ["loadstring [ReplacedBeforeStartup]"] = "HOOK_LOADSTRING_PRE_REPLACED",

    ["game.HttpGet [LuaClosure, Upvalue]"] = "HOOK_HTTPGET_LUA_UPVALUE",
    ["game.HttpGet [ReplacedAfterStartup]"] = "HOOK_HTTPGET_REPLACED",

    ["game.HttpGetAsync [LuaClosure, Upvalue]"] = "HOOK_HTTPGETASYNC_LUA_UPVALUE",
    ["game.HttpGetAsync [ReplacedAfterStartup]"] = "HOOK_HTTPGETASYNC_REPLACED",

    ["game.__namecall [Hooked]"] = "HOOK_NAMECALL_HOOKED",
    ["game.__namecall [RestoreFailed]"] = "HOOK_NAMECALL_RESTORE_FAILED",

    ["debug.getinfo [LuaClosure, Upvalue]"] = "ENV_DEBUG_GETINFO_HOOKED",
    ["debug.info [LuaClosure, Upvalue]"] = "ENV_DEBUG_INFO_HOOKED",
    ["debug.traceback [LuaClosure, Upvalue]"] = "ENV_TRACEBACK_ABNORMAL",

    ["env.debug.getinfo [Missing]"] = "ENV_DEBUG_GETINFO_MISSING",
    ["env.debug.getinfo [Hooked]"] = "ENV_DEBUG_GETINFO_HOOKED",
    ["env.debug.info [Hooked]"] = "ENV_DEBUG_INFO_HOOKED",
    ["env.traceback [Abnormal]"] = "ENV_TRACEBACK_ABNORMAL",
    ["env.getfenv [Hooked]"] = "ENV_GETFENV_HOOKED",
    ["env.rawget [Hooked]"] = "ENV_RAWGET_HOOKED",
    ["env.setmetatable [Hooked]"] = "ENV_SETMETATABLE_HOOKED",
}

local YE_ERROR_CODE_CACHE = {}
local YE_ERROR_RANDOM = nil

pcall(function()
    if Random and Random.new then
        YE_ERROR_RANDOM = Random.new()
    end
end)

local function yeRandomDigit()
    if YE_ERROR_RANDOM then
        return tostring(YE_ERROR_RANDOM:NextInteger(0, 9))
    end
    return tostring(math.random(0, 9))
end

local function yeEncodeErrorCode(code)
    local codeText = tostring(code)
    local parts = {}

    for i = 1, #codeText do
        local digit = codeText:sub(i, i)
        parts[#parts + 1] = yeRandomDigit() .. yeRandomDigit() .. digit
    end

    return table.concat(parts, " ")
end

local function yeGetErrorCodeByKey(key)
    return YE_ERROR_CODE_MAP[key] or YE_ERROR_CODE_MAP.HOOK_UNKNOWN
end

local function yeGetDisplayErrorCode(key)
    if YE_ERROR_CODE_CACHE[key] then
        return YE_ERROR_CODE_CACHE[key]
    end

    local encoded = yeEncodeErrorCode(yeGetErrorCodeByKey(key))
    YE_ERROR_CODE_CACHE[key] = encoded

    return encoded
end

local function yeGetDetectedDisplayCode(label)
    local key = YE_DETECTED_CODE_KEY_MAP[label] or "HOOK_UNKNOWN"
    return yeGetDisplayErrorCode(key)
end

local function yeGetDisplayDetected(items)
    local result = {}

    for i, item in ipairs(items) do
        result[i] = yeGetDetectedDisplayCode(item)
    end

    return result
end

local function yeGetStageCodeKey(stageName)
    if stageName == "加载前检测" then
        return "WARN_STAGE_BEFORE_LOAD"
    end

    if stageName == "执行前检测" then
        return "WARN_STAGE_BEFORE_EXECUTE"
    end

    return "WARN_STAGE_UNKNOWN"
end

local function yeWarn(codeKey, ...)
    warn("[Ye AntiSpy][" .. yeGetDisplayErrorCode(codeKey) .. "]", ...)
end

local function yeAddDetected(label)
    for _, v in ipairs(YE_ANTI_DETECTED) do
        if v == label then
            return
        end
    end

    YE_ANTI_DETECTED[#YE_ANTI_DETECTED + 1] = label
end

local function yeGetGlobalValue(name)
    local value = nil

    if getfenv then
        pcall(function()
            local env = getfenv()
            if type(env) == "table" then
                value = env[name]
            end
        end)
    end

    if value ~= nil then
        return value
    end

    pcall(function()
        value = _G[name]
    end)

    if value ~= nil then
        return value
    end

    if getgenv then
        pcall(function()
            local env = getgenv()
            if type(env) == "table" then
                value = env[name]
            end
        end)
    end

    return value
end

local function yeTryRestoreFunction(fn)
    if not YE_AntiConfig.TryRestoreHookedFunction then
        return false
    end

    local restore = yeGetGlobalValue("restorefunction")

    if type(restore) ~= "function" then
        return false
    end

    local ok = pcall(function()
        restore(fn)
    end)

    return ok == true
end

local function yeTryIsFunctionHooked(fn)
    local checker = yeGetGlobalValue("isfunctionhooked")

    if type(checker) ~= "function" then
        return false
    end

    local ok, result = pcall(checker, fn)
    return ok and result == true
end

local function yeCheckHookedByExecutorApi(label, fn)
    if fn == nil then
        return
    end

    local checker = yeGetGlobalValue("isfunctionhooked")

    if type(checker) ~= "function" then
        return
    end

    local ok, result = pcall(checker, fn)

    if ok and result == true then
        local restored = yeTryRestoreFunction(fn)

        if restored then
            task.wait(0.05)

            local ok2, result2 = pcall(checker, fn)

            if ok2 and result2 == false then
                return
            end
        end

        yeAddDetected(label .. " [LuaClosure, Upvalue]")
    end
end

local function yeIsLuaHooked(fn)
    if fn == nil then
        return false
    end

    if iscclosure then
        local ok, result = pcall(iscclosure, fn)
        if ok and result == false then
            return true
        end
    end

    if debug and debug.getinfo then
        local ok, info = pcall(debug.getinfo, fn, "S")
        if ok and info and info.what == "Lua" then
            return true
        end
    end

    if debug and debug.info then
        local ok, source = pcall(debug.info, fn, "s")
        if ok and type(source) == "string" then
            if source ~= "[C]" and source ~= "=[C]" then
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    return false
end

local function yeHasUpvalues(fn)
    if fn == nil then
        return false
    end

    if not (debug and debug.getupvalue) then
        return false
    end

    local ok, name = pcall(debug.getupvalue, fn, 1)
    return ok and name ~= nil
end

local function yeCheckFunction(label, fn)
    if fn == nil then
        return
    end

    local luaHooked = yeIsLuaHooked(fn)
    local upvalueHooked = yeHasUpvalues(fn)

    if luaHooked and upvalueHooked then
        yeAddDetected(label .. " [LuaClosure, Upvalue]")
    end
end

local function yeGetRawMetatableSafe(obj)
    local mt = nil

    if getrawmetatable then
        pcall(function()
            mt = getrawmetatable(obj)
        end)
    end

    return mt
end

local function yeGetNamecallFunction()
    local mt = yeGetRawMetatableSafe(game)

    if type(mt) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end

    return mt.__namecall
end

local function yeCheckNamecallHook()
    local namecall = yeGetNamecallFunction()

    if type(namecall) ~= "function" then
        return
    end

    local checker = yeGetGlobalValue("isfunctionhooked")
    local hookedByApi = false

    if type(checker) == "function" then
        local ok, result = pcall(checker, namecall)
        hookedByApi = ok and result == true
    end

    if hookedByApi then
        local restored = yeTryRestoreFunction(namecall)

        if restored then
            task.wait(0.05)

            local namecallAfter = yeGetNamecallFunction()
            local stillHooked = false

            if type(checker) == "function" and type(namecallAfter) == "function" then
                local ok2, result2 = pcall(checker, namecallAfter)
                stillHooked = ok2 and result2 == true
            end

            if not stillHooked then
                return
            end
        end

        yeAddDetected("game.__namecall [Hooked]")
        return
    end
end

local function yeCheckPreReplacedLoadstring()
    if YE_REAL_ENV and type(YE_REAL_ENV.loadstring) == "function" then
        if loadstring ~= YE_REAL_ENV.loadstring then
            local currentHooked = false

            if yeTryIsFunctionHooked(loadstring) then
                currentHooked = true
            end

            if yeIsLuaHooked(loadstring) or yeHasUpvalues(loadstring) or currentHooked then
                local restored = yeTryRestoreFunction(loadstring)

                if restored then
                    task.wait(0.05)

                    if loadstring == YE_REAL_ENV.loadstring then
                        return
                    end
                end

                yeAddDetected("loadstring [ReplacedBeforeStartup]")
            end
        end
    end
end

local function yeCheckCriticalFunctionHooked()
    if not (debug and debug.getinfo) then
        yeAddDetected("env.debug.getinfo [Missing]")
    else
        yeCheckHookedByExecutorApi("debug.getinfo", debug.getinfo)

        if yeIsLuaHooked(debug.getinfo) and yeHasUpvalues(debug.getinfo) then
            yeAddDetected("env.debug.getinfo [Hooked]")
        end
    end

    if debug and debug.info then
        yeCheckHookedByExecutorApi("debug.info", debug.info)

        if yeIsLuaHooked(debug.info) and yeHasUpvalues(debug.info) then
            yeAddDetected("env.debug.info [Hooked]")
        end
    end

    if debug and debug.traceback then
        yeCheckHookedByExecutorApi("debug.traceback", debug.traceback)

        if yeIsLuaHooked(debug.traceback) and yeHasUpvalues(debug.traceback) then
            yeAddDetected("debug.traceback [LuaClosure, Upvalue]")
        end
    end

    if getfenv and YE_ORIGINAL_GETFENV and getfenv ~= YE_ORIGINAL_GETFENV then
        yeAddDetected("env.getfenv [Hooked]")
    end

    if rawget and YE_ORIGINAL_RAWGET and rawget ~= YE_ORIGINAL_RAWGET then
        yeAddDetected("env.rawget [Hooked]")
    end

    if setmetatable and YE_ORIGINAL_SETMETATABLE and setmetatable ~= YE_ORIGINAL_SETMETATABLE then
        yeAddDetected("env.setmetatable [Hooked]")
    end
end

local function yeCheckTracebackAbnormal()
    if not (debug and debug.traceback) then
        return
    end

    local ok, stack = pcall(debug.traceback)

    if not ok then
        yeAddDetected("env.traceback [Abnormal]")
        return
    end

    if type(stack) == "string" then
        local lowerStack = stack:lower()

        if lowerStack:find("stack overflow") or lowerStack:find("c stack overflow") then
            yeAddDetected("env.traceback [Abnormal]")
        end
    end
end

local function yeScanHookOnlyEnvironment()
    yeCheckCriticalFunctionHooked()
    yeCheckTracebackAbnormal()
end

local function yeScanAntiSpy()
    YE_ANTI_DETECTED = {}

    yeScanHookOnlyEnvironment()

    yeCheckNamecallHook()
    yeCheckPreReplacedLoadstring()

    if loadstring ~= nil then
        yeCheckHookedByExecutorApi("loadstring", loadstring)
        yeCheckFunction("loadstring", loadstring)
    end

    yeCheckHookedByExecutorApi("game.HttpGet", game.HttpGet)
    yeCheckFunction("game.HttpGet", game.HttpGet)

    if YE_ORIGINAL_HTTPGET ~= nil and game.HttpGet ~= YE_ORIGINAL_HTTPGET then
        yeAddDetected("game.HttpGet [ReplacedAfterStartup]")
    end

    yeCheckHookedByExecutorApi("game.HttpGetAsync", game.HttpGetAsync)
    yeCheckFunction("game.HttpGetAsync", game.HttpGetAsync)

    if YE_ORIGINAL_HTTPGETASYNC ~= nil and game.HttpGetAsync ~= YE_ORIGINAL_HTTPGETASYNC then
        yeAddDetected("game.HttpGetAsync [ReplacedAfterStartup]")
    end
end

local function yeCleanTestHooks()
    local cleaned = false
    local env = nil

    if getgenv then
        local ok, result = pcall(getgenv)
        if ok and type(result) == "table" then
            env = result
        end
    end

    if not env then
        env = _G
    end

    if env and env.YE_TEST_LOADSTRING_HOOKED and type(env.YE_TEST_ORIGINAL_LOADSTRING) == "function" then
        local original = env.YE_TEST_ORIGINAL_LOADSTRING

        pcall(function()
            loadstring = original
        end)

        if getgenv then
            pcall(function()
                getgenv().loadstring = original
            end)
        end

        env.YE_TEST_LOADSTRING_HOOKED = nil
        env.YE_TEST_ORIGINAL_LOADSTRING = nil

        cleaned = true
    end

    if env and env.YE_TEST_HTTPGET_HOOKED and type(env.YE_TEST_ORIGINAL_HTTPGET) == "function" then
        local original = env.YE_TEST_ORIGINAL_HTTPGET

        pcall(function()
            game.HttpGet = original
        end)

        env.YE_TEST_HTTPGET_HOOKED = nil
        env.YE_TEST_ORIGINAL_HTTPGET = nil

        cleaned = true
    end

    return cleaned
end

local function yeShowAntiWarning(items, onConfirm)
    if YE_ANTI_WARNED or not YE_AntiConfig.ShowWarning then
        if onConfirm then
            task.defer(onConfirm)
        end
        return
    end

    YE_ANTI_WARNED = true

    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "YeAntiSpyWarning"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling

    local ok = pcall(function()
        gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    end)

    if not ok then
        gui.Parent = Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    end

    local bg = Instance.new("Frame")
    bg.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
    bg.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bg.Parent = gui

    local cardH = math.clamp(230 + #items * 18, 230, 420)

    local card = Instance.new("Frame")
    card.Size = UDim2.new(0, 460, 0, 0)
    card.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -230, 0.5, 0)
    card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 28)
    card.BorderSizePixel = 0
    card.Parent = bg

    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
    corner.Parent = card

    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 48)
    title.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 45, 45)
    title.BorderSizePixel = 0
    title.Text = "啊咧？拒绝启动，环境似乎有些异常呢喵"
    title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    title.TextSize = 16
    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    title.Parent = card

    local titleCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    titleCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
    titleCorner.Parent = title

    local titleFix = Instance.new("Frame")
    titleFix.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
    titleFix.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -16)
    titleFix.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 45, 45)
    titleFix.BorderSizePixel = 0
    titleFix.Parent = title

    local msg = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    msg.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 58)
    msg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 58)
    msg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    msg.Text = "我是夜养的猫娘，\n让我帮你看看喵，似乎是http被hook了呢，\n你难道是想要我的源码吗，\n不给！"
    msg.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
    msg.TextSize = 13
    msg.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    msg.TextWrapped = true
    msg.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    msg.Parent = card

    local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, cardH - 178)
    list.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 126)
    list.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(13, 13, 20)
    list.BorderSizePixel = 0
    list.ScrollBarThickness = 4
    list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, #items * 20 + 8)
    list.Parent = card

    local listCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    listCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    listCorner.Parent = list

    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    layout.Parent = list

    for i, item in ipairs(items) do
        local row = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 18)
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        row.Text = "  • " .. tostring(item)
        row.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 105)
        row.TextSize = 11
        row.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        row.LayoutOrder = i
        row.Parent = list
    end

    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 110, 0, 34)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -55, 1, -44)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(65, 135, 255)
    btn.Text = "知道了"
    btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    btn.TextSize = 14
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.Parent = card

    local btnCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    btnCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    btnCorner.Parent = btn

    btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(85, 155, 255)
        }):Play()
    end)

    btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(65, 135, 255)
        }):Play()
    end)

    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 460, 0, 0),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -230, 0.5, 0)
        }):Play()

        task.delay(0.2, function()
            gui:Destroy()

            if onConfirm then
                onConfirm()
            end
        end)
    end)

    TweenService:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 460, 0, cardH),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -230, 0.5, -cardH / 2)
    }):Play()
end

local function yeWaitForWarningConfirm(items)
    local confirmed = false

    yeShowAntiWarning(items, function()
        confirmed = true
    end)

    if YE_AntiConfig.WaitConfirmBeforeContinue then
        while not confirmed do
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
    end
end

local function yeHandleDetection(stageName)
    if #YE_ANTI_DETECTED <= 0 then
        return true
    end

    yeWarn(yeGetStageCodeKey(stageName), "检测阶段异常")
    yeWarn("WARN_HIGH_RISK_ITEMS", "高风险检测项：", table.concat(yeGetDisplayDetected(YE_ANTI_DETECTED), " | "))

    yeWaitForWarningConfirm(yeGetDisplayDetected(YE_ANTI_DETECTED))

    if YE_AntiConfig.AutoCleanTestHook then
        local cleaned = yeCleanTestHooks()

        if cleaned then
            yeWarn("WARN_TEST_HOOK_CLEANED", "已清理测试 Hook，重新检测中...")

            yeScanAntiSpy()

            if #YE_ANTI_DETECTED <= 0 then
                yeWarn("WARN_TEST_HOOK_CLEANED_CONTINUE", "测试 Hook 已清理，继续执行。")
                return true
            end

            yeWarn("WARN_TEST_HOOK_STILL_ABNORMAL", "清理测试 Hook 后仍有异常：", table.concat(yeGetDisplayDetected(YE_ANTI_DETECTED), " | "))
        end
    end

    yeWarn("WARN_BLOCK_MAIN_SCRIPT", "高风险 Hook，已阻止加载主脚本。")
    return false
end

local function yeSafeCompile(source, chunkName)
    local attempts = {}

    attempts[#attempts + 1] = {
        name = "YE_ORIGINAL_LOADSTRING_WITH_CHUNK",
        fn = YE_ORIGINAL_LOADSTRING,
        withChunk = true,
    }

    attempts[#attempts + 1] = {
        name = "YE_ORIGINAL_LOADSTRING_NO_CHUNK",
        fn = YE_ORIGINAL_LOADSTRING,
        withChunk = false,
    }

    if YE_BOOT_LOADSTRING ~= YE_ORIGINAL_LOADSTRING then
        attempts[#attempts + 1] = {
            name = "YE_BOOT_LOADSTRING_WITH_CHUNK",
            fn = YE_BOOT_LOADSTRING,
            withChunk = true,
        }

        attempts[#attempts + 1] = {
            name = "YE_BOOT_LOADSTRING_NO_CHUNK",
            fn = YE_BOOT_LOADSTRING,
            withChunk = false,
        }
    end

    if type(loadstring) == "function" and loadstring ~= YE_ORIGINAL_LOADSTRING and loadstring ~= YE_BOOT_LOADSTRING then
        local currentLooksHooked = false

        if yeTryIsFunctionHooked(loadstring) then
            currentLooksHooked = true
        end

        if yeIsLuaHooked(loadstring) and yeHasUpvalues(loadstring) then
            currentLooksHooked = true
        end

        if not currentLooksHooked then
            attempts[#attempts + 1] = {
                name = "CURRENT_LOADSTRING_WITH_CHUNK",
                fn = loadstring,
                withChunk = true,
            }

            attempts[#attempts + 1] = {
                name = "CURRENT_LOADSTRING_NO_CHUNK",
                fn = loadstring,
                withChunk = false,
            }
        end
    end

    local lastErr = nil

    for _, attempt in ipairs(attempts) do
        if type(attempt.fn) == "function" then
            local okCompile, compiled

            if attempt.withChunk then
                okCompile, compiled = pcall(function()
                    return attempt.fn(source, chunkName or "YeProtectedScript")
                end)
            else
                okCompile, compiled = pcall(function()
                    return attempt.fn(source)
                end)
            end

            if okCompile and type(compiled) == "function" then
                return true, compiled, attempt.name
            end

            lastErr = compiled
        end
    end

    return false, lastErr, "FAILED"
end

local function safeLoadRemote(url, chunkName)
    yeScanAntiSpy()

    if #YE_ANTI_DETECTED > 0 then
        local okToContinue = yeHandleDetection("加载前检测")
        if not okToContinue then
            return false
        end
    end

    if type(url) ~= "string" or url == "" then
        yeWarn("WARN_URL_INVALID", "url 无效")
        return false
    end

    if YE_ORIGINAL_HTTPGET == nil then
        yeWarn("WARN_HTTPGET_MISSING", "当前环境不存在 game.HttpGet")
        return false
    end

    if YE_ORIGINAL_LOADSTRING == nil then
        yeWarn("WARN_LOADSTRING_MISSING", "当前环境不存在 loadstring")
        return false
    end

    yeScanAntiSpy()

    if #YE_ANTI_DETECTED > 0 then
        local okToContinue = yeHandleDetection("加载前检测")
        if not okToContinue then
            return false
        end
    end

    local okSource, source = pcall(function()
        return YE_ORIGINAL_HTTPGET(game, url)
    end)

    if not okSource or type(source) ~= "string" then
        yeWarn("WARN_FETCH_FAILED", "获取远程脚本失败")
        return false
    end

    if #source < 50 then
        yeWarn("WARN_SOURCE_TOO_SHORT", "远程源码异常，长度过短")
        return false
    end

    if source:find("404: Not Found") or source:find("Not Found") then
        yeWarn("WARN_SOURCE_LINK_INVALID", "远程源码链接可能失效")
        return false
    end

    yeScanAntiSpy()

    if #YE_ANTI_DETECTED > 0 then
        local okToContinue = yeHandleDetection("执行前检测")
        if not okToContinue then
            return false
        end
    end

    local okCompile, compiled = yeSafeCompile(source, chunkName)

    if not okCompile or type(compiled) ~= "function" then
        yeWarn("WARN_COMPILE_FAILED", "编译远程脚本失败：", tostring(compiled))
        return false
    end

    local okRun, runErr = pcall(compiled)

    if not okRun then
        yeWarn("WARN_RUN_FAILED", "执行远程脚本失败：", tostring(runErr))
        return false
    end

    return true
end

        local hasWritefile = pcall(function() return type(writefile) == "function" end)
        local hasReadfile = pcall(function() return type(readfile) == "function" end)
        local hasIsfile = pcall(function() return type(isfile) == "function" end)
        return hasWritefile and hasReadfile and hasIsfile
    end

    local fileSystemSupported = hasFileSystemSupport()

    local function checkIgnoreFile()
        if not fileSystemSupported then return false end
        local ok, exists = pcall(function()
            return isfile("qq_group_ignored.txt")
        end)
        return ok and exists
    end

        if not fileSystemSupported then return false end

        end)

                            if elements.joinGlow then
                                TweenService:Create(elements.joinGlow, TweenInfo.new(0.2), { Thickness = 0, Transparency = 0.8 }):Play()

                        end)
                        elements.joinButton.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
                            TweenService:Create(elements.joinButton, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Back), { Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.52, 0, 0, 0) }):Play()
                        end)
                    end
                end

                local function animateEntrance()
                    if container then
                        container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        TweenService:Create(container, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }):Play()
                    end
                    if backdrop then
                        backdrop.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        TweenService:Create(backdrop, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.4 }):Play()
                    end
                end

                local parented = pcall(function()
                    self.gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
                end)

                if not parented then
                    self.gui.Parent = Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
                end

                self.gui.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
                    if parent == nil then
                        local b = Lighting:FindFirstChild("JunkieUIBlur")
                        if b then b:Destroy() end
                    end
                end)

                self.close = function(self)
                    if not self.gui then return end
                    getgenv().UI_CLOSED = true
                    local c = self.elements.container
                    local b = self.elements.backdrop
                    local bl = Lighting:FindFirstChild("JunkieUIBlur")

                    TweenService:Create(c, TweenInfo.new(0.2), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                    TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.2), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()

                    task.wait(0.2)
                    if bl then bl:Destroy() end
                    self.gui:Destroy()
                    self.gui = nil
                end

                setupAnimations()
                animateEntrance()
                return self.gui
            end
        end
    end

    local function playSuccessAnimation(container)
        local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

        container.ClipsDescendants = true

        for _, v in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("TextLabel") then
                TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                    TextTransparency = 1
                }):Play()
            elseif v:IsA("ImageLabel") then
                TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                    ImageTransparency = 1
                }):Play()
            elseif v:IsA("Frame") or v:IsA("TextButton") then
                if v ~= container then
                    TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                        BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    }):Play()
                end
            elseif v:IsA("UIStroke") then
                TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                    Transparency = 1
                }):Play()
            end
        end

        task.wait(0.25)

        local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
        holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        holder.Parent = container

        local circle = Instance.new("Frame")
        circle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 80, 0, 80)
        circle.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
        circle.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        circle.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(34, 197, 94)
        circle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        circle.Parent = holder

        Instance.new("UICorner", circle).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local glow = Instance.new("Frame")
        glow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 16, 1, 16)
        glow.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
        glow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        glow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        glow.Parent = circle

        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(34, 197, 94)
        stroke.Thickness = 3
        stroke.Transparency = 1
        stroke.Parent = glow

        Instance.new("UICorner", glow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local check = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        check.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        check.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        check.Text = "✓"
        check.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        check.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        check.TextSize = 0
        check.TextTransparency = 1
        check.Parent = circle

        TweenService:Create(circle,
            TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Back),
            {BackgroundTransparency = 0.15, Size = UDim2.new(0, 90, 0, 90)}
        ):Play()

        task.wait(0.1)

        TweenService:Create(stroke,
            TweenInfo.new(0.5),
            {Transparency = 0.3}
        ):Play()

        TweenService:Create(glow,
            TweenInfo.new(0.5),
            {Size = UDim2.new(1, 24, 1, 24)}
        ):Play()

        task.wait(0.15)

        TweenService:Create(check,
            TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back),
            {TextSize = 52, TextTransparency = 0}
        ):Play()

        task.wait(0.3)

        TweenService:Create(circle,
            TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true),
            {Size = UDim2.new(0, 92, 0, 92)}
        ):Play()
    end

    local UI = {}
    UI.__index = UI

    function UI.new()
        local self = setmetatable({}, UI)
        self.player = Players.LocalPlayer
        self.gui = nil
        self._connections = {}
        return self
    end

    UI.createUI = function(self)
        local UIFactory = loadUIFactory()
        if UIFactory then
            local uiBuilder = UIFactory(Colors, Players, TweenService, UserInputService, Lighting)
            uiBuilder(self)
        end

        table.insert(self._connections, self.elements.rejectButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if self.elements.ignoreChecked and self.elements.ignoreChecked() then
                saveIgnoreFile()
            end
            self:close()
        end))

        table.insert(self._connections, self.elements.joinButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local btn = self.elements.joinButton
            local label = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")

            if setclipboard then
                setclipboard("1081045774")
            end

            if label then
                label.Text = "已复制"
            end

            playSuccessAnimation(self.elements.container)

            saveIgnoreFile()

            task.wait(2)

            self:close()
        end))

        return self.gui
    end

    local ui = UI.new()
    ui:createUI()

    if not getgenv().UI_CLOSED then
        while not getgenv().UI_CLOSED do
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end

    return true
end)()

local function getLoadingGuiParent()
	local ok, coreGui = pcall(function()
		return game:GetService("CoreGui")
	end)

	if ok and coreGui then
		return coreGui
	end

	return player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
end

--==================================================
-- 工具函数
--==================================================

local active = true
local closing = false
local mainStarted = false
local renderConnection

local function startMainOnce()
	if mainStarted then
		return
	end

	mainStarted = true
	loadingFinished = true
	loadingFinishedEvent:Fire()
end

local function create(className, props, children)
	local obj = Instance.new(className)

	for key, value in pairs(props or {}) do
		obj[key] = value
	end

	for _, child in ipairs(children or {}) do
		child.Parent = obj
	end

	return obj
end

local function tween(obj, time, props, style, direction)
	local info = TweenInfo.new(
		time,
		style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,
		direction or Enum.EasingDirection.Out
	)

	local tw = TweenService:Create(obj, info, props)
	tw:Play()

	return tw
end

local function clamp01(value)
	return math.clamp(value, 0, 1)
end

local function randomHex(length)
	local chars = "0123456789ABCDEF"
	local result = table.create(length)

	for i = 1, length do
		local index = math.random(1, #chars)
		result[i] = string.sub(chars, index, index)
	end

	return table.concat(result)
end

local function utf8Split(text)
	local result = {}

	for _, codepoint in utf8.codes(text) do
		table.insert(result, utf8.char(codepoint))
	end

	return result
end

local function makeCorner(radius)
	return create("UICorner", {
		CornerRadius = radius
	})
end

local function setGroupTransparency(root, targetTransparency, fadeInfo)
	for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
		if obj:IsA("Frame") then
			TweenService:Create(obj, fadeInfo, {
				BackgroundTransparency = targetTransparency
			}):Play()
		elseif obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") then
			TweenService:Create(obj, fadeInfo, {
				TextTransparency = targetTransparency,
				BackgroundTransparency = targetTransparency
			}):Play()
		elseif obj:IsA("ImageLabel") then
			TweenService:Create(obj, fadeInfo, {
				ImageTransparency = targetTransparency,
				BackgroundTransparency = targetTransparency
			}):Play()
		elseif obj:IsA("UIStroke") then
			TweenService:Create(obj, fadeInfo, {
				Transparency = targetTransparency
			}):Play()
		end
	end
end

local snowLayer = create("Frame", {

	Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
	BackgroundTransparency = 1,
	ClipsDescendants = true,
	ZIndex = 4,
	Parent = background

local snowflakes = {}

local function makeSnowflake(layerName, count, sizeMin, sizeMax, speedMin, speedMax, alphaMin, alphaMax, driftMin, driftMax, zIndex)
	if not ENABLE_SNOW then
		return
	end

	for i = 1, count do
		local size = math.random(sizeMin, sizeMax)
		local x = math.random()
		local y = math.random()

		local flake = create("Frame", {
			Name = layerName,
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.fromScale(x, y),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size),
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 248, 255),
			BackgroundTransparency = math.random(alphaMin, alphaMax) / 100,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			ZIndex = zIndex,
			Parent = snowLayer
		}, {
			makeCorner(UDim.new(1, 0))
		})

		local stroke = nil

		if layerName == "NearSnow" then
			stroke = create("UIStroke", {
				Thickness = 1,
				Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
				Transparency = 0.78,
				Parent = flake
			})
		end

		table.insert(snowflakes, {
			frame = flake,
			stroke = stroke,
			x = x,
			y = y,
			speed = math.random(speedMin, speedMax) / 10000,
			drift = math.random(driftMin, driftMax) / 100000,
			swayAmount = math.random(6, 18) / 10000,
			swaySpeed = math.random(34, 82) / 100,
			phase = math.random() * math.pi * 2,
			rotateSpeed = math.random(-18, 18),
			layerName = layerName
		})
	end
end

makeSnowflake("FarSnow", FAR_SNOW_COUNT, 2, 3, 12, 22, 86, 95, -6, 6, 5)
makeSnowflake("MidSnow", MID_SNOW_COUNT, 3, 4, 20, 34, 74, 88, -10, 10, 6)
makeSnowflake("NearSnow", NEAR_SNOW_COUNT, 5, 7, 30, 48, 60, 76, -14, 14, 7)

local function updateCardSize()
	local screenSize = background.AbsoluteSize

	if screenSize.X <= 0 or screenSize.Y <= 0 then
		return
	end

	local width = math.clamp(screenSize.X * 0.5, 360, 500)
	local height = math.clamp(screenSize.Y * 0.43, 300, 342)

	if screenSize.X < 600 then
		width = math.clamp(screenSize.X * 0.88, 320, 430)
		height = math.clamp(screenSize.Y * 0.5, 300, 350)
	end

	card.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, height)
end

updateCardSize()
background:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(updateCardSize)

--==================================================
-- 关闭动画
--==================================================

local function fadeOutAndStart()
	local fadeInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)

	setGroupTransparency(gui, 1, fadeInfo)

	TweenService:Create(blur, fadeInfo, {
		Size = 0
	}):Play()

	task.delay(0.54, function()
		if gui and gui.Parent then
			gui:Destroy()
		end

		if blur and blur.Parent then
			blur:Destroy()
		end

		startMainOnce()
	end)
end

local function animateProgressToWelcome(duration)
	local startScale = clamp01(progressBar.Size.X.Scale)
	local startClock = os.clock()
	local connection

	connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		local now = os.clock()
		local alpha = clamp01((now - startClock) / duration)
		local eased = 1 - math.pow(1 - alpha, 3)
		local value = startScale + (1 - startScale) * eased

		progressBar.Size = UDim2.fromScale(value, 1)
		percentText.Text = tostring(math.floor(value * 100 + 0.5)) .. "%"

		if alpha >= 1 then
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil

			progressBar.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			percentText.Text = "100%"

			task.delay(WELCOME_HOLD_TIME, function()
				fadeOutAndStart()
			end)
		end
	end)
end

local function closeLoading(wasSkipped)
	if closing then
		return
	end

	closing = true
	active = false

	if renderConnection then
		renderConnection:Disconnect()
		renderConnection = nil
	end

	skipButton.Active = false
	skipButton.AutoButtonColor = false

	if wasSkipped then
		skipButton.Text = "启动中"
		statusText.Text = "正在加载 UI库，请稍候"
		animateProgressToWelcome(SKIP_COMPLETE_TIME)
	else
		statusText.Text = "UI库加载完成"
		animateProgressToWelcome(0.1)
	end
end

local loadingEventDestroyed = false
local progressTween = nil
local loadingIntroReady = false
local loadingIntroReadyEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")

local function markLoadingIntroReady()
	if loadingIntroReady then
		return
	end

	loadingIntroReady = true
	loadingIntroReadyEvent:Fire()
end

local function waitLoadingIntroReady()
	if loadingIntroReady then
		return
	end

	loadingIntroReadyEvent.Event:Wait()
end

local function destroyLoadingEventOnce()
	if loadingEventDestroyed then
		return
	end

	loadingEventDestroyed = true

	pcall(function()
		loadingFinishedEvent:Destroy()
	end)

	pcall(function()
		loadingIntroReadyEvent:Destroy()
	end)
end

local function waitLoadingClosed()
	if not loadingFinished then
		loadingFinishedEvent.Event:Wait()
	end

	destroyLoadingEventOnce()
end

local function setLoadingStatus(title, content)
	local titleText = tostring(title or "")
	local contentText = tostring(content or "")

	if contentText ~= "" then
		statusText.Text = titleText .. "  /  " .. contentText
	elseif titleText ~= "" then
		statusText.Text = titleText
	end
end

local function setLoadingProgress(value, duration)
	local target = clamp01(tonumber(value) or 0)
	local time = tonumber(duration) or 0

	waitLoadingIntroReady()

	if progressTween then
		pcall(function()
			progressTween:Cancel()
		end)
		progressTween = nil
	end

	if time > 0 then
		progressTween = TweenService:Create(
			progressBar,
			TweenInfo.new(time, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
			{Size = UDim2.fromScale(target, 1)}
		)

		progressTween:Play()
	else
		progressBar.Size = UDim2.fromScale(target, 1)
	end
end

local function closeLoadingOnlyAndWait(finalStatus)
	if finalStatus then
		setLoadingStatus(finalStatus)
	end

	if progressTween then
		pcall(function()
			progressTween:Cancel()
		end)
		progressTween = nil
	end

	if not closing then
		closing = true
		active = false

		if renderConnection then
			renderConnection:Disconnect()
			renderConnection = nil
		end

		skipButton.Active = false
		skipButton.AutoButtonColor = false

		fadeOutAndStart()
	end

	waitLoadingClosed()

	local env = nil
	if getgenv then
		pcall(function()
			env = getgenv()
		end)
	end
	env = env or _G

	if env then
		env.YeLiquidLoading = nil
	end
end

local function finishLoadingAndWait()
	if progressTween then
		pcall(function()
			progressTween:Cancel()
		end)
		progressTween = nil
	end

	if not closing then
		setLoadingStatus("UI库加载完成")
		closeLoading(false)
	end

	waitLoadingClosed()

	local env = nil
	if getgenv then
		pcall(function()
			env = getgenv()
		end)
	end
	env = env or _G

	if env then
		env.YeLiquidLoading = nil
	end
end

do
	local env = nil

	if getgenv then
		pcall(function()
			env = getgenv()
		end)
	end

	env = env or _G

	if env then
		env.YeLiquidLoading = {
			SetStatus = function(_, title, content)
				setLoadingStatus(title, content)
			end,

			SetProgress = function(_, value, duration)
				setLoadingProgress(value, duration)
			end,

			FinishAndWait = function()
				finishLoadingAndWait()
			end,

			CloseAndWait = function(_, finalStatus)
				closeLoadingOnlyAndWait(finalStatus)
			end,
		}
	end
end

skipButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	if closing then
		return
	end

	setLoadingStatus("正在加载 UI库", "加载完成前暂时不能跳过")
end)

card.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.53)
card.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(430, 292)

task.defer(function()
	updateCardSize()

	progressBar.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)
	percentText.Text = "0%"

	tween(background, 0.45, {
		BackgroundTransparency = BACKGROUND_DIM_TRANSPARENCY
	})

	tween(card, 0.58, {
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		BackgroundTransparency = GLASS_CARD_TRANSPARENCY
	}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)

	task.delay(0.62, function()
		progressBar.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)
		percentText.Text = "0%"
		markLoadingIntroReady()
	end)
end)

--==================================================
-- 主动画循环
--==================================================

local startTime = os.clock()
local lastTime = os.clock()

local statusTimer = 0
local globalWind = 0

-- 状态文字现在由 UI库加载流程接管，不再显示随机神秘字符
local statusList = {}

renderConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	if not active then
		return
	end

	local now = os.clock()
	local dt = now - lastTime
	lastTime = now

	local progress = clamp01(progressBar.Size.X.Scale)

	-- 进度由后续 UI库加载流程控制：先到 60%，成功后再到 100%
	percentText.Text = tostring(math.floor(progress * 100 + 0.5)) .. "%"

	-- 状态文字由 FallbackNotify 搬到这里显示，不再拼接随机字符
	statusTimer += dt

	-- 雪点
	globalWind = math.sin(now * 0.26) * 0.00028

	for _, snow in ipairs(snowflakes) do
		local frame = snow.frame

		snow.y += snow.speed * (dt * 60)
		snow.x += (snow.drift + globalWind) * (dt * 60)

		local sway = math.sin(now * snow.swaySpeed + snow.phase) * snow.swayAmount
		local visualX = snow.x + sway

		if snow.y > 1.08 then
			snow.y = -0.08
			snow.x = math.random()

			if snow.layerName == "FarSnow" then
				frame.BackgroundTransparency = math.random(86, 95) / 100
			elseif snow.layerName == "MidSnow" then
				frame.BackgroundTransparency = math.random(74, 88) / 100
			else
				frame.BackgroundTransparency = math.random(60, 76) / 100
			end
		end

		if visualX < -0.08 then
			snow.x = 1.08
		elseif visualX > 1.08 then
			snow.x = -0.08
		end

		frame.Position = UDim2.fromScale(visualX, snow.y)
		frame.Rotation += snow.rotateSpeed * dt
	end

	-- 干净玻璃动态
	backgroundGradient.Rotation = 22 + math.sin(now * 0.16) * 3
	cardGradient.Rotation = 118 + math.sin(now * 0.24) * 4
	statusGradient.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(now * 0.7) * 0.06, 0)
	skipGradient.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(now * 0.7) * 0.06, 0)

	glassMist.BackgroundTransparency = GLASS_MIST_TRANSPARENCY + math.sin(now * 0.85) * 0.012

	-- 图标区域
	iconWrapGradient.Rotation = 45 + math.sin(now * 0.5) * 6
	iconRing.Rotation = (now * RING_ROTATION_SPEED) % 360

	-- 图标本体禁用旋转，避免不对称图标看起来发歪
	icon.Rotation = 0

	-- 进度条
	progressGradient.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(now * 1.0) * 0.1, 0)
	progressShine.Position = UDim2.fromScale((now * 0.66) % 1.35 - 0.18, 0.5)

	-- 极轻微颜色漂移
	local hue = 0.56 + math.sin(now * 0.08) * 0.018

	cardStroke.Color = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 0.1, 1)
	iconWrapStroke.Color = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 0.08, 1)
	iconRingStroke.Color = Color3.fromHSV(hue + 0.02, 0.1, 1)
	statusStroke.Color = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 0.08, 1)
	progressBackStroke.Color = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 0.06, 1)
	skipStroke.Color = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 0.08, 1)

	-- 不再按固定时间自动关闭，等待 UI库加载结果决定关闭时机
end)

--==================================================
-- 加载动画保持显示，后续 UI库加载流程会通过 YeLiquidLoading 接管进度与关闭
--==================================================

end)()
