--恭喜你找到了我的源码
-- by 夜
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

-- =================== 夜脚本错误解释 ===================
local function TranslateScriptError(errorText)
    local raw = tostring(errorText or "未知错误")
    local lower = raw:lower()

    local rules = {
        {
            keywords = { "attempt to index nil" },
            meaning = "脚本正在访问一个不存在的对象。常见原因：对象未加载、路径错误、变量为 nil。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "attempt to call a nil value" },
            meaning = "脚本正在调用一个不存在的函数。常见原因：函数名拼写错误、库未加载成功、函数未定义。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "attempt to perform arithmetic" },
            meaning = "脚本正在对非数字内容进行数学计算。常见原因：数值为 nil、字符串、table，或滑块返回值格式异常。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "attempt to concatenate" },
            meaning = "脚本正在拼接无法直接拼接的内容。常见原因：nil、table、Instance、Color3、Vector3 未经过 tostring 处理。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "is not a valid member" },
            meaning = "脚本访问了对象不存在的成员。常见原因：对象名称错误、大小写不一致、目标对象尚未生成。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "infinite yield possible" },
            meaning = "WaitForChild 等待时间过长。常见原因：目标对象不存在、名称写错、父级路径错误。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "out of local registers" },
            meaning = "同一作用域内 local 变量过多，超过 Lua/Luau 限制。常见解决方式：使用 (function() ... end)() 隔离大块代码。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "exceeded limit 200" },
            meaning = "local 寄存器数量超过 200。普通 do ... end 无法解决，需要使用函数作用域隔离。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "expected", "got" },
            meaning = "参数类型不匹配。报错中 expected 表示需要的类型，got 表示实际传入的类型。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "invalid argument" },
            meaning = "函数参数无效。常见原因：参数数量错误、类型错误、传入 nil。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "httpget" },
            meaning = "网络请求失败或 HttpGet 不可用。常见原因：链接失效、网络异常、执行环境不支持 HttpGet。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "loadstring" },
            meaning = "远程代码加载失败。常见原因：链接内容不是 Lua 代码、链接返回 404/HTML、代码存在语法问题。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "drawing" },
            meaning = "Drawing API 不可用或创建失败。常见原因：当前执行环境不支持 Drawing。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "hookmetamethod" },
            meaning = "hookmetamethod 不可用或调用失败。常见原因：执行环境不支持该函数，或参数格式不正确。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "newcclosure" },
            meaning = "newcclosure 不可用。常见原因：执行环境不支持该函数。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "nil with 'value'" },
            meaning = "脚本正在读取某个 .Value，但前面的对象为 nil。常见原因：Options 或 Toggles 中对应控件未创建成功。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "nil with 'enabledtoggle'" },
            meaning = "Toggles.EnabledToggle 不存在。常见原因：UI 初始化中断，或 Toggles 被局部作用域隐藏。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "nil with 'fovradiusslider'" },
            meaning = "Options.FOVRadiusSlider 不存在。常见原因：FOV 半径滑块创建失败，或 UI 代码提前报错中断。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "nil with 'ui settings'" },
            meaning = "Tabs['UI Settings'] 不存在。常见原因：UI Settings 标签页未创建，或 Tabs 被局部作用域隐藏。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "nil with 'tag'" },
            meaning = "脚本正在访问 Window.Tag，但 Window 可能没有创建成功。常见原因：WindUI:CreateWindow 返回 nil，或 UI 库初始化异常。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "nil with 'tab'" },
            meaning = "脚本正在访问 Window.Tab，但 Window 可能没有创建成功。常见原因：CreateWindow 失败后脚本继续执行。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "nil with 'toggle'" },
            meaning = "脚本正在访问某个 Tab.Toggle，但前面的 Tab 对象为 nil。常见原因：Tab 创建失败、变量名写错、或 UI 初始化中断。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "cannot resume dead coroutine" },
            meaning = "脚本尝试重新启动已经结束的协程。常见原因：coroutine 变量未重置或重复 resume。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "attempt to yield across metamethod" },
            meaning = "脚本在不允许等待的环境中执行了 wait、task.wait 或 WaitForChild。常见于 hook 或 metamethod 回调。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "unexpected symbol" },
            meaning = "脚本中出现 Lua 无法识别的符号。常见原因：多余字符、缺少逗号、注释写法错误、结构粘贴错位。"
        },
        {
            keywords = { "expected <eof>" },
            meaning = "脚本结尾结构异常。常见原因：多了 end、少了函数闭合、重复粘贴代码块。"
        },
    }

    local matched = {}

    for _, rule in ipairs(rules) do
        local ok = true

        for _, keyword in ipairs(rule.keywords) do
            if not string.find(lower, keyword, 1, true) then
                ok = false
                break
            end
        end

        if ok then
            table.insert(matched, rule.meaning)
        end
    end

    if #matched == 0 then
        return "暂未匹配到具体解释。建议复制报错原文反馈给作者"
    end

    return table.concat(matched, "\n\n")
end

-- =================== 可忽略资源错误判断 ===================
local function IsIgnorableImageAssetError(errorText)
    local text = tostring(errorText or ""):lower()

    local imageKeywords = {
        "failed to load asset",
        "image failed to load",
        "failed to load image",
        "unable to load image",
        "rbxassetid://",
        "rbxthumb://",
        "contentprovider",
        "preloadasync",
        "asset is not approved",
        "asset type does not match",
        "http error",
        "request failed",
        "assetdelivery",
        "thumbnail",
        "icon",
        "image",
        "decal",
        "texture",
    }

    local hasImageKeyword = false

    for _, keyword in ipairs(imageKeywords) do
        if string.find(text, keyword, 1, true) then
            hasImageKeyword = true
            break
        end
    end

    if not hasImageKeyword then
        return false
    end

    local realScriptErrorKeywords = {
        "attempt to index nil",
        "attempt to call a nil value",
        "attempt to perform arithmetic",
        "attempt to concatenate",
        "is not a valid member",
        "out of local registers",
        "cannot resume dead coroutine",
        "attempt to yield across metamethod",
        "expected",
        "got",
        "invalid argument",
    }

    for _, keyword in ipairs(realScriptErrorKeywords) do
        if string.find(text, keyword, 1, true) then
            return false
        end
    end

    return true
end

-- =================== GUI 父级获取 ===================
local function GetErrorGuiParent()
    local parent = nil

    pcall(function()
        if gethui then
            parent = gethui()
        end
    end)

    if parent then
        return parent
    end

    pcall(function()
        parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    end)

    if parent then
        return parent
    end

    local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local playerGui = player and player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")

    return playerGui
end

-- =================== 夜脚本统一关闭 / 清理 ===================
local function YeGetGuiParents()
    local parents = {}

    local function addParent(parent)
        if parent and not table.find(parents, parent) then
            table.insert(parents, parent)
        end
    end

    pcall(function()
        if gethui then
            addParent(gethui())
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        addParent(game:GetService("CoreGui"))
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
        local playerGui = player and player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        addParent(playerGui)
    end)

    return parents
end

local function YeDestroyGuiByName(guiName)
    guiName = tostring(guiName or "")

    if guiName == "" then
        return
    end

    for _, parent in ipairs(YeGetGuiParents()) do
        pcall(function()
            local obj = parent:FindFirstChild(guiName)
            if obj then
                obj:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end
end

local function YeDestroyDrawingObjects()
    local env = nil

    pcall(function()
        if getgenv then
            env = getgenv()
        end
    end)

    if type(env) ~= "table" then
        return
    end

    local drawingNames = {
        "FOVCircle",
        "AimbotFOVCircle",
        "SilentAimFOVCircle",
        "YeFOVCircle",
    }

    for _, name in ipairs(drawingNames) do
        pcall(function()
            local obj = env[name]
            if obj and type(obj.Remove) == "function" then
                obj:Remove()
            elseif obj and type(obj.Destroy) == "function" then
                obj:Destroy()
            end
            env[name] = nil
        end)
    end
end

local function YeDestroyWindowObject()
    local env = nil

    pcall(function()
        if getgenv then
            env = getgenv()
        end
    end)

    if type(env) ~= "table" then
        return
    end

    local window = env.YE_WINDUI_WINDOW or env.Window or env.YeWindow

    if not window then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        if type(window.Destroy) == "function" then
            window:Destroy()
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if type(window.Close) == "function" then
            window:Close()
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if type(window.CloseWindow) == "function" then
            window:CloseWindow()
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local main = window.UIElements and window.UIElements.Main

        if main then
            local current = main

            while current and current.Parent and not current:IsA("ScreenGui") do
                current = current.Parent
            end

            if current and current:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                current:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end)

    env.YE_WINDUI_WINDOW = nil
end

local function YeCloseWholeScript(skipErrorPopup)
    local env = nil

    pcall(function()
        if getgenv then
            env = getgenv()
        end
    end)

    if type(env) == "table" then
        env.YE_SCRIPT_CLOSED = true
        env.YE_SCRIPT_STOPPED = true
        env.UI_CLOSED = true

        -- 顺手关掉一些常见开关，免得 UI 没了后台还在加班，资本家看了都沉默。
        env.AimbotEnabled = false
        env.SilentAimEnabled = false
        env.ESPEnabled = false
        env.ForceChatEnabled = false
        env.FeatureDisplayEnabled = false
    end

    pcall(function()
        if type(env) == "table" and type(env.YE_DESTROY_HEALTH_DISPLAY) == "function" then
            env.YE_DESTROY_HEALTH_DISPLAY()
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if type(env) == "table" and type(env.YE_DESTROY_FEATURE_DISPLAY) == "function" then
            env.YE_DESTROY_FEATURE_DISPLAY()
        end
    end)

    pcall(YeDestroyWindowObject)
    pcall(YeDestroyDrawingObjects)

    -- 已知独立 UI
    YeDestroyGuiByName("FeatureDisplay")       -- 右上角夜脚本 / 功能显示
    YeDestroyGuiByName("SelfHealthDisplay")    -- 血量显示
    YeDestroyGuiByName("FOVCircle_UI")          -- FOV 圈
    YeDestroyGuiByName("YeScriptIntro")         -- 加载动画
    YeDestroyGuiByName("YeAntiSpyWarning")      -- 反 Spy 提示
    YeDestroyGuiByName("QQGroupUI")             -- QQ 群提示
    YeDestroyGuiByName("FullUIToggle")          -- 一键清屏独立 UI
    YeDestroyGuiByName("AimbotQuickSwitch")     -- 自瞄快捷开关，如果有
    YeDestroyGuiByName("AimbotQuickToggle")     -- 自瞄快捷开关，如果有

    -- 常见 WindUI 名字兜底
    YeDestroyGuiByName("WindUI")
    YeDestroyGuiByName("WindUI_Library")
    YeDestroyGuiByName("NightHub")
    YeDestroyGuiByName("NightHubUI")
    YeDestroyGuiByName("NightHub_Window")

    if not skipErrorPopup then
        YeDestroyGuiByName("ScriptLoadErrorPopup")
    end

    warn("[夜脚本] 已执行关闭脚本清理")
end

pcall(function()
    if getgenv then
        local env = getgenv()
        env.YE_CLOSE_WHOLE_SCRIPT = YeCloseWholeScript
    end
end)

-- =================== UI 工具 ===================
local function CreateErrorTextLabel(parent, text, position, size, color, font, textSize)
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Position = position
    label.Size = size
    label.Font = font or Enum.Font.Gotham
    label.TextSize = textSize or 13
    label.TextColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    label.TextWrapped = true
    label.Text = text
    label.TextTransparency = 1
    label.Parent = parent

    return label
end

local function CreateErrorTextBox(parent, text, position, size, textColor, font)
    local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
    box.Position = position
    box.Size = size
    box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(13, 13, 17)
    box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    box.MultiLine = true
    box.TextEditable = false
    box.TextWrapped = true
    box.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    box.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    box.Font = font or Enum.Font.Code
    box.TextSize = 12
    box.TextColor3 = textColor or Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
    box.TextTransparency = 1
    box.Text = text
    box.Parent = parent

    local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 7)
    padding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 7)
    padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
    padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
    padding.Parent = box

    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    corner.Parent = box

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Thickness = 1
    stroke.Transparency = 1
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    stroke.Parent = box

    return box
end

-- =================== 报错弹窗 ===================

local YE_AUTHOR_QQ = "3942770149"
local YE_RELOAD_SCRIPT_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ylt410/roblox-Script/refs/heads/main/yejiaoben"

local function YeIsWindowRelatedError(errorText)
    local lower = tostring(errorText or ""):lower()

    if lower:find("with 'tab'", 1, true) or lower:find('with "tab"', 1, true) then
        return true
    end

    if lower:find("attempt to index nil", 1, true)
        and (
            lower:find("tab", 1, true)
            or lower:find("tag", 1, true)
            or lower:find("window", 1, true)
            or lower:find("windui", 1, true)
            or lower:find("createwindow", 1, true)
        ) then
        return true
    end

    if lower:find("windui", 1, true)
        or lower:find("createwindow", 1, true)
        or lower:find("window:tab", 1, true)
        or lower:find("window:tag", 1, true)
        or lower:find("settheme", 1, true)
        or lower:find("backgroundimage", 1, true) then
        return true
    end

    return false
end

local function YeResetReloadFlags()
    pcall(function()
        if getgenv then
            local env = getgenv()

            if type(env) == "table" then
                env.YE_SCRIPT_CLOSED = nil
                env.YE_SCRIPT_STOPPED = nil
                env.UI_CLOSED = false

                env.AimbotEnabled = false
                env.SilentAimEnabled = false
                env.ESPEnabled = false
                env.ForceChatEnabled = false
                env.FeatureDisplayEnabled = false
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function YeTweenBoxStroke(box, transparency)
    pcall(function()
        local stroke = box and box:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")

        if stroke then
            TweenService:Create(stroke, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                Transparency = transparency
            }):Play()
        end
    end)
end

local function ShowScriptLoadError(errorText)
    local rawError = tostring(errorText or "未知错误")
    local isWindowRelatedError = YeIsWindowRelatedError(rawError)
    local meaning = TranslateScriptError(rawError)

    if isWindowRelatedError then
        meaning = meaning
            .. "\n\n已识别到可能是窗口 / UI 创建异常。"
            .. "\n你可以点击“重新加载”，它会先清理旧 UI，再重新执行脚本。"
            .. "\n如果重新加载后还是失败，请联系作者 QQ：" .. YE_AUTHOR_QQ
    end

    warn("[执行错误]\n" .. rawError .. "\n\n参考此处解决：\n" .. meaning)

    local parent = GetErrorGuiParent()

    if not parent then
        return
    end

    local oldGui = parent:FindFirstChild("ScriptLoadErrorPopup")
    if oldGui then
        oldGui:Destroy()
    end

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "ScriptLoadErrorPopup"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling

    pcall(function()
        if protectgui then
            protectgui(gui)
        end
    end)

    gui.Parent = parent

    local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
    holder.Name = "Holder"
    holder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.53)
    holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(500, 330)
    holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    holder.Parent = gui

    local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
    scale.Scale = 0.92
    scale.Parent = holder

    local shadow = Instance.new("Frame")
    shadow.Name = "Shadow"
    shadow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 10)
    shadow.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    shadow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    shadow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    shadow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    shadow.Parent = holder

    local shadowCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    shadowCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)
    shadowCorner.Parent = shadow

    local main = Instance.new("Frame")
    main.Name = "Main"
    main.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    main.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 20, 24)
    main.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    main.BorderSizePixel = 0
    main.Parent = holder

    local mainCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    mainCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)
    mainCorner.Parent = main

    local mainStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    mainStroke.Thickness = 1
    mainStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90)
    mainStroke.Transparency = 1
    mainStroke.Parent = main

    local topBar = Instance.new("Frame")
    topBar.Name = "TopBar"
    topBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 48)
    topBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(58, 26, 32)
    topBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    topBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    topBar.Parent = main

    local topCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    topCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)
    topCorner.Parent = topBar

    local topFix = Instance.new("Frame")
    topFix.Name = "TopFix"
    topFix.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -14)
    topFix.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14)
    topFix.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(58, 26, 32)
    topFix.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    topFix.BorderSizePixel = 0
    topFix.Parent = topBar

    local accent = Instance.new("Frame")
    accent.Name = "Accent"
    accent.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 3)
    accent.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -3)
    accent.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 82, 82)
    accent.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    accent.BorderSizePixel = 0
    accent.Parent = topBar

    local title = CreateErrorTextLabel(
        topBar,
        "脚本执行出错",
        UDim2.fromOffset(18, 0),
        UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, 0),
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 235, 235),
        Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        16
    )

    local close = Instance.new("TextButton")
    close.Name = "Close"
    close.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
    close.Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0)
    close.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30)
    close.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(64, 30, 36)
    close.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    close.Text = "×"
    close.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    close.TextSize = 22
    close.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 225, 225)
    close.TextTransparency = 1
    close.BorderSizePixel = 0
    close.Parent = topBar

    local closeCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    closeCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    closeCorner.Parent = close

    local explanationTitle = CreateErrorTextLabel(
        main,
        "参考此处解决：",
        UDim2.fromOffset(18, 66),
        UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 18),
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 150),
        Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        13
    )

    local explanationBox = CreateErrorTextBox(
        main,
        meaning,
        UDim2.fromOffset(18, 88),
        UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 76),
        Color3.fromRGB(245, 225, 190),
        Enum.Font.Gotham
    )

    local rawTitle = CreateErrorTextLabel(
        main,
        "报错原文",
        UDim2.fromOffset(18, 175),
        isWindowRelatedError and UDim2.new(1, -360, 0, 18) or UDim2.new(1, -230, 0, 18),
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 170),
        Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        13
    )

    local closeScript = Instance.new("TextButton")
    closeScript.Name = "CloseScript"
    closeScript.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    closeScript.Position = isWindowRelatedError and UDim2.new(1, -246, 0, 170) or UDim2.new(1, -138, 0, 170)
    closeScript.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(100, 28)
    closeScript.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(115, 45, 52)
    closeScript.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    closeScript.BorderSizePixel = 0
    closeScript.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    closeScript.TextSize = 12
    closeScript.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    closeScript.TextTransparency = 1
    closeScript.Text = "关闭脚本"
    closeScript.Parent = main

    local closeScriptCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    closeScriptCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    closeScriptCorner.Parent = closeScript

    local reloadScript = nil

    if isWindowRelatedError then
        reloadScript = Instance.new("TextButton")
        reloadScript.Name = "ReloadScript"
        reloadScript.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
        reloadScript.Position = UDim2.new(1, -138, 0, 170)
        reloadScript.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(100, 28)
        reloadScript.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(75, 115, 255)
        reloadScript.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        reloadScript.BorderSizePixel = 0
        reloadScript.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        reloadScript.TextSize = 12
        reloadScript.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        reloadScript.TextTransparency = 1
        reloadScript.Text = "重新加载"
        reloadScript.Parent = main

        local reloadScriptCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        reloadScriptCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        reloadScriptCorner.Parent = reloadScript
    end

    local copy = Instance.new("TextButton")
    copy.Name = "Copy"
    copy.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    copy.Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 170)
    copy.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(112, 28)
    copy.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 82, 82)
    copy.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    copy.BorderSizePixel = 0
    copy.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    copy.TextSize = 12
    copy.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    copy.TextTransparency = 1
    copy.Text = "复制原文"
    copy.Parent = main

    local copyCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    copyCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    copyCorner.Parent = copy

    local rawBox = CreateErrorTextBox(
        main,
        rawError,
        UDim2.fromOffset(18, 204),
        UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 78),
        Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235),
        Enum.Font.Code
    )

    local hintText = "完整错误已输出到控制台 warn。点“关闭脚本”会清理主UI、血量显示和右上角夜脚本标。"

    if isWindowRelatedError then
        hintText = "识别到窗口 / UI 异常。点“重新加载”会先清理旧 UI 再重新执行；再次失败请联系作者 QQ：" .. YE_AUTHOR_QQ
    end

    local hint = CreateErrorTextLabel(
        main,
        hintText,
        UDim2.fromOffset(18, 292),
        UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 32),
        Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 170),
        Enum.Font.Gotham,
        11
    )

    local closing = false

    local function PlayCloseAnimation()
        if closing then
            return
        end

        closing = true

        local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)

        TweenService:Create(scale, tweenInfo, {
            Scale = 0.9,
        }):Play()

        TweenService:Create(holder, tweenInfo, {
            Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.53),
        }):Play()

        TweenService:Create(main, tweenInfo, {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        }):Play()

        TweenService:Create(shadow, tweenInfo, {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        }):Play()

        TweenService:Create(mainStroke, tweenInfo, {
            Transparency = 1,
        }):Play()

        TweenService:Create(topBar, tweenInfo, {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        }):Play()

        TweenService:Create(topFix, tweenInfo, {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        }):Play()

        TweenService:Create(accent, tweenInfo, {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        }):Play()

        TweenService:Create(close, tweenInfo, {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            TextTransparency = 1,
        }):Play()

        TweenService:Create(closeScript, tweenInfo, {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            TextTransparency = 1,
        }):Play()

        if reloadScript then
            TweenService:Create(reloadScript, tweenInfo, {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextTransparency = 1,
            }):Play()
        end

        TweenService:Create(copy, tweenInfo, {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            TextTransparency = 1,
        }):Play()

        YeTweenBoxStroke(explanationBox, 1)
        YeTweenBoxStroke(rawBox, 1)

        for _, item in ipairs({
            title,
            explanationTitle,
            rawTitle,
            explanationBox,
            rawBox,
            hint,
        }) do
            pcall(function()
                TweenService:Create(item, tweenInfo, {
                    TextTransparency = 1,
                    BackgroundTransparency = item:IsA("TextBox") and 1 or 1,
                }):Play()
            end)
        end

        task.delay(0.22, function()
            if gui and gui.Parent then
                gui:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end

    close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(PlayCloseAnimation)

    closeScript.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        closeScript.Text = "关闭中"
        closeScript.Active = false
        closeScript.AutoButtonColor = false

        YeCloseWholeScript(true)

        PlayCloseAnimation()
    end)

    if reloadScript then
        local reloading = false

        reloadScript.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if reloading then
                return
            end

            reloading = true

            reloadScript.Text = "重新加载中"
            reloadScript.Active = false
            reloadScript.AutoButtonColor = false

            closeScript.Active = false
            closeScript.AutoButtonColor = false

            copy.Active = false
            copy.AutoButtonColor = false

            YeCloseWholeScript(true)
            PlayCloseAnimation()

            task.delay(0.32, function()
                YeResetReloadFlags()

                local ok, err = pcall(function()
                    local source = game:HttpGet(YE_RELOAD_SCRIPT_URL)

                    if type(source) ~= "string" or #source < 20 then
                        error("重新加载失败：远程脚本内容为空或过短")
                    end

                    if type(loadstring) ~= "function" then
                        error("重新加载失败：当前环境不存在 loadstring")
                    end

                    local fn, compileErr = loadstring(source)

                    if type(fn) ~= "function" then
                        error("重新加载失败：loadstring 编译失败：" .. tostring(compileErr))
                    end

                    fn()
                end)

                if not ok then
                    warn("[夜脚本] 重新加载失败：", tostring(err))

                    ShowScriptLoadError(
                        "重新加载失败：" .. tostring(err)
                        .. "\n\n如果再次加载失败，请联系作者 QQ：" .. YE_AUTHOR_QQ
                    )
                end
            end)
        end)
    end

    copy.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local copied = false

        pcall(function()
            if setclipboard then
                setclipboard(rawError)
                copied = true
            end
        end)

        if copied then
            copy.Text = "已复制"
        else
            copy.Text = "无法复制"
        end

        task.delay(1.1, function()
            if copy and copy.Parent then
                copy.Text = "复制原文"
            end
        end)
    end)

    local openTween = TweenInfo.new(0.26, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    local fadeTween = TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)

    TweenService:Create(scale, openTween, {
        Scale = 1,
    }):Play()

    TweenService:Create(holder, openTween, {
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
    }):Play()

    TweenService:Create(main, fadeTween, {
        BackgroundTransparency = 0,
    }):Play()

    TweenService:Create(shadow, fadeTween, {
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.68,
    }):Play()

    TweenService:Create(mainStroke, fadeTween, {
        Transparency = 0.15,
    }):Play()

    TweenService:Create(topBar, fadeTween, {
        BackgroundTransparency = 0,
    }):Play()

    TweenService:Create(topFix, fadeTween, {
        BackgroundTransparency = 0,
    }):Play()

    TweenService:Create(accent, fadeTween, {
        BackgroundTransparency = 0,
    }):Play()

    TweenService:Create(close, fadeTween, {
        BackgroundTransparency = 0,
        TextTransparency = 0,
    }):Play()

    TweenService:Create(closeScript, fadeTween, {
        BackgroundTransparency = 0,
        TextTransparency = 0,
    }):Play()

    if reloadScript then
        TweenService:Create(reloadScript, fadeTween, {
            BackgroundTransparency = 0,
            TextTransparency = 0,
        }):Play()
    end

    TweenService:Create(copy, fadeTween, {
        BackgroundTransparency = 0,
        TextTransparency = 0,
    }):Play()

    YeTweenBoxStroke(explanationBox, 0.72)
    YeTweenBoxStroke(rawBox, 0.72)

    for _, item in ipairs({
        title,
        explanationTitle,
        rawTitle,
        explanationBox,
        rawBox,
        hint,
    }) do
        pcall(function()
            TweenService:Create(item, fadeTween, {
                TextTransparency = 0,
                BackgroundTransparency = item:IsA("TextBox") and 0 or 1,
            }):Play()
        end)
    end
end
local __ScriptSuccess, __ScriptError = xpcall(function()
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
--[[ =================== Anti-Deobfuscation Core v2 ===================

local _w = 0
local _t0 = os.clock()
local _seed = math.random(1000, 9999)
local _threshold = math.floor(_seed / _seed) + math.floor(2 * 1) -- == 3，不明显

-- =================== 内部flag（混淆化） ===================
local function _f(x)
    _w = _w + (x == x and 1 or 0)
end

-- =================== 检测1：玩家链 ===================
local function _c1()
    local ok, r = pcall(function()
        local p = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
        return p and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") ~= nil
    end)
    if not (ok and r) then _f(1) end
end

-- =================== 检测2：Instance真实性 ===================
local function _c2()
    local ok, r = pcall(function()
        local g = game
        return typeof(g) == "Instance"
            and g:IsA("DataModel")
            and g.ClassName == "DataModel"
    end)
    if not (ok and r) then _f(2) end
end

-- =================== 检测3：服务一致性 ===================
local function _c3()
    local ok, r = pcall(function()
        local ws1 = game:GetService("Workspace")
        local ws2 = workspace
        return ws1 == ws2 and ws1 ~= nil
    end)
    if not (ok and r) then _f(3) end
end

-- =================== 检测4：身份 ===================
local function _c4()
    local ok, r = pcall(function()
        local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
        return lp ~= nil
            and typeof(lp.UserId) == "number"
            and lp.UserId > 0
            and game.JobId ~= ""
    end)
    if not (ok and r) then _f(4) end
end

-- =================== 检测5：执行环境API ===================
local function _c5()
    -- executor应该有这些，但反混淆bot环境里不一定有
    local ok, r = pcall(function()
        return type(getfenv) == "function"
            and type(setfenv) == "function"
    end)
    if not (ok and r) then _f(5) end
end

-- =================== 检测6：时序异常（bot通常极快） ===================
local function _c6()
    local t1 = os.clock()
    -- 正常Roblox客户端初始化需要一定时间
    -- bot脚本通常在ms级别内跑完所有检测
    local elapsed = t1 - _t0
    if elapsed < 0.001 then
        _f(6) -- 极不可能在真实客户端里这么快
    end
end

-- =================== 检测7：DataModel深度验证 ===================
local function _c7()
    local ok, r = pcall(function()
        local rs = game:GetService("RunService")
        -- 真实客户端：IsClient或IsStudio必有一个为true
        return rs:IsClient() or rs:IsStudio()
    end)
    if not (ok and r) then _f(7) end
end

-- =================== 检测8：随机延迟噪声（对抗静态分析） ===================
local _noise = (function()
    local s = 0
    for i = 1, 10 do s = s + math.sin(i) end
    return s > 0 and 0 or 1 -- 始终为0，但计算过程让静态分析困难
end)()

-- =================== 运行检测 ===================
_c1(); _c2(); _c3(); _c4(); _c5(); _c6(); _c7()

-- =================== 卡死核心 ===================
local function _melt()
    -- 先申请大量内存
    task.spawn(function()
        local bomb = {}
        while true do
            for i = 1, 500 do
                bomb[#bomb+1] = string.rep("x", 1024)
            end
            task.wait(0) -- 让它继续占用帧
        end
    end)

    -- 多线程CPU压力
    for _ = 1, 16 do
        task.spawn(function()
            local x = 0
            while true do
                x = math.sqrt(x + math.random())
            end
        end)
    end

    -- 主线程锁
    local _lock = true
    while _lock do
        local _x = _noise
        for i = 1, 1e6 do _x = _x + i * 0.000001 end
        _lock = _x >= 0 or _x < 0 -- 永远true
    end
end

-- =================== 延迟判定（迷惑分析者） ===================
-- 不立即触发，等一帧后判定，让bot以为通过了
task.defer(function()
    if _w + _noise >= _threshold then
        -- 再等一小段随机时间，增加不确定性
        task.wait(math.random() * 0.5)
        _melt()
    end
end)

-- =================== 分布式守卫（插入你的关键逻辑前） ===================
local function guard()
    if _w + _noise >= _threshold then
        task.spawn(_melt)
        error("") -- 中断当前线程
    end
end

-- =================== 使用方法 ===================
-- 在你的主要功能代码的关键节点前调用：
-- guard()
-- 例如：
-- guard()
-- local aimbot = loadAimbot()
-- guard()
-- local ui = buildUI()
]]
do
    local url = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ylt410/roblox-Script/refs/heads/main/公告"
    local timeout = 3

    local finished = false
    local expired = false

    task.delay(timeout, function()
        if not finished then
            expired = true
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        local okGet, source = pcall(function()
            return game:HttpGet(url)
        end)

        finished = true

        if expired then
            return
        end

        if not okGet or type(source) ~= "string" or source == "" then
            return
        end

        local okCompile, fn = pcall(function()
            return loadstring(source)
        end)

        if expired then
            return
        end

        if not okCompile or type(fn) ~= "function" then
            return
        end

        pcall(function()
            fn()
        end)
    end)
end
local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")

local WINDUI_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ylt410/roblox-Script/refs/heads/main/5pawVUk=.txt"

local WINDUI_CACHE_FOLDER = "NightHub_Cache"
local WINDUI_CACHE_FILE = WINDUI_CACHE_FOLDER .. "/57yT5a2YVUnlupM="

-- 等游戏基础加载，避免 SetCore 太早调用失败
if not game:IsLoaded() then
    pcall(function()
        game.Loaded:Wait()
    end)
end

local function FallbackNotify(title, content, duration, forceNative)
    title = tostring(title or "通知")
    content = tostring(content or "")
    duration = duration or 4

    if not forceNative then
        local loader = nil

        pcall(function()
            local env = getgenv and getgenv() or _G
            loader = env and env.YeLiquidLoading
        end)

        if type(loader) == "table" and type(loader.SetStatus) == "function" then
            local ok = pcall(function()
                loader:SetStatus(title, content)
            end)

            if ok then
                return
            end
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        for _ = 1, 30 do
            local ok = pcall(function()
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = title,
                    Text = content,
                    Duration = duration
                })
            end)

            if ok then
                return
            end

            task.wait(0.2)
        end

        warn("[通知失败] " .. title .. " | " .. content)
    end)
end

local function RunWithTimeout(name, timeout, callback)
    local finished = false
    local success = false
    local result = nil
    local err = nil

    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, res = pcall(callback)

        success = ok

        if ok then
            result = res
        else
            err = res
        end

        finished = true
    end)

    local startTime = os.clock()

    while not finished and os.clock() - startTime < timeout do
        task.wait(0.05)
    end

    if not finished then
        return false, nil, tostring(name) .. "超时"
    end

    if not success then
        return false, nil, tostring(err)
    end

    return true, result, nil
end

local function CompileScript(content)
    local minimumContentLength = 10000

    if type(content) ~= "string" then
        return false, nil, "脚本内容不是字符串"
    end

    if #content < minimumContentLength then
        return false, nil, "脚本内容长度异常：" .. tostring(#content)
            .. " 字节，最低要求：" .. tostring(minimumContentLength) .. " 字节"
    end

    local ok, fnOrErr = pcall(function()
        return loadstring(content)
    end)

    if not ok then
        return false, nil, "loadstring 编译异常：" .. tostring(fnOrErr)
    end

    if type(fnOrErr) ~= "function" then
        return false, nil, "loadstring 没有返回函数"
    end

    return true, fnOrErr, nil
end
local function CanUseFileCache()
    return type(isfile) == "function"
        and type(readfile) == "function"
        and type(writefile) == "function"
end

local function EnsureCacheFolder()
    if type(isfolder) == "function" and type(makefolder) == "function" then
        local ok, exists = pcall(function()
            return isfolder(WINDUI_CACHE_FOLDER)
        end)

        if ok and not exists then
            pcall(function()
                makefolder(WINDUI_CACHE_FOLDER)
            end)
        end
    end
end

local function ReadWindUICache()
    if not CanUseFileCache() then
        return nil, "当前环境不支持文件缓存"
    end

    local ok, exists = pcall(function()
        return isfile(WINDUI_CACHE_FILE)
    end)

    if not ok or not exists then
        return nil, "没有缓存UI"
    end

    local readOk, content = pcall(function()
        return readfile(WINDUI_CACHE_FILE)
    end)

    if not readOk then
        return nil, "读取缓存UI失败：" .. tostring(content)
    end

    if type(content) ~= "string" or #content < 50 then
        return nil, "缓存UI内容异常"
    end

    return content, nil
end

local function WriteWindUICache(content)
    if not CanUseFileCache() then
        return false, "当前环境不支持文件缓存"
    end

    if type(content) ~= "string" or #content < 50 then
        return false, "拒绝写入异常UI内容"
    end

    EnsureCacheFolder()

    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        writefile(WINDUI_CACHE_FILE, content)
    end)

    if not ok then
        return false, "写入缓存UI失败：" .. tostring(err)
    end

    return true, nil
end

local function ExecuteWindUIContent(content, executeTimeout)
    local compileOk, compiledFunc, compileErr = CompileScript(content)

    if not compileOk then
        return false, nil, compileErr
    end

    local runOk, lib, runErr = RunWithTimeout("UI库执行", executeTimeout, function()
        return compiledFunc()
    end)

    if not runOk then
        return false, nil, runErr
    end

    if type(lib) ~= "table" then
        return false, nil, "UI库执行完成，但返回值不是 table：" .. typeof(lib)
    end

    if type(lib.CreateWindow) ~= "function" then
        return false, nil, "UI库缺少 CreateWindow 方法"
    end

    return true, lib, nil
end

local function tryLoadWindUI(retries, httpTimeout, executeTimeout)
    retries = retries or 5
    httpTimeout = httpTimeout or 7
    executeTimeout = executeTimeout or 7

    local lastErr = "未知错误"

    -- 1. 优先读取缓存
    FallbackNotify("正在读取缓存", "正在检查本地 UI库缓存", 2)

    local cachedContent, cacheErr = ReadWindUICache()

    if cachedContent then
        FallbackNotify("已读取缓存UI继续执行", "正在使用本地缓存UI", 3)
        print("[WindUI缓存] 已读取缓存UI")

        local cacheOk, cacheLib, cacheRunErr = ExecuteWindUIContent(cachedContent, executeTimeout)

        if cacheOk and cacheLib then
            return cacheLib, nil
        end

        lastErr = "缓存UI不可用：" .. tostring(cacheRunErr)
        warn("[WindUI缓存] " .. lastErr)

        FallbackNotify("缓存UI异常", "缓存损坏，正在重新下载UI", 3)
    else
        warn("[WindUI缓存] " .. tostring(cacheErr))
    end

    -- 2. 缓存没有或缓存坏了，再下载
    for i = 1, retries do
        print("[WindUI] 外链加载尝试 #" .. i)

        FallbackNotify(
            "加载中",
            "正在加载 UI库，第 " .. i .. "/" .. retries .. " 次",
            2
        )

        -- 不再加时间戳，减少代理/CDN抽风概率
        local requestUrl = WINDUI_URL

        local getOk, content, getErr = RunWithTimeout("HttpGet", httpTimeout, function()
            return game:HttpGet(requestUrl)
        end)

if not getOk then
    lastErr = getErr

elseif type(content) ~= "string" then
    lastErr = "HttpGet 返回内容不是字符串"

elseif #content < 10000 then
    lastErr = "远程UI库内容长度异常："
        .. tostring(#content)
        .. " 字节，最低要求：10000 字节"

else
    local runOk, lib, runErr = ExecuteWindUIContent(content, executeTimeout)
            if runOk and lib then
                local saveOk, saveErr = WriteWindUICache(content)

                if saveOk then
                    print("[WindUI缓存] UI库已写入缓存")
                    FallbackNotify("UI缓存完成", "UI库已保存，下次优先本地读取", 2)
                else
                    warn("[WindUI缓存] " .. tostring(saveErr))
                end

                return lib, nil
            end

            lastErr = runErr or "UI库执行失败"
        end

        warn("[WindUI] 第 " .. i .. " 次加载失败：" .. tostring(lastErr))

        FallbackNotify(
            "加载重试",
            "UI库第 " .. i .. " 次加载失败：" .. tostring(lastErr),
            3
        )

        task.wait(math.min(1 + i * 0.2, 2))
    end

    return nil, lastErr
end

-- 修改这里：控制加载动画从 0% 到 60% 的时间，单位：秒
local function getUILibraryLoadTo60Time()
    return 0.35
end

-- 注入提示：加载动画先到 60%，然后开始真正加载 UI库
do
    local loader = nil

    pcall(function()
        local env = getgenv and getgenv() or _G
        loader = env and env.YeLiquidLoading
    end)

    if type(loader) == "table" and type(loader.SetStatus) == "function" and type(loader.SetProgress) == "function" then
        local loadTo60Time = tonumber(getUILibraryLoadTo60Time()) or 1
        loadTo60Time = math.max(loadTo60Time, 0)

        loader:SetStatus("正在加载 UI库", "准备读取缓存和网络资源")
        loader:SetProgress(0.6, loadTo60Time)
        task.wait(loadTo60Time)
    else
        FallbackNotify("夜脚本", "基础脚本已加载，正在加载 UI库", 3)
        FallbackNotify("加载中", "开始载入 UI，网络差会自动重试", 3)
    end
end

-- 启动加载
local WindUI, loadErr = tryLoadWindUI(5, 7, 7)

-- 加载失败处理
if not WindUI then
    do
        local loader = nil

        pcall(function()
            local env = getgenv and getgenv() or _G
            loader = env and env.YeLiquidLoading
        end)

        if type(loader) == "table" and type(loader.CloseAndWait) == "function" then
            pcall(function()
                loader:SetStatus("UI库加载失败", "正在关闭加载界面")
                loader:CloseAndWait("UI库加载失败")
            end)
        end
    end

    FallbackNotify(
        "加载失败",
        "UI库加载失败，请更换网络或重新注入\n错误：" .. tostring(loadErr),
        10,
        true
    )

    warn("[WindUI] 最终加载失败：" .. tostring(loadErr))
    return
end

-- 成功提示：先让加载动画显示完成并关闭，再创建主 UI，避免两个界面撞在一起
do
    local loader = nil

    pcall(function()
        local env = getgenv and getgenv() or _G
        loader = env and env.YeLiquidLoading
    end)

    if type(loader) == "table" and type(loader.FinishAndWait) == "function" then
        local okFinish, finishErr = pcall(function()
            loader:FinishAndWait()
        end)

        if not okFinish then
            warn("[加载动画] 关闭失败：" .. tostring(finishErr))
        end
    end
end

print("[WindUI] 加载成功")

local BgFile = "NightBackground.txt"
local BgPresetFile = "NightBgPreset.txt"
local BgTransFile = "NightBgTrans.txt"

local BackgroundEnabled = false
local BgTransValue = 0.55
local SavedBgPreset = "默认背景"

local BgImage = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ylt410/Liquid-glass-script/refs/heads/main/E7B068E4-0859-420F-A6B1-AF519C804C39.png"

pcall(function()
    if isfile and isfile(BgFile) then
        local saved = readfile(BgFile)
        BackgroundEnabled = saved ~= "false"
    end
end)

pcall(function()
    if isfile and isfile(BgTransFile) then
        local saved = tonumber(readfile(BgTransFile))
        if saved then
            BgTransValue = math.clamp(saved, 0, 1)
        end
    end
end)

pcall(function()
    if isfile and isfile(BgPresetFile) then
        local saved = readfile(BgPresetFile)
        if saved and saved ~= "" then
            SavedBgPreset = saved
        end
    end
end)

if BgPresets and BgPresets[SavedBgPreset] then
    BgImage = BgPresets[SavedBgPreset]
end

        },
    }, {
        Rotation = 0,
    }),

    Background = Color3.fromHex("#070A12"),

--====================================================
-- 主题读取 / 保存
--====================================================

local ThemeFile = "NightTheme.txt"
local CurrentTheme = "Dark"

local ThemeList = {
    "Dark",
    "Light",
    "BlackGold",
    "Aurora",
    "Cyan",
    "Blue",
}

local ValidThemes = {}

for _, themeName in ipairs(ThemeList) do
    ValidThemes[themeName] = true
end

pcall(function()
    if isfile and isfile(ThemeFile) then
        local saved = readfile(ThemeFile)

        if saved then
            saved = tostring(saved):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        end

        if saved and saved ~= "" and ValidThemes[saved] then
            CurrentTheme = saved
        end
    end
end)

local function SaveTheme(themeName)
    if not ValidThemes[themeName] then
        return false
    end

    CurrentTheme = themeName

    pcall(function()
        if writefile then
            writefile(ThemeFile, themeName)
        end
    end)

    return true
end

local function SetAndSaveTheme(themeName)
    if not ValidThemes[themeName] then
        themeName = "Dark"
    end

    CurrentTheme = themeName

    pcall(function()
        WindUI:SetTheme(themeName)
    end)

    SaveTheme(themeName)
end

-- =================== 强制解锁第三人称 ===================

local ThirdPersonUnlock = {
    Enabled = false,
    Connection = nil
}
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local function ApplyUnlock()

    pcall(function()
        -- ⭐核心：解除锁定
        if LocalPlayer.CameraMode ~= Enum.CameraMode.Classic then
            LocalPlayer.CameraMode = Enum.CameraMode.Classic
        end

        -- ⭐允许缩放
        LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = 0.5
        LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 50
    end)
end

local function EnableUnlock()

    if ThirdPersonUnlock.Connection then return end

    ThirdPersonUnlock.Enabled = true

    ApplyUnlock()

    -- ⭐持续对抗游戏锁定（关键）
    ThirdPersonUnlock.Connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not ThirdPersonUnlock.Enabled then return end
        ApplyUnlock()
    end)
end

local function DisableUnlock()

    ThirdPersonUnlock.Enabled = false

    if ThirdPersonUnlock.Connection then
        ThirdPersonUnlock.Connection:Disconnect()
        ThirdPersonUnlock.Connection = nil
    end

    -- ⚠️ 不强制改回（避免干扰游戏）
end

-- ⭐重生修复
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.wait(0.5)
    if ThirdPersonUnlock.Enabled then
        ApplyUnlock()
    end
end)
-- =================== 功能显示系统（终极进阶版） ===================

local function NightLoadBool(fileName, defaultValue)
    local value = defaultValue

    pcall(function()
        if isfile and readfile and isfile(fileName) then
            local saved = tostring(readfile(fileName))

            if saved == "true" then
                value = true
            elseif saved == "false" then
                value = false
            end
        end
    end)

    return value
end

local function NightSaveBool(fileName, value)
    pcall(function()
        if writefile then
            writefile(fileName, value and "true" or "false")
        end
    end)
end

local FeatureDisplayFile = "NightFeatureDisplayEnabled.txt"

local FeatureDisplayEnabled = NightLoadBool(FeatureDisplayFile, true)
local EnabledFeatures = {}
local FeatureItems = {}
local FeatureGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
FeatureGui.Name = "FeatureDisplay"
local parented = pcall(function()
	FeatureGui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
end)

if not parented then
	FeatureGui.Parent = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
end

local Container = Instance.new("Frame")
Container.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
Container.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 10)
Container.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 300)
Container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
Container.Parent = FeatureGui

local UIList = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
UIList.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
UIList.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
UIList.Parent = Container

local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

local function Rainbow()
    return Color3.fromHSV((tick()*0.25)%1,1,1)
end

-- =================== 刷新 ===================
local function RefreshFeatureUI()

    Container.Visible = FeatureDisplayEnabled
    if not FeatureDisplayEnabled then return end

    -- 删除旧的（带动画）
    for name, item in pairs(FeatureItems) do
        if not table.find(EnabledFeatures, name) then

if item then

    TweenService:Create(item, TweenInfo.new(
        0.25,
        Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
        Enum.EasingDirection.In
    ), {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, item.Size.X.Offset, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1
    }):Play()

    for _, v in pairs(item:GetChildren()) do
        if v:IsA("TextLabel") then
            TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                TextTransparency = 1,
                TextStrokeTransparency = 1
            }):Play()
        end
    end

    task.delay(0.25, function()
        if item then item:Destroy() end
    end)
end
            FeatureItems[name] = nil
        end
    end

    -- 添加新UI
    for _, name in ipairs(EnabledFeatures) do

        if FeatureItems[name] then continue end

        local textSize = TextService:GetTextSize(
            name, 14, Enum.Font.SourceSansBold, Vector2.new(1000,20)
        )

        local width = textSize.X + 10

        local item = Instance.new("Frame")
        item.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 16)
        item.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        item.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0,0,0)
        item.BorderSizePixel = 0
        item.Parent = Container

        Instance.new("UICorner", item).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)

        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1,-6,1,0)
        label.Position = UDim2.new(0,3,0,0)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Text = name
        label.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        label.TextSize = 14
        label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        label.TextTransparency = 1
        label.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
        label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0,0,0)
        label.Parent = item

        -- 🌈 彩虹
        task.spawn(function()
            while label.Parent do
                label.TextColor3 = Rainbow()
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end)

        -- 飞入动画
        task.spawn(function()

            task.wait()

            TweenService:Create(item, TweenInfo.new(
                0.4,
                Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
                Enum.EasingDirection.Out
            ), {
                Size = UDim2.new(0, width, 0, 20),
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
            }):Play()

            task.wait(0.1)

            TweenService:Create(label, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {
                TextTransparency = 0,
                TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
            }):Play()

        end)

        FeatureItems[name] = item
    end
end

-- =================== 添加 ===================
local function AddFeature(name)

    if name == "夜脚本" then
        for i, v in ipairs(EnabledFeatures) do
            if v == "夜脚本" then
                table.remove(EnabledFeatures, i)
                break
            end
        end
        table.insert(EnabledFeatures, 1, name)
    else
        if not table.find(EnabledFeatures, name) then
            table.insert(EnabledFeatures, name)
        end
    end

    RefreshFeatureUI()
end

-- =================== 删除 ===================
local function RemoveFeature(name)
    for i, v in ipairs(EnabledFeatures) do
        if v == name then
            table.remove(EnabledFeatures, i)
            break
        end
    end
    RefreshFeatureUI()
end

    if not part then

            or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

local TargetWalkSpeed = 16
local SpeedEnabled = false
-- 新增：自定义跳跃状态和数值
local CustomJumpEnabled = false
local CustomJumpValue = 50
local Connections = {}

    Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
end)

    repeat task.wait() LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer until LocalPlayer

-- ===================
-- 新增：强制锁定跳跃高度（解决重生失效 & 服务器限制）
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if CustomJumpEnabled then
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if char then
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                -- 核心修复：同时接管两种跳跃模式，强制系统听你的
                hum.UseJumpPower = true 
                hum.JumpPower = CustomJumpValue
                -- 为了防止个别极端防作弊检测，顺便把 JumpHeight 也按比例改了 (通常 Power 50 对应 Height 7.2)
                hum.JumpHeight = CustomJumpValue * (7.2 / 50) 
            end
        end
    end
end)
-- ================= 速度锁定系统（防重生失效） =================
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()

    if not SpeedEnabled then return end

    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end

    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return end

    -- ⭐防服务器改回
    if hum.WalkSpeed ~= TargetWalkSpeed then
        hum.WalkSpeed = TargetWalkSpeed
    end

end)

-- ⭐重生自动恢复
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)

    task.wait(0.5) -- 等Humanoid加载

    if not SpeedEnabled then return end

    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        hum.WalkSpeed = TargetWalkSpeed
    end

end)
-- --- 核心变量：记录原始数值与状态 ---

local OriginalJump = 50
local InfiniteJumpEnabled = false
local FastRunSpeed = 0
local FastRunConnection = nil

-- 自动记录服务器原本的速度和跳跃
task.spawn(function()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
    OriginalWalkSpeed = hum.WalkSpeed
    OriginalJump = (hum.JumpPower > 0) and hum.JumpPower or hum.JumpHeight
end)

-- 无限跳监听逻辑

    if InfiniteJumpEnabled then
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        if character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
            character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"):ChangeState("Jumping")
        end
    end
end)

local function ClearPlayerESP()
    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj.Name == "PlayerESP_Highlight" or obj.Name == "PlayerESP_Info" or obj.Name == "PlayerESP_Box" then
            obj:Destroy()
        end
    end
end

local function UpdatePlayerESP()

    if not PLAYER_ESP.Enabled then return end -- ⭐核心

    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then

            local char = p.Character
            local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if hum and head and root and hum.Health > -500 then

                local isTeam = (p.Team == LocalPlayer.Team)
                local filtered = PLAYER_ESP.TeamCheck and isTeam
                local color = p.TeamColor.Color

                -- ===== 高亮 =====
                local high = char:FindFirstChild("PlayerESP_Highlight")

                if PLAYER_ESP.HighlightEnabled then
                    if not high then
                        high = Instance.new("Highlight", char)
                        high.Name = "PlayerESP_Highlight"
                    end
                    high.FillColor = color
                    high.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
                elseif high then
                    high:Destroy()
                end

                -- ===== 方框 =====
                local box = char:FindFirstChild("PlayerESP_Box")

                if PLAYER_ESP.BoxEnabled and not filtered then
                    if not box then
                        box = Instance.new("BillboardGui", char)
                        box.Name = "PlayerESP_Box"
                        box.Size = UDim2.new(4.5,0,6,0)
                        box.AlwaysOnTop = true
                        box.Adornee = root

                        local f = Instance.new("Frame", box)
                        f.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
                        f.BackgroundTransparency = 1

                        local s = Instance.new("UIStroke", f)
                        s.Thickness = 1.5
                    end

                    box.Frame.UIStroke.Color = color
                elseif box then
                    box:Destroy()
                end

                -- ===== 信息 =====
                local info = char:FindFirstChild("PlayerESP_Info")

                if not filtered then
                    if not info then
                        info = Instance.new("BillboardGui", char)
                        info.Name = "PlayerESP_Info"
                        info.Size = UDim2.new(0,200,0,50)
                        info.AlwaysOnTop = true
                        info.Adornee = head
                        info.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0,3.5,0)

                        local txt = Instance.new("TextLabel", info)
                        txt.Name = "Label"
                        txt.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
                        txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        txt.RichText = true
                        txt.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
                        txt.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
                    end

                    local text = ""

-- ⭐ 名字（白色加粗）
if PLAYER_ESP.ShowName then
    text = "<font color='#ffffff'><b>"..p.DisplayName.."</b></font>\n"
end

-- ⭐ 血量（你原来的红绿）
if PLAYER_ESP.ShowHealth then
    local hp = math.floor(hum.Health)

    local hpColor = (hp > 50 and "#55ff55" or "#ff5555")

    text = text .. "<font color='"..hpColor.."'>HP: "..hp.."</font> "
end

-- ⭐ 距离（白色）
if PLAYER_ESP.ShowDist then
    local dist = math.floor((Camera.CFrame.Position - root.Position).Magnitude)

    text = text .. "<font color='#ffffff'>| "..dist.."m</font>"
end

info.Label.Text = text
                elseif info then
                    info:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
--旧互动物体透视
local InteractESP = { Enabled = false, Color = Color3.fromRGB(0,255,0), Highlights = {} }

local function IsInteractive_Old(obj)
    if not obj then return false end
    return obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") or obj:IsA("ClickDetector")
end

local function FindAdornPart_Old(target)
    if not target then return nil end
    if target:IsA("BasePart") then return target end
    if target:IsA("Model") then
        if target.PrimaryPart then return target.PrimaryPart end
        for _, c in pairs(target:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("BasePart") then return c end end
    end
    return nil
end

local function AddInteractESP(target)
    if not target then return end
    if InteractESP.Highlights[target] then return end
    if not (target:IsA("BasePart") or target:IsA("Model")) then return end
    local ok, hl = pcall(function()
        local h = Instance.new("Highlight")
        h.Name = "InteractESP"
        h.Adornee = target
        h.FillColor = InteractESP.Color
        h.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        h.FillTransparency = 0.5
        h.OutlineTransparency = 0
        h.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        h.Parent = target
        return h
    end)
    if not ok then return end
    InteractESP.Highlights[target] = hl
    local adornPart = FindAdornPart_Old(target)
    if adornPart then
        if adornPart:FindFirstChild("InteractLabel") then return end
        local prompt = target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt") or (target.Parent and target.Parent:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt"))
        local text = (prompt and (prompt.ActionText ~= "" and prompt.ActionText)) or "可互动"
        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bb.Name = "InteractLabel"
        bb.Adornee = adornPart
        bb.Size = UDim2.new(0,120,0,30)
        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0,3,0)
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bb.Parent = adornPart
        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Text = text
        label.TextColor3 = InteractESP.Color
        label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
        label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0,0,0)
        label.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        label.TextSize = 14
        label.Parent = bb
    end
    pcall(function()
        target.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
            if not parent and InteractESP.Highlights[target] then
                pcall(function() InteractESP.Highlights[target]:Destroy() end)
                InteractESP.Highlights[target] = nil
                pcall(function()
                    if target.GetDescendants then
                        for _, v in pairs(target:GetDescendants()) do
                            if v:IsA("BillboardGui") and v.Name == "InteractLabel" then pcall(function() v:Destroy() end) end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end)
end

local function RemoveInteractESP(target)
    if not target then return end
    if InteractESP.Highlights[target] then
        pcall(function() if InteractESP.Highlights[target] and InteractESP.Highlights[target].Parent then InteractESP.Highlights[target]:Destroy() end end)
        InteractESP.Highlights[target] = nil
    end
    pcall(function()
        if target and target.GetDescendants then
            for _, v in pairs(target:GetDescendants()) do
                if v:IsA("BillboardGui") and v.Name == "InteractLabel" then pcall(function() v:Destroy() end) end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function UpdateInteractColor(color)
    InteractESP.Color = color
    for target, hl in pairs(InteractESP.Highlights) do
        if hl and hl.Parent then pcall(function() hl.FillColor = color end) else InteractESP.Highlights[target] = nil end
        pcall(function()
            if target and target.GetDescendants then
                for _, v in pairs(target:GetDescendants()) do
                    if v:IsA("BillboardGui") and v.Name == "InteractLabel" then
                        local txt = v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")
                        if txt then txt.TextColor3 = color end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

local function ToggleInteractESP(state)
    InteractESP.Enabled = state
    if state then
        task.spawn(function()
            for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if IsInteractive_Old(obj) and obj.Parent then pcall(function() AddInteractESP(obj.Parent) end) end
            end
        end)
        if not Connections.Interact then
            Connections.Interact = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(child)
                task.delay(1, function()
                    if child and IsInteractive_Old(child) and child.Parent then AddInteractESP(child.Parent)
                    elseif child and child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Parent then
                        for _, c in pairs(child:GetDescendants()) do
                            if IsInteractive_Old(c) and c.Parent then AddInteractESP(c.Parent) end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end
    else
        local toRemove = {}
        for target, _ in pairs(InteractESP.Highlights) do table.insert(toRemove, target) end
        for _, target in ipairs(toRemove) do RemoveInteractESP(target) end
        if Connections.Interact then pcall(function() Connections.Interact:Disconnect() end) Connections.Interact = nil end
    end
end

--新互动物体透视
local NewInteractESP = { Enabled = false, Color = Color3.fromRGB(0,255,0), Highlights = {} }

local function IsInteractive_New(o) return o and (o:IsA("ProximityPrompt") or o:IsA("ClickDetector")) end

local function GetInteractiveTarget(node)
    local p = node
    while p do if p:IsA("BasePart") or p:IsA("Model") then return p end p = p.Parent end
    return nil
end

local function FindAdornPart_New(t)
    if not t then return nil end
    if t:IsA("BasePart") then return t end
    if t:IsA("Model") then
        if t.PrimaryPart then return t.PrimaryPart end
        for _, c in pairs(t:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("BasePart") then return c end end
    end
    return nil
end

local function AddNewInteractESP(target)
    if not target then return end
    if NewInteractESP.Highlights[target] then return end
    local ok, h = pcall(function()
        local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
        hl.Name = "NewInteractESP"
        hl.Adornee = target
        hl.FillColor = NewInteractESP.Color
        hl.OutlineColor = Color3.new(1,1,1)
        hl.FillTransparency = .5
        hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
        hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        hl.Parent = target
        return hl
    end)
    if not ok or not h then return end
    NewInteractESP.Highlights[target] = h
    local part = FindAdornPart_New(target)
    if part and not part:FindFirstChild("NewInteractLabel") then
        pcall(function()
            local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            bb.Name = "NewInteractLabel"
            bb.Adornee = part
            bb.Size = UDim2.new(0,120,0,30)
            bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0,3,0)
            bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
            bb.Parent = part
            local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            label.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
            label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            label.TextStrokeTransparency = .4
            label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0,0,0)
            label.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
            label.TextSize = 14
            label.TextColor3 = NewInteractESP.Color
            label.Parent = bb
            local displayName = target.Name or ""
            if displayName == "" or displayName == "Part" or displayName == "Model" or displayName == "BasePart" then label.Text = "可互动" else label.Text = displayName end
        end)
    end
    pcall(function()
        target.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
            if not parent then
                if NewInteractESP.Highlights[target] then
                    pcall(function() NewInteractESP.Highlights[target]:Destroy() end)
                    NewInteractESP.Highlights[target] = nil
                end
                pcall(function()
                    for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                        if obj:IsA("BillboardGui") and obj.Name == "NewInteractLabel" then
                            local ador = obj.Adornee
                            if not ador or not ador:IsDescendantOf(game) then pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end)
end

local function UpdateNewInteractColor(color)
    NewInteractESP.Color = color
    for target, hl in pairs(NewInteractESP.Highlights) do if hl and hl.Parent then pcall(function() hl.FillColor = color end) end end
    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("BillboardGui") and obj.Name == "NewInteractLabel" then
            local txt = obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")
            if txt then pcall(function() txt.TextColor3 = color end) end
        end
    end
end

local function ToggleNewInteractESP(state)
    NewInteractESP.Enabled = state
    if state then
        task.spawn(function()
            for _, v in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if IsInteractive_New(v) then local t = GetInteractiveTarget(v) if t then AddNewInteractESP(t) end end
            end
        end)
        if not Connections.NewInteract then
            Connections.NewInteract = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(c)
                task.delay(0.05, function()
                    if c and c.GetDescendants then
                        for _, d in pairs(c:GetDescendants()) do if IsInteractive_New(d) then local t = GetInteractiveTarget(d) if t then AddNewInteractESP(t) end end end
                    end
                    if IsInteractive_New(c) then local t = GetInteractiveTarget(c) if t then AddNewInteractESP(t) end end
                end)
            end)
        end
    else
        for t, h in pairs(NewInteractESP.Highlights) do pcall(function() h:Destroy() end) end
        NewInteractESP.Highlights = {}
        pcall(function()
            for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("BillboardGui") and obj.Name == "NewInteractLabel" then pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
            end
        end)
        if Connections.NewInteract then pcall(function() Connections.NewInteract:Disconnect() end) Connections.NewInteract = nil end
    end
end
do
-- =================== 作者检测模块（墙体检测+优化缩放版） ===================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local DevESP = {
    Enabled = true,
    SelfEnabled = false,
    Targets = {
        ["ylt410"] = "author",
       ["52554jdhbbddjr"] = "old",
        ["suxmv"] = "toptime" -- ✅ 新增
    },
    Objects = {}
}

local function RainbowColor()
    return Color3.fromHSV((tick()*0.2) % 1, 1, 1)
end

-- 判断是否被墙挡住
local function IsVisible(head, camera)
    if not head or not camera then return false end

    local origin = camera.CFrame.Position
    local direction = (head.Position - origin)

    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {
        Players.LocalPlayer.Character
    }

    local result = Workspace:Raycast(origin, direction, params)

    if not result then
        return true
    end

    if result.Instance and not result.Instance:IsDescendantOf(head.Parent) then
        return false
    end

    return true
end

function AddDevESP(player)

    if not DevESP.Enabled then return end
    if player == Players.LocalPlayer and not DevESP.SelfEnabled then return end

    local role = DevESP.Targets[player.Name]
    if not role then return end

    local function apply(character)

        local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head") or character:WaitForChild("Head",5)
        if not head then return end

        if head:FindFirstChild("DevTag") then
            head.DevTag:Destroy()
        end

        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bb.Name = "DevTag"
        bb.Adornee = head
        bb.Size = UDim2.new(0,110,0,22)
        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0,2.6,0)
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bb.MaxDistance = math.huge
        bb.LightInfluence = 0
        bb.Parent = head

        local container = Instance.new("Frame")
        container.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
        container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        container.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5)
        container.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0)
        container.Parent = bb

        local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
        scale.Scale = 1
        scale.Parent = container

        local parentUI = container

        -- =================== 样式区 ===================

        if role == "author" then
            local bg = Instance.new("Frame")
            bg.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
            bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35,35,35)
            bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
            bg.BorderSizePixel = 0
            bg.Parent = container

            local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
            corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)
            corner.Parent = bg

            local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,215,0)
            stroke.Thickness = 1.2
            stroke.Parent = bg

            parentUI = bg

        elseif role == "toptime" then -- ✅ 新样式（红框）
            local bg = Instance.new("Frame")
            bg.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
            bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35,35,35)
            bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
            bg.BorderSizePixel = 0
            bg.Parent = container

            local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
            corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)
            corner.Parent = bg

            local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0) -- 红色边框
            stroke.Thickness = 1.4
            stroke.Parent = bg

            parentUI = bg
        end

        -- =================== 文本 ===================

        local txt = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        txt.Size = UDim2.new(1,-6,1,-4)
        txt.Position = UDim2.new(0,3,0,2)
        txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        txt.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        txt.TextSize = 12

        if role == "author" then
            txt.Text = "夜脚本作者"
        elseif role == "old" then
            txt.Text = "夜脚本老用户"
        elseif role == "toptime" then
            txt.Text = "最高使用时长（580分钟）" -- ✅ 新标签
        end

        txt.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
        txt.Parent = parentUI

        if role == "old" then
            txt.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            txt.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120,120,120)
        else
            txt.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
        end

        DevESP.Objects[player] = {
            Head = head,
            Text = txt,
            Scale = scale,
            Billboard = bb
        }
    end

    if player.Character then
        apply(player.Character)
    end

    player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        task.wait(1)
        apply(char)
    end)
end

-- 初始化
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    AddDevESP(plr)
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(AddDevESP)

-- 主循环
task.spawn(function()
    while true do

        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera

        if cam then
            for player, data in pairs(DevESP.Objects) do
                if data.Head and data.Head.Parent then

                    data.Text.TextColor3 = RainbowColor()

                    local visible = IsVisible(data.Head, cam)
                    data.Billboard.Enabled = visible

                    if visible then
                        local dist = (cam.CFrame.Position - data.Head.Position).Magnitude
                        local s = math.clamp(1.2 - (dist / 200), 0.6, 1.2)
                        data.Scale.Scale = s
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        task.wait(0.2)
    end
end)

-- 初始化（重复段保留原样）
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    AddDevESP(plr)
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(AddDevESP)
end
--夜视
local VisualModule = { NormalNightVision=false, SuperNightVision=false, NoFog=false }

-- 普通夜视 (Brightness=12)
local function ApplyNormalNightVision(enable)
    VisualModule.NormalNightVision = enable
    if enable then
        Lighting.Brightness=10
        Lighting.Ambient=Color3.fromRGB(220,220,220)
        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient=Color3.fromRGB(220,220,220)
        Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale=1
        Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale=1
        Lighting.GlobalShadows=false
        Lighting.ClockTime=8
        Lighting.ColorShift_Top=Color3.new(0,0,0)
        Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom=Color3.new(0,0,0)
        Notify("普通夜视开启","成功",2)
    else
        -- 恢复
        for k,v in pairs(OriginalLighting) do pcall(function() Lighting[k] = v end) end
        Notify("普通夜视关闭","成功",2)
    end
end

-- 超级夜视 
local function ApplySuperNightVision(enable)
    VisualModule.SuperNightVision = enable
    if enable then

        Lighting.Brightness = 70
        Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
        Lighting.ClockTime = 12
        Lighting.FogEnd = 100000 
        Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 1
        -- 移除曝光补偿，仅靠高 Brightness 提升亮度，避免白屏
        Notify("超级夜视开启","成功",2)
    else
        -- 恢复
        for k,v in pairs(OriginalLighting) do pcall(function() Lighting[k] = v end) end
        Notify("超级夜视关闭","成功",2)
    end
end

-- 去雾 (修复版：支持 Atmosphere 还原)
local function ApplyNoFog(enable)
    VisualModule.NoFog = enable
    if enable then
        Lighting.FogStart = 0
        Lighting.FogEnd = math.huge
        Lighting.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(200,200,220)
        
        -- 保存并修改 Atmosphere
        for _, obj in pairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            if obj:IsA("Atmosphere") then
                if not OriginalAtmosphere[obj] then
                    OriginalAtmosphere[obj] = {
                        Density = obj.Density,
                        Offset = obj.Offset,
                        Glare = obj.Glare,
                        Haze = obj.Haze
                    }
                end
                pcall(function() obj.Density=0 obj.Offset=0 obj.Glare=0 obj.Haze=0 end)
            end
        end
        Notify("去雾开启","成功",2)
    else
        -- 恢复 Legacy Fog
        Lighting.FogStart = OriginalLighting.FogStart
        Lighting.FogEnd = OriginalLighting.FogEnd
        Lighting.FogColor = OriginalLighting.FogColor
        
        -- 恢复 Atmosphere
        for obj, props in pairs(OriginalAtmosphere) do
            if obj and obj.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    obj.Density = props.Density
                    obj.Offset = props.Offset
                    obj.Glare = props.Glare
                    obj.Haze = props.Haze
                end)
            end
        end
        OriginalAtmosphere = {} 
        
        Notify("去雾关闭","成功",2)
    end
end

-- 保持状态 (防止游戏强制刷回)
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.wait(1)
    if VisualModule.NormalNightVision then ApplyNormalNightVision(true) end
    if VisualModule.SuperNightVision then ApplySuperNightVision(true) end
    if VisualModule.NoFog then ApplyNoFog(true) end
end)

    Title = "加载Rob脚本",
    Callback = function()
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Zyb150933/ROB/refs/heads/main/ROB.V2"))()
        end)

        if ok then
            Notify("成功", "Rob脚本已加载", 2, "success")
        else
            Notify("错误", tostring(err), 3, "error")
        end
    end
})


;(function()
local TabAttr = Window:Tab({
    Title = "玩家功能",
    Icon = "zap",
    Locked = false,
})
