-- ================= 速度 =================
TabAttr:Toggle({
    Title = "开启速度修改",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        SpeedEnabled = v

        local hum = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            if v then
                hum.WalkSpeed = TargetWalkSpeed
                AddFeature("速度修改")
            else
                hum.WalkSpeed = OriginalWalkSpeed
                RemoveFeature("速度修改")
            end
        end
    end
})

TabAttr:Input({
    Title = "速度数值",
    Desc = "请输入 0 - 400 之间的数值",
    Placeholder = "默认 16",
    Callback = function(value)
        -- 将输入的字符串转换为数字
        local numValue = tonumber(value)
        
        if numValue then
            -- 限制数值范围（可选，为了防止数值过大导致崩溃）
            numValue = math.clamp(numValue, 0, 400)
            TargetWalkSpeed = numValue

            if SpeedEnabled then
                local char = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then
                    hum.WalkSpeed = numValue
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

-- ================= 快速跑步 =================

TabAttr:Toggle({
    Title = "开启快速跑步",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(enabled)

        if enabled then
            if sudu then sudu:Disconnect() end

            sudu = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
                if lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and lp.Character.Humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
                    lp.Character:TranslateBy(lp.Character.Humanoid.MoveDirection * Speed / 0.5)
                end
            end)

            AddFeature("快速跑步")
        else
            if sudu then
                sudu:Disconnect()
                sudu = nil
            end

            RemoveFeature("快速跑步")
        end

    end
})

TabAttr:Input({
    Title = "快速跑步强度",
    Placeholder = "输入速度 (0-200)",
    Default = tostring(Speed or 0),

    Callback = function(text)

        local num = tonumber(text)

        if num then
            num = math.clamp(num, 0, 200) -- ⭐限制范围
            Speed = num
               end

    end
})

local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local CustomJumpEnabled = false
local CustomJumpValue = 50
local CustomJumpMode = "修改原生跳跃"

local LastJumpTime = 0
local JumpCooldown = 0.08

local JumpStateConnection = nil

local OriginalJumpData = {
    Humanoid = nil,
    UseJumpPower = nil,
    JumpPower = nil,
    JumpHeight = nil
}

local function GetCharacterParts()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return nil, nil, nil end

    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    return char, hum, root
end

local function SaveOriginalJump(hum)
    if not hum then return end

    if OriginalJumpData.Humanoid ~= hum then
        OriginalJumpData.Humanoid = hum
        OriginalJumpData.UseJumpPower = hum.UseJumpPower
        OriginalJumpData.JumpPower = hum.JumpPower

        pcall(function()
            OriginalJumpData.JumpHeight = hum.JumpHeight
        end)
    end
end

local function RestoreOriginalJump(hum)
    if not hum then return end
    if OriginalJumpData.Humanoid ~= hum then return end

    if OriginalJumpData.UseJumpPower ~= nil then
        hum.UseJumpPower = OriginalJumpData.UseJumpPower
    end

    if OriginalJumpData.JumpPower ~= nil then
        hum.JumpPower = OriginalJumpData.JumpPower
    end

    if OriginalJumpData.JumpHeight ~= nil then
        pcall(function()
            hum.JumpHeight = OriginalJumpData.JumpHeight
        end)
    end
end

local function JumpPowerToHeight(power)
    return (power * power) / (2 * workspace.Gravity)
end

local function GetOriginalJumpPower(hum)
    SaveOriginalJump(hum)

    if OriginalJumpData.UseJumpPower == false and OriginalJumpData.JumpHeight then
        return math.sqrt(2 * workspace.Gravity * OriginalJumpData.JumpHeight)
    end

    return OriginalJumpData.JumpPower or hum.JumpPower or 50
end

local function GetExtraJumpHeight(hum)
    local originalPower = GetOriginalJumpPower(hum)
    local originalHeight = JumpPowerToHeight(originalPower)
    local targetHeight = JumpPowerToHeight(CustomJumpValue)

    return math.max(0, targetHeight - originalHeight)
end

local function ApplyCurrentJumpMode()
    local _, hum = GetCharacterParts()
    if not hum then return end

    SaveOriginalJump(hum)

    if not CustomJumpEnabled then
        RestoreOriginalJump(hum)
        return
    end

    if CustomJumpMode == "修改原生跳跃" then
        hum.UseJumpPower = true
        hum.JumpPower = CustomJumpValue
    else
        RestoreOriginalJump(hum)
    end
end

local function ApplyPushJump()
    local _, hum, root = GetCharacterParts()
    if not hum or not root then return end

    SaveOriginalJump(hum)
    RestoreOriginalJump(hum)

    local originalPower = GetOriginalJumpPower(hum)
    if CustomJumpValue <= originalPower then
        return
    end

    task.delay(0.03, function()
        if not CustomJumpEnabled then return end
        if CustomJumpMode ~= "推力补偿" then return end
        if not root.Parent then return end

        local currentYVelocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y
        local needVelocity = math.max(0, CustomJumpValue - currentYVelocity)

        if needVelocity <= 0 then return end

        root:ApplyImpulse(Vector3.new(
            0,
            root.AssemblyMass * needVelocity,
            0
        ))
    end)
end

local function ApplyTeleportJump()
    local _, hum, root = GetCharacterParts()
    if not hum or not root then return end

    SaveOriginalJump(hum)
    RestoreOriginalJump(hum)

    local targetHeight = JumpPowerToHeight(CustomJumpValue)
    local originalHeight = JumpPowerToHeight(GetOriginalJumpPower(hum))

    if targetHeight <= originalHeight then
        return
    end

    local startY = root.Position.Y
    local targetY = startY + targetHeight

    task.delay(0.12, function()
        if not CustomJumpEnabled then return end
        if CustomJumpMode ~= "传送补偿" then return end
        if not root.Parent then return end

        local currentCFrame = root.CFrame
        local currentY = currentCFrame.Position.Y
        local offsetY = targetY - currentY

        if offsetY <= 0 then return end

        -- 保留当前朝向，只改高度
        root.CFrame = currentCFrame + Vector3.new(0, offsetY, 0)
    end)
end

local function OnRealJump()
    if not CustomJumpEnabled then return end
    if CustomJumpMode == "修改原生跳跃" then return end

    local now = os.clock()
    if now - LastJumpTime < JumpCooldown then return end
    LastJumpTime = now

    if CustomJumpMode == "推力补偿" then
        ApplyPushJump()
    elseif CustomJumpMode == "传送补偿" then
        ApplyTeleportJump()
    end
end

local function BindJumpStateListener(hum)
    if JumpStateConnection then
        JumpStateConnection:Disconnect()
        JumpStateConnection = nil
    end

    if not hum then return end

    JumpStateConnection = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, newState)
        -- 只监听真正进入跳跃状态，不监听按键
        if newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping then
            OnRealJump()
        end
    end)
end

local JumpModeSaveFile = "CustomJumpMode.txt"

local JumpModeValues = {
    "修改原生跳跃",
    "推力补偿",
    "传送补偿"
}

local function IsValidJumpMode(mode)
    for _, v in ipairs(JumpModeValues) do
        if v == mode then
            return true
        end
    end

    return false
end

local function LoadJumpMode()
    if isfile and readfile and isfile(JumpModeSaveFile) then
        local ok, result = pcall(function()
            return readfile(JumpModeSaveFile)
        end)

        if ok and IsValidJumpMode(result) then
            return result
        end
    end

    return "修改原生跳跃"
end

local function SaveJumpMode(mode)
    if writefile and IsValidJumpMode(mode) then
        pcall(function()
            writefile(JumpModeSaveFile, mode)
        end)
    end
end

CustomJumpMode = LoadJumpMode()

local _, currentHum = GetCharacterParts()
if currentHum then
    SaveOriginalJump(currentHum)
    BindJumpStateListener(currentHum)
end

LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.wait(0.5)

    OriginalJumpData.Humanoid = nil
    OriginalJumpData.UseJumpPower = nil
    OriginalJumpData.JumpPower = nil
    OriginalJumpData.JumpHeight = nil

    local _, hum = GetCharacterParts()
    if hum then
        SaveOriginalJump(hum)
        BindJumpStateListener(hum)
    end

    ApplyCurrentJumpMode()
end)

local JumpModeDropdown = TabAttr:Dropdown({
    Title = "跳跃模式",
    Desc = "选择跳跃修改方式",
    Values = JumpModeValues,
    Value = CustomJumpMode,
    Callback = function(option)
        CustomJumpMode = option
        SaveJumpMode(option)
        ApplyCurrentJumpMode()
    end
})

TabAttr:Toggle({
    Title = "开启跳跃修改",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        CustomJumpEnabled = v

        ApplyCurrentJumpMode()

        if v then
            AddFeature("跳跃修改")
        else
            RemoveFeature("跳跃修改")
        end
    end
})

TabAttr:Slider({
    Title = "跳跃数值",
    Value = {
        Min = 50,
        Max = 600,
        Default = 50,
    },
    Increment = 1,
    Callback = function(value)
        CustomJumpValue = value
        ApplyCurrentJumpMode()
    end
})
do
-- ================= 重力控制系统 =================

local GravityEnabled = false
local CustomGravity = 196.2
local OriginalGravity = workspace.Gravity

-- ⭐防止被游戏改回
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()

    if not GravityEnabled then return end

    if workspace.Gravity ~= CustomGravity then
        workspace.Gravity = CustomGravity
    end

end)

-- ⭐开启
local function EnableGravity(value)

    value = tonumber(value)
    if not value then return end

    OriginalGravity = workspace.Gravity
    CustomGravity = math.clamp(value, 0, 500)

    workspace.Gravity = CustomGravity
    GravityEnabled = true

end

-- ⭐关闭（恢复原本）
local function DisableGravity()

    GravityEnabled = false

    if OriginalGravity then
        workspace.Gravity = OriginalGravity
    else
        workspace.Gravity = 196.2
    end

end
TabAttr:Toggle({
    Title = "重力控制",
    Desc = "开启后可自定义重力",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)

        if state then
            EnableGravity(CustomGravity)
            AddFeature("重力控制")
        else
            DisableGravity()
            RemoveFeature("重力控制")
        end

    end
})
TabAttr:Input({
    Title = "重力数值",
    Desc = "默认196.2，越低越像月球",
    Value = "196.2",
    Callback = function(val)

        local num = tonumber(val)
        if not num then return end

        CustomGravity = math.clamp(num, 0, 500)

        if GravityEnabled then
            workspace.Gravity = CustomGravity
        end

    end
})
end
-- ================= 无限跳 =================

TabAttr:Toggle({
    Title = "无限跳跃",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        InfiniteJumpEnabled = v

        if v then
            AddFeature("无限跳")
        else
            RemoveFeature("无限跳")
        end
    end
})
do
-- ================= 穿墙 =================

local NoclipConnection = nil
local CharacterConnection = nil
local OriginalCollision = {}

TabAttr:Toggle({
    Title = "穿墙",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(enabled)

        if enabled then

            OriginalCollision = {}

            if CharacterConnection then
                CharacterConnection:Disconnect()
            end

            CharacterConnection = Players.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                OriginalCollision = {}
            end)

            if NoclipConnection then
                NoclipConnection:Disconnect()
            end

            NoclipConnection = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
                local character = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                if not character then return end

                for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        if OriginalCollision[part] == nil then
                            OriginalCollision[part] = part.CanCollide
                        end
                        part.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end)

            AddFeature("穿墙")

        else

            if NoclipConnection then
                NoclipConnection:Disconnect()
                NoclipConnection = nil
            end

            if CharacterConnection then
                CharacterConnection:Disconnect()
                CharacterConnection = nil
            end

            for part, state in pairs(OriginalCollision) do
                if typeof(part) == "Instance" and part.Parent then
                    part.CanCollide = state
                end
            end

            OriginalCollision = {}

            RemoveFeature("穿墙")
        end

    end
})
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer

local invis_on = false
local savedTransparency = {}
local invisChairName = "invischair"

local function getCharacter()
    return player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
end

local function getRootAndTorso(character)
    local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local torso = character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
    return root, torso
end

local function saveAndSetTransparency(character, transparency)
    for _, obj in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("Decal") then
            if savedTransparency[obj] == nil then
                savedTransparency[obj] = obj.Transparency
            end

            if obj.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                obj.Transparency = transparency
            end
        end
    end
end

local function restoreTransparency()
    for obj, value in pairs(savedTransparency) do
        if obj and obj.Parent then
            obj.Transparency = value
        end
    end

    table.clear(savedTransparency)
end

local function removeInvisChair()
    local chair = workspace:FindFirstChild(invisChairName)
    if chair then
        chair:Destroy()
    end
end

local function EnableInvisibility()
    if invis_on then
        return
    end

    invis_on = true

    local character = getCharacter()
    local root, torso = getRootAndTorso(character)

    if not root or not torso then
        invis_on = false
        return
    end

    local savedpos = root.CFrame

    task.wait()
    character:MoveTo(Vector3.new(-25.95, 84, 3537.55))
    task.wait(0.15)

    removeInvisChair()

    local seat = Instance.new("Seat")
    seat.Name = invisChairName
    seat.Anchored = false
    seat.CanCollide = false
    seat.Transparency = 1
    seat.Position = Vector3.new(-25.95, 84, 3537.55)
    seat.Parent = workspace

    local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
    weld.Part0 = seat
    weld.Part1 = torso
    weld.Parent = seat

    task.wait()
    seat.CFrame = savedpos

    saveAndSetTransparency(character, 0.5)
end

local function DisableInvisibility()
    if not invis_on then
        return
    end

    invis_on = false

    removeInvisChair()
    restoreTransparency()
end

TabAttr:Toggle({
    Title = "隐身",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            EnableInvisibility()

            if AddFeature then
                AddFeature("隐身")
            end
        else
            DisableInvisibility()

            if RemoveFeature then
                RemoveFeature("隐身")
            end
        end
    end
})

player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    invis_on = false
    removeInvisChair()
    table.clear(savedTransparency)

    if RemoveFeature then
        RemoveFeature("隐身")
    end
end)
end
--// 死亡后原地复活 / 动态复活时间检测 / 重复死亡自动暂停版

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

--// 功能状态
local RespawnAtDeathEnabled = false

--// 位置记录
local LastAliveCFrame = nil
local DeathCFrame = nil

--// 连接
local CharacterAddedConn = nil
local HumanoidDiedConn = nil
local TrackConn = nil

--// 传送配置
local TeleportHoldTime = 0.35 -- 复活后强制传送持续时间，太高会浮空
local TeleportYOffset = 2 -- 传送回死亡点时向上偏移，防止卡地

--// 重复死亡检测配置
local RepeatDeathLimit = 3 -- 检测窗口内死亡几次后暂停原位复活
local ExtraDeathBuffer = 3 -- 额外缓冲时间，用来防止加载/延迟导致漏判

local MinRepeatDeathWindow = 10 -- 最小检测窗口
local MaxRepeatDeathWindow = 45 -- 最大检测窗口
local DefaultRespawnTime = 5 -- 读取不到游戏复活时间时的默认值

--// 死亡时间记录
local RecentDeathTimes = {}

--// 实际复活时间估算
local LastDeathClock = 0
local ObservedRespawnTime = nil

local function SendNotify(title, text, duration)
    task.spawn(function()
        for _ = 1, 8 do
            local ok = pcall(function()
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = title,
                    Text = text,
                    Duration = duration or 4
                })
            end)

            if ok then
                return
            end

            task.wait(0.4)
        end

        warn(title .. " - " .. text)
    end)
end

local function DisconnectConn(conn)
    if conn then
        conn:Disconnect()
    end
end

local function DisconnectAllRespawnConns()
    DisconnectConn(CharacterAddedConn)
    DisconnectConn(HumanoidDiedConn)
    DisconnectConn(TrackConn)

    CharacterAddedConn = nil
    HumanoidDiedConn = nil
    TrackConn = nil
end

local function GetCharacter()
    return LocalPlayer.Character
end

local function GetHumanoid(character)
    if not character then
        return nil
    end

    return character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end

local function GetRoot(character)
    if not character then
        return nil
    end

    return character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
end

local function GetGameRespawnTime()
    -- 优先用实际观测到的复活时间，因为有些游戏会自定义复活逻辑
    if typeof(ObservedRespawnTime) == "number" and ObservedRespawnTime > 0 then
        return ObservedRespawnTime
    end

    local ok, respawnTime = pcall(function()
        return Players.RespawnTime
    end)

    if ok and typeof(respawnTime) == "number" and respawnTime > 0 then
        return respawnTime
    end

    return DefaultRespawnTime
end

local function GetRepeatDeathWindow()
    local gameRespawnTime = GetGameRespawnTime()

    local window = (gameRespawnTime * RepeatDeathLimit) + ExtraDeathBuffer

    return math.clamp(window, MinRepeatDeathWindow, MaxRepeatDeathWindow)
end

local function AddRecentDeath()
    local now = os.clock()
    local repeatDeathWindow = GetRepeatDeathWindow()

    table.insert(RecentDeathTimes, now)

    for i = #RecentDeathTimes, 1, -1 do
        if now - RecentDeathTimes[i] > repeatDeathWindow then
            table.remove(RecentDeathTimes, i)
        end
    end

    return #RecentDeathTimes, repeatDeathWindow
end

local function ClearDeathRecords()
    RecentDeathTimes = {}
    LastDeathClock = 0
    ObservedRespawnTime = nil
end

local function PauseRespawnAtDeathByRepeatDeath(repeatDeathWindow)
    RespawnAtDeathEnabled = false

    DeathCFrame = nil
    LastAliveCFrame = nil
    ClearDeathRecords()

    DisconnectAllRespawnConns()

    pcall(function()
        RemoveFeature("死亡原地复活")
    end)

    local windowText = repeatDeathWindow and tostring(math.floor(repeatDeathWindow)) or "短时间"

    SendNotify(
        "原位复活已暂停",
        "检测到 " .. windowText .. " 秒内重复死亡，已暂停原位复活",
        5
    )
end

local function StartTrackingPosition()
    DisconnectConn(TrackConn)
    TrackConn = nil

    TrackConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not RespawnAtDeathEnabled then
            return
        end

        local character = GetCharacter()
        local humanoid = GetHumanoid(character)
        local root = GetRoot(character)

        if humanoid and root and humanoid.Health > 0 then
            LastAliveCFrame = root.CFrame
        end
    end)
end

local function BindDeath(character)
    DisconnectConn(HumanoidDiedConn)
    HumanoidDiedConn = nil

    if not character then
        return
    end

    local humanoid = GetHumanoid(character)
    if not humanoid then
        humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
    end

    if not humanoid then
        return
    end

    HumanoidDiedConn = humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
        if not RespawnAtDeathEnabled then
            return
        end

        LastDeathClock = os.clock()

        local root = GetRoot(character)

        if root then
            DeathCFrame = root.CFrame
        elseif LastAliveCFrame then
            DeathCFrame = LastAliveCFrame
        end

      end)
end

local function ForceTeleportCharacter(character, targetCFrame)
    if not character or not targetCFrame then
        return
    end

    local root = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
    local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)

    if not root or not humanoid then
        return
    end

    while character.Parent == nil do
        task.wait()
    end

    local finalCFrame = targetCFrame + Vector3.new(0, TeleportYOffset, 0)
    local startTime = os.clock()

    -- 短时间连续传送，防止出生点逻辑把你拽回去
    while RespawnAtDeathEnabled and character.Parent and os.clock() - startTime < TeleportHoldTime do
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

        character:PivotTo(finalCFrame)

        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end

    -- 放开物理，减少浮空
    if RespawnAtDeathEnabled and character.Parent and root.Parent then
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -4, 0)
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

        pcall(function()
            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
        end)
    end
end

local function UpdateObservedRespawnTime()
    if LastDeathClock <= 0 then
        return
    end

    local respawnDelay = os.clock() - LastDeathClock

    -- 过滤掉明显不正常的数据
    if respawnDelay >= 0.5 and respawnDelay <= MaxRepeatDeathWindow then
        ObservedRespawnTime = respawnDelay
    end
end

local function OnCharacterAdded(character)
    UpdateObservedRespawnTime()

    if not RespawnAtDeathEnabled then
        return
    end

    BindDeath(character)
    StartTrackingPosition()

    local targetCFrame = DeathCFrame or LastAliveCFrame

    if targetCFrame then
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(0.12)
            ForceTeleportCharacter(character, targetCFrame)
        end)
    end
end

local function EnableRespawnAtDeath()
    RespawnAtDeathEnabled = true

    DeathCFrame = nil
    LastAliveCFrame = nil
    ClearDeathRecords()

    DisconnectAllRespawnConns()

    local character = GetCharacter()
    local root = GetRoot(character)

    if root then
        LastAliveCFrame = root.CFrame
    end

    if character then
        BindDeath(character)
        StartTrackingPosition()
    end

    CharacterAddedConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
        OnCharacterAdded(character)
    end)
end

local function DisableRespawnAtDeath()
    RespawnAtDeathEnabled = false

    DeathCFrame = nil
    LastAliveCFrame = nil
    ClearDeathRecords()

    DisconnectAllRespawnConns()
end
TabAttr:Toggle({
    Title = "死亡原地复活",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        RespawnAtDeathEnabled = v

        if v then
            EnableRespawnAtDeath()

            pcall(function()
                AddFeature("死亡原地复活")
            end)
        else
            DisableRespawnAtDeath()

            pcall(function()
                RemoveFeature("死亡原地复活")
            end)
        end
    end
})
--// 防甩飞：低性能占用版，预防碰撞 + 异常速度兜底，不拉回位置
do
    local PlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunServiceService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local LocalPlayerObj = PlayersService.LocalPlayer

    local AntiFlingEnabled = false
    local AntiFlingConnections = {}
    local AntiFlingOriginalCollision = {}
    local AntiFlingProcessedParts = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

    local AntiFlingLastSelfCheck = 0
    local AntiFlingSelfCheckInterval = 0.08
    local AntiFlingRunId = 0

    -- 甩飞脚本通常是几百万/几亿级别，十万能防恶意又不容易误伤正常玩法
    local AntiFlingMaxLinearVelocity = 100000
    local AntiFlingMaxAngularVelocity = 100000

    local AntiFlingForceClasses = {
        BodyVelocity = true,
        BodyAngularVelocity = true,
        BodyGyro = true,
        BodyPosition = true,
        BodyForce = true,
        BodyThrust = true,

        LinearVelocity = true,
        AngularVelocity = true,
        VectorForce = true,
        AlignPosition = true,
        AlignOrientation = true,
    }

    local function AntiFlingNotify(title, text, duration, icon)
        title = tostring(title or "防甩飞")
        text = tostring(text or "")
        duration = duration or 3
        icon = icon or "info"

        if type(Notify) == "function" then
            pcall(function()
                Notify(title, text, duration, icon)
            end)
            return
        end

        if type(SendNotify) == "function" then
            pcall(function()
                SendNotify(title, text, duration)
            end)
            return
        end

        if WindUI and type(WindUI.Notify) == "function" then
            pcall(function()
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = title,
                    Content = text,
                    Duration = duration,
                    Icon = icon,
                })
            end)
        end
    end

    local function AntiFlingSafeAddFeature(name)
        pcall(function()
            if type(AddFeature) == "function" then
                AddFeature(name)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function AntiFlingSafeRemoveFeature(name)
        pcall(function()
            if type(RemoveFeature) == "function" then
                RemoveFeature(name)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function AntiFlingDisconnectAll()
        for _, conn in ipairs(AntiFlingConnections) do
            if conn then
                pcall(function()
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end)
            end
        end

        AntiFlingConnections = {}
    end

    local function AntiFlingGetCharacter()
        return LocalPlayerObj and LocalPlayerObj.Character
    end

    local function AntiFlingGetHumanoid(character)
        if not character then
            return nil
        end

        return character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    end

    local function AntiFlingGetRoot(character)
        if not character then
            return nil
        end

        return character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
            or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
    end

    local function AntiFlingRememberCollision(part)
        if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then
            return
        end

        if AntiFlingOriginalCollision[part] ~= nil then
            return
        end

        local oldCanCollide = true
        local oldCanTouch = true

        pcall(function()
            oldCanCollide = part.CanCollide
        end)

        pcall(function()
            oldCanTouch = part.CanTouch
        end)

        AntiFlingOriginalCollision[part] = {
            CanCollide = oldCanCollide,
            CanTouch = oldCanTouch,
        }
    end

    local function AntiFlingMakePartNoCollide(part)
        if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then
            return
        end

        if AntiFlingProcessedParts[part] ~= true then
            AntiFlingProcessedParts[part] = true
            AntiFlingRememberCollision(part)
        end

        pcall(function()
            if part.CanCollide ~= false then
                part.CanCollide = false
            end
        end)

        -- 这行可以挡一部分靠触碰/碰撞链恶心人的玩法；关闭时会恢复
        pcall(function()
            if part.CanTouch ~= false then
                part.CanTouch = false
            end
        end)
    end

    local function AntiFlingRestoreCollision()
        for part, oldState in pairs(AntiFlingOriginalCollision) do
            if part and part.Parent and part:IsA("BasePart") and type(oldState) == "table" then
                pcall(function()
                    part.CanCollide = oldState.CanCollide
                end)

                pcall(function()
                    part.CanTouch = oldState.CanTouch
                end)
            end
        end

        AntiFlingOriginalCollision = {}
    end

    local function AntiFlingNoCollideCharacter(character)
        if not character then
            return
        end

        for _, obj in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                AntiFlingMakePartNoCollide(obj)
            end
        end
    end

    local function AntiFlingNoCollideOtherPlayers()
        for _, player in ipairs(PlayersService:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= LocalPlayerObj then
                AntiFlingNoCollideCharacter(player.Character)
            end
        end
    end

    local function AntiFlingIsSuspiciousForce(obj)
        if typeof(obj) ~= "Instance" then
            return false
        end

        if not AntiFlingForceClasses[obj.ClassName] then
            return false
        end

        -- 避免乱删工具内部正常约束
        if obj:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool") then
            return false
        end

        return true
    end

    local function AntiFlingClearSuspiciousForces(character)
        if not character then
            return
        end

        for _, obj in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
            if AntiFlingIsSuspiciousForce(obj) then
                pcall(function()
                    obj:Destroy()
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    local function AntiFlingZeroPart(part)
        if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then
            return
        end

        pcall(function()
            part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)

        pcall(function()
            part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)

        -- 兼容旧属性。老 Roblox 属性，老到像远古石板，但还可能有用。
        pcall(function()
            part.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        end)

        pcall(function()
            part.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
    end

    local function AntiFlingZeroCharacter(character)
        local root = AntiFlingGetRoot(character)

        if root then
            AntiFlingZeroPart(root)
        end
    end

    local function AntiFlingVelocityTooHigh(part)
        if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then
            return false
        end

        local linearTooHigh = false
        local angularTooHigh = false

        pcall(function()
            linearTooHigh = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > AntiFlingMaxLinearVelocity
        end)

        pcall(function()
            angularTooHigh = part.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude > AntiFlingMaxAngularVelocity
        end)

        return linearTooHigh or angularTooHigh
    end

    local function AntiFlingHookSelfCharacter(character)
        if not character then
            return
        end

        AntiFlingClearSuspiciousForces(character)

        table.insert(AntiFlingConnections, character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
            if not AntiFlingEnabled then
                return
            end

            if AntiFlingIsSuspiciousForce(obj) then
                pcall(function()
                    obj:Destroy()
                end)
            end
        end))
    end

    local function AntiFlingHookOtherCharacter(character)
        if not character then
            return
        end

        AntiFlingNoCollideCharacter(character)

        table.insert(AntiFlingConnections, character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
            if not AntiFlingEnabled then
                return
            end

            if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                AntiFlingMakePartNoCollide(obj)
            end
        end))
    end

    local function AntiFlingHookPlayer(player)
        if not player or player == LocalPlayerObj then
            return
        end

        if player.Character then
            AntiFlingHookOtherCharacter(player.Character)
        end

        table.insert(AntiFlingConnections, player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
            if not AntiFlingEnabled then
                return
            end

            task.wait(0.15)

            if AntiFlingEnabled then
                AntiFlingHookOtherCharacter(character)
            end
        end))
    end

    local function AntiFlingStep()
        if not AntiFlingEnabled then
            return
        end

        local now = os.clock()
        if now - AntiFlingLastSelfCheck < AntiFlingSelfCheckInterval then
            return
        end
        AntiFlingLastSelfCheck = now

        local character = AntiFlingGetCharacter()
        local humanoid = AntiFlingGetHumanoid(character)
        local root = AntiFlingGetRoot(character)

        if not character or not humanoid or not root or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
            return
        end

        if not AntiFlingVelocityTooHigh(root) then
            return
        end

        AntiFlingZeroCharacter(character)

        pcall(function()
            humanoid.Sit = false
        end)

        pcall(function()
            humanoid.PlatformStand = false
        end)

        pcall(function()
            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        end)
    end

    local function AntiFlingStart()
        if AntiFlingEnabled then
            return
        end

        AntiFlingEnabled = true
        AntiFlingDisconnectAll()

        AntiFlingOriginalCollision = {}
        AntiFlingProcessedParts = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        AntiFlingLastSelfCheck = 0
        AntiFlingRunId = AntiFlingRunId + 1

        local selfCharacter = AntiFlingGetCharacter()
        AntiFlingHookSelfCharacter(selfCharacter)

        for _, player in ipairs(PlayersService:GetPlayers()) do
            AntiFlingHookPlayer(player)
        end

        table.insert(AntiFlingConnections, PlayersService.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
            if AntiFlingEnabled then
                AntiFlingHookPlayer(player)
            end
        end))

        table.insert(AntiFlingConnections, LocalPlayerObj.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newCharacter)
            if not AntiFlingEnabled then
                return
            end

            task.wait(0.25)

            if AntiFlingEnabled then
                AntiFlingHookSelfCharacter(newCharacter)
                AntiFlingNoCollideOtherPlayers()
            end
        end))

        -- 只保留一个 Heartbeat。之前三条事件一起跑，手机掉帧就是这么被我烤出来的。
        table.insert(AntiFlingConnections, RunServiceService.Heartbeat:Connect(AntiFlingStep))

        -- 低频补扫：防止有些角色后续生成新部件但事件没抓到
        local thisRunId = AntiFlingRunId
        task.spawn(function()
            while AntiFlingEnabled and AntiFlingRunId == thisRunId do
                AntiFlingNoCollideOtherPlayers()
                task.wait(1)
            end
        end)

        AntiFlingNoCollideOtherPlayers()

        AntiFlingSafeAddFeature("防甩飞")
        AntiFlingNotify("防甩飞", "已开启", 3, "success")
    end

    local function AntiFlingStop()
        AntiFlingEnabled = false
        AntiFlingRunId = AntiFlingRunId + 1

        AntiFlingDisconnectAll()
        AntiFlingRestoreCollision()

        AntiFlingProcessedParts = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

        AntiFlingSafeRemoveFeature("防甩飞")
        AntiFlingNotify("防甩飞", "已关闭", 2, "info")
    end

    TabAttr:Toggle({
        Title = "防甩飞",
        Desc = "可能导致甩飞不可用",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            if v then
                AntiFlingStart()
            else
                AntiFlingStop()
            end
        end
    })
end
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local BackPostEnabled = false
local BackPostRenderConn = nil
local BackPostCharConn = nil
local BackPostHumanoid = nil

local function stopBackPost()
    BackPostEnabled = false

    if BackPostRenderConn then
        BackPostRenderConn:Disconnect()
        BackPostRenderConn = nil
    end

    if BackPostCharConn then
        BackPostCharConn:Disconnect()
        BackPostCharConn = nil
    end

    if BackPostHumanoid and BackPostHumanoid.Parent then
        BackPostHumanoid.AutoRotate = true
    end

    BackPostHumanoid = nil
end

local function bindBackPostCharacter(character)
    if BackPostRenderConn then
        BackPostRenderConn:Disconnect()
        BackPostRenderConn = nil
    end

    local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    local rootPart = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)

    if not humanoid or not rootPart then
        return
    end

    BackPostHumanoid = humanoid
    humanoid.AutoRotate = false

    BackPostRenderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not BackPostEnabled then
            return
        end

        if not character.Parent or not rootPart.Parent or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
            return
        end

        local moveDir = humanoid.MoveDirection

        if moveDir.Magnitude > 0 then
            rootPart.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(rootPart.Position, rootPart.Position - moveDir)
        end
    end)
end

local function startBackPost()
    BackPostEnabled = true

    if LocalPlayer.Character then
        bindBackPostCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
    end

    BackPostCharConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
        if BackPostEnabled then
            bindBackPostCharacter(character)
        end
    end)
end

TabAttr:Toggle({
    Title = "背身单打",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            startBackPost()
            AddFeature("背身单打")
        else
            stopBackPost()
            RemoveFeature("背身单打")
        end
    end
})
-- ================= 背包 / 快捷栏补丁 =================
do
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
    local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    -- ================= 基础工具 =================

    local function BackpackNotify(title, content, duration, icon)
        title = tostring(title or "背包")
        content = tostring(content or "")
        duration = duration or 3
        icon = icon or "info"

        if type(Notify) == "function" then
            pcall(function()
                Notify(title, content, duration, icon)
            end)
            return
        end

        if WindUI and type(WindUI.Notify) == "function" then
            pcall(function()
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = title,
                    Content = content,
                    Duration = duration,
                    Icon = icon,
                })
            end)
        end
    end

    local function SafeAddFeature(name)
        pcall(function()
            if AddFeature then
                AddFeature(name)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function SafeRemoveFeature(name)
        pcall(function()
            if RemoveFeature then
                RemoveFeature(name)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function GetGuiParent()
        local ok, coreGui = pcall(function()
            return game:GetService("CoreGui")
        end)

        if ok and coreGui then
            return coreGui
        end

        return LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    end

    local function GetBackpackCoreEnabled()
        local ok, result = pcall(function()
            return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack)
        end)

        if ok and type(result) == "boolean" then
            return true, result
        end

        return false, nil
    end

    local function SetBackpackCoreEnabledOnce(enabled)
        return pcall(function()
            StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack, enabled)
        end)
    end

    local function SetBackpackCoreEnabledRetry(enabled)
        for _ = 1, 20 do
            local ok = SetBackpackCoreEnabledOnce(enabled)

            if ok then
                return true
            end

            task.wait(0.15)
        end

        return false
    end

    local function GetBackpack()
        return LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    end

    local function GetCharacter()
        return LocalPlayer.Character
    end

    local function GetHumanoid()
        local character = GetCharacter()
        if not character then
            return nil
        end

        return character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    end

    local function IsTool(obj)
        return typeof(obj) == "Instance" and obj:IsA("Tool")
    end

    local function GetToolTexture(tool)
        if not IsTool(tool) then
            return ""
        end

        local ok, texture = pcall(function()
            return tool.TextureId
        end)

        if ok and type(texture) == "string" then
            return texture
        end

        return ""
    end

    local function GetToolTip(tool)
        if not IsTool(tool) then
            return ""
        end

        local ok, tooltip = pcall(function()
            return tool.ToolTip
        end)

        if ok and type(tooltip) == "string" then
            return tooltip
        end

        return ""
    end

    local function GetToolKey(tool)
        if not IsTool(tool) then
            return nil
        end

        return tostring(tool.Name) .. "|" .. tostring(GetToolTexture(tool)) .. "|" .. tostring(GetToolTip(tool))
    end

    local function GetAllTools()
        local tools = {}
        local seen = {}

        local backpack = GetBackpack()
        local character = GetCharacter()

        if backpack then
            for _, obj in ipairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do
                if IsTool(obj) and not seen[obj] then
                    seen[obj] = true
                    table.insert(tools, obj)
                end
            end
        end

        if character then
            for _, obj in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                if IsTool(obj) and not seen[obj] then
                    seen[obj] = true
                    table.insert(tools, obj)
                end
            end
        end

        return tools
    end

    local function SyncToolOrder(orderTable)
        local currentTools = GetAllTools()
        local exists = {}

        for _, tool in ipairs(currentTools) do
            exists[tool] = true
        end

        for i = #orderTable, 1, -1 do
            local tool = orderTable[i]

            if not exists[tool] or not tool.Parent then
                table.remove(orderTable, i)
            end
        end

        local alreadyAdded = {}

        for _, tool in ipairs(orderTable) do
            alreadyAdded[tool] = true
        end

        for _, tool in ipairs(currentTools) do
            if not alreadyAdded[tool] then
                table.insert(orderTable, tool)
            end
        end

        return orderTable
    end

    local function FindToolByKey(key, usedTools)
        if not key then
            return nil
        end

        usedTools = usedTools or {}

        for _, tool in ipairs(GetAllTools()) do
            if IsTool(tool) and not usedTools[tool] and GetToolKey(tool) == key then
                return tool
            end
        end

        return nil
    end

    local function EquipOrUnequipTool(tool)
        if not IsTool(tool) or not tool.Parent then
            return
        end

        local humanoid = GetHumanoid()
        local character = GetCharacter()

        if not humanoid or not character then
            BackpackNotify("背包", "角色还没加载完", 2, "alert-triangle")
            return
        end

        pcall(function()
            if tool.Parent == character then
                humanoid:UnequipTools()
            else
                humanoid:EquipTool(tool)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function CreateCorner(parent, radius)
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 6)
        corner.Parent = parent
        return corner
    end

    local function CreateStroke(parent, color, thickness, transparency)
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Color = color or Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 90)
        stroke.Thickness = thickness or 1
        stroke.Transparency = transparency or 0.25
        stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        stroke.Parent = parent
        return stroke
    end

    local function ClearChildrenExceptLayout(parent)
        if not parent then
            return
        end

        for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
            if not child:IsA("UIListLayout")
                and not child:IsA("UIGridLayout")
                and not child:IsA("UIPadding")
            then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end

    local function GetViewportWidth()
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if camera then
            return camera.ViewportSize.X
        end

        return 800
    end

    local function DisconnectConnectionList(list)
        for _, conn in ipairs(list) do
            pcall(function()
                conn:Disconnect()
            end)
        end

        for i = #list, 1, -1 do
            list[i] = nil
        end
    end

    local function GetInputPosition(input)
        if input and input.Position then
            return Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        end

        local mouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
        return Vector2.new(mouse.X, mouse.Y)
    end

    local function IsPointInsideGui(guiObject, point, padding)
        if not guiObject or not guiObject.Parent or not point then
            return false
        end

        padding = padding or 0

        local pos = guiObject.AbsolutePosition
        local size = guiObject.AbsoluteSize

        return point.X >= pos.X - padding
            and point.X <= pos.X + size.X + padding
            and point.Y >= pos.Y - padding
            and point.Y <= pos.Y + size.Y + padding
    end

    local function TrimText(str)
        str = tostring(str or "")
        return str:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
    end

    local DisableCustomBackpack

    -- ================= 1. 强制启用系统背包 =================

    TabAttr:Button({
        Title = "强制启用系统背包",
        Callback = function()
            if DisableCustomBackpack then
                DisableCustomBackpack(true)
            end

            local coreOk, enabled = GetBackpackCoreEnabled()

            if coreOk and enabled then
                BackpackNotify("系统背包", "原游戏已启用背包，请勿重复点击", 3, "info")
                return
            end

            local setOk = SetBackpackCoreEnabledRetry(true)

            if setOk then
                BackpackNotify("系统背包", "背包已强制启用", 3, "check")
            else
                BackpackNotify("系统背包", "当前环境无法强制启用系统背包", 3, "x")
            end
        end
    })

    -- ================= 2. 自绘背包 =================

    local CustomBackpackEnabled = false
    local CustomOriginalBackpackEnabled = nil

    local CustomGui = nil
    local CustomHolder = nil
    local CustomHotbar = nil
    local CustomBagButton = nil
    local CustomBagPanel = nil
    local CustomBagGrid = nil
    local CustomBagGridLayout = nil
    local CustomSearchBox = nil

    local CustomConnections = {}
    local CustomToolOrder = {}

    local CustomHotbarSlots = {}
    local CustomHotbarKeys = {}
    local CustomSlotFrames = {}
    local CustomSlotStrokes = {}

    local CustomRefreshQueued = false
    local CustomRefreshAfterDrag = false
    local CustomLayoutInitialized = false

    local CustomSearchText = ""
    local CustomHotbarCount = 3

    local CustomDropSlot = nil

    local CUSTOM_SLOT_SIZE = 50
    local CUSTOM_SLOT_GAP = 5
    local CUSTOM_BAG_BUTTON_WIDTH = 42
    local DRAG_THRESHOLD = 9

    local COLOR_GLASS_DARK = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 22)
    local COLOR_GLASS_SLOT = Color3.fromRGB(32, 32, 38)
    local COLOR_STROKE = Color3.fromRGB(145, 145, 155)
    local COLOR_STROKE_DIM = Color3.fromRGB(92, 92, 102)
    local COLOR_TEXT = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
    local COLOR_TEXT_DIM = Color3.fromRGB(175, 175, 185)
    local COLOR_EQUIPPED = Color3.fromRGB(135, 185, 255)
    local COLOR_DROP = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 120)

    local DragState = nil
    local DragGhost = nil

    local CustomQueueRefresh
    local CustomRefreshNow
    local CustomStartWatchers

    local function SetCustomScrollingEnabled(enabled)
        pcall(function()
            if CustomBagGrid then
                CustomBagGrid.ScrollingEnabled = enabled
            end

            if CustomHotbar then
                CustomHotbar.ScrollingEnabled = enabled
            end
        end)
    end

    local function IsToolAssignedToHotbar(tool)
        if not IsTool(tool) then
            return false
        end

        for i = 1, CustomHotbarCount do
            if CustomHotbarSlots[i] == tool then
                return true
            end
        end

        return false
    end

    local function RemoveToolFromHotbar(tool)
        if not IsTool(tool) then
            return
        end

        for i = 1, 20 do
            if CustomHotbarSlots[i] == tool then
                CustomHotbarSlots[i] = nil
                CustomHotbarKeys[i] = nil
            end
        end
    end

    local function AssignToolToHotbar(index, tool)
        index = tonumber(index)

        if not index or index < 1 or index > CustomHotbarCount then
            return false
        end

        if not IsTool(tool) or not tool.Parent then
            return false
        end

        RemoveToolFromHotbar(tool)

        CustomHotbarSlots[index] = tool
        CustomHotbarKeys[index] = GetToolKey(tool)

        return true
    end

    local function SwapHotbarSlots(a, b)
        if not a or not b or a == b then
            return false
        end

        if a < 1 or a > CustomHotbarCount or b < 1 or b > CustomHotbarCount then
            return false
        end

        CustomHotbarSlots[a], CustomHotbarSlots[b] = CustomHotbarSlots[b], CustomHotbarSlots[a]
        CustomHotbarKeys[a], CustomHotbarKeys[b] = CustomHotbarKeys[b], CustomHotbarKeys[a]

        return true
    end

    local function ToolMatchesSearch(tool)
        if not IsTool(tool) then
            return false
        end

        local query = TrimText(CustomSearchText):lower()

        if query == "" then
            return true
        end

        local name = tostring(tool.Name or ""):lower()
        local tooltip = tostring(GetToolTip(tool) or ""):lower()

        return name:find(query, 1, true) ~= nil
            or tooltip:find(query, 1, true) ~= nil
    end

    local function CleanCustomHotbarSlots()
        local tools = GetAllTools()
        local exists = {}
        local usedTools = {}

        for _, tool in ipairs(tools) do
            exists[tool] = true
        end

        for i = 1, 20 do
            if i > CustomHotbarCount then
                CustomHotbarSlots[i] = nil
                CustomHotbarKeys[i] = nil
            else
                local tool = CustomHotbarSlots[i]
                local key = CustomHotbarKeys[i]

                if IsTool(tool) and exists[tool] and tool.Parent then
                    CustomHotbarKeys[i] = GetToolKey(tool)
                    usedTools[tool] = true
                elseif key then
                    local replacement = FindToolByKey(key, usedTools)

                    if replacement then
                        CustomHotbarSlots[i] = replacement
                        CustomHotbarKeys[i] = GetToolKey(replacement)
                        usedTools[replacement] = true
                    else
                        CustomHotbarSlots[i] = nil
                        -- 不清 key，重生后同名同纹理 Tool 回来还能恢复
                    end
                else
                    CustomHotbarSlots[i] = nil
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function InitializeCustomLayoutIfNeeded()
        if CustomLayoutInitialized then
            return
        end

        SyncToolOrder(CustomToolOrder)

        for i = 1, CustomHotbarCount do
            local tool = CustomToolOrder[i]
            CustomHotbarSlots[i] = tool
            CustomHotbarKeys[i] = tool and GetToolKey(tool) or nil
        end

        CustomLayoutInitialized = true
    end

    local function FillEmptySlotsWhenCountIncreased()
        SyncToolOrder(CustomToolOrder)

        for i = 1, CustomHotbarCount do
            if not CustomHotbarSlots[i] and not CustomHotbarKeys[i] then
                for _, tool in ipairs(CustomToolOrder) do
                    if IsTool(tool) and not IsToolAssignedToHotbar(tool) then
                        CustomHotbarSlots[i] = tool
                        CustomHotbarKeys[i] = GetToolKey(tool)
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function ResetCustomLayout()
        for index in pairs(CustomHotbarSlots) do
            CustomHotbarSlots[index] = nil
        end

        for index in pairs(CustomHotbarKeys) do
            CustomHotbarKeys[index] = nil
        end

        SyncToolOrder(CustomToolOrder)

        for i = 1, CustomHotbarCount do
            local tool = CustomToolOrder[i]
            CustomHotbarSlots[i] = tool
            CustomHotbarKeys[i] = tool and GetToolKey(tool) or nil
        end

        CustomLayoutInitialized = true
    end

    local function GetNearestHotbarSlot(point)
        if not point then
            return nil
        end

        local testPoints = { point }

        -- Roblox / CoreGui / IgnoreGuiInset 在不同环境下可能有坐标偏移
        local okInset, inset = pcall(function()
            return GuiService:GetGuiInset()
        end)

        if okInset and typeof(inset) == "Vector2" then
            table.insert(testPoints, Vector2.new(point.X - inset.X, point.Y - inset.Y))
            table.insert(testPoints, Vector2.new(point.X + inset.X, point.Y + inset.Y))
        end

        -- 有些执行环境 Input.Position 和实际 UI 有半格左右偏差
        -- 所以额外测试一圈小偏移点，解决“放旁边能替换，放正上方不行”
        local smallOffset = math.floor(CUSTOM_SLOT_SIZE * 0.45)

        table.insert(testPoints, Vector2.new(point.X - smallOffset, point.Y))
        table.insert(testPoints, Vector2.new(point.X + smallOffset, point.Y))
        table.insert(testPoints, Vector2.new(point.X, point.Y - smallOffset))
        table.insert(testPoints, Vector2.new(point.X, point.Y + smallOffset))

        table.insert(testPoints, Vector2.new(point.X - smallOffset, point.Y - smallOffset))
        table.insert(testPoints, Vector2.new(point.X + smallOffset, point.Y - smallOffset))
        table.insert(testPoints, Vector2.new(point.X - smallOffset, point.Y + smallOffset))
        table.insert(testPoints, Vector2.new(point.X + smallOffset, point.Y + smallOffset))

        -- 第一层：优先矩形命中
        for _, testPoint in ipairs(testPoints) do
            for index = 1, CustomHotbarCount do
                local slotFrame = CustomSlotFrames[index]

                if IsPointInsideGui(slotFrame, testPoint, 10) then
                    return index
                end
            end
        end

        -- 第二层：只要在快捷栏附近，就吸附最近槽位
        local nearHotbar = false

        for _, testPoint in ipairs(testPoints) do
            if CustomHolder and IsPointInsideGui(CustomHolder, testPoint, 55) then
                nearHotbar = true
                break
            end
        end

        if nearHotbar then
            local bestIndex = nil
            local bestDistance = math.huge

            for index = 1, CustomHotbarCount do
                local slotFrame = CustomSlotFrames[index]

                if slotFrame and slotFrame.Parent then
                    local pos = slotFrame.AbsolutePosition
                    local size = slotFrame.AbsoluteSize
                    local center = Vector2.new(pos.X + size.X / 2, pos.Y + size.Y / 2)

                    for _, testPoint in ipairs(testPoints) do
                        local dx = math.abs(testPoint.X - center.X)
                        local dy = math.abs(testPoint.Y - center.Y)

                        -- Y 权重高一点，避免拖到上方面板时乱吸附
                        local distance = dx + dy * 1.4

                        if dy <= size.Y * 2.0 and distance < bestDistance then
                            bestDistance = distance
                            bestIndex = index
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            if bestIndex then
                return bestIndex
            end
        end

        -- 第三层：GuiService 兜底
        local okObjects, objects = pcall(function()
            return GuiService:GetGuiObjectsAtPosition(point.X, point.Y)
        end)

        if okObjects and type(objects) == "table" then
            for _, obj in ipairs(objects) do
                local current = obj

                while current do
                    local attrOk, index = pcall(function()
                        return current:GetAttribute("YeHotbarIndex")
                    end)

                    if attrOk and type(index) == "number" then
                        return index
                    end

                    current = current.Parent
                end
            end
        end

        return nil
    end

    local function UpdateDropHighlight(index)
        for slotIndex, stroke in pairs(CustomSlotStrokes) do
            if stroke and stroke.Parent then
                if index and slotIndex == index then
                    stroke.Color = COLOR_DROP
                    stroke.Thickness = 2
                    stroke.Transparency = 0.02
                else
                    local tool = CustomHotbarSlots[slotIndex]
                    local character = GetCharacter()
                    local equipped = tool and character and tool.Parent == character

                    stroke.Color = equipped and COLOR_EQUIPPED or COLOR_STROKE_DIM
                    stroke.Thickness = equipped and 2 or 1
                    stroke.Transparency = equipped and 0.05 or 0.28
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function CustomUpdateSize()
        if not CustomHolder or not CustomHotbar then
            return
        end

        local screenWidth = GetViewportWidth()
        local maxWidth = math.floor(screenWidth * 0.94)

        local wantedSlotWidth = CustomHotbarCount * (CUSTOM_SLOT_SIZE + CUSTOM_SLOT_GAP)
        local scrollerWidth = math.clamp(
            wantedSlotWidth,
            CUSTOM_SLOT_SIZE + CUSTOM_SLOT_GAP,
            math.max(120, maxWidth - CUSTOM_BAG_BUTTON_WIDTH - 10)
        )

        CustomHolder.Size = UDim2.new(0, scrollerWidth + CUSTOM_BAG_BUTTON_WIDTH + 10, 0, 60)
        CustomHotbar.Size = UDim2.new(0, scrollerWidth, 0, 54)
        CustomHotbar.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, wantedSlotWidth + 6, 0, 0)

        if CustomBagPanel then
            local panelWidth = math.min(maxWidth, 410)
            CustomBagPanel.Size = UDim2.new(0, panelWidth, 0, 265)
        end
    end

    local function DestroyDragGhost()
        if DragGhost then
            DragGhost:Destroy()
            DragGhost = nil
        end
    end

    local function CreateToolIcon(parent, tool, size, yOffset)
        local texture = GetToolTexture(tool)

        if texture ~= "" then
            local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            icon.Name = "Icon"
            icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size)
            icon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -size / 2, 0.5, yOffset or -size / 2)
            icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            icon.Image = texture
            icon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
            icon.ZIndex = parent.ZIndex + 1
            icon.Parent = parent
            return icon
        end

        return nil
    end

    local function CreateDragGhost(tool, point)
        DestroyDragGhost()

        if not CustomGui or not IsTool(tool) then
            return
        end

        local ghost = Instance.new("Frame")
        ghost.Name = "DragGhost"
        ghost.Size = UDim2.new(0, CUSTOM_SLOT_SIZE, 0, CUSTOM_SLOT_SIZE)
        ghost.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        ghost.Position = UDim2.new(0, point.X, 0, point.Y)
        ghost.BackgroundColor3 = COLOR_GLASS_SLOT
        ghost.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
        ghost.BorderSizePixel = 0
        ghost.ZIndex = 300
        ghost.Parent = CustomGui

        CreateCorner(ghost, 6)
        CreateStroke(ghost, COLOR_DROP, 2, 0.02)

        CreateToolIcon(ghost, tool, 28, -13)

        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        label.Name = "Name"
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 14)
        label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -16)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Text = tostring(tool.Name)
        label.TextColor3 = COLOR_TEXT
        label.TextSize = 8
        label.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        label.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        label.ZIndex = 301
        label.Parent = ghost

        DragGhost = ghost
    end

    local function UpdateDragGhost(point)
        if DragGhost then
            DragGhost.Position = UDim2.new(0, point.X, 0, point.Y)
        end
    end

    local function ApplyDropResult(dropState, point)
        if not dropState or not IsTool(dropState.tool) then
            return false
        end

        local targetSlot = dropState.hoverSlot or CustomDropSlot or GetNearestHotbarSlot(point)

        if targetSlot and targetSlot >= 1 and targetSlot <= CustomHotbarCount then
            if dropState.sourceKind == "hotbar" then
                return SwapHotbarSlots(dropState.sourceIndex, targetSlot)
            end

            if dropState.sourceKind == "bag" then
                return AssignToolToHotbar(targetSlot, dropState.tool)
            end
        end

        if dropState.sourceKind == "hotbar" and CustomBagPanel and CustomBagPanel.Visible then
            if IsPointInsideGui(CustomBagPanel, point, 12) then
                local sourceIndex = dropState.sourceIndex

                if sourceIndex and sourceIndex >= 1 and sourceIndex <= CustomHotbarCount then
                    CustomHotbarSlots[sourceIndex] = nil
                    CustomHotbarKeys[sourceIndex] = nil
                    return true
                end
            end
        end

        return false
    end

    local function BeginDragCandidate(sourceKind, sourceIndex, tool, input)
        if not CustomBackpackEnabled or not IsTool(tool) then
            return
        end

        local point = GetInputPosition(input)

        CustomDropSlot = nil
        UpdateDropHighlight(nil)

        DragState = {
            sourceKind = sourceKind,
            sourceIndex = sourceIndex,
            tool = tool,
            startPoint = point,
            currentPoint = point,
            hoverSlot = nil,
            dragging = false,
        }
    end

    local function BindToolInput(guiObject, sourceKind, sourceIndex, tool)
        guiObject.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            then
                return
            end

            BeginDragCandidate(sourceKind, sourceIndex, tool, input)
        end)
    end

    local function CreateSlotVisual(parent, data)
        local sourceKind = data.SourceKind
        local sourceIndex = data.SourceIndex
        local tool = data.Tool
        local equipped = data.Equipped
        local layoutOrder = data.LayoutOrder or 1
        local indexText = data.IndexText or ""
        local isHotbar = sourceKind == "hotbar"

        local slot = Instance.new("TextButton")
        slot.Name = sourceKind .. "_" .. tostring(sourceIndex or layoutOrder)
        slot.Size = UDim2.new(0, CUSTOM_SLOT_SIZE, 0, CUSTOM_SLOT_SIZE)
        slot.BackgroundColor3 = COLOR_GLASS_SLOT
        slot.BackgroundTransparency = equipped and 0.18 or 0.32
        slot.BorderSizePixel = 0
        slot.AutoButtonColor = true
        slot.Text = ""
        slot.ClipsDescendants = true
        slot.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder
        slot.Parent = parent

        if isHotbar then
            slot:SetAttribute("YeHotbarIndex", sourceIndex)
            CustomSlotFrames[sourceIndex] = slot
        end

        CreateCorner(slot, 6)

        local stroke = CreateStroke(
            slot,
            equipped and COLOR_EQUIPPED or COLOR_STROKE_DIM,
            equipped and 2 or 1,
            equipped and 0.05 or 0.28
        )

        if isHotbar then
            CustomSlotStrokes[sourceIndex] = stroke
        end

        if indexText ~= "" then
            local indexLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            indexLabel.Name = "Index"
            indexLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 14)
            indexLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 2)
            indexLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            indexLabel.Text = tostring(indexText)
            indexLabel.TextColor3 = equipped and COLOR_EQUIPPED or COLOR_TEXT_DIM
            indexLabel.TextSize = 10
            indexLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            indexLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            indexLabel.ZIndex = slot.ZIndex + 1
            indexLabel.Parent = slot
        end

        if tool then
            CreateToolIcon(slot, tool, 28, -14)

            local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            nameLabel.Name = "Name"
            nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 13)
            nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -15)
            nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            nameLabel.Text = tostring(tool.Name)
            nameLabel.TextColor3 = COLOR_TEXT
            nameLabel.TextSize = 8
            nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            nameLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            nameLabel.ZIndex = slot.ZIndex + 1
            nameLabel.Parent = slot

            BindToolInput(slot, sourceKind, sourceIndex, tool)
        end

        return slot
    end

    local function RefreshHotbar()
        if not CustomBackpackEnabled or not CustomHotbar then
            return
        end

        CustomUpdateSize()
        ClearChildrenExceptLayout(CustomHotbar)

        for index in pairs(CustomSlotFrames) do
            CustomSlotFrames[index] = nil
        end

        for index in pairs(CustomSlotStrokes) do
            CustomSlotStrokes[index] = nil
        end

        local character = GetCharacter()

        for i = 1, CustomHotbarCount do
            local tool = CustomHotbarSlots[i]
            local equipped = tool and character and tool.Parent == character

            local indexText = tostring(i)
            if i == 10 then
                indexText = "0"
            end

            CreateSlotVisual(CustomHotbar, {
                SourceKind = "hotbar",
                SourceIndex = i,
                Tool = tool,
                Equipped = equipped,
                LayoutOrder = i,
                IndexText = indexText,
            })
        end
    end

    local function RefreshBag()
        if not CustomBackpackEnabled or not CustomBagPanel or not CustomBagGrid then
            return
        end

        ClearChildrenExceptLayout(CustomBagGrid)

        local tools = SyncToolOrder(CustomToolOrder)
        local character = GetCharacter()
        local shownCount = 0

        for _, tool in ipairs(tools) do
            if not IsToolAssignedToHotbar(tool) and ToolMatchesSearch(tool) then
                shownCount = shownCount + 1

                local equipped = character and tool.Parent == character

                CreateSlotVisual(CustomBagGrid, {
                    SourceKind = "bag",
                    SourceIndex = nil,
                    Tool = tool,
                    Equipped = equipped,
                    LayoutOrder = shownCount,
                    IndexText = "",
                })
            end
        end

        if shownCount <= 0 then
            local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            empty.Name = "Empty"
            empty.Size = UDim2.new(0, CUSTOM_SLOT_SIZE * 2, 0, CUSTOM_SLOT_SIZE)
            empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            empty.Text = TrimText(CustomSearchText) ~= "" and "没有搜索结果" or "空"
            empty.TextColor3 = COLOR_TEXT_DIM
            empty.TextSize = 13
            empty.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            empty.LayoutOrder = 1
            empty.Parent = CustomBagGrid
        end

        task.defer(function()
            if CustomBagGrid and CustomBagGridLayout then
                local h = CustomBagGridLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y
                CustomBagGrid.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(h + 10, 60))
            end
        end)
    end

    CustomRefreshNow = function()
        if not CustomBackpackEnabled then
            return
        end

        SetBackpackCoreEnabledOnce(false)

        SyncToolOrder(CustomToolOrder)
        InitializeCustomLayoutIfNeeded()
        CleanCustomHotbarSlots()

        RefreshHotbar()

        if CustomBagPanel and CustomBagPanel.Visible then
            RefreshBag()
        end
    end

    CustomQueueRefresh = function()
        if not CustomBackpackEnabled then
            return
        end

        if DragState then
            CustomRefreshAfterDrag = true
            return
        end

        if CustomRefreshQueued then
            return
        end

        CustomRefreshQueued = true

        task.defer(function()
            CustomRefreshQueued = false

            if CustomBackpackEnabled and not DragState then
                CustomRefreshNow()
            else
                CustomRefreshAfterDrag = true
            end
        end)
    end

    local function ForceRefreshAfterRespawn()
        if not CustomBackpackEnabled then
            return
        end

        SetBackpackCoreEnabledOnce(false)
        CustomQueueRefresh()

        local delays = { 0.25, 0.75, 1.5, 2.5 }

        for _, delayTime in ipairs(delays) do
            task.delay(delayTime, function()
                if CustomBackpackEnabled then
                    SetBackpackCoreEnabledOnce(false)

                    if CustomStartWatchers then
                        CustomStartWatchers()
                    end

                    CustomQueueRefresh()
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    CustomStartWatchers = function()
        DisconnectConnectionList(CustomConnections)

        local backpack = GetBackpack()
        local character = GetCharacter()

        if backpack then
            table.insert(CustomConnections, backpack.ChildAdded:Connect(CustomQueueRefresh))
            table.insert(CustomConnections, backpack.ChildRemoved:Connect(CustomQueueRefresh))
        end

        if character then
            table.insert(CustomConnections, character.ChildAdded:Connect(CustomQueueRefresh))
            table.insert(CustomConnections, character.ChildRemoved:Connect(CustomQueueRefresh))
        end

        table.insert(CustomConnections, LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            ForceRefreshAfterRespawn()
        end))

        table.insert(CustomConnections, LocalPlayer.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == "Backpack" and CustomBackpackEnabled then
                task.delay(0.15, function()
                    if CustomBackpackEnabled then
                        CustomStartWatchers()
                        CustomQueueRefresh()
                    end
                end)
            end
        end))

        table.insert(CustomConnections, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
            if gameProcessed or not CustomBackpackEnabled then
                return
            end

            if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
                return
            end

            local keyToIndex = {
                [Enum.KeyCode.One] = 1,
                [Enum.KeyCode.Two] = 2,
                [Enum.KeyCode.Three] = 3,
                [Enum.KeyCode.Four] = 4,
                [Enum.KeyCode.Five] = 5,
                [Enum.KeyCode.Six] = 6,
                [Enum.KeyCode.Seven] = 7,
                [Enum.KeyCode.Eight] = 8,
                [Enum.KeyCode.Nine] = 9,
                [Enum.KeyCode.Zero] = 10,
            }

            local index = keyToIndex[input.KeyCode]
            if not index or index > CustomHotbarCount then
                return
            end

            local tool = CustomHotbarSlots[index]

            if tool then
                EquipOrUnequipTool(tool)
                CustomQueueRefresh()
            end
        end))

        table.insert(CustomConnections, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if not DragState then
                return
            end

            if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            then
                return
            end

            local point = GetInputPosition(input)
            DragState.currentPoint = point

            local hoverSlot = GetNearestHotbarSlot(point)
            DragState.hoverSlot = hoverSlot
            CustomDropSlot = hoverSlot

            if DragState.dragging then
                UpdateDropHighlight(hoverSlot)
            end

            if not DragState.dragging then
                local delta = point - DragState.startPoint

                if delta.Magnitude >= DRAG_THRESHOLD then
                    DragState.dragging = true
                    SetCustomScrollingEnabled(false)
                    CreateDragGhost(DragState.tool, point)
                    UpdateDropHighlight(hoverSlot)
                end
            end

            if DragState.dragging then
                UpdateDragGhost(point)
            end
        end))

        table.insert(CustomConnections, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if not DragState then
                return
            end

            if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            then
                return
            end

            local state = DragState
            local point = GetInputPosition(input)

            if state.currentPoint then
                point = state.currentPoint
            end

            local wasDragging = state.dragging

            if wasDragging then
                ApplyDropResult(state, point)
            else
                if IsTool(state.tool) then
                    EquipOrUnequipTool(state.tool)
                end
            end

            DragState = nil
            CustomDropSlot = nil

            DestroyDragGhost()
            SetCustomScrollingEnabled(true)
            UpdateDropHighlight(nil)

            if CustomRefreshAfterDrag then
                CustomRefreshAfterDrag = false
            end

            CustomQueueRefresh()
        end))
    end

    local function CustomCreateGui()
        if CustomGui then
            CustomGui:Destroy()
            CustomGui = nil
        end

        CustomGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        CustomGui.Name = "YeCustomBackpack"
        CustomGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        CustomGui.IgnoreGuiInset = false
        CustomGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        CustomGui.Parent = GetGuiParent()

        CustomHolder = Instance.new("Frame")
        CustomHolder.Name = "HotbarHolder"
        CustomHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
        CustomHolder.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -13)
        CustomHolder.BackgroundColor3 = COLOR_GLASS_DARK
        CustomHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 0.42
        CustomHolder.BorderSizePixel = 0
        CustomHolder.Parent = CustomGui

        CreateCorner(CustomHolder, 8)
        CreateStroke(CustomHolder, COLOR_STROKE, 1, 0.48)

        CustomBagButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
        CustomBagButton.Name = "BagButton"
        CustomBagButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, CUSTOM_BAG_BUTTON_WIDTH, 0, CUSTOM_SLOT_SIZE)
        CustomBagButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 5)
        CustomBagButton.BackgroundColor3 = COLOR_GLASS_SLOT
        CustomBagButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.30
        CustomBagButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
        CustomBagButton.AutoButtonColor = true
        CustomBagButton.Text = "..."
        CustomBagButton.TextColor3 = COLOR_TEXT
        CustomBagButton.TextSize = 18
        CustomBagButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        CustomBagButton.Parent = CustomHolder

        CreateCorner(CustomBagButton, 6)
        CreateStroke(CustomBagButton, COLOR_STROKE_DIM, 1, 0.28)

        CustomHotbar = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        CustomHotbar.Name = "HotbarScroller"
        CustomHotbar.Position = UDim2.new(0, CUSTOM_BAG_BUTTON_WIDTH + 10, 0, 5)
        CustomHotbar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        CustomHotbar.BorderSizePixel = 0
        CustomHotbar.ScrollBarThickness = 2
        CustomHotbar.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.X
        CustomHotbar.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        CustomHotbar.Parent = CustomHolder

        local slotLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        slotLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
        slotLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        slotLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, CUSTOM_SLOT_GAP)
        slotLayout.Parent = CustomHotbar

        CustomBagPanel = Instance.new("Frame")
        CustomBagPanel.Name = "BagPanel"
        CustomBagPanel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
        CustomBagPanel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -82)
        CustomBagPanel.BackgroundColor3 = COLOR_GLASS_DARK
        CustomBagPanel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.32
        CustomBagPanel.BorderSizePixel = 0
        CustomBagPanel.Visible = false
        CustomBagPanel.Parent = CustomGui

        CreateCorner(CustomBagPanel, 8)
        CreateStroke(CustomBagPanel, COLOR_STROKE, 1, 0.45)

        local panelTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        panelTitle.Name = "Title"
        panelTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 28)
        panelTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 4)
        panelTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        panelTitle.Text = "背包"
        panelTitle.TextColor3 = COLOR_TEXT
        panelTitle.TextSize = 14
        panelTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        panelTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        panelTitle.Parent = CustomBagPanel

        local hint = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        hint.Name = "Hint"
        hint.Size = UDim2.new(0, 190, 0, 24)
        hint.Position = UDim2.new(1, -198, 0, 6)
        hint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        hint.Text = "点击使用，拖动整理"
        hint.TextColor3 = COLOR_TEXT_DIM
        hint.TextSize = 11
        hint.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        hint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        hint.Parent = CustomBagPanel

        CustomSearchBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
        CustomSearchBox.Name = "SearchBox"
