        CustomSearchBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 28)
        CustomSearchBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 34)
        CustomSearchBox.BackgroundColor3 = COLOR_GLASS_SLOT
        CustomSearchBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0.38
        CustomSearchBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
        CustomSearchBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        CustomSearchBox.PlaceholderText = "搜索物品..."
        CustomSearchBox.Text = CustomSearchText
        CustomSearchBox.TextColor3 = COLOR_TEXT
        CustomSearchBox.PlaceholderColor3 = COLOR_TEXT_DIM
        CustomSearchBox.TextSize = 13
        CustomSearchBox.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        CustomSearchBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        CustomSearchBox.Parent = CustomBagPanel

        CreateCorner(CustomSearchBox, 6)
        CreateStroke(CustomSearchBox, COLOR_STROKE_DIM, 1, 0.35)

        local searchPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        searchPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
        searchPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
        searchPadding.Parent = CustomSearchBox

        CustomSearchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            CustomSearchText = CustomSearchBox.Text or ""

            if CustomBackpackEnabled then
                CustomQueueRefresh()
            end
        end)

        CustomBagGrid = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        CustomBagGrid.Name = "BagGrid"
        CustomBagGrid.Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 1, -74)
        CustomBagGrid.Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 68)
        CustomBagGrid.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        CustomBagGrid.BorderSizePixel = 0
        CustomBagGrid.ScrollBarThickness = 3
        CustomBagGrid.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        CustomBagGrid.Parent = CustomBagPanel

        CustomBagGridLayout = Instance.new("UIGridLayout")
        CustomBagGridLayout.CellSize = UDim2.new(0, CUSTOM_SLOT_SIZE, 0, CUSTOM_SLOT_SIZE)
        CustomBagGridLayout.CellPadding = UDim2.new(0, CUSTOM_SLOT_GAP, 0, CUSTOM_SLOT_GAP)
        CustomBagGridLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        CustomBagGridLayout.Parent = CustomBagGrid

        CustomBagButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if not CustomBagPanel then
                return
            end

            CustomBagPanel.Visible = not CustomBagPanel.Visible
            CustomQueueRefresh()
        end)

        CustomUpdateSize()
    end

    local function EnableCustomBackpack()
        if CustomBackpackEnabled then
            return
        end

        local ok, enabled = GetBackpackCoreEnabled()
        CustomOriginalBackpackEnabled = ok and enabled or true

        CustomBackpackEnabled = true

        SetBackpackCoreEnabledRetry(false)

        CustomCreateGui()
        CustomStartWatchers()
        CustomQueueRefresh()

        SafeAddFeature("自绘背包")
        BackpackNotify("自绘背包", "已开启", 2, "check")
    end

    DisableCustomBackpack = function(silent)
        if not CustomBackpackEnabled then
            return
        end

        CustomBackpackEnabled = false
        DragState = nil
        CustomDropSlot = nil

        DestroyDragGhost()
        SetCustomScrollingEnabled(true)
        UpdateDropHighlight(nil)

        DisconnectConnectionList(CustomConnections)

        if CustomGui then
            CustomGui:Destroy()
            CustomGui = nil
        end

        CustomHolder = nil
        CustomHotbar = nil
        CustomBagButton = nil
        CustomBagPanel = nil
        CustomBagGrid = nil
        CustomBagGridLayout = nil
        CustomSearchBox = nil

        for index in pairs(CustomSlotFrames) do
            CustomSlotFrames[index] = nil
        end

        for index in pairs(CustomSlotStrokes) do
            CustomSlotStrokes[index] = nil
        end

        SetBackpackCoreEnabledRetry(CustomOriginalBackpackEnabled ~= false)
        CustomOriginalBackpackEnabled = nil

        SafeRemoveFeature("自绘背包")

        if not silent then
            BackpackNotify("自绘背包", "已关闭，已恢复原背包状态", 2, "check")
        end
    end

    TabAttr:Toggle({
        Title = "自绘背包",
        Desc = "半透明背包，可拖动物品整理快捷栏",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            if v then
                EnableCustomBackpack()
            else
                DisableCustomBackpack(false)
            end
        end
    })

    TabAttr:Input({
        Title = "快捷栏数量",
        Desc = "输入 1 - 20",
        Placeholder = "默认 3",
        Default = tostring(CustomHotbarCount),
        Callback = function(text)
            local num = tonumber(text)

            if not num then
                BackpackNotify("快捷栏数量", "请输入数字", 2, "x")
                return
            end

            local oldCount = CustomHotbarCount
            CustomHotbarCount = math.clamp(math.floor(num), 1, 20)

            if CustomHotbarCount > oldCount then
                FillEmptySlotsWhenCountIncreased()
            end

            for index in pairs(CustomHotbarSlots) do
                if index > CustomHotbarCount then
                    CustomHotbarSlots[index] = nil
                    CustomHotbarKeys[index] = nil
                end
            end

            if CustomBackpackEnabled then
                CustomUpdateSize()
                CustomQueueRefresh()
                BackpackNotify("快捷栏数量", "已设置为：" .. tostring(CustomHotbarCount), 2, "check")
            else
                BackpackNotify("快捷栏数量", "已设置为 " .. tostring(CustomHotbarCount) .. "，开启后生效", 2, "info")
            end
        end
    })

    TabAttr:Button({
        Title = "重置背包布局",
        Desc = "按当前工具顺序重新填充快捷栏",
        Callback = function()
            ResetCustomLayout()

            if CustomBackpackEnabled then
                CustomQueueRefresh()
            end

            BackpackNotify("自绘背包", "快捷栏布局已重置", 2, "check")
        end
    })
end

-- ================= 镜头灵敏度修改 V3 =================
do
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local CameraSensitivityEnabled = false
    local CameraSensitivityValue = 1

    local SensitivityConfigFile = "YeScript_CameraSensitivity.txt"

    local OriginalMouseDeltaSensitivity = nil
    local OriginalUserGameMouseSensitivity = nil

    local OriginalCameraType = nil
    local OriginalCameraSubject = nil
    local OriginalCameraCFrame = nil
    local OriginalCameraFocus = nil
    local OriginalFieldOfView = nil

    local SensitivityConnections = {}
    local SensitivityRenderName = "YeCameraSensitivityV3RenderStep"
    local SensitivityRenderBound = false

    local CameraYaw = 0
    local CameraPitch = 0
    local CameraZoom = 10
    local PendingCameraDelta = Vector2.new(0, 0)

    -- ================= 通知 =================

    local function SensitivityNotify(title, content, duration, icon)
        title = tostring(title or "镜头灵敏度")
        content = tostring(content or "")
        duration = duration or 2
        icon = icon or "info"

        if type(Notify) == "function" then
            pcall(function()
                Notify(title, content, duration, icon)
            end)
            return
        end

        if WindUI and type(WindUI.Notify) == "function" then
            pcall(function()
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = title,
                    Content = content,
                    Duration = duration,
                    Icon = icon,
                })
            end)
        end
    end

    local function SafeAddSensitivityFeature()
        pcall(function()
            if AddFeature then
                AddFeature("镜头灵敏度")
            end
        end)
    end

    local function SafeRemoveSensitivityFeature()
    pcall(function()
        if RemoveFeature then
            RemoveFeature("镜头灵敏度")
        end
    end)
end
    -- ================= 自动记忆 =================

    local function HasFileApi()
        local ok = pcall(function()
            return type(writefile) == "function"
                and type(readfile) == "function"
                and type(isfile) == "function"
        end)

        return ok
            and type(writefile) == "function"
            and type(readfile) == "function"
            and type(isfile) == "function"
    end

    local function LoadSavedSensitivity()
        local saved = nil

        if HasFileApi() then
            pcall(function()
                if isfile(SensitivityConfigFile) then
                    saved = tonumber(readfile(SensitivityConfigFile))
                end
            end)
        end

        if saved == nil and getgenv then
            pcall(function()
                saved = tonumber(getgenv().YE_SAVED_CAMERA_SENSITIVITY)
            end)
        end

        if saved ~= nil then
            CameraSensitivityValue = math.clamp(saved, 0.01, 10)
        end
    end

    local function SaveSensitivity(value)
        value = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 1, 0.01, 10)

        if getgenv then
            pcall(function()
                getgenv().YE_SAVED_CAMERA_SENSITIVITY = value
            end)
        end

        if HasFileApi() then
            pcall(function()
                writefile(SensitivityConfigFile, tostring(value))
            end)
        end
    end

    LoadSavedSensitivity()

    -- ================= Roblox 设置记录 / 恢复 =================

    local function GetUserGameSettings()
        local settingsObj = nil

        pcall(function()
            settingsObj = UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings")
        end)

        return settingsObj
    end

    local function RecordOriginalRobloxSensitivity()
        if OriginalMouseDeltaSensitivity == nil then
            pcall(function()
                OriginalMouseDeltaSensitivity = UserInputService.MouseDeltaSensitivity
            end)
        end

        if OriginalUserGameMouseSensitivity == nil then
            local gameSettings = GetUserGameSettings()

            if gameSettings then
                pcall(function()
                    OriginalUserGameMouseSensitivity = gameSettings.MouseSensitivity
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    local function ApplyRobloxSensitivity()
        local value = math.clamp(tonumber(CameraSensitivityValue) or 1, 0.01, 10)

        pcall(function()
            UserInputService.MouseDeltaSensitivity = value
        end)

        local gameSettings = GetUserGameSettings()

        if gameSettings then
            pcall(function()
                gameSettings.MouseSensitivity = value
            end)
        end
    end

    local function RestoreOriginalRobloxSensitivity()
        if OriginalMouseDeltaSensitivity ~= nil then
            pcall(function()
                UserInputService.MouseDeltaSensitivity = OriginalMouseDeltaSensitivity
            end)
        end

        if OriginalUserGameMouseSensitivity ~= nil then
            local gameSettings = GetUserGameSettings()

            if gameSettings then
                pcall(function()
                    gameSettings.MouseSensitivity = OriginalUserGameMouseSensitivity
                end)
            end
        end

        OriginalMouseDeltaSensitivity = nil
        OriginalUserGameMouseSensitivity = nil
    end

    -- ================= 相机记录 / 恢复 =================

    local function GetCharacter()
        return LocalPlayer.Character
    end

    local function GetHumanoid()
        local character = GetCharacter()
        if not character then
            return nil
        end

        return character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    end

    local function GetRootPart()
        local character = GetCharacter()
        if not character then
            return nil
        end

        return character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            or character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            or character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
    end

    local function GetCameraTargetPosition()
        local root = GetRootPart()

        if root then
            return root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.8, 0)
        end

        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if camera then
            return camera.Focus.Position
        end

        return Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
    end

    local function RecordOriginalCameraState()
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then
            return
        end

        if OriginalCameraType == nil then
            OriginalCameraType = camera.CameraType
        end

        if OriginalCameraSubject == nil then
            OriginalCameraSubject = camera.CameraSubject
        end

        if OriginalCameraCFrame == nil then
            OriginalCameraCFrame = camera.CFrame
        end

        if OriginalCameraFocus == nil then
            OriginalCameraFocus = camera.Focus
        end

        if OriginalFieldOfView == nil then
            OriginalFieldOfView = camera.FieldOfView
        end
    end

    local function InitCustomCameraAngles()
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then
            CameraYaw = 0
            CameraPitch = 0
            CameraZoom = 10
            return
        end

        local targetPosition = GetCameraTargetPosition()
        local cameraPosition = camera.CFrame.Position
        local offset = cameraPosition - targetPosition

        CameraZoom = math.clamp(offset.Magnitude, 4, 25)

        local look = camera.CFrame.LookVector

        CameraYaw = math.atan2(-look.X, -look.Z)
        CameraPitch = math.asin(math.clamp(look.Y, -0.95, 0.95))
        CameraPitch = math.clamp(CameraPitch, -1.25, 1.25)
    end

    local function EnableScriptableCamera()
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then
            return
        end

        pcall(function()
            camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
        end)
    end

    local function RestoreOriginalCameraState()
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then
            return
        end

        local humanoid = GetHumanoid()

        pcall(function()
            if OriginalCameraSubject and OriginalCameraSubject.Parent then
                camera.CameraSubject = OriginalCameraSubject
            elseif humanoid then
                camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
            end
        end)

        pcall(function()
            camera.CameraType = OriginalCameraType or Enum.CameraType.Custom
        end)

        pcall(function()
            if OriginalFieldOfView ~= nil then
                camera.FieldOfView = OriginalFieldOfView
            end
        end)

        pcall(function()
            if OriginalCameraCFrame ~= nil then
                camera.CFrame = OriginalCameraCFrame
            end
        end)

        pcall(function()
            if OriginalCameraFocus ~= nil then
                camera.Focus = OriginalCameraFocus
            end
        end)

        -- 延迟再还一次，防止 Roblox 默认相机脚本下一帧又抢状态。
        task.defer(function()
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not cam then
                return
            end

            pcall(function()
                if OriginalCameraSubject and OriginalCameraSubject.Parent then
                    cam.CameraSubject = OriginalCameraSubject
                elseif humanoid then
                    cam.CameraSubject = humanoid
                end
            end)

            pcall(function()
                cam.CameraType = OriginalCameraType or Enum.CameraType.Custom
            end)
        end)

        OriginalCameraType = nil
        OriginalCameraSubject = nil
        OriginalCameraCFrame = nil
        OriginalCameraFocus = nil
        OriginalFieldOfView = nil
    end

    -- ================= 输入捕获 =================

    local function DisconnectSensitivityConnections()
        for _, conn in ipairs(SensitivityConnections) do
            pcall(function()
                conn:Disconnect()
            end)
        end

        SensitivityConnections = {}
    end

    local function ClampInputDelta(delta)
        local x = math.clamp(delta.X, -120, 120)
        local y = math.clamp(delta.Y, -120, 120)

        return Vector2.new(x, y)
    end

    local function IsRightSideTouch(input)
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then
            return true
        end

        local viewport = camera.ViewportSize

        -- 手机端左侧一般是移动摇杆区域，右侧才更像镜头区域。
        return input.Position.X >= viewport.X * 0.35
    end

    local function StartSensitivityInputCapture()
        DisconnectSensitivityConnections()

        table.insert(SensitivityConnections, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
            if not CameraSensitivityEnabled then
                return
            end

            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                PendingCameraDelta += ClampInputDelta(input.Delta)
                return
            end

            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                if gameProcessed then
                    return
                end

                if not IsRightSideTouch(input) then
                    return
                end

                PendingCameraDelta += ClampInputDelta(input.Delta)
                return
            end

            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
                local wheel = input.Position.Z

                if wheel ~= 0 then
                    CameraZoom = math.clamp(CameraZoom - wheel, 3, 30)
                end
            end
        end))
    end

    -- ================= 自定义相机核心 =================

    local function ApplyCameraCollision(targetPosition, desiredPosition)
        local character = GetCharacter()
        local direction = desiredPosition - targetPosition
        local distance = direction.Magnitude

        if distance <= 0.1 then
            return desiredPosition
        end

        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
        params.IgnoreWater = true

        if character then
            params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { character }
        else
            params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {}
        end

        local result = workspace:Raycast(targetPosition, direction, params)

        if result then
            return result.Position - direction.Unit * 0.35
        end

        return desiredPosition
    end

    local function UpdateCustomCamera()
        if not CameraSensitivityEnabled then
            return
        end

        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then
            PendingCameraDelta = Vector2.new(0, 0)
            return
        end

        EnableScriptableCamera()
        ApplyRobloxSensitivity()

        local delta = PendingCameraDelta
        PendingCameraDelta = Vector2.new(0, 0)

        local value = math.clamp(tonumber(CameraSensitivityValue) or 1, 0.01, 10)

        -- 这里才是真正控制高低灵敏度的地方。
        -- value 越小，每一帧改变的角度越小，低灵敏度就真的生效。
        local baseScale = 0.0026

        if math.abs(delta.X) > 0.01 or math.abs(delta.Y) > 0.01 then
            CameraYaw = CameraYaw - delta.X * baseScale * value
            CameraPitch = CameraPitch - delta.Y * baseScale * value
            CameraPitch = math.clamp(CameraPitch, -1.25, 1.25)
        end

        local targetPosition = GetCameraTargetPosition()

        local rotation = CFrame.fromEulerAnglesYXZ(CameraPitch, CameraYaw, 0)
        local lookVector = rotation.LookVector

        local desiredPosition = targetPosition - lookVector * CameraZoom
        local finalPosition = ApplyCameraCollision(targetPosition, desiredPosition)

        pcall(function()
            camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(finalPosition, targetPosition)
            camera.Focus = CFrame.new(targetPosition)
        end)
    end

    local function BindSensitivityRender()
        pcall(function()
            RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(SensitivityRenderName)
        end)

        RunService:BindToRenderStep(
            SensitivityRenderName,
            Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 2,
            function()
                UpdateCustomCamera()
            end
        )

        SensitivityRenderBound = true
    end

    local function UnbindSensitivityRender()
        if SensitivityRenderBound then
            pcall(function()
                RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(SensitivityRenderName)
            end)

            SensitivityRenderBound = false
        end

        -- 顺手清一下上一版可能残留的名字，防止你测试时没删干净。
        pcall(function()
            RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("YeCameraSensitivityRenderStep")
        end)
    end

    -- ================= 开关逻辑 =================

    local function EnableCameraSensitivity()
        if CameraSensitivityEnabled then
            return
        end

        RecordOriginalRobloxSensitivity()
        RecordOriginalCameraState()
        InitCustomCameraAngles()

        CameraSensitivityEnabled = true
        PendingCameraDelta = Vector2.new(0, 0)

        ApplyRobloxSensitivity()
        EnableScriptableCamera()
        StartSensitivityInputCapture()
        BindSensitivityRender()

        SafeAddSensitivityFeature()

        SensitivityNotify(
            "镜头灵敏度",
            "已开启，当前数值：" .. tostring(CameraSensitivityValue),
            2,
            "check"
        )
    end

    local function DisableCameraSensitivity()
        if not CameraSensitivityEnabled then
            return
        end

        CameraSensitivityEnabled = false
        PendingCameraDelta = Vector2.new(0, 0)

        DisconnectSensitivityConnections()
        UnbindSensitivityRender()

        RestoreOriginalRobloxSensitivity()
        RestoreOriginalCameraState()

        SafeRemoveSensitivityFeature()

        SensitivityNotify(
            "镜头灵敏度",
            "已关闭，已恢复原本灵敏度",
            2,
            "check"
        )
    end

    TabAttr:Toggle({
        Title = "修改镜头灵敏度",
        Desc = "支持自动记忆",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            if v then
                EnableCameraSensitivity()
            else
                DisableCameraSensitivity()
            end
        end
    })

    TabAttr:Input({
        Title = "镜头灵敏度",
        Desc = "建议 0.1 - 5，支持 0.01 - 10",
        Placeholder = "默认 1",
        Default = tostring(CameraSensitivityValue),
        Value = tostring(CameraSensitivityValue),
        Callback = function(text)
            local num = tonumber(text)

            if not num then
                SensitivityNotify(
                    "镜头灵敏度",
                    "请输入数字",
                    2,
                    "x"
                )
                return
            end

            CameraSensitivityValue = math.clamp(num, 0.01, 10)
            SaveSensitivity(CameraSensitivityValue)

            if CameraSensitivityEnabled then
                ApplyRobloxSensitivity()

                SensitivityNotify(
                    "镜头灵敏度",
                    "已设置并保存为：" .. tostring(CameraSensitivityValue),
                    2,
                    "check"
                )
            else
                SensitivityNotify(
                    "镜头灵敏度",
                    "已保存为 " .. tostring(CameraSensitivityValue) .. "，开启后生效",
                    2,
                    "info"
                )
            end
        end
    })
end
end)()
;
(function()
local TabOther = Window:Tab({
    Title = "通用",
    Icon = "info",
    Locked = false,
})

-- X-Ray 配置
local XrayEnabled = false
local XrayTransparency = 0.65

local XrayOriginal = {}
local XrayConnections = {}

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local function IsCharacterPart(part)
    local model = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    if not model then
        return false
    end

    if model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
        return true
    end

    return false
end

local function ApplyXrayToPart(part)
    if not XrayEnabled then
        return
    end

    if not part:IsA("BasePart") then
        return
    end

    -- 不处理玩家角色
    if IsCharacterPart(part) then
        return
    end

    if XrayOriginal[part] == nil then
        XrayOriginal[part] = part.LocalTransparencyModifier
    end

    part.LocalTransparencyModifier = XrayTransparency
end

local function RefreshXray()
    if not XrayEnabled then
        return
    end

    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        ApplyXrayToPart(obj)
    end
end

local function StartXray()
    if XrayEnabled then
        return
    end

    XrayEnabled = true

    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        ApplyXrayToPart(obj)
    end

    XrayConnections.DescendantAdded = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        task.wait()
        ApplyXrayToPart(obj)
    end)

    if type(AddFeature) == "function" then
        AddFeature("X-Ray")
    end
end

local function StopXray()
    if not XrayEnabled then
        return
    end

    XrayEnabled = false

    for _, conn in pairs(XrayConnections) do
        if conn then
            conn:Disconnect()
        end
    end

    table.clear(XrayConnections)

    for part, oldValue in pairs(XrayOriginal) do
        if part and part.Parent and part:IsA("BasePart") then
            part.LocalTransparencyModifier = oldValue
        end
    end

    table.clear(XrayOriginal)

    if type(RemoveFeature) == "function" then
        RemoveFeature("X-Ray")
    end
end

TabOther:Toggle({
    Title = "X光（地图透视）",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)

        if v then
            StartXray()
        else
            StopXray()
        end

    end
})

TabOther:Slider({
    Title = "X光透明度",
    Value = {
        Min = 0,
        Max = 100,
        Default = 65,
    },
    Increment = 1,
    Callback = function(value)
        XrayTransparency = value / 100
        RefreshXray()
    end
})
;(function()
-- =================== 自由视角模块（PC + 触控稳定版 / 不锚定人物） ===================

local Freecam = {
    Speed = 2,
    VerticalSpeed = 2,
    Sensitivity = 0.01,

    Enabled = false,
    Rig = nil,
    Loop = nil,

    Yaw = 0,
    Pitch = 0,

    MoveInput = Vector2.zero,
    KeyboardMove = Vector3.zero,

    Connections = {},

    Touch = {
        Move = nil,
        Look = nil,
        MoveStart = nil
    },

    Keys = {
        W = false,
        A = false,
        S = false,
        D = false,
        Up = false,
        Down = false,
    },

    RightMouseDown = false,

    OldCameraType = nil,
    OldHumanoidState = nil,
}

local function FreecamGetCharParts()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then
        return nil, nil, nil
    end

    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    return char, root, hum
end

local function FreecamSaveHumanoidState(hum)
    if not hum then
        return
    end

    Freecam.OldHumanoidState = {
        WalkSpeed = hum.WalkSpeed,
        JumpPower = hum.JumpPower,
        JumpHeight = hum.JumpHeight,
        UseJumpPower = hum.UseJumpPower,
    }
end

local function FreecamBlockWalkRunJump()
    local _, _, hum = FreecamGetCharParts()
    if not hum then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        hum.WalkSpeed = 0
        hum.Jump = false

        -- 不碰 Sit / PlatformStand / Anchored，避免车被定住
        if hum.UseJumpPower then
            hum.JumpPower = 0
        else
            hum.JumpHeight = 0
        end

        hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
    end)
end

local function FreecamRestoreHumanoidState()
    local _, _, hum = FreecamGetCharParts()
    if not hum then
        return
    end

    local old = Freecam.OldHumanoidState

    pcall(function()
        if SpeedEnabled then
            hum.WalkSpeed = TargetWalkSpeed
        elseif old and old.WalkSpeed then
            hum.WalkSpeed = old.WalkSpeed
        else
            hum.WalkSpeed = OriginalWalkSpeed or 16
        end

        if CustomJumpEnabled then
            hum.UseJumpPower = true
            hum.JumpPower = CustomJumpValue
            hum.JumpHeight = CustomJumpValue * (7.2 / 50)
        elseif old then
            hum.UseJumpPower = old.UseJumpPower

            if old.JumpPower then
                hum.JumpPower = old.JumpPower
            end

            if old.JumpHeight then
                hum.JumpHeight = old.JumpHeight
            end
        else
            hum.JumpPower = OriginalJump or 50
            hum.JumpHeight = (OriginalJump or 50) * (7.2 / 50)
        end
    end)

    Freecam.OldHumanoidState = nil
end

local function FreecamRefreshKeyboardMove()
    local x = 0
    local y = 0
    local z = 0

    if Freecam.Keys.A then
        x -= 1
    end

    if Freecam.Keys.D then
        x += 1
    end

    if Freecam.Keys.W then
        y += 1
    end

    if Freecam.Keys.S then
        y -= 1
    end

    if Freecam.Keys.Up then
        z += 1
    end

    if Freecam.Keys.Down then
        z -= 1
    end

    Freecam.KeyboardMove = Vector3.new(x, y, z)
end

local function FreecamSetKey(keyCode, state)
    if keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.W then
        Freecam.Keys.W = state
    elseif keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.A then
        Freecam.Keys.A = state
    elseif keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.S then
        Freecam.Keys.S = state
    elseif keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.D then
        Freecam.Keys.D = state
    elseif keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space or keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E then
        Freecam.Keys.Up = state
    elseif keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl or keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q then
        Freecam.Keys.Down = state
    end

    FreecamRefreshKeyboardMove()
end

-- ✅ 初始化输入：触控 + PC 键盘鼠标
local function InitFreecamInput()
    if Freecam._InputInited then
        return
    end

    Freecam._InputInited = true

    table.insert(Freecam.Connections, UserInputService.TouchStarted:Connect(function(t)
        if not Freecam.Enabled then
            return
        end

        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then
            return
        end

        local half = camera.ViewportSize.X / 2

        if t.Position.X < half then
            if not Freecam.Touch.Move then
                Freecam.Touch.Move = t
                Freecam.Touch.MoveStart = t.Position
                Freecam.MoveInput = Vector2.zero
            end
        else
            if not Freecam.Touch.Look then
                Freecam.Touch.Look = t
            end
        end
    end))

    table.insert(Freecam.Connections, UserInputService.TouchMoved:Connect(function(t)
        if not Freecam.Enabled then
            return
        end

        if t == Freecam.Touch.Move and Freecam.Touch.MoveStart then
            local delta = t.Position - Freecam.Touch.MoveStart

            Freecam.MoveInput = Vector2.new(
                math.clamp(delta.X / 80, -1, 1),
                math.clamp(-delta.Y / 80, -1, 1)
            )
        elseif t == Freecam.Touch.Look then
            local d = t.Delta

            Freecam.Yaw -= d.X * Freecam.Sensitivity
            Freecam.Pitch = math.clamp(
                Freecam.Pitch - d.Y * Freecam.Sensitivity,
                math.rad(-85),
                math.rad(85)
            )
        end
    end))

    table.insert(Freecam.Connections, UserInputService.TouchEnded:Connect(function(t)
        if t == Freecam.Touch.Move then
            Freecam.Touch.Move = nil
            Freecam.Touch.MoveStart = nil
            Freecam.MoveInput = Vector2.zero
        end

        if t == Freecam.Touch.Look then
            Freecam.Touch.Look = nil
        end
    end))

    table.insert(Freecam.Connections, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
        if not Freecam.Enabled then
            return
        end

        if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
            return
        end

        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
            Freecam.RightMouseDown = true
            return
        end

        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
            FreecamSetKey(input.KeyCode, true)
        end
    end))

    table.insert(Freecam.Connections, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
            Freecam.RightMouseDown = false
            return
        end

        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
            FreecamSetKey(input.KeyCode, false)
        end
    end))

    table.insert(Freecam.Connections, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not Freecam.Enabled then
            return
        end

        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
            return
        end

        if not Freecam.RightMouseDown then
            return
        end

        local d = input.Delta

        Freecam.Yaw -= d.X * Freecam.Sensitivity
        Freecam.Pitch = math.clamp(
            Freecam.Pitch - d.Y * Freecam.Sensitivity,
            math.rad(-85),
            math.rad(85)
        )
    end))
end

local function StartFreecam()
    if Freecam.Enabled then
        return
    end

    local char, root, hum = FreecamGetCharParts()
    if not char or not root or not hum then
        return
    end

    Freecam.Enabled = true
    InitFreecamInput()

    FreecamSaveHumanoidState(hum)
    FreecamBlockWalkRunJump()

    if not Freecam.Rig then
        Freecam.Rig = Instance.new("Part")
        Freecam.Rig.Name = "YeFreecamRig"
        Freecam.Rig.Anchored = true
        Freecam.Rig.CanCollide = false
        Freecam.Rig.Transparency = 1
        Freecam.Rig.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
        Freecam.Rig.Parent = workspace
    end

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then
        return
    end

    Freecam.Rig.CFrame = camera.CFrame
    Freecam.OldCameraType = camera.CameraType

    camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable

    local x, y = camera.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
    Freecam.Yaw = y
    Freecam.Pitch = x

    Freecam.Loop = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not Freecam.Enabled then
            return
        end

        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera or not Freecam.Rig then
            return
        end

        -- 每帧压住走路 / 跑步 / 跳跃，但不锚定人物，不禁坐下，不开 PlatformStand
        FreecamBlockWalkRunJump()

        local rotCF =
            CFrame.Angles(0, Freecam.Yaw, 0) *
            CFrame.Angles(Freecam.Pitch, 0, 0)

        local cf = CFrame.new(Freecam.Rig.Position) * rotCF

        local touchMove = Freecam.MoveInput
        local keyMove = Freecam.KeyboardMove

        local planar = Vector2.new(
            touchMove.X + keyMove.X,
            touchMove.Y + keyMove.Y
        )

        if planar.Magnitude > 1 then
            planar = planar.Unit
        end

        local vertical = math.clamp(keyMove.Z, -1, 1)

        local move =
            (cf.RightVector * planar.X) +
            (cf.LookVector * planar.Y) +
            (Vector3.yAxis * vertical)

        if move.Magnitude > 1 then
            move = move.Unit
        end

        Freecam.Rig.CFrame =
            CFrame.new(Freecam.Rig.Position + move * Freecam.Speed) * rotCF

        camera.CFrame = Freecam.Rig.CFrame
    end)
end

local function StopFreecam()
    if not Freecam.Enabled then
        return
    end

    Freecam.Enabled = false

    if Freecam.Loop then
        Freecam.Loop:Disconnect()
        Freecam.Loop = nil
    end

    for _, conn in pairs(Freecam.Connections) do
        pcall(function()
            conn:Disconnect()
        end)
    end

    Freecam.Connections = {}
    Freecam._InputInited = false

    Freecam.MoveInput = Vector2.zero
    Freecam.KeyboardMove = Vector3.zero

    Freecam.Touch.Move = nil
    Freecam.Touch.Look = nil
    Freecam.Touch.MoveStart = nil

    Freecam.RightMouseDown = false

    for key in pairs(Freecam.Keys) do
        Freecam.Keys[key] = false
    end

    FreecamRestoreHumanoidState()

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera then
        camera.CameraType = Freecam.OldCameraType or Enum.CameraType.Custom
    end
end

-- ================= 自由视角（唯一保留通知） =================

TabOther:Toggle({
    Title = "自由视角",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)

        if v then
            StartFreecam()
            Notify("自由视角", "已开启", 2, "success")
            AddFeature("自由视角")
        else
            StopFreecam()
            Notify("自由视角", "已关闭", 2, "info")
            RemoveFeature("自由视角")
        end

    end
})

TabOther:Slider({
    Title = "自由视角速度",
    Value = {
        Min = 1,
        Max = 20,
        Default = 2,
    },
    Increment = 0.5,
    Callback = function(value)
        Freecam.Speed = value
    end
})
end)()
;(function()
-- ================= 防摔 =================
-- ================= 防摔落伤害2（平滑位移补偿） =================

local AntiFall2Enabled = false
local AntiFall2Connection = nil

local function StartAntiFall2(character)

    if AntiFall2Connection then
        AntiFall2Connection:Disconnect()
        AntiFall2Connection = nil
    end

    local root = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    local lastY = root.Position.Y
    local CHECK_INTERVAL = 14

    AntiFall2Connection = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()

        if not AntiFall2Enabled then return end
        if not character.Parent then return end

        local currentPosition = root.Position
        local fallDistance = lastY - currentPosition.Y

        -- 触发保护
        if fallDistance >= CHECK_INTERVAL then

            local currentVel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity

            -- ⭐核心补偿（防抖）
            root.CFrame = root.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -0.5, 0)

            -- ⭐速度重置
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(currentVel.X, -10, currentVel.Z)

            lastY = root.Position.Y
        end

        -- 上升时刷新安全高度
        if currentPosition.Y > lastY then
            lastY = currentPosition.Y
        end

    end)
end

-- 启动监听
Players.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.5)
    StartAntiFall2(char)
end)

if Players.LocalPlayer.Character then
    StartAntiFall2(Players.LocalPlayer.Character)
end
-- =================== 防摔落伤害模块 (最终稳定版) ===================

local AntiFallEnabled = false
local AntiFallConnection = nil

-- =================== 核心 ===================
local function StartAntiFall(character)

    local root = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
    if not root then return end

    -- 清理旧连接
    if AntiFallConnection then
        AntiFallConnection:Disconnect()
        AntiFallConnection = nil
    end

    AntiFallConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()

        if not AntiFallEnabled then return end
        if not character.Parent then return end
        if not root or not root.Parent then return end

        local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity

        -- 关键：同帧操作（不能改）
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = velocity

    end)

end

-- =================== 开关 ===================
local function ToggleAntiFall(state)

    AntiFallEnabled = state

    if state then
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character

        if char then
            char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            StartAntiFall(char)
        end

        Notify("防摔落伤害","已开启",1)

    else
        if AntiFallConnection then
            AntiFallConnection:Disconnect()
            AntiFallConnection = nil
        end

        Notify("防摔落伤害","已关闭",1)
    end

end

-- =================== 重生修复（关键） ===================
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)

    if not AntiFallEnabled then return end

    -- 等角色完全加载
    local root = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
    if not root then return end

    -- 延迟一点点，避免刚生成没物理
    task.wait(0.2)

    if AntiFallEnabled then
        StartAntiFall(char)
    end

end)

TabOther:Toggle({
    Title = "防摔落伤害",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)

        ToggleAntiFall(v)

        if v then
            AddFeature("防摔")
        else
            RemoveFeature("防摔")
        end

    end
})

TabOther:Toggle({
    Title = "防摔落伤害2（1没用再开）",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        AntiFall2Enabled = state

        if state then
            Notify("防摔落伤害2", "已开启", 3)
            AddFeature("防摔落伤害2")
        else
            Notify("防摔落伤害2", "已关闭", 3)
            RemoveFeature("防摔落伤害2")
        end
    end
})
end)()

(function()
-- ================= 瞬移模块（PC + 移动端取消 + 美化UI / 不锚定人物）=================

local TP_Module = {
    Gui = nil,
    Connections = {},
    ExitMode = nil,
}

local TP_Loaded = false

function EnableTPUI()
    if TP_Loaded then
        if TP_Module.Gui then
            TP_Module.Gui.Enabled = true
        end
        return
    end

    TP_Loaded = true

    -- ================= 服务 =================
    local PlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
    local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

    local LP = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
    local Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

    local cameraConn = Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
        Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    end)

    table.insert(TP_Module.Connections, cameraConn)

    -- ================= 状态 =================
    local Mode = false
    local Root = nil
    local Hum = nil

    local OldCF = nil
    local OldType = nil
    local OldHumanoidState = nil

    local FixedY = 0

    local MoveInput = Vector2.zero
    local KeyMove = Vector2.zero

    local TouchMove = nil
    local TouchStart = nil

    local Speed = 3
    local HeightSpeed = 4

    local UpHolding = false
    local DownHolding = false

    local Keys = {
        W = false,
        A = false,
        S = false,
        D = false,
        Up = false,
        Down = false,
    }

    local function AddConnection(conn)
        table.insert(TP_Module.Connections, conn)
        return conn
    end

    local function SafeTween(obj, info, props)
        pcall(function()
            if obj and obj.Parent then
                TweenService:Create(obj, info, props):Play()
            end
        end)
    end

    local function GetGuiParent()
        local parent = nil

        pcall(function()
            if gethui then
                parent = gethui()
            end
        end)

        if parent then
            return parent
        end

        pcall(function()
            parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        end)

        if parent then
            return parent
        end

        return LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    end

    local function MakeCorner(obj, radius)
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 10)
        corner.Parent = obj
        return corner
    end

    local function MakeStroke(obj, color, thickness, transparency)
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Color = color or Color3.fromRGB(100, 170, 255)
        stroke.Thickness = thickness or 1
        stroke.Transparency = transparency or 0.35
        stroke.Parent = obj
        return stroke
    end

    local function MakeGradient(obj, c1, c2)
        local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, c1),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, c2),
        })
        gradient.Rotation = 90
        gradient.Parent = obj
        return gradient
    end

    local function MakeButton(parent, name, text, pos, size, color1, color2)
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        btn.Name = name
        btn.Parent = parent
        btn.Position = pos
        btn.Size = size
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = color1
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.Text = text
        btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        btn.TextSize = 14
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold

        MakeCorner(btn, 12)
        MakeStroke(btn, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 1, 0.82)
        MakeGradient(btn, color1, color2)

        btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            SafeTween(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                BackgroundColor3 = color2,
            })
        end)

        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            SafeTween(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                BackgroundColor3 = color1,
            })
        end)

        btn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
            SafeTween(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.08), {
                Size = UDim2.new(size.X.Scale, size.X.Offset - 2, size.Y.Scale, size.Y.Offset - 2),
            })
        end)

        btn.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
            SafeTween(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.08), {
                Size = size,
            })
        end)

        return btn
    end

    -- ================= UI =================
    local Gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    Gui.Name = "YeLightningTPUI"
    Gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    Gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    Gui.Enabled = true
    Gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling

    pcall(function()
        if protectgui then
            protectgui(Gui)
        end
    end)

    Gui.Parent = GetGuiParent()
    TP_Module.Gui = Gui

    local Panel = Instance.new("Frame")
    Panel.Name = "LightningPanel"
    Panel.Parent = Gui
    Panel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
    Panel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0.42, 0)
    Panel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(142, 178)
    Panel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 22, 32)
    Panel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
    Panel.BorderSizePixel = 0

    MakeCorner(Panel, 16)
    MakeStroke(Panel, Color3.fromRGB(95, 170, 255), 1, 0.35)
    MakeGradient(Panel, Color3.fromRGB(24, 30, 45), Color3.fromRGB(13, 17, 28))

    local Glow = Instance.new("Frame")
    Glow.Name = "Glow"
    Glow.Parent = Panel
    Glow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    Glow.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    Glow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 18, 1, 18)
    Glow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 150, 255)
    Glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.92
    Glow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Glow.ZIndex = 0

    MakeCorner(Glow, 20)

    local Title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    Title.Name = "Title"
    Title.Parent = Panel
    Title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 8)
    Title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20)
    Title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Title.Text = "瞬移面板"
    Title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 240, 255)
    Title.TextSize = 14
    Title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    Title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    local SubTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    SubTitle.Name = "SubTitle"
    SubTitle.Parent = Panel
    SubTitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 29)
    SubTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 16)
    SubTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    SubTitle.Text = "T确认 / 移动端点按钮"
    SubTitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(145, 160, 180)
    SubTitle.TextSize = 10
    SubTitle.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        -- ================= 顶部拖动区域：只能拖标题栏，按钮区域不能拖 =================
    local DragArea = Instance.new("TextButton")
    DragArea.Name = "DragArea"
    DragArea.Parent = Panel
    DragArea.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    DragArea.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 48)
    DragArea.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    DragArea.BorderSizePixel = 0
    DragArea.Text = ""
    DragArea.AutoButtonColor = false
    DragArea.Active = true
    DragArea.ZIndex = 20

    -- 防止透明拖动层把标题视觉盖没
    Title.ZIndex = 21
    SubTitle.ZIndex = 21
    DragArea.ZIndex = 22

    local DraggingPanel = false
    local DragStartPosition = nil
    local PanelStartPosition = nil
    local DragInputObject = nil

    AddConnection(DragArea.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            return
        end

        DraggingPanel = true
        DragStartPosition = input.Position
        PanelStartPosition = Panel.Position

        input.Changed:Connect(function()
            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                DraggingPanel = false
                DragInputObject = nil
            end
        end)
    end))

    AddConnection(DragArea.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            DragInputObject = input
        end
    end))

    AddConnection(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not DraggingPanel then
            return
        end

        if input ~= DragInputObject then
            return
        end

        if not DragStartPosition or not PanelStartPosition then
            return
        end

        local delta = input.Position - DragStartPosition

        Panel.Position = UDim2.new(
            PanelStartPosition.X.Scale,
            PanelStartPosition.X.Offset + delta.X,
            PanelStartPosition.Y.Scale,
            PanelStartPosition.Y.Offset + delta.Y
        )
    end))

    local Btn = MakeButton(
        Panel,
        "MainButton",
        "瞬移",
        UDim2.fromOffset(10, 54),
        UDim2.fromOffset(122, 36),
        Color3.fromRGB(52, 118, 255),
        Color3.fromRGB(95, 70, 255)
    )

    local CancelBtn = MakeButton(
        Panel,
        "CancelButton",
        "取消",
        UDim2.fromOffset(10, 96),
        UDim2.fromOffset(122, 30),
        Color3.fromRGB(170, 55, 65),
        Color3.fromRGB(115, 35, 45)
    )

    CancelBtn.Visible = false

    local UpBtn = MakeButton(
        Panel,
        "UpButton",
        "上升",
        UDim2.fromOffset(10, 132),
        UDim2.fromOffset(58, 30),
        Color3.fromRGB(45, 150, 100),
        Color3.fromRGB(30, 110, 80)
    )

    local DownBtn = MakeButton(
        Panel,
        "DownButton",
        "下降",
        UDim2.fromOffset(74, 132),
        UDim2.fromOffset(58, 30),
        Color3.fromRGB(45, 110, 170),
        Color3.fromRGB(35, 75, 130)
    )

    UpBtn.Visible = false
    DownBtn.Visible = false

    local CrossRing = Instance.new("Frame")
    CrossRing.Name = "CrossRing"
    CrossRing.Parent = Gui
    CrossRing.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 32)
    CrossRing.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    CrossRing.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    CrossRing.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    CrossRing.Visible = false

    MakeCorner(CrossRing, 16)
    MakeStroke(CrossRing, Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70), 2, 0.15)

    local Cross = Instance.new("Frame")
    Cross.Name = "CrossDot"
    Cross.Parent = CrossRing
    Cross.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8)
    Cross.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    Cross.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    Cross.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
    Cross.BorderSizePixel = 0

    MakeCorner(Cross, 8)

    local Hint = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    Hint.Name = "Hint"
    Hint.Parent = Gui
    Hint.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
    Hint.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -28)
    Hint.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(420, 28)
    Hint.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 16, 24)
    Hint.BackgroundTransparency = 0.16
    Hint.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Hint.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(225, 235, 255)
    Hint.TextSize = 12
    Hint.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    Hint.Text = "PC：T进入/确认，WASD移动，Space/E上升，Ctrl/Q下降，Backspace/Esc取消"
    Hint.Visible = false

    MakeCorner(Hint, 10)
    MakeStroke(Hint, Color3.fromRGB(95, 170, 255), 1, 0.65)

    -- ================= 人物移动锁：不锚定，不禁坐下，不 PlatformStand =================
    local function RefreshChar()
        local char = LP.Character

        if not char then
            Root = nil
            Hum = nil
            return
        end

        Root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        Hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    end

    local function SaveHumanoidState()
        RefreshChar()

        if not Hum then
            return
        end

        OldHumanoidState = {
            WalkSpeed = Hum.WalkSpeed,
            JumpPower = Hum.JumpPower,
            JumpHeight = Hum.JumpHeight,
            UseJumpPower = Hum.UseJumpPower,
        }
    end

    local function BlockWalkRunJump()
        RefreshChar()

        if not Hum then
            return
        end

        pcall(function()
            Hum.WalkSpeed = 0
            Hum.Jump = false

            if Hum.UseJumpPower then
                Hum.JumpPower = 0
            else
                Hum.JumpHeight = 0
            end

            -- 只清掉人物移动输入，不 Anchored，不 PlatformStand，不改 Sit。
            Hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
        end)
    end

    local function RestoreHumanoidState()
        RefreshChar()

        if not Hum then
            return
        end

        local old = OldHumanoidState

        pcall(function()
            if SpeedEnabled then
                Hum.WalkSpeed = TargetWalkSpeed
            elseif old and old.WalkSpeed then
                Hum.WalkSpeed = old.WalkSpeed
            else
                Hum.WalkSpeed = OriginalWalkSpeed or 16
            end

            if CustomJumpEnabled then
                Hum.UseJumpPower = true
                Hum.JumpPower = CustomJumpValue
                Hum.JumpHeight = CustomJumpValue * (7.2 / 50)
            elseif old then
                Hum.UseJumpPower = old.UseJumpPower

                if old.JumpPower then
                    Hum.JumpPower = old.JumpPower
                end

                if old.JumpHeight then
                    Hum.JumpHeight = old.JumpHeight
                end
            else
                Hum.JumpPower = OriginalJump or 50
                Hum.JumpHeight = (OriginalJump or 50) * (7.2 / 50)
            end
        end)

        OldHumanoidState = nil
    end

    -- ================= 模式 UI =================
    local function SetModeUI(enabled)
        if enabled then
            Btn.Text = "确认地点"
            CancelBtn.Visible = true
            UpBtn.Visible = true
            DownBtn.Visible = true
            CrossRing.Visible = true
            Hint.Visible = true

            SafeTween(Panel, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(142, 178),
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.04,
            })
        else
            Btn.Text = "瞬移"
            CancelBtn.Visible = false
            UpBtn.Visible = false
            DownBtn.Visible = false
            CrossRing.Visible = false
            Hint.Visible = false

            SafeTween(Panel, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(142, 96),
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.08,
            })
        end
    end

    SetModeUI(false)

    -- ================= 核心 =================
    local function Enter()
        RefreshChar()

        if not Root or not Camera then
            return
        end

        OldCF = Camera.CFrame
        OldType = Camera.CameraType

        SaveHumanoidState()
        BlockWalkRunJump()

        Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
        FixedY = Root.Position.Y + 150

        Camera.CFrame =
            CFrame.new(Root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 150, 0))
            * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), 0, 0)

        SetModeUI(true)
    end

    local function Exit()
        RestoreHumanoidState()

        if Camera then
            Camera.CameraType = OldType or Enum.CameraType.Custom

            if OldCF then
                Camera.CFrame = OldCF
            end
        end

        MoveInput = Vector2.zero
        KeyMove = Vector2.zero
        TouchMove = nil
        TouchStart = nil

        for key in pairs(Keys) do
            Keys[key] = false
        end

        UpHolding = false
        DownHolding = false

        SetModeUI(false)
    end

    local function GetCenterRay()
        if not Camera then
            return nil
        end

        local absPos = CrossRing.AbsolutePosition
        local absSize = CrossRing.AbsoluteSize

        local x = absPos.X + absSize.X / 2
        local y = absPos.Y + absSize.Y / 2

        local ray = Camera:ViewportPointToRay(x, y)

        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LP.Character }

        local result = Workspace:Raycast(
            ray.Origin,
            Vector3.new(0, -5000, 0),
            params
        )

        return result and result.Position or ray.Origin
    end

    local function TP()
        RefreshChar()

        if not Root then
            return
        end

        local pos = GetCenterRay()

        if not pos then
            return
        end

        Root.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
    end

    local function StartMode()
        if Mode then
            return
        end

        Mode = true
        Enter()
    end

    local function ConfirmMode()
        if not Mode then
            return
        end

        Mode = false

        TP()
        Exit()
    end

    local function CancelMode()
        if not Mode then
            return
        end

        Mode = false
        Exit()
    end

    TP_Module.ExitMode = CancelMode

    -- ================= PC 输入 =================
    local function RefreshKeyMove()
        local x = 0
        local y = 0

        if Keys.A then
            x -= 1
        end

        if Keys.D then
            x += 1
        end

        if Keys.W then
            y -= 1
        end

        if Keys.S then
            y += 1
        end

        KeyMove = Vector2.new(x, y)

        if KeyMove.Magnitude > 1 then
            KeyMove = KeyMove.Unit
        end
    end

    local function SetMoveKey(keyCode, state)
        if keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.W then
            Keys.W = state
        elseif keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.A then
            Keys.A = state
        elseif keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.S then
            Keys.S = state
        elseif keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.D then
            Keys.D = state
        elseif keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space or keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E then
            Keys.Up = state
        elseif keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl or keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q then
            Keys.Down = state
        end

        RefreshKeyMove()
    end

    AddConnection(UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if not Gui.Enabled then
            return
        end

        if UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then
            return
        end

        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
            return
        end

        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.T then
            if Mode then
                ConfirmMode()
            else
                StartMode()
            end

            return
        end

        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
            CancelMode()
            return
        end

        if Mode then
            SetMoveKey(input.KeyCode, true)
        end
    end))

    AddConnection(UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
            return
        end

        SetMoveKey(input.KeyCode, false)
    end))

    -- ================= 移动端触控移动 =================
    AddConnection(UIS.TouchStarted:Connect(function(t)
        if not Mode or not Camera then
            return
        end

        if t.Position.X < Camera.ViewportSize.X / 2 then
            TouchMove = t
            TouchStart = t.Position
        end
    end))

    AddConnection(UIS.TouchMoved:Connect(function(t)
        if not Mode then
            return
        end

        if t ~= TouchMove or not TouchStart then
            return
        end

        local delta = t.Position - TouchStart

        MoveInput = Vector2.new(
            math.clamp(delta.X / 50, -1, 1),
            math.clamp(delta.Y / 50, -1, 1)
        )
    end))

    AddConnection(UIS.TouchEnded:Connect(function(t)
        if t == TouchMove then
            TouchMove = nil
            TouchStart = nil
            MoveInput = Vector2.zero
        end
    end))

    -- ================= 按钮事件 =================
    AddConnection(Btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if Mode then
            ConfirmMode()
        else
            StartMode()
        end
    end))

    AddConnection(CancelBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        CancelMode()
    end))

    AddConnection(UpBtn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
        UpHolding = true
    end))

    AddConnection(UpBtn.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
        UpHolding = false
    end))

    AddConnection(UpBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        UpHolding = false
    end))

    AddConnection(DownBtn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
        DownHolding = true
    end))

    AddConnection(DownBtn.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
        DownHolding = false
    end))

    AddConnection(DownBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        DownHolding = false
    end))

    -- ================= 主循环 =================
    AddConnection(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not Mode or not Camera then
            return
        end

        BlockWalkRunJump()

        local finalMove = MoveInput + KeyMove

        if finalMove.Magnitude > 1 then
            finalMove = finalMove.Unit
        end

        local pos = Camera.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(
            finalMove.X * Speed,
            0,
            finalMove.Y * Speed
        )

        if UpHolding or Keys.Up then
            FixedY += HeightSpeed
        end

        if DownHolding or Keys.Down then
            FixedY -= HeightSpeed
        end

        Camera.CFrame =
            CFrame.new(Vector3.new(pos.X, FixedY, pos.Z))
            * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), 0, 0)
    end))
end

function DisableTPUI()
    if TP_Module.ExitMode then
        pcall(function()
            TP_Module.ExitMode()
        end)
    end

    if TP_Module.Gui then
        TP_Module.Gui.Enabled = false
    end
end
end)()
TabOther:Toggle({
    Title = "闪电尖兵大招",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)

        if v then
            EnableTPUI()
            AddFeature("瞬移")
        else
            DisableTPUI()
            RemoveFeature("瞬移")
        end

    end
})
-- ================= 安全加载 =================

local function SafeLoad(url, name)

    Notify("加载中", "正在加载 "..name, 2, "info")

    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        loadstring(game:HttpGet(url))()
    end)

    if ok then
        Notify("成功", name.." 已加载", 2, "success")
    else
        Notify("错误", name.." 加载失败\n"..tostring(err), 3, "error")
    end
end

TabOther:Button({
    Title = "一键清屏（有独立UI可关闭）",
    Callback = function()
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
-- ================== 全GUI隐藏（可拖拽版） ==================
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

local Hidden = false
local Stored = {}

-- 🖱 创建自己的UI
local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
ScreenGui.Name = "FullUIToggle"
ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
ScreenGui.Parent = PlayerGui

local Button = Instance.new("TextButton")
Button.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 30)
Button.Position = UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 10)
Button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20,20,20)
Button.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
Button.Text = "Hide ALL UI"
Button.Parent = ScreenGui

Instance.new("UICorner", Button)

-- ================== 拖拽逻辑 ==================

local dragging = false
local dragInput, startPos, startFramePos

Button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 
    or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = true
        startPos = input.Position
        startFramePos = Button.Position

        input.Changed:Connect(function()
            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                dragging = false
            end
        end)
    end
end)

Button.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement 
    or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragInput = input
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if input == dragInput and dragging then
        local delta = input.Position - startPos
        Button.Position = UDim2.new(
            startFramePos.X.Scale,
            startFramePos.X.Offset + delta.X,
            startFramePos.Y.Scale,
            startFramePos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
        )
    end
end)

-- ================== 原逻辑 ==================

local function IsUI(obj)
    return obj:IsA("GuiObject")
end

local function ShouldSkip(obj)
    return obj:IsDescendantOf(ScreenGui)
end

local function Process(container, hide)
    for _, obj in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do
        if IsUI(obj) and not ShouldSkip(obj) then
            if hide then
                if not Stored[obj] then
                    Stored[obj] = obj.Visible
                end
                obj.Visible = false
            else
                if Stored[obj] ~= nil then
                    obj.Visible = Stored[obj]
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

local function Toggle(state)
    if state then
        Stored = {}
        Process(PlayerGui, true)
        pcall(function()
            Process(CoreGui, true)
        end)
    else
        Process(PlayerGui, false)
        pcall(function()
            Process(CoreGui, false)
        end)
        Stored = {}
    end
end

Button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    Hidden = not Hidden
    Toggle(Hidden)
    Button.Text = Hidden and "Show ALL UI" or "Hide ALL UI"
end)

local function Hook(container)
    container.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if Hidden and obj:IsA("GuiObject") and not ShouldSkip(obj) then
            Stored[obj] = obj.Visible
            obj.Visible = false
        end
    end)
end

Hook(PlayerGui)
pcall(function()
    Hook(CoreGui)
end)
        end)

        if ok then
            Notify("成功", "已加载", 2, "success")
        else
            Notify("错误", tostring(err), 3, "error")
        end
    end
})

TabOther:Button({
    Title = "点击传送工具（夜脚本重制版）",
    Callback = function()
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
-- 服务
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

-- 玩家
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local Mouse = LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
local Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

-- 工具
local Tool = Instance.new("Tool")
Tool.Name = "点击传送道具"
Tool.RequiresHandle = false

Tool.Activated:Connect(function()
    local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not Character then return end

    local HRP = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not HRP then return end

    -- Raycast 参数（忽略自己）
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {Character}
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist

    -- 从相机朝鼠标方向发射射线
    local unitRay = Camera:ScreenPointToRay(Mouse.X, Mouse.Y)
    local rayOrigin = unitRay.Origin
    local rayDirection = unitRay.Direction * 100000 -- 距离可调

    local result = Workspace:Raycast(rayOrigin, rayDirection, params)

    -- ❌ 没打到任何东西 = 空气 → 禁止传送
    if not result then
        warn("点到空气，取消传送")
        return
    end

    -- ✅ 命中物体 → 直接传送到命中点上方一点
    local hitPos = result.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
    HRP.CFrame = CFrame.new(hitPos)
end)

Tool.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("Backpack")
end)
        if ok then
            Notify("成功", "已加载", 2, "success")
        else
            Notify("错误", tostring(err), 3, "error")
        end
    end
})
-- =================== 状态 ===================
local Enabled = false
local Connections = {}

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

-- =================== 清理 ===================
local function DisconnectAll()
    for _, c in pairs(Connections) do
        if c then c:Disconnect() end
    end
    table.clear(Connections)
end

-- =================== 判断是否异常 ===================
local function IsRagdollState(humanoid)
    local s = humanoid:GetState()
    return s == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
        or s == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
        or s == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
end

-- =================== 只在异常时修复 ===================
local function FixCharacter(character)
    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not humanoid or not root then return end

    -- 1️⃣ 状态恢复
    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)

    -- 2️⃣ 只在“飞走时”减速（不是锁死）
    if root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 80 then
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity *= 0.2
    end

    -- 3️⃣ 删除ragdoll约束
    for _, v in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or v:IsA("HingeConstraint") then
            v:Destroy()
        end
    end
end

-- =================== 应用 ===================
local function Apply(character)
    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then return end

    pcall(function()
        humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
    end)

    -- 状态触发修复
    table.insert(Connections,
        humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, new)
            if not Enabled then return end

            if new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then

                FixCharacter(character)
            end
        end)
    )

    -- 心跳检测（低频修复，不干扰操作）
    table.insert(Connections,
        RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not Enabled then return end

            if IsRagdollState(humanoid) then
                FixCharacter(character)
            end
        end)
    )
end

-- =================== 主函数 ===================
function ToggleAntiRagdollUltimate(state)
    Enabled = state

    DisconnectAll()

    if not state then return end

    if LocalPlayer.Character then
        Apply(LocalPlayer.Character)
    end

    table.insert(Connections,
        LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            if not Enabled then return end
            task.wait(0.3)
            Apply(char)
        end)
    )
end
TabOther:Toggle({
    Title = "防布娃娃",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)

        ToggleAntiRagdollUltimate(v)

        if v then
            AddFeature("防布娃娃")
        else
            RemoveFeature("防布娃娃")
        end

    end
})
-- ================= 强制显示聊天框 =================

local ForceChatEnabled = false
local SavedChatState = nil

local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local function GetCurrentChatState()
    local state = {
        CoreChatEnabled = nil,
        ChatWindowEnabled = nil,
        ChatInputBarEnabled = nil,
        PlayerGuiChatEnabled = nil,
    }

    -- 旧版 / CoreGui 聊天状态
    pcall(function()
        state.CoreChatEnabled = StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat)
    end)

    -- 新版聊天窗口状态
    pcall(function()
        if TextChatService.ChatWindowConfiguration then
            state.ChatWindowEnabled = TextChatService.ChatWindowConfiguration.Enabled
        end
    end)

    -- 新版聊天输入栏状态
    pcall(function()
        if TextChatService.ChatInputBarConfiguration then
            state.ChatInputBarEnabled = TextChatService.ChatInputBarConfiguration.Enabled
        end
    end)

    -- 有些游戏的旧聊天 GUI
    pcall(function()
        local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        if playerGui then
            local chatGui = playerGui:FindFirstChild("Chat")
            if chatGui and chatGui:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                state.PlayerGuiChatEnabled = chatGui.Enabled
            end
        end
    end)

    return state
end

local function ForceShowChat()
    -- 旧版 / 默认 CoreGui 聊天
    pcall(function()
        StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat, true)
    end)

    -- 激活聊天
    pcall(function()
        StarterGui:SetCore("ChatActive", true)
    end)

    -- 新版 TextChatService 聊天窗口
    pcall(function()
        if TextChatService.ChatWindowConfiguration then
            TextChatService.ChatWindowConfiguration.Enabled = true
        end
    end)

    -- 新版 TextChatService 输入栏
    pcall(function()
        if TextChatService.ChatInputBarConfiguration then
            TextChatService.ChatInputBarConfiguration.Enabled = true
        end
    end)

    -- PlayerGui 里的旧聊天 GUI
    pcall(function()
        local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        if playerGui then
            local chatGui = playerGui:FindFirstChild("Chat")
            if chatGui and chatGui:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                chatGui.Enabled = true
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function RestoreChatState()
    if not SavedChatState then
        return
    end

    -- 只有成功检测到原状态时，才恢复
    -- 检测不到就不乱动，免得一脚把聊天框踹没

    pcall(function()
        if SavedChatState.CoreChatEnabled ~= nil then
            StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled(
                Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat,
                SavedChatState.CoreChatEnabled
            )
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if TextChatService.ChatWindowConfiguration 
            and SavedChatState.ChatWindowEnabled ~= nil then

            TextChatService.ChatWindowConfiguration.Enabled =
                SavedChatState.ChatWindowEnabled
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if TextChatService.ChatInputBarConfiguration
            and SavedChatState.ChatInputBarEnabled ~= nil then

            TextChatService.ChatInputBarConfiguration.Enabled =
                SavedChatState.ChatInputBarEnabled
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        if playerGui then
            local chatGui = playerGui:FindFirstChild("Chat")
            if chatGui and chatGui:IsA("ScreenGui")
                and SavedChatState.PlayerGuiChatEnabled ~= nil then

                chatGui.Enabled = SavedChatState.PlayerGuiChatEnabled
            end
        end
    end)

    SavedChatState = nil
end

TabOther:Toggle({
    Title = "强制显示聊天框",
    Default = false,

    Callback = function(state)

        ForceChatEnabled = state

        if state then
            -- 打开前先记录游戏原本聊天框状态
            SavedChatState = GetCurrentChatState()

            AddFeature("强制显示聊天框")
            ForceShowChat()

        else
            RemoveFeature("强制显示聊天框")

            -- 关闭时恢复到打开前的状态
            RestoreChatState()
        end
    end
})

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.5) do
        if ForceChatEnabled then
            ForceShowChat()
        end
    end
end)
TabOther:Button({
    Title = "碰飞",
    Callback = function()
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ylt410/roblox-Script/refs/heads/main/碰飞"))()
        end)

        if ok then
            Notify("成功", "碰飞已加载", 2, "success")
        else
            Notify("错误", tostring(err), 3, "error")
        end
    end
})
end)()

    Locked = false,
