-- ================= NPC =================
-- ================= NPC 透视：低负载版 =================

local NPC_BLUE = Color3.fromRGB(0, 162, 255)
local NPC_BLUE_HEX = "#00a2ff"

local NPCESP = {
    Enabled = false,

    HighlightEnabled = false,
    BoxEnabled = false,
    ShowName = false,
    ShowHealth = false,

    Color = NPC_BLUE,

    Highlights = {},
    Boxes = {},
    Infos = {},

    Tracked = {},
    HealthConnections = {},
    ModelConnections = {},

    WorldConnection = nil,
    ScanRunning = false,
}

local function EscapeNPCRichText(str)
    str = tostring(str or "")
    str = str:gsub("&", "&amp;")
    str = str:gsub("<", "&lt;")
    str = str:gsub(">", "&gt;")
    str = str:gsub('"', "&quot;")
    str = str:gsub("'", "&#39;")
    return str
end

local function SafeDestroyNPCObject(obj)
    if obj then
        pcall(function()
            if obj.Parent then
                obj:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end
end

local function DisconnectNPCConnection(conn)
    if conn then
        pcall(function()
            conn:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
end

local function IsPlayerCharacter(model)
    if not model then
        return false
    end

    return game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model) ~= nil
end

local function GetNPCModelFromObject(obj)
    if not obj then
        return nil
    end

    if obj:IsA("Model") then
        if obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") then
            return obj
        end

        return nil
    end

    if obj:IsA("Humanoid") then
        local parent = obj.Parent
        if parent and parent:IsA("Model") then
            return parent
        end
    end

    return nil
end

local function IsValidNPCModel(model)
    if not model or not model.Parent then
        return false
    end

    if not model:IsA("Model") then
        return false
    end

    if IsPlayerCharacter(model) then
        return false
    end

    if not model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") then
        return false
    end

    return true
end

local function GetNPCPart(model)
    if not model then
        return nil
    end

    local root = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if root and root:IsA("BasePart") then
        return root
    end

    local torso = model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or model:FindFirstChild("Torso")
    if torso and torso:IsA("BasePart") then
        return torso
    end

    local head = model:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if head and head:IsA("BasePart") then
        return head
    end

    if model.PrimaryPart and model.PrimaryPart:IsA("BasePart") then
        return model.PrimaryPart
    end

    for _, obj in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            return obj
        end
    end

    return nil
end

local function GetNPCHeadPart(model)
    if not model then
        return nil
    end

    local head = model:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if head and head:IsA("BasePart") then
        return head
    end

    return GetNPCPart(model)
end

local function GetNPCDisplayName(model)
    if not model then
        return "NPC"
    end

    local hum = model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")

    if hum then
        local ok, displayName = pcall(function()
            return hum.DisplayName
        end)

        if ok and type(displayName) == "string" and displayName ~= "" then
            return displayName
        end
    end

    if type(model.Name) == "string" and model.Name ~= "" then
        return model.Name
    end

    return "NPC"
end

local function GetNPCHPText(hum)
    if not hum then
        return "HP://", "#ffffff"
    end

    local ok, health = pcall(function()
        return hum.Health
    end)

    if not ok or type(health) ~= "number" then
        return "HP://", "#ffffff"
    end

    local hp = math.floor(health)

    if hp < 0 then
        hp = 0
    end

    local hpColor = hp > 50 and "#55ff55" or "#ff5555"

    return "HP: " .. tostring(hp), hpColor
end

local function HasNPCInfoEnabled()
    return NPCESP.ShowName or NPCESP.ShowHealth
end

local function RemoveNPCESP(model)
    if not model then
        return
    end

    SafeDestroyNPCObject(NPCESP.Highlights[model])
    SafeDestroyNPCObject(NPCESP.Boxes[model])
    SafeDestroyNPCObject(NPCESP.Infos[model])

    DisconnectNPCConnection(NPCESP.HealthConnections[model])
    DisconnectNPCConnection(NPCESP.ModelConnections[model])

    NPCESP.Highlights[model] = nil
    NPCESP.Boxes[model] = nil
    NPCESP.Infos[model] = nil

    NPCESP.HealthConnections[model] = nil
    NPCESP.ModelConnections[model] = nil
    NPCESP.Tracked[model] = nil
end

local function UpdateNPCInfoText(model)
    local info = NPCESP.Infos[model]
    if not info or not info.Parent then
        return
    end

    local label = info:FindFirstChild("Label")
    if not label then
        return
    end

    local hum = model and model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
    local text = ""

    if NPCESP.ShowName then
        text = text .. "<font color='" .. NPC_BLUE_HEX .. "'><b>" .. EscapeNPCRichText(GetNPCDisplayName(model)) .. "</b></font>"
    end

    if NPCESP.ShowHealth then
        if text ~= "" then
            text = text .. "\n"
        end

        local hpText, hpColor = GetNPCHPText(hum)
        text = text .. "<font color='" .. hpColor .. "'>" .. EscapeNPCRichText(hpText) .. "</font> "
    end

    label.Text = text
end

local function CreateOrUpdateNPCHighlight(model)
    local high = NPCESP.Highlights[model]

    if not NPCESP.HighlightEnabled then
        if high then
            SafeDestroyNPCObject(high)
            NPCESP.Highlights[model] = nil
        end

        return
    end

    if not high or not high.Parent then
        high = model:FindFirstChild("NPCESP_Highlight")

        if not high then
            high = Instance.new("Highlight")
            high.Name = "NPCESP_Highlight"
            high.Parent = model
        end

        NPCESP.Highlights[model] = high
    end

    high.Enabled = true
    high.Adornee = model
    high.FillColor = NPCESP.Color
    high.OutlineColor = NPCESP.Color
    high.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop

    -- 只补绑一次，不再疯狂延迟轰炸手机
    task.defer(function()
        if NPCESP.Enabled and NPCESP.HighlightEnabled and model and model.Parent and high and high.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                high.Adornee = model
                high.Enabled = true
            end)
        end
    end)
end

local function CreateOrUpdateNPCBox(model)
    local root = GetNPCPart(model)
    local box = NPCESP.Boxes[model]

    if not NPCESP.BoxEnabled or not root then
        if box then
            SafeDestroyNPCObject(box)
            NPCESP.Boxes[model] = nil
        end

        return
    end

    if not box or not box.Parent then
        box = model:FindFirstChild("NPCESP_Box")

        if not box then
            box = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            box.Name = "NPCESP_Box"
            box.Size = UDim2.new(4.5, 0, 6, 0)
            box.AlwaysOnTop = true
            box.Adornee = root
            box.Parent = model

            local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
            frame.Name = "Frame"
            frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            frame.Parent = box

            local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            stroke.Name = "UIStroke"
            stroke.Thickness = 1.5
            stroke.Parent = frame
        end

        NPCESP.Boxes[model] = box
    end

    box.Adornee = root

    local frame = box:FindFirstChild("Frame")
    local stroke = frame and frame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")

    if stroke then
        stroke.Color = NPCESP.Color
        stroke.Thickness = 1.5
    end
end

local function CreateOrUpdateNPCInfo(model)
    local head = GetNPCHeadPart(model)
    local info = NPCESP.Infos[model]

    if not HasNPCInfoEnabled() or not head then
        if info then
            SafeDestroyNPCObject(info)
            NPCESP.Infos[model] = nil
        end

        return
    end

    if not info or not info.Parent then
        info = model:FindFirstChild("NPCESP_Info")

        if not info then
            info = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            info.Name = "NPCESP_Info"
            info.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 50)
            info.AlwaysOnTop = true
            info.Adornee = head
            info.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0)
            info.Parent = model

            local txt = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            txt.Name = "Label"
            txt.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            txt.RichText = true
            txt.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
            txt.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            txt.Parent = info
        end

        NPCESP.Infos[model] = info
    end

    info.Adornee = head
    info.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0)

    UpdateNPCInfoText(model)
end

local function ApplyNPCESP(model)
    if not NPCESP.Enabled then
        return
    end

    if not IsValidNPCModel(model) then
        return
    end

    local hum = model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
    if not hum then
        RemoveNPCESP(model)
        return
    end

    local root = GetNPCPart(model)
    if not root then
        return
    end

    NPCESP.Tracked[model] = true

    CreateOrUpdateNPCHighlight(model)
    CreateOrUpdateNPCBox(model)
    CreateOrUpdateNPCInfo(model)

    if not NPCESP.HealthConnections[model] then
        NPCESP.HealthConnections[model] = hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function()
            if NPCESP.Enabled and NPCESP.ShowHealth then
                UpdateNPCInfoText(model)
            end
        end)
    end

    if not NPCESP.ModelConnections[model] then
        NPCESP.ModelConnections[model] = model.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
            if not parent then
                RemoveNPCESP(model)
            end
        end)
    end
end

local function TryAddNPCFromObject(obj)
    if not NPCESP.Enabled then
        return
    end

    local model = GetNPCModelFromObject(obj)

    if model then
        ApplyNPCESP(model)
    end
end

local function ScanNPCOnce()
    if not NPCESP.Enabled then
        return
    end

    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("Model") or obj:IsA("Humanoid") then
            TryAddNPCFromObject(obj)
        end
    end
end

local function CleanInvalidNPCESP()
    for model, _ in pairs(NPCESP.Tracked) do
        if not model or not model.Parent or not IsValidNPCModel(model) then
            RemoveNPCESP(model)
        end
    end
end

local function StartLightNPCScanner()
    if NPCESP.ScanRunning then
        return
    end

    NPCESP.ScanRunning = true

    task.spawn(function()
        while NPCESP.Enabled do
            CleanInvalidNPCESP()

            -- 低频补扫，防止部分 NPC 静默生成导致漏掉
            ScanNPCOnce()

            -- 8 秒一次，不再把手机当服务器压榨
            task.wait(8)
        end

        NPCESP.ScanRunning = false
    end)
end

local function ForceRefreshNPCESP()
    NPCESP.Color = NPC_BLUE

    if not NPCESP.Enabled then
        return
    end

    for model, _ in pairs(NPCESP.Tracked) do
        if model and model.Parent then
            ApplyNPCESP(model)
        end
    end

    ScanNPCOnce()
end

local function UpdateNPCESPColor(color)
    -- NPC 除血量外统一蓝色，不接受外部乱改色
    NPCESP.Color = NPC_BLUE
    ForceRefreshNPCESP()
end

local function ToggleNPCESP(state)
    NPCESP.Enabled = state
    NPCESP.Color = NPC_BLUE

    if state then
        ScanNPCOnce()

        if not NPCESP.WorldConnection then
            NPCESP.WorldConnection = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if not NPCESP.Enabled then
                    return
                end

                -- 只处理 Model / Humanoid，不碰 BasePart，不再拿地图零件开刀
                if child:IsA("Model") or child:IsA("Humanoid") then
                    task.defer(function()
                        TryAddNPCFromObject(child)
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end

        StartLightNPCScanner()
    else
        DisconnectNPCConnection(NPCESP.WorldConnection)
        NPCESP.WorldConnection = nil

        local toRemove = {}

        for model, _ in pairs(NPCESP.Tracked) do
            table.insert(toRemove, model)
        end

        for model, _ in pairs(NPCESP.Highlights) do
            table.insert(toRemove, model)
        end

        for model, _ in pairs(NPCESP.Boxes) do
            table.insert(toRemove, model)
        end

        for model, _ in pairs(NPCESP.Infos) do
            table.insert(toRemove, model)
        end

        for _, model in ipairs(toRemove) do
            RemoveNPCESP(model)
        end
    end
end
-- ================= NPC =================
TabVisual:Toggle({
    Title = "NPC透视总开关",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        ToggleNPCESP(v)

        if v then
            AddFeature("NPC透视")
        else
            RemoveFeature("NPC透视")
        end
    end
})

TabVisual:Toggle({
    Title = "NPC高亮",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        NPCESP.HighlightEnabled = v
        ForceRefreshNPCESP()
    end
})

TabVisual:Toggle({
    Title = "NPC方框显示",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        NPCESP.BoxEnabled = v
        ForceRefreshNPCESP()
    end
})

TabVisual:Toggle({
    Title = "NPC名字显示",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        NPCESP.ShowName = v
        ForceRefreshNPCESP()
    end
})

TabVisual:Toggle({
    Title = "NPC血量显示",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        NPCESP.ShowHealth = v
        ForceRefreshNPCESP()
    end
});
(function()

-- ================= 玩家透视专用变量 =================
local PLAYER_ESP = {
    Enabled = false,
    HighlightEnabled = false,
    BoxEnabled = false,
    TeamCheck = false,
    ShowName = false,
    ShowHealth = false,
    ShowDist = false,
    TracerEnabled = false,
    TracerPosition = nil
}

-- ================= 服务 =================
local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

-- ================= 性能参数 =================
local PLAYER_ESP_UPDATE_RATE = 0.15
local PLAYER_ESP_MIN_HEALTH = -500

-- ================= Tracer 设置 =================
local TracerColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
local TracerThickness = 1.5
local TracerPositionFile = "NightPlayerESPTracerPosition.txt"

-- ================= 人数显示设置 =================
local CountDisplayWanted = false
local CountDisplayPosition = "顶部"
local CountDisplayFile = "NightCountDisplayPosition.txt"
local CountUpdateRate = 0.5

local CountGui = nil
local CountLabel = nil
local CountConnection = nil
local CountAcc = 0

-- ================= 骨骼绘制设置 =================
local SkeletonWanted = false
local SkeletonEnabled = false
local SkeletonThickness = 1.5
local SkeletonDefaultColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
local SkeletonCache = {}
local SkeletonRenderName = "__Night_SkeletonESP_Render"

-- ================= 防重复执行清理 =================
local RUNTIME_KEY = "__Night_PlayerESP_Runtime"

pcall(function()
    RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(SkeletonRenderName)
end)

pcall(function()
    if _G[RUNTIME_KEY] and type(_G[RUNTIME_KEY].Cleanup) == "function" then
        _G[RUNTIME_KEY].Cleanup()
    end
end)

local Runtime = {
    Connections = {}
}

_G[RUNTIME_KEY] = Runtime

local function TrackConnection(conn)
    if conn then
        table.insert(Runtime.Connections, conn)
    end

    return conn
end

-- ================= 运行变量 =================
local PlayerESPMainToggle = nil
local PlayerESPQuickGui = nil
local PlayerESPQuickHolder = nil
local PlayerESPQuickText = nil
local PlayerESPQuickDot = nil
local PlayerESPQuickGradient = nil

local PlayerESPUpdateConnection = nil
local PlayerESPTracerConnection = nil
local PlayerESPUpdateAcc = 0

local PLAYER_ESP_CACHE = {}

-- ================= 安全工具函数 =================
local function SafeDestroy(obj)
    if obj then
        pcall(function()
            obj:Destroy()
        end)
    end
end

local function SafeRemoveDrawing(obj)
    if obj then
        pcall(function()
            obj.Visible = false
        end)

        pcall(function()
            if obj.Remove then
                obj:Remove()
            end
        end)

        pcall(function()
            if obj.Destroy then
                obj:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end
end

local function EscapeRichText(str)
    str = tostring(str or "")
    str = str:gsub("&", "&amp;")
    str = str:gsub("<", "&lt;")
    str = str:gsub(">", "&gt;")
    str = str:gsub("\"", "&quot;")
    str = str:gsub("'", "&apos;")
    return str
end

local function GetCurrentCamera()
    return Workspace.CurrentCamera
end

local function IsAliveEnough(hum)
    return hum and hum.Health > PLAYER_ESP_MIN_HEALTH
end

local function HasInfoEnabled()
    return PLAYER_ESP.ShowName or PLAYER_ESP.ShowHealth or PLAYER_ESP.ShowDist
end

local function GetPlayerTeamColor(p)
    local color = TracerColor

    pcall(function()
        if p and p.TeamColor then
            color = p.TeamColor.Color
        end
    end)

    return color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
end

local function IsSameTeam(p)
    if not LocalPlayer or not p then
        return false
    end

    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        return p.Team == LocalPlayer.Team
    end)

    return ok and result or false
end

-- ================= 文件读取保存 =================
local function LoadTracerPosition()
    local saved = nil

    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(TracerPositionFile) then
            saved = readfile(TracerPositionFile)
        end
    end)

    if saved == "顶部" or saved == "底部" then
        return saved
    end

    return "底部"
end

local function SaveTracerPosition(pos)
    if pos ~= "顶部" and pos ~= "底部" then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        if writefile then
            writefile(TracerPositionFile, pos)
        end
    end)
end

PLAYER_ESP.TracerPosition = LoadTracerPosition()

local function LoadCountPosition()
    local saved = nil

    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(CountDisplayFile) then
            saved = readfile(CountDisplayFile)
        end
    end)

    if saved == "顶部" or saved == "底部" then
        return saved
    end

    return "顶部"
end

local function SaveCountPosition(pos)
    if pos ~= "顶部" and pos ~= "底部" then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        if writefile then
            writefile(CountDisplayFile, pos)
        end
    end)
end

CountDisplayPosition = LoadCountPosition()

-- ================= Drawing =================
local function CreateTracer()
    if not Drawing then
        return nil
    end

    local line = Drawing.new("Line")
    line.Visible = false
    line.Color = TracerColor
    line.Thickness = TracerThickness
    line.Transparency = 1
    return line
end

local function CreateSkeletonLine()
    if not Drawing then
        return nil
    end

    local line = Drawing.new("Line")
    line.Visible = false
    line.Color = SkeletonDefaultColor
    line.Thickness = SkeletonThickness
    line.Transparency = 1
    return line
end

-- ================= 玩家缓存 =================
local function DestroyPlayerCache(cache)
    if not cache then
        return
    end

    SafeDestroy(cache.Highlight)
    SafeDestroy(cache.Box)
    SafeDestroy(cache.Info)
    SafeRemoveDrawing(cache.Tracer)

    cache.Highlight = nil
    cache.Box = nil
    cache.Info = nil
    cache.Tracer = nil
    cache.LastText = nil
end

local function ClearPlayerESP()
    for _, cache in pairs(PLAYER_ESP_CACHE) do
        DestroyPlayerCache(cache)
    end

    PLAYER_ESP_CACHE = {}

    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local char = p.Character

        if char then
            SafeDestroy(char:FindFirstChild("PlayerESP_Highlight"))
            SafeDestroy(char:FindFirstChild("PlayerESP_Info"))
            SafeDestroy(char:FindFirstChild("PlayerESP_Box"))
        end
    end
end

local function GetCache(p, char)
    local cache = PLAYER_ESP_CACHE[p]

    if cache and cache.Character ~= char then
        DestroyPlayerCache(cache)
        PLAYER_ESP_CACHE[p] = nil
        cache = nil
    end

    if not cache then
        cache = {
            Character = char,
            Highlight = nil,
            Box = nil,
            Info = nil,
            Tracer = nil,
            LastText = nil
        }

        PLAYER_ESP_CACHE[p] = cache
    end

    return cache
end

-- ================= 射线 =================
local function HideAllPlayerTracers()
    for _, cache in pairs(PLAYER_ESP_CACHE) do
        if cache and cache.Tracer then
            cache.Tracer.Visible = false
        end
    end
end

local function UpdatePlayerTracers()
    if not PLAYER_ESP.Enabled or not PLAYER_ESP.TracerEnabled then
        HideAllPlayerTracers()
        return
    end

    if not Drawing then
        return
    end

    local cam = GetCurrentCamera()

    if not cam then
        HideAllPlayerTracers()
        return
    end

    local viewportSize = cam.ViewportSize
    local fromPos

    if PLAYER_ESP.TracerPosition == "顶部" then
        fromPos = Vector2.new(viewportSize.X / 2, 0)
    else
        fromPos = Vector2.new(viewportSize.X / 2, viewportSize.Y)
    end

    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer then
            local char = p.Character
            local cache = PLAYER_ESP_CACHE[p]

            if not char or not char.Parent then
                if cache and cache.Tracer then
                    cache.Tracer.Visible = false
                end
                continue
            end

            cache = GetCache(p, char)

            local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if not root or not IsAliveEnough(hum) then
                if cache.Tracer then
                    cache.Tracer.Visible = false
                end
                continue
            end

            if PLAYER_ESP.TeamCheck and IsSameTeam(p) then
                if cache.Tracer then
                    cache.Tracer.Visible = false
                end
                continue
            end

            if not cache.Tracer then
                local ok, line = pcall(CreateTracer)

                if ok and line then
                    cache.Tracer = line
                else
                    continue
                end
            end

            local pos, visible = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position)

            if visible and pos.Z > 0 then
                cache.Tracer.Visible = true
                cache.Tracer.Color = GetPlayerTeamColor(p)
                cache.Tracer.Thickness = TracerThickness
                cache.Tracer.From = fromPos
                cache.Tracer.To = Vector2.new(pos.X, pos.Y)
            else
                cache.Tracer.Visible = false
            end
        end
    end
end

-- ================= 玩家透视主体 =================
local function UpdatePlayerESP()
    if not PLAYER_ESP.Enabled then
        return
    end

    local cam = GetCurrentCamera()

    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then
            local char = p.Character
            local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local cache = GetCache(p, char)

            if hum and head and root and IsAliveEnough(hum) then
                local filtered = PLAYER_ESP.TeamCheck and IsSameTeam(p)
                local color = GetPlayerTeamColor(p)

                -- ===== 高亮：保留原逻辑，不受队伍检测影响 =====
                local high = cache.Highlight

                if not high or not high.Parent then
                    high = char:FindFirstChild("PlayerESP_Highlight")
                    cache.Highlight = high
                end

                if PLAYER_ESP.HighlightEnabled then
                    if not high then
                        high = Instance.new("Highlight")
                        high.Name = "PlayerESP_Highlight"
                        high.Parent = char
                        cache.Highlight = high
                    end

                    high.FillColor = color
                    high.OutlineColor = color
                    high.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
                elseif high then
                    SafeDestroy(high)
                    cache.Highlight = nil
                end

                -- ===== 方框 =====
                local box = cache.Box

                if not box or not box.Parent then
                    box = char:FindFirstChild("PlayerESP_Box")
                    cache.Box = box
                end

                if PLAYER_ESP.BoxEnabled and not filtered then
                    if not box then
                        box = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                        box.Name = "PlayerESP_Box"
                        box.Size = UDim2.new(4.5, 0, 6, 0)
                        box.AlwaysOnTop = true
                        box.Adornee = root
                        box.Parent = char

                        local f = Instance.new("Frame")
                        f.Name = "Frame"
                        f.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                        f.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        f.Parent = box

                        local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
                        s.Thickness = 1.5
                        s.Parent = f

                        cache.Box = box
                    else
                        box.Adornee = root
                    end

                    local frame = box:FindFirstChild("Frame")
                    local stroke = frame and frame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")

                    if stroke then
                        stroke.Color = color
                    end
                elseif box then
                    SafeDestroy(box)
                    cache.Box = nil
                end

                -- ===== 信息 =====
                local info = cache.Info

                if not info or not info.Parent then
                    info = char:FindFirstChild("PlayerESP_Info")
                    cache.Info = info
                end

                if not filtered and HasInfoEnabled() then
                    if not info then
                        info = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                        info.Name = "PlayerESP_Info"
                        info.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 50)
                        info.AlwaysOnTop = true
                        info.Adornee = head
                        info.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0)
                        info.Parent = char

                        local txt = Instance.new("TextLabel")
txt.Name = "Label"
txt.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
txt.RichText = true

-- 只补兜底颜色，不改文字大小
txt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
txt.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
txt.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5

txt.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium

txt.Parent = info
cache.Info = info
                    else
                        info.Adornee = head
                    end

                    local text = ""

                    if PLAYER_ESP.ShowName then
                        local safeName = EscapeRichText(p.DisplayName)
                        text = "<font color=\"#ffffff\"><b>" .. safeName .. "</b></font>\n"
                    end

                    if PLAYER_ESP.ShowHealth then
                        local hp = math.floor(hum.Health)
                        local hpColor = (hp > 50 and "#55ff55" or "#ff5555")
                        text = text .. "<font color=\"" .. hpColor .. "\">HP: " .. hp .. "</font> "
                    end

                    if PLAYER_ESP.ShowDist and cam then
                        local dist = math.floor((cam.CFrame.Position - root.Position).Magnitude)
                        text = text .. "<font color=\"#ffffff\">| " .. dist .. "m</font>"
                    end

                    local label = info and info:FindFirstChild("Label")

                    if label then
                        if cache.LastText ~= text then
                            cache.LastText = text
                            label.Text = text
                        end
                    end
                elseif info then
                    SafeDestroy(info)
                    cache.Info = nil
                    cache.LastText = nil
                end
            else
                DestroyPlayerCache(cache)
                PLAYER_ESP_CACHE[p] = nil
            end
        else
            local cache = PLAYER_ESP_CACHE[p]

            if cache then
                DestroyPlayerCache(cache)
                PLAYER_ESP_CACHE[p] = nil
            end
        end
    end
end

local function StartPlayerTracerUpdater()
    if PlayerESPTracerConnection then
        return
    end

    PlayerESPTracerConnection = TrackConnection(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        UpdatePlayerTracers()
    end))
end

local function StopPlayerTracerUpdater()
    if PlayerESPTracerConnection then
        pcall(function()
            PlayerESPTracerConnection:Disconnect()
        end)

        PlayerESPTracerConnection = nil
    end

    HideAllPlayerTracers()
end

local function StartPlayerESPUpdater()
    if PlayerESPUpdateConnection then
        return
    end

    PlayerESPUpdateAcc = PLAYER_ESP_UPDATE_RATE

    PlayerESPUpdateConnection = TrackConnection(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        if not PLAYER_ESP.Enabled then
            return
        end

        PlayerESPUpdateAcc = PlayerESPUpdateAcc + dt

        if PlayerESPUpdateAcc < PLAYER_ESP_UPDATE_RATE then
            return
        end

        PlayerESPUpdateAcc = 0
        UpdatePlayerESP()
    end))
end

local function StopPlayerESPUpdater()
    if PlayerESPUpdateConnection then
        pcall(function()
            PlayerESPUpdateConnection:Disconnect()
        end)

        PlayerESPUpdateConnection = nil
    end

    PlayerESPUpdateAcc = 0
end

local function ForceRefreshPlayerESP()
    if PLAYER_ESP.Enabled then
        UpdatePlayerESP()
    end
end

-- ================= 人数显示函数 =================
local function GetCountGuiParent()
    local parent = nil

    pcall(function()
        if gethui then
            parent = gethui()
        end
    end)

    if parent then
        return parent
    end

    pcall(function()
        parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    end)

    return parent or game.CoreGui
end

local function ApplyCountPosition()
    if not CountLabel then
        return
    end

    if CountDisplayPosition == "底部" then
        CountLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
        CountLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -12)
    else
        CountLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
        CountLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 12)
    end
end

local function CreateCountDisplay()
    if CountGui and CountGui.Parent then
        return
    end

    local GuiParent = GetCountGuiParent()

    pcall(function()
        local old = GuiParent:FindFirstChild("NightCountDisplayGui")

        if old then
            old:Destroy()
        end
    end)

    CountGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    CountGui.Name = "NightCountDisplayGui"
    CountGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    CountGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    CountGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    CountGui.Parent = GuiParent

    CountLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    CountLabel.Name = "CountLabel"
    CountLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(420, 28)
    CountLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    CountLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
    CountLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    CountLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    CountLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.45
    CountLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    CountLabel.TextSize = 16
    CountLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    CountLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    CountLabel.Text = "局内人数0     存活人数0"
    CountLabel.Visible = false
    CountLabel.ZIndex = 999
    CountLabel.Parent = CountGui

    ApplyCountPosition()
end

local function GetCountInfo()
    local total = 0
    local alive = 0

    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        total = total + 1

        local char = p.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

        if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
            alive = alive + 1
        end
    end

    return total, alive
end

local function UpdateCountDisplay()
    if not CountDisplayWanted or not PLAYER_ESP.Enabled then
        if CountLabel then
            CountLabel.Visible = false
        end

        return
    end

    CreateCountDisplay()

    if not CountLabel then
        return
    end

    local total, alive = GetCountInfo()
    local text = "局内人数" .. tostring(total) .. "     存活人数" .. tostring(alive)

    if CountLabel.Text ~= text then
        CountLabel.Text = text
    end

    CountLabel.Visible = true
    ApplyCountPosition()
end

local function StartCountDisplay()
    if CountConnection then
        return
    end

    CountAcc = CountUpdateRate

    CountConnection = TrackConnection(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        if not CountDisplayWanted or not PLAYER_ESP.Enabled then
            return
        end

        CountAcc = CountAcc + dt

        if CountAcc < CountUpdateRate then
            return
        end

        CountAcc = 0
        UpdateCountDisplay()
    end))

    UpdateCountDisplay()
end

local function StopCountDisplay()
    if CountConnection then
        pcall(function()
            CountConnection:Disconnect()
        end)

        CountConnection = nil
    end

    CountAcc = 0

    if CountLabel then
        CountLabel.Visible = false
    end
end

-- ================= 骨骼绘制函数 =================
local function GetPlayerSkeletonColor(player)
    local color = SkeletonDefaultColor

    pcall(function()
        if player and player.Team ~= nil then
            color = player.TeamColor.Color
        else
            color = SkeletonDefaultColor
        end
    end)

    return color or SkeletonDefaultColor
end

local R15Bones = {
    { "Head", "UpperTorso" },
    { "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso" },

    { "UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm" },
    { "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm" },
    { "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand" },

    { "UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm" },
    { "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm" },
    { "RightLowerArm", "RightHand" },

    { "LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg" },
    { "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg" },
    { "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot" },

    { "LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg" },
    { "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg" },
    { "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot" },
}

local R6Bones = {
    { "Head", "Torso" },
    { "Torso", "Left Arm" },
    { "Torso", "Right Arm" },
    { "Torso", "Left Leg" },
    { "Torso", "Right Leg" },
}

local function GetBoneList(character)
    if character and character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") then
        return R15Bones
    end

    return R6Bones
end

local function HideSkeletonLines(cache)
    if not cache or not cache.Lines then
        return
    end

    for _, line in ipairs(cache.Lines) do
        if line then
            line.Visible = false
        end
    end
end

local function ClearSkeletonForPlayer(player)
    local cache = SkeletonCache[player]

    if cache then
        if cache.Lines then
            for _, line in ipairs(cache.Lines) do
                SafeRemoveDrawing(line)
            end
        end

        SkeletonCache[player] = nil
    end
end

local function ClearAllSkeletons()
    for player, _ in pairs(SkeletonCache) do
        ClearSkeletonForPlayer(player)
    end

    SkeletonCache = {}
end

local function GetSkeletonCache(player)
    local cache = SkeletonCache[player]

    if not cache then
        cache = {
            Lines = {}
        }

        SkeletonCache[player] = cache
    end

    return cache
end

local function EnsureLineCount(cache, count)
    for i = 1, count do
        if not cache.Lines[i] then
            local ok, line = pcall(CreateSkeletonLine)

            if ok and line then
                cache.Lines[i] = line
            end
        end
    end

    for i = count + 1, #cache.Lines do
        if cache.Lines[i] then
            SafeRemoveDrawing(cache.Lines[i])
            cache.Lines[i] = nil
        end
    end
end

local function GetPartScreenPosition(camera, part)
    if not camera or not part then
        return nil, false
    end

    local pos, visible = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)

    if not visible or pos.Z <= 0 then
        return nil, false
    end

    return Vector2.new(pos.X, pos.Y), true
end

local function UpdateSkeletonForPlayer(player, camera)
    if player == LocalPlayer then
        return
    end

    local cache = GetSkeletonCache(player)
    local character = player.Character

    if not character or not character.Parent then
        HideSkeletonLines(cache)
        return
    end

    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

    if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
        HideSkeletonLines(cache)
        return
    end

    local bones = GetBoneList(character)
    EnsureLineCount(cache, #bones)

    local color = GetPlayerSkeletonColor(player)

    for i, pair in ipairs(bones) do
        local partA = character:FindFirstChild(pair[1])
        local partB = character:FindFirstChild(pair[2])
        local line = cache.Lines[i]

        if line and partA and partB then
            local screenA, visibleA = GetPartScreenPosition(camera, partA)
            local screenB, visibleB = GetPartScreenPosition(camera, partB)

            if visibleA and visibleB then
                line.Visible = true
                line.Color = color
                line.Thickness = SkeletonThickness
                line.From = screenA
                line.To = screenB
            else
                line.Visible = false
            end
        elseif line then
            line.Visible = false
        end
    end
end

local function UpdateAllSkeletons()
    if not SkeletonEnabled or not SkeletonWanted or not PLAYER_ESP.Enabled then
        for _, cache in pairs(SkeletonCache) do
            HideSkeletonLines(cache)
        end

        return
    end

    if not Drawing then
        return
    end

    local camera = GetCurrentCamera()

    if not camera then
        return
    end

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        UpdateSkeletonForPlayer(player, camera)
    end
end

local function StartSkeletonESP()
    if not SkeletonWanted or not PLAYER_ESP.Enabled then
        return
    end

    SkeletonEnabled = true

    pcall(function()
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(SkeletonRenderName)
    end)

    RunService:BindToRenderStep(
        SkeletonRenderName,
        Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value,
        function()
            UpdateAllSkeletons()
        end
    )
end

local function StopSkeletonESP()
    SkeletonEnabled = false

    pcall(function()
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(SkeletonRenderName)
    end)

    for _, cache in pairs(SkeletonCache) do
        HideSkeletonLines(cache)
    end
end

TrackConnection(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
    local cache = PLAYER_ESP_CACHE[p]

    if cache then
        DestroyPlayerCache(cache)
        PLAYER_ESP_CACHE[p] = nil
    end

    ClearSkeletonForPlayer(p)
end))

-- ================= 玩家透视状态统一函数 =================
local function SetPlayerESPState(v)
    v = not not v

    PLAYER_ESP.Enabled = v

    if v then
        StartPlayerESPUpdater()

        if PLAYER_ESP.TracerEnabled then
            StartPlayerTracerUpdater()
        end

        if CountDisplayWanted then
            StartCountDisplay()
            UpdateCountDisplay()
        end

        if SkeletonWanted then
            StartSkeletonESP()
        end

        ForceRefreshPlayerESP()
    else
        StopPlayerESPUpdater()
        StopPlayerTracerUpdater()
        StopCountDisplay()
        StopSkeletonESP()
        ClearPlayerESP()
    end

    if v then
        AddFeature("玩家透视")
    else
        RemoveFeature("玩家透视")
    end

    if PlayerESPQuickText then
        PlayerESPQuickText.Text = v and "透视开" or "透视关"
    end

    if PlayerESPQuickDot then
        PlayerESPQuickDot.BackgroundColor3 = v
            and Color3.fromRGB(80, 190, 255)
            or Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 140)
    end
end

-- ================= 创建玩家透视快捷开关 =================
local function CreatePlayerESPQuickSwitch()
    if PlayerESPQuickGui then
        return
    end

    local PosFile = "NightPlayerESPQuickPos.txt"

    local function GetGuiParent()
        local parent = nil

        pcall(function()
            if gethui then
                parent = gethui()
            end
        end)

        if parent then
            return parent
        end

        pcall(function()
            parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        end)

        return parent or game.CoreGui
    end

    local GuiParent = GetGuiParent()

    pcall(function()
        local old = GuiParent:FindFirstChild("PlayerESPQuickSwitchGui")
        if old then
            old:Destroy()
        end
    end)

    local function GetViewportSize()
        local camera = GetCurrentCamera()

        if camera then
            return camera.ViewportSize
        end

        return Vector2.new(1920, 1080)
    end

    local function ClampToScreen(pos)
        local viewport = GetViewportSize()
        local size = PlayerESPQuickHolder and PlayerESPQuickHolder.AbsoluteSize or Vector2.new(94, 38)
        local padding = 8

        local x = math.clamp(
            pos.X,
            padding,
            math.max(padding, viewport.X - size.X - padding)
        )

        local y = math.clamp(
            pos.Y,
            padding,
            math.max(padding, viewport.Y - size.Y - padding)
        )

        return Vector2.new(x, y)
    end

    PlayerESPQuickGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    PlayerESPQuickGui.Name = "PlayerESPQuickSwitchGui"
    PlayerESPQuickGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    PlayerESPQuickGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    PlayerESPQuickGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    PlayerESPQuickGui.Parent = GuiParent

    PlayerESPQuickHolder = Instance.new("Frame")
    PlayerESPQuickHolder.Name = "PlayerESPQuickHolder"
    PlayerESPQuickHolder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(94, 38)
    PlayerESPQuickHolder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -118, 0.52, 0)
    PlayerESPQuickHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    PlayerESPQuickHolder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    PlayerESPQuickHolder.Active = true
    PlayerESPQuickHolder.Visible = false
    PlayerESPQuickHolder.ZIndex = 999
    PlayerESPQuickHolder.Parent = PlayerESPQuickGui

    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(PosFile) then
            local data = readfile(PosFile)
            local x, y = string.match(data, "([^,]+),([^,]+)")

            x = tonumber(x)
            y = tonumber(y)

            if x and y then
                local savedPos = Vector2.new(x, y)
                savedPos = ClampToScreen(savedPos)

                PlayerESPQuickHolder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(savedPos.X, savedPos.Y)
            end
        end
    end)

    local Button = Instance.new("TextButton")
    Button.Name = "Button"
    Button.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    Button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    Button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 24)
    Button.BackgroundTransparency = 0.06
    Button.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Button.AutoButtonColor = false
    Button.Text = ""
    Button.Active = true
    Button.ZIndex = 1000
    Button.Parent = PlayerESPQuickHolder

    local ButtonCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    ButtonCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    ButtonCorner.Parent = Button

    local RainbowStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    RainbowStroke.Name = "PlayerESPQuickRainbowStroke"
    RainbowStroke.Thickness = 2.4
    RainbowStroke.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    RainbowStroke.Transparency = 0
    RainbowStroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Round
    RainbowStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    RainbowStroke.Parent = Button

    PlayerESPQuickGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    PlayerESPQuickGradient.Name = "PlayerESPQuickRainbowGradient"
    PlayerESPQuickGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.16, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.33, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.50, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.66, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.83, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
    })
    PlayerESPQuickGradient.Rotation = 0
    PlayerESPQuickGradient.Parent = RainbowStroke

    local InnerStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    InnerStroke.Name = "InnerSoftStroke"
    InnerStroke.Thickness = 1
    InnerStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    InnerStroke.Transparency = 0.86
    InnerStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    InnerStroke.Parent = Button

    PlayerESPQuickDot = Instance.new("Frame")
    PlayerESPQuickDot.Name = "Dot"
    PlayerESPQuickDot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
    PlayerESPQuickDot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 9, 0.5, 0)
    PlayerESPQuickDot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16)
    PlayerESPQuickDot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 140)
    PlayerESPQuickDot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    PlayerESPQuickDot.ZIndex = 1001
    PlayerESPQuickDot.Parent = Button

    local DotCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    DotCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    DotCorner.Parent = PlayerESPQuickDot

    local DotStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    DotStroke.Thickness = 1
    DotStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    DotStroke.Transparency = 0.6
    DotStroke.Parent = PlayerESPQuickDot

    PlayerESPQuickText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    PlayerESPQuickText.Name = "Text"
    PlayerESPQuickText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    PlayerESPQuickText.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(31, 0)
    PlayerESPQuickText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 1, 0)
    PlayerESPQuickText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    PlayerESPQuickText.TextSize = 12
    PlayerESPQuickText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    PlayerESPQuickText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 245)
    PlayerESPQuickText.Text = "透视关"
    PlayerESPQuickText.ZIndex = 1001
    PlayerESPQuickText.Parent = Button

    local RainbowConnection = nil

    RainbowConnection = TrackConnection(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        if not PlayerESPQuickGradient or not PlayerESPQuickGradient.Parent then
            if RainbowConnection then
                RainbowConnection:Disconnect()
                RainbowConnection = nil
            end

            return
        end

        if not PlayerESPQuickHolder or not PlayerESPQuickHolder.Visible then
            return
        end

        PlayerESPQuickGradient.Rotation = (PlayerESPQuickGradient.Rotation + dt * 160) % 360
    end))

    -- ================= 玩家透视快捷开关拖拽逻辑 =================
    local isDragging = false
    local dragStart = nil
    local startPos = nil
    local activeTouch = nil
    local hasMoved = false

    local DRAG_DISTANCE = 6

    local function SaveCurrentPosition()
        if not PlayerESPQuickHolder then
            return
        end

        pcall(function()
            if writefile then
                writefile(
                    PosFile,
                    tostring(math.floor(PlayerESPQuickHolder.AbsolutePosition.X))
                        .. ","
                        .. tostring(math.floor(PlayerESPQuickHolder.AbsolutePosition.Y))
                )
            end
        end)
    end

    local function ApplyDragPosition(input)
        if not isDragging then
            return
        end

        if not dragStart or not startPos then
            return
        end

        if not PlayerESPQuickHolder then
            return
        end

        local delta = input.Position - dragStart

        if math.abs(delta.X) > DRAG_DISTANCE
            or math.abs(delta.Y) > DRAG_DISTANCE then
            hasMoved = true
        end

        local rawPos = Vector2.new(
            startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
            startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
        )

        rawPos = ClampToScreen(rawPos)

        PlayerESPQuickHolder.Position = UDim2.new(
            0,
            rawPos.X,
            0,
            rawPos.Y
        )
    end

    local function PlayPressAnim()
        pcall(function()
            TweenService:Create(
                PlayerESPQuickHolder,
                TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                {
                    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(100, 42)
                }
            ):Play()
        end)
    end

    local function PlayReleaseAnim()
        pcall(function()
            TweenService:Create(
                PlayerESPQuickHolder,
                TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                {
                    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(94, 38)
                }
            ):Play()
        end)
    end

    TrackConnection(Button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and not activeTouch) then

            isDragging = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = PlayerESPQuickHolder.Position
            hasMoved = false

            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                activeTouch = input
            end

            PlayPressAnim()

            local changedConn
            changedConn = input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    if changedConn then
                        changedConn:Disconnect()
                        changedConn = nil
                    end

                    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                        if input == activeTouch then
                            activeTouch = nil
                            isDragging = false
                            dragStart = nil
                            startPos = nil

                            if hasMoved then
                                SaveCurrentPosition()
                            end

                            PlayReleaseAnim()
                        end
                    else
                        isDragging = false
                        dragStart = nil
                        startPos = nil

                        if hasMoved then
                            SaveCurrentPosition()
                        end

                        PlayReleaseAnim()
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end))

    TrackConnection(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not isDragging then
            return
        end

        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
            ApplyDragPosition(input)
        elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input == activeTouch then
            ApplyDragPosition(input)
        end
    end))

    TrackConnection(Button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if hasMoved then
            hasMoved = false
            return
        end

        local newState = not PLAYER_ESP.Enabled

        if PlayerESPMainToggle then
            pcall(function()
                PlayerESPMainToggle:Set(newState)
            end)
        end

        SetPlayerESPState(newState)
    end))

    SetPlayerESPState(PLAYER_ESP.Enabled)
end

-- ================= 运行清理 =================
Runtime.Cleanup = function()
    StopPlayerESPUpdater()
    StopPlayerTracerUpdater()
    StopCountDisplay()
    StopSkeletonESP()
    ClearPlayerESP()
    ClearAllSkeletons()

    if PlayerESPQuickGui then
        SafeDestroy(PlayerESPQuickGui)
        PlayerESPQuickGui = nil
    end

    if CountGui then
        SafeDestroy(CountGui)
        CountGui = nil
        CountLabel = nil
    end

    for _, conn in ipairs(Runtime.Connections) do
        pcall(function()
            conn:Disconnect()
        end)
    end

    Runtime.Connections = {}
end

;(function()
-- =================== 自身血量 / FPS / Ping 显示（统一叠放修复版） ===================

local HealthPosFile = "NightHealthPos.txt"
local HealthEnabledFile = "NightHealthDisplayEnabled.txt"
local FPSDisplayFile = "NightFPSDisplayEnabled.txt"
local PingDisplayFile = "NightPingDisplayEnabled.txt"

local function HealthLoadBool(fileName, defaultValue)
    local value = defaultValue

    pcall(function()
        if isfile and readfile and isfile(fileName) then
            local saved = tostring(readfile(fileName))

            if saved == "true" then
                value = true
            elseif saved == "false" then
                value = false
            end
        end
    end)

    return value
end

local function HealthSaveBool(fileName, value)
    pcall(function()
        if writefile then
            writefile(fileName, value and "true" or "false")
        end
    end)
end

local HealthDisplay = {
    Enabled = HealthLoadBool(HealthEnabledFile, true),
    Position = "LeftTop",

    Frame = nil,
    Layout = nil,
    Label = nil,

    Humanoid = nil,
    HealthConn = nil,
    MaxHealthConn = nil,
    CharacterConn = nil,

    HealthVisible = false,
    LastText = nil,
    LastColor = nil
}

local PerformanceDisplay = {
    FPS = HealthLoadBool(FPSDisplayFile, false),
    Ping = HealthLoadBool(PingDisplayFile, false),

    FPSLabel = nil,
    PingLabel = nil,

    Connection = nil,
    Frames = 0,
    Acc = 0
}

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local HealthColorHigh = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
local HealthColorMid = Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 0)
local HealthColorLow = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)

-- ================= 读取保存位置 =================
pcall(function()
    if isfile and readfile and isfile(HealthPosFile) then
        local saved = tostring(readfile(HealthPosFile))
        if saved == "LeftTop"
            or saved == "RightTop"
            or saved == "LeftBottom"
            or saved == "RightBottom" then

            HealthDisplay.Position = saved
        end
    end
end)

-- ================= UI =================
local HealthGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
HealthGui.Name = "SelfHealthDisplay"
HealthGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
HealthGui.IgnoreGuiInset = false
HealthGui.DisplayOrder = 999999

local parentedHealthGui = pcall(function()
    HealthGui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
end)

if not parentedHealthGui then
    HealthGui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
end

local function CreateDisplayLabel(text, color)
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.35
    label.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    label.TextSize = 14
    label.TextScaled = false
    label.TextWrapped = false
    label.RichText = false
    label.TextColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    label.Text = text
    label.Visible = false
    label.ZIndex = 5
    return label
end

local function CreateHealthUI()
    HealthGui:ClearAllChildren()

    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Name = "HealthStatsStack"
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 160, 0, 18)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.ClipsDescendants = false
    frame.ZIndex = 5
    frame.Parent = HealthGui

    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
    layout.Parent = frame

    local hpLabel = CreateDisplayLabel("HP: -- / --", HealthColorHigh)
    hpLabel.Name = "HPLabel"
    hpLabel.LayoutOrder = 1
    hpLabel.Parent = frame

    local fpsLabel = CreateDisplayLabel("FPS: --", Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
    fpsLabel.Name = "FPSLabel"
    fpsLabel.LayoutOrder = 2
    fpsLabel.Parent = frame

    local pingLabel = CreateDisplayLabel("Ping: --", Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
    pingLabel.Name = "PingLabel"
    pingLabel.LayoutOrder = 3
    pingLabel.Parent = frame

    HealthDisplay.Frame = frame
    HealthDisplay.Layout = layout
    HealthDisplay.Label = hpLabel

    PerformanceDisplay.FPSLabel = fpsLabel
    PerformanceDisplay.PingLabel = pingLabel
end

local function RefreshDisplayStack()
    local frame = HealthDisplay.Frame
    if not frame then return end

    local count = 0

    if HealthDisplay.Label then
        HealthDisplay.Label.Visible = HealthDisplay.HealthVisible == true
        if HealthDisplay.Label.Visible then
            count += 1
        end
    end

    if PerformanceDisplay.FPSLabel then
        PerformanceDisplay.FPSLabel.Visible = PerformanceDisplay.FPS == true
        if PerformanceDisplay.FPSLabel.Visible then
            count += 1
        end
    end

    if PerformanceDisplay.PingLabel then
        PerformanceDisplay.PingLabel.Visible = PerformanceDisplay.Ping == true
        if PerformanceDisplay.PingLabel.Visible then
            count += 1
        end
    end

    if count <= 0 then
        frame.Visible = false
        frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 160, 0, 18)
        return
    end

    frame.Visible = true
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 160, 0, count * 18 + math.max(0, count - 1) * 2)
end

-- ================= 位置 =================
local function UpdatePosition()
    local frame = HealthDisplay.Frame
    if not frame then return end

    RefreshDisplayStack()

    local align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    if HealthDisplay.Position == "LeftTop" then
        frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
        align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    elseif HealthDisplay.Position == "RightTop" then
        frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
        frame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 10)
        align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right

    elseif HealthDisplay.Position == "LeftBottom" then
        frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
        frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -10)
        align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    elseif HealthDisplay.Position == "RightBottom" then
        frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
        frame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -10)
        align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    end

    if HealthDisplay.Label then
        HealthDisplay.Label.TextXAlignment = align
    end

    if PerformanceDisplay.FPSLabel then
        PerformanceDisplay.FPSLabel.TextXAlignment = align
    end

    if PerformanceDisplay.PingLabel then
        PerformanceDisplay.PingLabel.TextXAlignment = align
    end
end

-- ================= Ping 获取 =================
local function GetPingText()
    local result = "--"

    pcall(function()
        local stats = game:GetService("Stats")
        local network = stats:FindFirstChild("Network")
        local serverStats = network and network:FindFirstChild("ServerStatsItem")
        local dataPing = serverStats and serverStats:FindFirstChild("Data Ping")

        if not dataPing then
            return
        end

        local okString, valueString = pcall(function()
            return dataPing:GetValueString()
        end)

        if okString and type(valueString) == "string" and valueString ~= "" then
            local num = tonumber(valueString:match("([%d%.]+)"))

            if num then
                result = tostring(math.floor(num + 0.5)) .. "ms"
                return
            end
        end

        local okValue, value = pcall(function()
            return dataPing:GetValue()
        end)

        if okValue and type(value) == "number" then
            -- 有些环境会把 44.93 返回成 4493，顺手修掉，免得 UI 像发烧了一样
            if value > 1000 and value < 10000 then
                value = value / 100
            end

            result = tostring(math.floor(value + 0.5)) .. "ms"
        end
    end)

    return result
end

-- ================= FPS / Ping 更新 =================
local function StopPerformanceUpdater()
    if PerformanceDisplay.Connection then
        PerformanceDisplay.Connection:Disconnect()
        PerformanceDisplay.Connection = nil
    end

    PerformanceDisplay.Frames = 0
    PerformanceDisplay.Acc = 0
end

local function StartPerformanceUpdater()
    if PerformanceDisplay.Connection then return end

    local RunServiceForStats = game:GetService("RunService")

    PerformanceDisplay.Connection = RunServiceForStats.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        if not PerformanceDisplay.FPS and not PerformanceDisplay.Ping then
            StopPerformanceUpdater()
            return
        end

        PerformanceDisplay.Frames += 1
        PerformanceDisplay.Acc += dt

        if PerformanceDisplay.Acc < 0.5 then
            return
        end

        local fps = math.floor((PerformanceDisplay.Frames / PerformanceDisplay.Acc) + 0.5)

        if PerformanceDisplay.FPSLabel and PerformanceDisplay.FPS then
            PerformanceDisplay.FPSLabel.Text = "FPS: " .. tostring(fps)
        end

        if PerformanceDisplay.PingLabel and PerformanceDisplay.Ping then
            PerformanceDisplay.PingLabel.Text = "Ping: " .. GetPingText()
        end

        PerformanceDisplay.Frames = 0
        PerformanceDisplay.Acc = 0

        RefreshDisplayStack()
        UpdatePosition()
    end)
end

local function ApplyPerformanceDisplay()
    if PerformanceDisplay.FPS or PerformanceDisplay.Ping then
        StartPerformanceUpdater()
    else
        StopPerformanceUpdater()
    end

    RefreshDisplayStack()
    UpdatePosition()
end

-- ================= 断开旧连接 =================
local function DisconnectHealthConnections()
    if HealthDisplay.HealthConn then
        HealthDisplay.HealthConn:Disconnect()
        HealthDisplay.HealthConn = nil
    end

    if HealthDisplay.MaxHealthConn then
        HealthDisplay.MaxHealthConn:Disconnect()
        HealthDisplay.MaxHealthConn = nil
    end

    HealthDisplay.Humanoid = nil
    HealthDisplay.HealthVisible = false
    HealthDisplay.LastText = nil
    HealthDisplay.LastColor = nil
end

-- ================= 血量更新：事件触发 =================
local function UpdateHealthDisplay()
    local label = HealthDisplay.Label
    if not label then return end

    if not HealthDisplay.Enabled then
        HealthDisplay.HealthVisible = false
        RefreshDisplayStack()
        UpdatePosition()
        return
    end

    local hum = HealthDisplay.Humanoid

    if not hum or not hum.Parent then
        HealthDisplay.HealthVisible = false
        RefreshDisplayStack()
        UpdatePosition()
        return
    end

    local current = math.floor(hum.Health)
    local max = math.floor(hum.MaxHealth)

    if max <= 0 then
        max = 1
    end

    if current < 0 then
        current = 0
    end

    local percent = current / max

    local color
    if percent > 0.6 then
        color = HealthColorHigh
    elseif percent > 0.3 then
        color = HealthColorMid
    else
        color = HealthColorLow
    end

    local text = "HP: " .. current .. " / " .. max

    if HealthDisplay.LastText ~= text then
        label.Text = text
        HealthDisplay.LastText = text
    end

    if HealthDisplay.LastColor ~= color then
        label.TextColor3 = color
        HealthDisplay.LastColor = color
    end

    HealthDisplay.HealthVisible = true

    RefreshDisplayStack()
    UpdatePosition()
end

-- ================= 绑定角色 =================
local function BindCharacter(char)
    DisconnectHealthConnections()

    if not HealthDisplay.Frame or not HealthDisplay.Label then
        CreateHealthUI()
    end

    if not char then
        UpdateHealthDisplay()
        return
    end

    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if not hum then
        hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 3)
    end

    if not hum then
        UpdateHealthDisplay()
        return
    end

    HealthDisplay.Humanoid = hum
    HealthDisplay.LastText = nil
    HealthDisplay.LastColor = nil

    HealthDisplay.HealthConn = hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function()
        UpdateHealthDisplay()
    end)

    HealthDisplay.MaxHealthConn = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("MaxHealth"):Connect(function()
        UpdateHealthDisplay()
    end)

    UpdateHealthDisplay()
end

-- ================= 初始化 =================
CreateHealthUI()
BindCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
ApplyPerformanceDisplay()
UpdatePosition()

-- ================= 重生 =================
HealthDisplay.CharacterConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.5)

    CreateHealthUI()
    BindCharacter(char)
    ApplyPerformanceDisplay()
    UpdatePosition()
end)
