-- ================= 秒互动 / 互动范围 =================

local InstantInteractEnabled = false
local InteractRangeEnabled = false
local InteractDistance = 20

-- 不用弱表，避免关闭时原始数据被 GC 或弱引用机制吞掉
local OriginalPromptDistances = {}
local OriginalPromptHoldDurations = {}

-- 缓存已经处理过的 Prompt，避免反复全图扫描
local PromptCache = {}

local PromptAddedConnection = nil
local PromptRemovingConnection = nil

-- ================= 工具函数 =================

local function GetPromptPart(prompt)
    local parent = prompt.Parent

    if not parent then
        return nil
    end

    if parent:IsA("BasePart") then
        return parent
    end

    local model = parent:IsA("Model") and parent or parent:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")

    if model then
        return model.PrimaryPart or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
    end

    return parent:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BasePart")
end

local function SavePromptDistance(prompt)
    if not prompt then
        return
    end

    if OriginalPromptDistances[prompt] == nil then
        OriginalPromptDistances[prompt] = prompt.MaxActivationDistance
    end
end

local function SavePromptHoldDuration(prompt)
    if not prompt then
        return
    end

    if OriginalPromptHoldDurations[prompt] == nil then
        OriginalPromptHoldDurations[prompt] = prompt.HoldDuration
    end
end

local function ApplyPrompt(prompt)
    if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then
        return
    end

    if not prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
        return
    end

    PromptCache[prompt] = true

    if InstantInteractEnabled then
        SavePromptHoldDuration(prompt)

        pcall(function()
            prompt.HoldDuration = 0
        end)
    end

    if InteractRangeEnabled then
        SavePromptDistance(prompt)

        pcall(function()
            prompt.MaxActivationDistance = InteractDistance
        end)
    end
end

local function RemovePromptFromCache(prompt)
    if not prompt then
        return
    end

    PromptCache[prompt] = nil
    OriginalPromptDistances[prompt] = nil
    OriginalPromptHoldDurations[prompt] = nil
end

local function RestorePromptDistances()
    local restoreList = {}

    for prompt, oldDistance in pairs(OriginalPromptDistances) do
        restoreList[#restoreList + 1] = {
            Prompt = prompt,
            Distance = oldDistance
        }
    end

    table.clear(OriginalPromptDistances)

    for index, data in ipairs(restoreList) do
        local prompt = data.Prompt

        if prompt and prompt.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                prompt.MaxActivationDistance = data.Distance
            end)
        end

        -- 数量太多时分帧恢复，防止关闭瞬间卡死
        if index % 80 == 0 then
            task.wait()
        end
    end
end

local function RestorePromptHoldDurations()
    local restoreList = {}

    for prompt, oldHoldDuration in pairs(OriginalPromptHoldDurations) do
        restoreList[#restoreList + 1] = {
            Prompt = prompt,
            HoldDuration = oldHoldDuration
        }
    end

    table.clear(OriginalPromptHoldDurations)

    for index, data in ipairs(restoreList) do
        local prompt = data.Prompt

        if prompt and prompt.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                prompt.HoldDuration = data.HoldDuration
            end)
        end

        -- 数量太多时分帧恢复，防止关闭瞬间卡死
        if index % 80 == 0 then
            task.wait()
        end
    end
end

local function RestoreAllPromptStates()
    RestorePromptDistances()
    RestorePromptHoldDurations()
end

-- ================= ProximityPrompt 扫描 =================

local function ScanAndApplyPrompts()
    if not InstantInteractEnabled and not InteractRangeEnabled then
        return
    end

    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
            ApplyPrompt(obj)
        end
    end
end

local function ReapplyCachedPrompts()
    if not InstantInteractEnabled and not InteractRangeEnabled then
        return
    end

    for prompt, _ in pairs(PromptCache) do
        if prompt and prompt.Parent then
            ApplyPrompt(prompt)
        else
            RemovePromptFromCache(prompt)
        end
    end
end

local function StartPromptWatcher()
    if PromptAddedConnection or PromptRemovingConnection then
        return
    end

    PromptAddedConnection = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
            ApplyPrompt(obj)
        end
    end)

    PromptRemovingConnection = workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(obj)
        if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
            RemovePromptFromCache(obj)
        end
    end)
end

local function StopPromptWatcherIfNeeded()
    if InstantInteractEnabled or InteractRangeEnabled then
        return
    end

    if PromptAddedConnection then
        PromptAddedConnection:Disconnect()
        PromptAddedConnection = nil
    end

    if PromptRemovingConnection then
        PromptRemovingConnection:Disconnect()
        PromptRemovingConnection = nil
    end

    table.clear(PromptCache)
end

-- ================= 秒互动开关 =================

Tababc:Toggle({
    Title = "秒互动",
    Default = false,

    Callback = function(state)

        InstantInteractEnabled = state

        if state then

            StartPromptWatcher()
            ScanAndApplyPrompts()

            AddFeature("秒互动")

        else

            RemoveFeature("秒互动")

            RestorePromptHoldDurations()
            StopPromptWatcherIfNeeded()

            Notify(
                "秒互动",
                "已恢复原本互动等待时间",
                3
            )
        end
    end
})

-- ================= 互动范围修改 =================

Tababc:Toggle({
    Title = "开启互动范围修改",
    Default = false,

    Callback = function(state)

        InteractRangeEnabled = state

        if state then

            StartPromptWatcher()
            ScanAndApplyPrompts()

            AddFeature("互动范围")

        else

            RemoveFeature("互动范围")

            RestorePromptDistances()
            StopPromptWatcherIfNeeded()

            Notify(
                "互动范围",
                "已恢复原本互动距离",
                3
            )
        end
    end
})

-- ================= 互动范围输入 =================

Tababc:Input({
    Title = "互动范围",
    Desc = "默认20",

    Placeholder = "输入范围",

    Callback = function(text)

        local num = tonumber(text)

        if num then

            InteractDistance = math.clamp(num, 1, 10000)

            Notify(
                "互动范围",
                "当前范围: " .. InteractDistance,
                3
            )

            if InteractRangeEnabled then
                ReapplyCachedPrompts()
            end

        else

            Notify(
                "错误",
                "请输入数字",
                3
            )

        end
    end
})

-- ================= 一键互动全部 =================

Tababc:Button({

    Title = "一键互动全部",

    Callback = function()

        if type(fireproximityprompt) ~= "function" then
            Notify(
                "错误",
                "当前环境不支持 fireproximityprompt",
                3
            )
            return
        end

        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not root then
            Notify(
                "错误",
                "角色未加载",
                3
            )
            return
        end

        if InstantInteractEnabled or InteractRangeEnabled then
            ReapplyCachedPrompts()
        end

        local interacted = 0

        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do

            if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then

                local part = GetPromptPart(obj)

                if part then

                    local distance = (
                        root.Position - part.Position
                    ).Magnitude

                    if distance <= InteractDistance then

                        local success = pcall(function()

                            if InstantInteractEnabled then
                                SavePromptHoldDuration(obj)
                                obj.HoldDuration = 0
                            end

                            if InteractRangeEnabled then
                                SavePromptDistance(obj)
                                obj.MaxActivationDistance = InteractDistance
                            end

                            fireproximityprompt(obj)
                        end)

                        if success then
                            interacted += 1
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        Notify(
            "一键互动",
            "已互动 " .. interacted .. " 个对象",
            3
        )

    end
})

-- ================= 可点击物品透视 =================

local ClickItemESPEnabled = false
local ClickItemNameEnabled = false

local ClickItemHighlights = {}
local ClickItemBillboards = {}

local ClickItemAddedConnection = nil
local ClickItemRemovingConnection = nil
local ClickItemScanQueued = false

local function GetClickItemAdornee(clickDetector)
    local parent = clickDetector.Parent

    if not parent then
        return nil
    end

    if parent:IsA("BasePart") or parent:IsA("Model") then
        return parent
    end

    local model = parent:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")

    if model then
        return model
    end

    return parent:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BasePart")
end

local function GetClickItemPart(clickDetector)
    local parent = clickDetector.Parent

    if not parent then
        return nil
    end

    if parent:IsA("BasePart") then
        return parent
    end

    local model = parent:IsA("Model") and parent or parent:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")

    if model then
        return model.PrimaryPart or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
    end

    return parent:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BasePart")
end

local function GetClickItemName(clickDetector)
    local parent = clickDetector.Parent

    if parent then
        return parent.Name
    end

    return clickDetector.Name
end

local function RemoveClickItemESP(detector)
    if ClickItemHighlights[detector] then
        ClickItemHighlights[detector]:Destroy()
        ClickItemHighlights[detector] = nil
    end

    if ClickItemBillboards[detector] then
        ClickItemBillboards[detector]:Destroy()
        ClickItemBillboards[detector] = nil
    end
end

local function ClearClickItemHighlights()
    for detector, highlight in pairs(ClickItemHighlights) do
        if highlight then
            highlight:Destroy()
        end

        ClickItemHighlights[detector] = nil
    end
end

local function ClearClickItemBillboards()
    for detector, billboard in pairs(ClickItemBillboards) do
        if billboard then
            billboard:Destroy()
        end

        ClickItemBillboards[detector] = nil
    end
end

local function ClearClickItemESP()
    ClearClickItemHighlights()
    ClearClickItemBillboards()
end

-- ================= ClickDetector 扫描 =================

local function ScanAndApplyClickItems()

    for detector, _ in pairs(ClickItemHighlights) do
        if not detector or not detector.Parent or not ClickItemESPEnabled then
            RemoveClickItemESP(detector)
        end
    end

    for detector, _ in pairs(ClickItemBillboards) do
        if not detector or not detector.Parent or not ClickItemNameEnabled then
            RemoveClickItemESP(detector)
        end
    end

    if not ClickItemESPEnabled and not ClickItemNameEnabled then
        return
    end

    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do

        if obj:IsA("ClickDetector") then

            local adornee = GetClickItemAdornee(obj)
            local part = GetClickItemPart(obj)

            if ClickItemESPEnabled and adornee and not ClickItemHighlights[obj] then
                local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
                highlight.Name = "_ClickableItemHighlight"
                highlight.Adornee = adornee
                highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
                highlight.FillTransparency = 0.55
                highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
                highlight.Parent = adornee

                ClickItemHighlights[obj] = highlight
            end

            if ClickItemNameEnabled and part and not ClickItemBillboards[obj] then
                local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                billboard.Name = "_ClickableItemName"
                billboard.Adornee = part
                billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
                billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 28)
                billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
                billboard.Parent = part

                local text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                text.Name = "NameText"
                text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                text.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                text.Text = GetClickItemName(obj)
                text.TextScaled = false
                text.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                text.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 93, 0)
                text.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
                text.Parent = billboard

                ClickItemBillboards[obj] = billboard
            end
        end
    end
end

local function QueueClickItemScan()

    if not ClickItemESPEnabled and not ClickItemNameEnabled then
        return
    end

    if ClickItemScanQueued then
        return
    end

    ClickItemScanQueued = true

    task.delay(0.15, function()

        ClickItemScanQueued = false

        if ClickItemESPEnabled or ClickItemNameEnabled then
            ScanAndApplyClickItems()
        end
    end)
end

local function StartClickItemWatcher()

    if ClickItemAddedConnection or ClickItemRemovingConnection then
        return
    end

    ClickItemAddedConnection = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if obj:IsA("ClickDetector") then
            QueueClickItemScan()
        end
    end)

    ClickItemRemovingConnection = workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(obj)
        if obj:IsA("ClickDetector") then
            RemoveClickItemESP(obj)
            QueueClickItemScan()
        end
    end)
end

local function StopClickItemWatcherIfNeeded()

    if ClickItemESPEnabled or ClickItemNameEnabled then
        return
    end

    if ClickItemAddedConnection then
        ClickItemAddedConnection:Disconnect()
        ClickItemAddedConnection = nil
    end

    if ClickItemRemovingConnection then
        ClickItemRemovingConnection:Disconnect()
        ClickItemRemovingConnection = nil
    end

    ClickItemScanQueued = false
end

-- ================= 透视可点击物品 =================

Tababc:Toggle({
    Title = "透视可点击物品",
    Default = false,

    Callback = function(state)

        ClickItemESPEnabled = state

        if state then

            StartClickItemWatcher()
            ScanAndApplyClickItems()

            AddFeature("可点击物品透视")

        else

            RemoveFeature("可点击物品透视")
            ClearClickItemHighlights()
            StopClickItemWatcherIfNeeded()

        end
    end
})

-- ================= 显示可点击物品名字 =================

Tababc:Toggle({
    Title = "显示可点击物品名字",
    Default = false,

    Callback = function(state)

        ClickItemNameEnabled = state

        if state then

            StartClickItemWatcher()
            ScanAndApplyClickItems()

            AddFeature("可点击物品名字")

        else

            RemoveFeature("可点击物品名字")
            ClearClickItemBillboards()
            StopClickItemWatcherIfNeeded()

        end
    end
})

    Callback = function()
        ScanAndApplyClickItems()

        Notify(
            "可点击物品ESP",
            "刷新完成",
            3
        )
    end
})

;(function()
local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local PlayerWhitelistDropdown = nil
local PlayerDropdown = nil

-- 修复问题 5: 持久化 fallback 假环境表
local dummyGenv = {}
local GET_GENV = type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv or function() return dummyGenv end

if type(ESP_SETTINGS) ~= "table" then ESP_SETTINGS = {} end
if type(AimbotTeamWhitelist) ~= "table" then AimbotTeamWhitelist = {} end

if type(ESP_SETTINGS.TeamCheck) ~= "boolean" then ESP_SETTINGS.TeamCheck = false end
if type(ESP_SETTINGS.FriendCheck) ~= "boolean" then ESP_SETTINGS.FriendCheck = false end 
if type(ESP_SETTINGS.WallCheck) ~= "boolean" then ESP_SETTINGS.WallCheck = false end
if type(ESP_SETTINGS.IgnoreSpawnShield) ~= "boolean" then ESP_SETTINGS.IgnoreSpawnShield = true end
if type(ESP_SETTINGS.SmoothAim) ~= "boolean" then ESP_SETTINGS.SmoothAim = false end
if type(ESP_SETTINGS.ManualOverrideEnabled) ~= "boolean" then ESP_SETTINGS.ManualOverrideEnabled = false end

local FOV = 100
pcall(function() if GET_GENV().FOV then FOV = tonumber(GET_GENV().FOV) or 100 end end)
local LerpAlpha = 1 

local function GetCamera()
    Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera or Camera
    return Camera
end

local AIMBOT_RUNTIME_KEY = "__Night_Aimbot_Runtime"

pcall(function()
    if _G[AIMBOT_RUNTIME_KEY] and type(_G[AIMBOT_RUNTIME_KEY].Cleanup) == "function" then
        _G[AIMBOT_RUNTIME_KEY].Cleanup()
    end
end)

local Runtime = {
    Connections = {},
    Threads = {} 
}
_G[AIMBOT_RUNTIME_KEY] = Runtime

local function TrackConnection(conn)
    if conn then table.insert(Runtime.Connections, conn) end
    return conn
end

local function TrackThread(thread)
    if thread then table.insert(Runtime.Threads, thread) end
    return thread
end

local function SafeDestroy(obj)
    if obj then pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
end

local function SafeNotify(title, content)
    local done = false
    pcall(function()
        if WindUI and WindUI.Notify then
            WindUI:Notify({ Title = title, Content = content, Duration = 3 })
            done = true
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if not done and Window and Window.Notify then
            Window:Notify({ Title = title, Content = content, Duration = 3 })
            done = true
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if not done then
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = title, Text = content, Duration = 3 })
        end
    end)
end

local function SafeUICall(parent, method, config)
    local dummy = { 
        Set = function() end, 
        Refresh = function() end, 
        Value = config.Default or {} 
    }
    if not parent or type(parent[method]) ~= "function" then 
        warn("UI 方法缺失: " .. tostring(method))
        SafeNotify("UI 异常", "控件方法 " .. tostring(method) .. " 不存在，部分功能将无法显示。")
        return dummy 
    end
    local ok, result = pcall(function() return parent[method](parent, config) end)
    if not ok then 
        warn("UI 控件构建失败 (".. method .."): " .. tostring(result)) 
        SafeNotify("UI 异常", "控件 " .. (config.Title or method) .. " 创建失败，请检查 UI 库兼容性。")
        return dummy
    end
    return result or dummy
end

local function SyncGlobal(key, value)
    pcall(function()
        local env = GET_GENV()
        if env then env[key] = value end
    end)
end

local AutoSaveFile = "NightAimbotAutoSave.txt"
local FullConfigFile = "NightAimbotFullConfig.txt"

-- ================= 核心自动记忆逻辑 =================
local AutoSaveEnabled = false
pcall(function()
    if isfile and isfile(AutoSaveFile) then
        AutoSaveEnabled = readfile(AutoSaveFile) == "true"
    end
end)

local function SaveFullConfig()
    if IsLoading or not AutoSaveEnabled then return end
    
    if type(writefile) ~= "function" then
        SafeNotify("配置保存失败", "当前执行器环境不支持 writefile 功能")
        return
    end

    local ok, err = pcall(function() 
        local config = {
            TargetMode = AimbotTargetMode,
            ShowFOV = ShowFOVCircle,
            TeamCheck = ESP_SETTINGS.TeamCheck,
            FriendCheck = ESP_SETTINGS.FriendCheck, 
            WallCheck = ESP_SETTINGS.WallCheck,
            IgnoreSpawnShield = ESP_SETTINGS.IgnoreSpawnShield,
            FOV = FOV,
            SmoothAim = ESP_SETTINGS.SmoothAim,
            PredictionEnabled = PredictionEnabled,
            ManualOverride = ESP_SETTINGS.ManualOverrideEnabled,
            AimPart = AimPart
        }
        writefile(FullConfigFile, HttpService:JSONEncode(config))
    end)
    if not ok then SafeNotify("配置保存失败", tostring(err)) end
end

local function UpdateFOVCircle()
    local activeCircle = nil
    pcall(function() activeCircle = GET_GENV().circle or circle end)
    if not activeCircle then return end

    local shouldShow = AimbotEnabled and ShowFOVCircle
    pcall(function()
        local isGui = typeof(activeCircle) == "Instance" and activeCircle:IsA("GuiObject")
        local isDrawing = false
        if not isGui then
            -- 修复问题 4: 严格校验 Drawing 对象，防止普通 table 假装自己是圆圈
            local ok, radius = pcall(function() return activeCircle.Radius end)
            isDrawing = ok and type(radius) == "number"
        end

        if isGui then
            local size = FOV * 2
            activeCircle.Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size)
            if activeCircle.Visible ~= shouldShow then activeCircle.Visible = shouldShow end
        elseif isDrawing then
            activeCircle.Radius = FOV
            if activeCircle.Visible ~= shouldShow then activeCircle.Visible = shouldShow end
        end
    end)
end

if AutoSaveEnabled then
    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(FullConfigFile) then
            local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(FullConfigFile))
            if type(data) == "table" then
                if data.TargetMode ~= nil and (data.TargetMode == "玩家" or data.TargetMode == "NPC" or data.TargetMode == "全部") then AimbotTargetMode = tostring(data.TargetMode) end
                if data.ShowFOV ~= nil then ShowFOVCircle = data.ShowFOV == true end
                if data.TeamCheck ~= nil then ESP_SETTINGS.TeamCheck = data.TeamCheck == true end
                if data.FriendCheck ~= nil then ESP_SETTINGS.FriendCheck = data.FriendCheck == true end
                if data.WallCheck ~= nil then ESP_SETTINGS.WallCheck = data.WallCheck == true end
                if data.IgnoreSpawnShield ~= nil then ESP_SETTINGS.IgnoreSpawnShield = data.IgnoreSpawnShield == true end
                if data.FOV ~= nil then FOV = math.clamp(tonumber(data.FOV) or 100, 10, 700) end
                if data.SmoothAim ~= nil then ESP_SETTINGS.SmoothAim = data.SmoothAim == true end
                if data.PredictionEnabled ~= nil then PredictionEnabled = data.PredictionEnabled == true end
                if data.ManualOverride ~= nil then ESP_SETTINGS.ManualOverrideEnabled = data.ManualOverride == true end
                if data.AimPart ~= nil and table.find(AimPartsList, tostring(data.AimPart)) then 
                    AimPart = tostring(data.AimPart) 
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- ================= 异步无延迟好友缓存系统 =================
local FriendsCache = {}

local function UpdateFriendStatus(player)
    if not player or player == LocalPlayer then return end
    -- 修复问题 3: 不再把这种短命线程塞进 Runtime.Threads，让 GC 自己干活
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, isFriend = pcall(function()
            return LocalPlayer:IsFriendsWith(player.UserId)
        end)
        if ok then
            FriendsCache[player.UserId] = isFriend
        end
    end)
end

TrackThread(task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(30) do
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            UpdateFriendStatus(p)
        end
    end
end))

local TARGET_SPAWN_IGNORE_TIME = 0.15
local TargetSpawnTime = {}
local CurrentTarget = nil
local ManualOverride = false
local LastManualMove = 0
local VisibilityCache = {}

local function MarkTargetSpawned(obj)
    if obj then TargetSpawnTime[obj] = tick() end
end

local function IsTargetRecentlySpawned(target)
    if not target or not target.Object then return false end
    local spawnTime = TargetSpawnTime[target.Object]
    if not spawnTime then return false end
    return tick() - spawnTime < TARGET_SPAWN_IGNORE_TIME
end

local function CleanupTargetMemory(obj)
    if not obj then return end
    TargetSpawnTime[obj] = nil
    if VisibilityCache[obj] then
        VisibilityCache[obj] = nil
    end
end

TrackConnection(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
    CleanupTargetMemory(player)
    FriendsCache[player.UserId] = nil
    if SelectedTarget == player then SelectedTarget = nil end
end))

local function LoadSmoothnessPercent()
    local saved = nil
    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(SmoothnessFile) then saved = tonumber(readfile(SmoothnessFile)) end
    end)
    if saved then 
        if saved > 0 and saved <= 1 then saved = saved * 100 end
        return math.clamp(math.floor(saved + 0.5), 1, 100) 
    end
    return 15
end

local function SaveSmoothnessPercent(v)
    v = tonumber(v)
    if not v then return end
    v = math.clamp(math.floor(v + 0.5), 1, 100)
    pcall(function() if type(writefile) == "function" then writefile(SmoothnessFile, tostring(v)) end end)
end

local SmoothnessPercent = LoadSmoothnessPercent()
LerpAlpha = math.clamp((101 - SmoothnessPercent) / 100, 0.01, 1)

local function LoadPredictionPercent()
    local saved = nil
    pcall(function() if isfile and isfile(PredictionFile) then saved = tonumber(readfile(PredictionFile)) end end)
    if saved then return math.clamp(math.floor(saved + 0.5), 0, 100) end
    return 15
end

local function SavePredictionPercent(v)
    v = tonumber(v)
    if not v then return end
    v = math.clamp(math.floor(v + 0.5), 0, 100)
    pcall(function() if type(writefile) == "function" then writefile(PredictionFile, tostring(v)) end end)
end

local PredictionPercent = LoadPredictionPercent()

-- 修复问题 1: 补回漏掉的预判时间函数
local function GetPredictionTime()
    if not PredictionEnabled then return 0 end
    return math.clamp(PredictionPercent / 100 * 0.35, 0, 0.35)
end

local function LoadDistanceValue(fileName, defaultValue)
    local saved = nil
    pcall(function() if isfile and isfile(fileName) then saved = tonumber(readfile(fileName)) end end)
    if saved then return math.clamp(math.floor(saved + 0.5), 50, 6000) end
    return math.clamp(math.floor((tonumber(defaultValue) or 1000) + 0.5), 50, 6000)
end

local function SaveDistanceValue(fileName, value)
    value = tonumber(value)
    if not value then return end
    value = math.clamp(math.floor(value + 0.5), 50, 6000)
    pcall(function() if type(writefile) == "function" then writefile(fileName, tostring(value)) end end)
end

-- ================= 距离与全局状态同步 =================
local PlayerMaxDistance = LoadDistanceValue(PlayerMaxDistanceFile, 1000)
local NPCMaxDistance = LoadDistanceValue(NPCMaxDistanceFile, 1000)
local MaxDistance = PlayerMaxDistance 

-- 修复问题 2 & 8: 函数整体下移以保证能拿到局部的 Distance 变量，并处理全部模式的最大值
local function SyncAllGlobalStates()
    SyncGlobal("FOV", FOV)
    SyncGlobal("Smoothness", LerpAlpha)
    
    if AimbotTargetMode == "NPC" then
        SyncGlobal("MaxDistance", NPCMaxDistance)
    elseif AimbotTargetMode == "全部" then
        SyncGlobal("MaxDistance", math.max(PlayerMaxDistance, NPCMaxDistance))
    else
        SyncGlobal("MaxDistance", PlayerMaxDistance)
    end
    
    UpdateFOVCircle()
end

local TabAimbot = nil
if typeof(Window) == "table" and type(Window.Tab) == "function" then
    TabAimbot = Window:Tab({ Title = "自瞄", Icon = "target", Locked = false })
end

-- ================= NPC 通用兼容 =================
local NPC_CLEAN_INTERVAL = 2
local LastNPCClean = 0
local CachedNPCModels = {}
local CachedNPCSeen = {}
local NPCWatchStarted = false
local MAX_NPC_TARGETS = 40

local function IsPlayerCharacter(model)
    if not model then return false end
    local ok, player = pcall(function() return Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model) end)
    return ok and player ~= nil
end

local function GetCharacterRoot(character)
    if not character then return nil end
    return character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character:FindFirstChild("Head") or character.PrimaryPart
end

local function IsValidNPCModel(model)
    if not model or not model:IsA("Model") then return false end
    if IsPlayerCharacter(model) then return false end
    local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return false end
    if not GetCharacterRoot(model) then return false end
    return true
end

local function AddNPCToCache(model)
    if not IsValidNPCModel(model) then return end
    if CachedNPCSeen[model] then return end
    CachedNPCSeen[model] = true
    table.insert(CachedNPCModels, model)
    MarkTargetSpawned(model)
end

local function TryAddNPCFromDescendant(obj)
    if not obj then return end
    if obj:IsA("Humanoid") then
        AddNPCToCache(obj.Parent)
        return
    end
    local model = obj:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    if model then AddNPCToCache(model) end
end

local function CleanNPCList(force)
    local now = tick()
    if not force and now - LastNPCClean < NPC_CLEAN_INTERVAL then return end
    LastNPCClean = now

    local newList = {}
    local newSeen = {}
    for _, model in ipairs(CachedNPCModels) do
        if model and model.Parent and IsValidNPCModel(model) then
            local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                newSeen[model] = true
                table.insert(newList, model)
            end
        end
    end
    CachedNPCModels = newList
    CachedNPCSeen = newSeen
end

local function StartNPCWatcher()
    if NPCWatchStarted then return end
    NPCWatchStarted = true

    TrackThread(task.spawn(function()
        local count = 0
        for _, obj in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("Humanoid") then
                AddNPCToCache(obj.Parent)
                count += 1
                if count % 80 == 0 then task.wait() end
            end
        end
    end))

    TrackConnection(Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        task.defer(function() TryAddNPCFromDescendant(obj) end)
    end))

    TrackConnection(Workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(obj)
        if obj:IsA("Model") and CachedNPCSeen[obj] then
            CachedNPCSeen[obj] = nil
            CleanupTargetMemory(obj) 
            for i = #CachedNPCModels, 1, -1 do
                if CachedNPCModels[i] == obj then
                    table.remove(CachedNPCModels, i)
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end))
end

local function RefreshNPCList(force)
    StartNPCWatcher()
    CleanNPCList(force)
    return CachedNPCModels
end

local NPC_TEAM_KEYS = { "Team", "TeamName", "Faction", "FactionName", "Group", "Camp", "Side" }

local function ValueToName(value)
    if value == nil then return nil end
    local valueType = typeof(value)
    if valueType == "string" then return value ~= "" and value or nil end
    if valueType == "number" or valueType == "boolean" then return tostring(value) end
    if valueType == "Instance" then return value.Name end
    return tostring(value)
end

local function GetNPCTeamName(model)
    if not model then return "NPC" end
    for _, key in ipairs(NPC_TEAM_KEYS) do
        local attr = nil
        pcall(function() attr = model:GetAttribute(key) end)
        local name = ValueToName(attr)
        if name then return name end
    end
    for _, key in ipairs(NPC_TEAM_KEYS) do
        local child = model:FindFirstChild(key)
        if child then
            if child:IsA("StringValue") or child:IsA("IntValue") or child:IsA("NumberValue") or child:IsA("BoolValue") then
                local name = ValueToName(child.Value)
                if name then return name end
            elseif child:IsA("ObjectValue") and child.Value then
                return child.Value.Name
            end
        end
    end
    if model.Parent and model.Parent ~= Workspace then return model.Parent.Name end
    return "NPC"
end

local function GetNPCTeamCount()
    local teams = {}
    for _, model in ipairs(RefreshNPCList(true)) do
        local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
            teams[GetNPCTeamName(model)] = true
        end
    end
    local count = 0
    for _ in pairs(teams) do count += 1 end
    return count
end

local function IsTargetKindAllowed(kind)
    if AimbotTargetMode == "全部" then return true end
    if AimbotTargetMode == "玩家" then return kind == "Player" end
    if AimbotTargetMode == "NPC" then return kind == "NPC" end
    return kind == "Player"
end

local function MakePlayerTarget(player)
    return { Kind = "Player", Object = player, Player = player, Character = player.Character, Name = player.Name }
end

local function MakeNPCTarget(model)
    return { Kind = "NPC", Object = model, Model = model, Character = model, Name = model.Name }
end

local function SameTarget(a, b)
    return a and b and a.Kind == b.Kind and a.Object == b.Object
end

local function GetTargetAimPart(target)
    if not target or not target.Character then return nil end
    local partNames = {"Head"}
    if AimPart == "头部" then
        partNames = {"Head"}
    elseif AimPart == "身体" then
        partNames = {"HumanoidRootPart", "UpperTorso", "Torso"}
    elseif AimPart == "左手" then
        partNames = {"LeftHand", "LeftUpperArm", "Left Arm"}
    elseif AimPart == "右手" then
        partNames = {"RightHand", "RightUpperArm", "Right Arm"}
    elseif AimPart == "左腿" then
        partNames = {"LeftFoot", "LeftUpperLeg", "Left Leg"}
    elseif AimPart == "右腿" then
        partNames = {"RightFoot", "RightUpperLeg", "Right Leg"}
    else
        partNames = {AimPart, "Head"}
    end
    for _, name in ipairs(partNames) do
        local foundPart = target.Character:FindFirstChild(name)
        if foundPart and foundPart:IsA("BasePart") then return foundPart end
    end
    return target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or target.Character.PrimaryPart
end

local TargetPredictionCache = {}
setmetatable(TargetPredictionCache, {__mode = "k"})

local function GetPredictedAimPosition(target, part)
    if not part then return nil end
    local predictionTime = GetPredictionTime()
    local position = part.Position
    if predictionTime <= 0 then return position end
    local camera = GetCamera()
    if camera and target and target.Character then
        local hum = target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local rootPart = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or target.Character.PrimaryPart
        if hum and rootPart then
            local velocity = rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            if hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude < 0.05 and velocity.Magnitude < 1.5 then return position end
            if velocity.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                local toTargetDir = (position - camera.CFrame.Position)
                if toTargetDir.Magnitude > 0.01 then 
                    toTargetDir = toTargetDir.Unit
                    local velUnit = velocity.Unit
                    local directionDot = math.abs(toTargetDir:Dot(velUnit))
                    if directionDot > 0.92 then return position end
                end
            end
            if velocity.Magnitude > 120 then velocity = velocity.Unit * 120 end
            return position + (velocity * predictionTime)
        end
    end
    
    local now = tick()
    local cache = TargetPredictionCache[part]
    if not cache then
        local initialVelocity = Vector3.zero
        pcall(function() initialVelocity = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity end)
        TargetPredictionCache[part] = { LastPosition = position, LastVelocity = initialVelocity, LastTime = now }
        return position + initialVelocity * predictionTime
    end
    
    local dt = now - cache.LastTime
    cache.LastTime = now
    if dt < 0.001 then dt = 0.001 elseif dt > 0.3 then dt = 0.03 end
    local deltaVelocity = (position - cache.LastPosition) / dt
    cache.LastPosition = position
    local engineVelocity = Vector3.zero
    local rootPart = target and target.Character and (target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or target.Character.PrimaryPart)
    if rootPart then pcall(function() engineVelocity = rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity end) else pcall(function() engineVelocity = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity end) end
    if engineVelocity.Magnitude < 1.5 and deltaVelocity.Magnitude < 3.5 then return position end
    local blendedVelocity = engineVelocity:Lerp(deltaVelocity, 0.25)
    return position + blendedVelocity * predictionTime
end

local function IsTargetAlive(target)
    local character = target and target.Character
    local hum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    return hum and hum.Health > 0
end

local function HasSpawnShieldTarget(target)
    if not target or not target.Character then return false end
    return target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("ForceField") ~= nil
end

local function IsSameTeamTarget(target)
    if not target then return false end
    if target.Kind == "Player" then
        if not target.Player or not LocalPlayer then return false end
        local ok, result = pcall(function() return target.Player.Team == LocalPlayer.Team end)
        return ok and result or false
    end
    if target.Kind == "NPC" then
        local npcTeam = GetNPCTeamName(target.Model)
        local localTeamName = nil
        pcall(function() if LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Team then localTeamName = LocalPlayer.Team.Name end end)
        if localTeamName and npcTeam == localTeamName then return true end
    end
    return false
end

local function SetupPlayerSpawnWatch(player)
    if not player or player == LocalPlayer then return end
    UpdateFriendStatus(player) 
    MarkTargetSpawned(player)
    TrackConnection(player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        MarkTargetSpawned(player)
    end))
end

for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    SetupPlayerSpawnWatch(p)
end

TrackConnection(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p)
    SetupPlayerSpawnWatch(p)
end))

local AimbotTeamCheckToggle = nil
local FriendCheckToggle = nil

local function ValidateNPCTeamCheck()
    if AimbotTargetMode == "NPC" and ESP_SETTINGS.TeamCheck then
        if GetNPCTeamCount() <= 1 then
            ESP_SETTINGS.TeamCheck = false
            SafeNotify("队伍检测已关闭", "只检测到一个 NPC 团队，无法开启队伍检测")
            if AimbotTeamCheckToggle then task.defer(function() pcall(function() AimbotTeamCheckToggle:Set(false) end) end) end
            return false
        end
    end
    return true
end

-- ================= 自瞄状态统一函数 =================
local AimbotMainToggle = nil
local AimbotQuickVisible = false
local AimbotQuickGui = nil
local AimbotQuickHolder = nil
local AimbotQuickText = nil
local AimbotQuickDot = nil
local AimbotQuickGradient = nil

local function SetAimbotState(v)
    v = not not v
    AimbotEnabled = v 
    
    if type(AddFeature) == "function" and type(RemoveFeature) == "function" then
        if v then AddFeature("自瞄") else RemoveFeature("自瞄") end
    end
    if AimbotQuickText then AimbotQuickText.Text = v and "自瞄开" or "自瞄关" end
    if AimbotQuickDot then
        AimbotQuickDot.BackgroundColor3 = v and Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 150) or Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 140)
    end
    UpdateFOVCircle()
end

-- ================= 创建快捷自瞄开关 =================
local function CreateAimbotQuickSwitch()
    if AimbotQuickGui then return end
    local PosFile = "NightAimbotQuickPos.txt"

    local function GetGuiParent()
        local parent = nil
        pcall(function() if gethui then parent = gethui() end end)
        if parent then return parent end
        pcall(function() parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
        return parent
    end

    local GuiParent = GetGuiParent()
    
    if GuiParent then
        pcall(function()
            local old = GuiParent:FindFirstChild("AimbotQuickSwitchGui")
            if old then old:Destroy() end
        end)
    else
        local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 3)
        if playerGui then
            pcall(function()
                local old = playerGui:FindFirstChild("AimbotQuickSwitchGui")
                if old then old:Destroy() end
            end)
        end
    end

    AimbotQuickGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    AimbotQuickGui.Name = "AimbotQuickSwitchGui"
    AimbotQuickGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    AimbotQuickGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    AimbotQuickGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling

    local okParent = pcall(function() AimbotQuickGui.Parent = GuiParent end)
    if not okParent or not AimbotQuickGui.Parent then
        local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 3)
        if playerGui then AimbotQuickGui.Parent = playerGui else return end
    end

    local function GetViewportSize()
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        return camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1920, 1080)
    end

    local function ClampToScreen(pos)
        local viewport = GetViewportSize()
        local size = AimbotQuickHolder and AimbotQuickHolder.AbsoluteSize or Vector2.new(94, 38)
        local padding = 8
        local x = math.clamp(pos.X, padding, math.max(padding, viewport.X - size.X - padding))
        local y = math.clamp(pos.Y, padding, math.max(padding, viewport.Y - size.Y - padding))
        return Vector2.new(x, y)
    end

    AimbotQuickHolder = Instance.new("Frame")
    AimbotQuickHolder.Name = "AimbotQuickHolder"
    AimbotQuickHolder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(94, 38)
    AimbotQuickHolder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -118, 0.45, 0)
    AimbotQuickHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    AimbotQuickHolder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    AimbotQuickHolder.Active = true
    AimbotQuickHolder.Visible = false
    AimbotQuickHolder.ZIndex = 999
    AimbotQuickHolder.Parent = AimbotQuickGui

    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(PosFile) then
            local data = readfile(PosFile)
            local x, y = string.match(data, "([^,]+),([^,]+)")
            if tonumber(x) and tonumber(y) then
                local savedPos = ClampToScreen(Vector2.new(tonumber(x), tonumber(y)))
                AimbotQuickHolder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(savedPos.X, savedPos.Y)
            end
        end
    end)

    local Button = Instance.new("TextButton")
    Button.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    Button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 24)
    Button.BackgroundTransparency = 0.06
    Button.Text = ""
    Button.Parent = AimbotQuickHolder
    Instance.new("UICorner", Button).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local RainbowStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    RainbowStroke.Thickness = 2.4
    RainbowStroke.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    RainbowStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    RainbowStroke.Parent = Button

    AimbotQuickGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    AimbotQuickGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.16, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.33, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.50, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.66, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.83, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
    })
    AimbotQuickGradient.Parent = RainbowStroke

    local InnerStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    InnerStroke.Thickness = 1
    InnerStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    InnerStroke.Transparency = 0.86
    InnerStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    InnerStroke.Parent = Button

    AimbotQuickDot = Instance.new("Frame")
    AimbotQuickDot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
    AimbotQuickDot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 9, 0.5, 0)
    AimbotQuickDot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16)
    AimbotQuickDot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 140)
    AimbotQuickDot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    AimbotQuickDot.Parent = Button
    Instance.new("UICorner", AimbotQuickDot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    
    local DotStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    DotStroke.Thickness = 1
    DotStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    DotStroke.Transparency = 0.6
    DotStroke.Parent = AimbotQuickDot

    AimbotQuickText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    AimbotQuickText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    AimbotQuickText.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(31, 0)
    AimbotQuickText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 1, 0)
    AimbotQuickText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    AimbotQuickText.TextSize = 12
    AimbotQuickText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    AimbotQuickText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 245)
    AimbotQuickText.Text = "自瞄关"
    AimbotQuickText.Parent = Button

    TrackConnection(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        if AimbotQuickGradient and AimbotQuickHolder and AimbotQuickHolder.Visible then
            AimbotQuickGradient.Rotation = (AimbotQuickGradient.Rotation + dt * 160) % 360
        end
    end))

    local isDragging, dragStart, startPos, activeTouch, hasMoved = false, nil, nil, nil, false
    local DRAG_DISTANCE = 6

    local function ApplyDragPosition(input)
        if not isDragging or not AimbotQuickHolder then return end
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        if math.abs(delta.X) > DRAG_DISTANCE or math.abs(delta.Y) > DRAG_DISTANCE then hasMoved = true end
        local rawPos = ClampToScreen(Vector2.new(startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y))
        AimbotQuickHolder.Position = UDim2.new(0, rawPos.X, 0, rawPos.Y)
    end

    TrackConnection(Button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and not activeTouch) then
            isDragging, dragStart, startPos, hasMoved = true, input.Position, AimbotQuickHolder.Position, false
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then activeTouch = input end
            pcall(function() TweenService:Create(AimbotQuickHolder, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(100, 42)}):Play() end)
            
            local changedConn
            changedConn = input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    if changedConn then changedConn:Disconnect(); changedConn = nil end
                    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input ~= activeTouch then return end
                    isDragging, activeTouch = false, nil
                    if hasMoved then pcall(function() if type(writefile) == "function" then writefile(PosFile, tostring(math.floor(AimbotQuickHolder.AbsolutePosition.X)) .. "," .. tostring(math.floor(AimbotQuickHolder.AbsolutePosition.Y))) end end) end
                    pcall(function() TweenService:Create(AimbotQuickHolder, TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(94, 38)}):Play() end)
                end
            end)
        end
    end))

    TrackConnection(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if isDragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input == activeTouch)) then
            ApplyDragPosition(input)
        end
    end))

    TrackConnection(Button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if hasMoved then hasMoved = false return end
        local newState = not AimbotEnabled
        if AimbotMainToggle then pcall(function() AimbotMainToggle:Set(newState) end) end
        SetAimbotState(newState)
    end))

    SetAimbotState(AimbotEnabled)
end

CreateAimbotQuickSwitch()

local AimbotPlayerList = {}

local function RefreshAimbotPlayerList()
    AimbotPlayerList = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer then table.insert(AimbotPlayerList, p.Name) end
    end
    local lastSelected = SelectedTarget and SelectedTarget.Name or nil

    if PlayerDropdown then pcall(function() PlayerDropdown:Refresh(AimbotPlayerList, lastSelected) end) end
    if PlayerBlacklistDropdown then pcall(function() PlayerBlacklistDropdown:Refresh(AimbotPlayerList, {}) end) end
    if PlayerWhitelistDropdown then pcall(function() PlayerWhitelistDropdown:Refresh(AimbotPlayerList, {}) end) end
end

local function RefreshTeamList()
    local teams = {}
    for _, t in ipairs(TeamsService:GetTeams()) do table.insert(teams, t.Name) end
    if TeamDropdown then pcall(function() TeamDropdown:Refresh(teams, {}) end) end
end

    end)
end

-- ================= 自瞄核心 =================
local WALLCHECK_PENETRATE_LIMIT = 10 
local WALLCHECK_CACHE_TIME = 0.05

local function IsTransparentRayPart(hit)
    if not hit then return false end
    local name = string.lower(hit.Name or "")
    if hit.Transparency >= 0.75 then return true end
    if hit.CanCollide == false and hit.Transparency >= 0.35 then return true end
    if hit.Material == Enum.Material.Glass then return true end
    if name:find("scope") or name:find("view") or name:find("camera") then return true end
    return false
end

local function ResetVisibilityCache(target)
    if target and target.Object then 
        VisibilityCache[target.Object] = nil 
    end
end

local function RayCanHitTarget(origin, targetPos, targetCharacter, rayParams)
    local direction = targetPos - origin
    if direction.Magnitude <= 0.01 then return false end

    local currentOrigin = origin
    local remaining = direction
    
    for _ = 1, WALLCHECK_PENETRATE_LIMIT do
         if remaining.Magnitude <= 0.01 then return false end
        local result = Workspace:Raycast(currentOrigin, remaining, rayParams)
        if not result then return true end
        local hit = result.Instance
         if hit and hit:IsDescendantOf(targetCharacter) then return true end
        if hit and IsTransparentRayPart(hit) then
            currentOrigin = result.Position + remaining.Unit * 0.08
            remaining = targetPos - currentOrigin
        else
            return false
         end
    end
    return false
end

local function isVisibleTarget(target, part)
    local camera = GetCamera()
    if not camera then return false end
    if not ESP_SETTINGS.WallCheck then return true end
    if not target or not target.Character or not part then return false end
    if IsTargetRecentlySpawned(target) then return false end

    VisibilityCache[target.Object] = VisibilityCache[target.Object] or {}
    local now = tick()
    local cached = VisibilityCache[target.Object][part.Name]
    if cached and now - cached.Time <= WALLCHECK_CACHE_TIME then return cached.Value end

    local origin = camera.CFrame.Position
    local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
    rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude 
    rayParams.IgnoreWater = true

    local ignoreList = {}
    if LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character then table.insert(ignoreList, LocalPlayer.Character) end
    table.insert(ignoreList, camera)

    local tool = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    if tool then table.insert(ignoreList, tool) end

    rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignoreList

    local mainVisible = RayCanHitTarget(origin, part.Position, target.Character, rayParams)
    if mainVisible then
        VisibilityCache[target.Object][part.Name] = { Time = now, Value = true }
        return true
    end

    local offsets = { Vector3.new(0.18, 0, 0), Vector3.new(-0.18, 0, 0), Vector3.new(0, 0.18, 0), Vector3.new(0, -0.18, 0) }
    local visibleCount = 0
    for _, offset in ipairs(offsets) do
        if RayCanHitTarget(origin, part.Position + offset, target.Character, rayParams) then
            visibleCount += 1
            if visibleCount >= 1 then
                VisibilityCache[target.Object][part.Name] = { Time = now, Value = true }
                return true
             end
        end
    end

    VisibilityCache[target.Object][part.Name] = { Time = now, Value = false }
    return false
end

local ManualMoveThreshold = 3.5
local ManualReleaseDelay = 0.2

TrackConnection(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
        local delta = input.Delta
        local movement = math.abs(delta.X) + math.abs(delta.Y)
        if ESP_SETTINGS.ManualOverrideEnabled and movement >= ManualMoveThreshold then
             ManualOverride = true
            LastManualMove = tick()
             CurrentTarget = nil
        end
    end
end))

local LastSwitchTime = 0
local SWITCH_DELAY = 0.25
local SWITCH_THRESHOLD = 0.7

local function shouldForceSwitch(target)
    if not target then return true end
    if not IsTargetKindAllowed(target.Kind) then return true end
    if not IsTargetAlive(target) then return true end
    if IsTargetRecentlySpawned(target) then return true end

    local part = GetTargetAimPart(target)
    if not part then return true end
    if not isVisibleTarget(target, part) then
         ResetVisibilityCache(target)
        return true
    end

    local camera = GetCamera()
    if not camera then return true end

    local pos, visible = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
    if not visible then return true end

    local center = camera.ViewportSize / 2
    local dist = (Vector2.new(pos.X, pos.Y) - center).Magnitude
    if dist > FOV then return true end

    return false
end

local function BuildTargetCandidates()
    local candidates = {}
    if AimbotTargetMode ==  "玩家" or AimbotTargetMode == "全部" then
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer then table.insert(candidates, MakePlayerTarget(p)) end
        end
    end

    if AimbotTargetMode == "NPC" or AimbotTargetMode == "全部" then
        local npcList = RefreshNPCList(false)
        local added = 0
        for _, model in ipairs(npcList) do
            table.insert(candidates, MakeNPCTarget(model))
             added += 1
            if added >= MAX_NPC_TARGETS then break end
        end
    end
    return candidates
end

-- ================= 【核心控制流：判定此目标是否该被过滤】 =================
local function ShouldSkipTarget(target)
    if not target then return true end

    -- 修复问题 7: 如果开启强锁但没选人，严格把所有人都挡在门外，不进行降级
    if LockTargetEnabled then
        if SelectedTarget == nil then return true end
        if target.Kind == "NPC" then return true end
        return target.Player ~= SelectedTarget
    end

    if target.Kind == "Player" then
        if AimbotPlayerBlacklistEnabled and AimbotPlayerBlacklist[target.Player.Name] then return true end
        if AimbotPlayerWhitelistEnabled and next(AimbotPlayerWhitelist) ~= nil and not AimbotPlayerWhitelist[target.Player.Name] then return true end
        if ESP_SETTINGS.FriendCheck and FriendsCache[target.Player.UserId] then return true end
        if AimbotTeamWhitelistEnabled and next(AimbotTeamWhitelist) ~= nil then
            if not target.Player.Team or not AimbotTeamWhitelist[target.Player.Team.Name] then return true end
         elseif ESP_SETTINGS.TeamCheck then
            if IsSameTeamTarget(target) then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    if target.Kind == "NPC" then
        if AimbotTeamWhitelistEnabled and next(AimbotTeamWhitelist) ~= nil then
            local npcTeam = GetNPCTeamName(target.Model)
            if not AimbotTeamWhitelist[npcTeam] then return true end
        elseif ESP_SETTINGS.TeamCheck then
            if IsSameTeamTarget(target) then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    return false
end

local function getTarget()
    local camera = GetCamera()
    if not camera then return nil end

    local now = tick()
    local center = camera.ViewportSize / 2
    local bestTarget = nil
    local bestDist = math.huge
    local candidates = BuildTargetCandidates()

    for _, target in ipairs(candidates) do
        if target and IsTargetKindAllowed(target.Kind) and IsTargetAlive(target) and not IsTargetRecentlySpawned(target) then
            if ESP_SETTINGS.IgnoreSpawnShield and HasSpawnShieldTarget(target) then continue end
            if ShouldSkipTarget(target) then continue end 

            local part = GetTargetAimPart(target)
            if part then
                local camPos = camera.CFrame.Position
                local dist3D = (part.Position - camPos).Magnitude
                local allowedDistance = target.Kind == "NPC" and NPCMaxDistance or PlayerMaxDistance
                if dist3D > allowedDistance then continue end

                if ESP_SETTINGS.WallCheck and not isVisibleTarget(target, part) then 
                    ResetVisibilityCache(target)
                    continue 
                end

                local pos, visible = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                if not visible then continue end
                local dist2D = (Vector2.new(pos.X, pos.Y) - center).Magnitude

                if dist2D <= FOV and dist2D < bestDist then
                     bestDist = dist2D
                    bestTarget = target
                end
            end
         end
    end

    if ESP_SETTINGS.ManualOverrideEnabled then
        CurrentTarget = bestTarget
        return bestTarget
    else
        if shouldForceSwitch(CurrentTarget) then
            CurrentTarget = bestTarget
            LastSwitchTime = now
            return bestTarget
        end
         if bestTarget then
            if now - LastSwitchTime < SWITCH_DELAY then return CurrentTarget end
            if CurrentTarget and not SameTarget(CurrentTarget, bestTarget) then
                local part = GetTargetAimPart(CurrentTarget)
                if part then
                     local pos, visible = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                    if visible then
                        local currentDist = (Vector2.new(pos.X, pos.Y) - center).Magnitude
                        if bestDist > currentDist * SWITCH_THRESHOLD then return CurrentTarget end
                    end
                end
            end
            CurrentTarget = bestTarget
            LastSwitchTime = now
            return bestTarget
        end
        return nil
    end
end

AimbotConnection = TrackConnection(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    local camera = GetCamera()
    if not camera then return end

    if ManualOverride then
        if tick() - LastManualMove >= ManualReleaseDelay then
            ManualOverride = false
        else
            return
        end
    end

    UpdateFOVCircle()

    if not AimbotEnabled then return end

    local target = getTarget()
    if target and target.Character then
        local part = GetTargetAimPart(target)
        if part then
            local aimPosition = GetPredictedAimPosition(target, part)
            if aimPosition then
                local camPos = camera.CFrame.Position
                local direction = aimPosition - camPos
                if direction.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                    local newCF = CFrame.new(camPos, camPos + direction.Unit)
                    if ESP_SETTINGS.SmoothAim then
                        camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame:Lerp(newCF, LerpAlpha)
                    else
                        camera.CFrame = newCF
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end))

Runtime.Cleanup = function()
    if AimbotConnection then
        pcall(function() AimbotConnection:Disconnect() end)
        AimbotConnection = nil
    end
    if AimbotQuickGui then
        SafeDestroy(AimbotQuickGui)
        AimbotQuickGui = nil
    end
    for _, conn in ipairs(Runtime.Connections) do
        pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
    end
    for _, thread in ipairs(Runtime.Threads) do
        pcall(function() task.cancel(thread) end)
    end
    Runtime.Connections = {}
    Runtime.Threads = {}
end

end)()
-- ====================================================================
-- 范围/Hitbox 终极完美整合版 (带UI自动记忆 + 目标分类Dropdown)
-- ====================================================================
;(function()
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

-- 0. 自动记忆配置文件路径 (保存在注入器的 workspace 目录下)
local configFileName = "Hitbox_DropdownMemory.json"

-- 1. 初始化全局与本地配置项
if not getgenv().HitboxConfig then
    getgenv().HitboxConfig = {
        Size = 10,
        Transparency = 0.7,
        Active = false,
        TeamCheck = false,
        Color = "红色",
        Material = "霓虹",
        RainbowColor = false,
        CheckCorpses = false,
        ShowOutline = false,
        EnableCollision = false,
        GlowEffect = false,
        PulseEffect = false,
        
        -- 新增：目标实体分类选项
        TargetEntity = "仅玩家", 
        TargetPart = "身体", 
        
        DefaultTransparency = 0,
        DefaultMaterial = "Plastic",
        UpdateInterval = 0.1,
        DefaultSizes = {
            ["Head"] = Vector3.new(1.2, 1.2, 1.2),
            ["HumanoidRootPart"] = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1)
        }
    }
end
local HitboxConfig = getgenv().HitboxConfig

-- 🔥 核心记忆功能：加载上次保存的下拉菜单选项
local function LoadMemory()
    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(configFileName) then
            local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(configFileName))
            if data.TargetPart then HitboxConfig.TargetPart = data.TargetPart end
            if data.TargetEntity then HitboxConfig.TargetEntity = data.TargetEntity end
        end
    end)
end
LoadMemory() -- 脚本运行第一时间读取记忆！

-- 🔥 核心记忆功能：保存当前下拉菜单选项
local function SaveMemory()
    pcall(function()
        if writefile then
            local data = {
                TargetPart = HitboxConfig.TargetPart,
                TargetEntity = HitboxConfig.TargetEntity
            }
            writefile(configFileName, HttpService:JSONEncode(data))
        end
    end)
end

-- 2. 核心映射表
local ColorMap = {
    ["红色"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0),
    ["蓝色"] = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 255),
    ["黄色"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0),
    ["绿色"] = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0),
    ["青色"] = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255),
    ["橙色"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 165, 0),
    ["紫色"] = Color3.fromRGB(128, 0, 128),
    ["白色"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
    ["黑色"] = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
    ["彩虹色"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
}

local MaterialMap = {
    ["霓虹"] = Enum.Material.Neon, ["塑料"] = Enum.Material.Plastic,
    ["木头"] = Enum.Material.Wood, ["石板"] = Enum.Material.Slate,
    ["混凝土"] = Enum.Material.Concrete, ["腐蚀金属"] = Enum.Material.CorrodedMetal,
    ["钻石板"] = Enum.Material.DiamondPlate, ["箔片"] = Enum.Material.Foil,
    ["草地"] = Enum.Material.Grass, ["冰"] = Enum.Material.Ice,
    ["大理石"] = Enum.Material.Marble, ["花岗岩"] = Enum.Material.Granite,
    ["砖块"] = Enum.Material.Brick, ["鹅卵石"] = Enum.Material.Pebble,
    ["沙子"] = Enum.Material.Sand, ["布料"] = Enum.Material.Fabric,
    ["光滑塑料"] = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, ["金属"] = Enum.Material.Metal,
    ["木板"] = Enum.Material.WoodPlanks, ["圆石"] = Enum.Material.Cobblestone
}

local PartMap = {
    ["头"] = "Head",
    ["身体"] = "HumanoidRootPart"
}

local playerCharacters = {}
local npcCharacters = {}
local loopRunning = false 

-- 3. 辅助功能函数
local function getRainbowColor()
    local hue = (tick() % 5) / 5
    return Color3.fromHSV(hue, 1, 1)
end

local function getPulseSize(baseSize)
    if not HitboxConfig.PulseEffect then return baseSize end
    local pulse = math.sin(tick() * 5) * 0.15 + 1
    return baseSize * pulse
end

local function isCorpse(character)
    if not character or not character.Parent then return true end
    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    return not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0
end

-- 4. 核心单体处理逻辑
local function modifyHitbox(character, resetMode)
    if not character or not character.Parent then return end
    
    pcall(function()
        local activePartName = PartMap[HitboxConfig.TargetPart] or "HumanoidRootPart"

        for _, partName in ipairs({"Head", "HumanoidRootPart"}) do
            local part = character:FindFirstChild(partName)
            if part then
                if HitboxConfig.Active and not resetMode and partName == activePartName then
                    local currentSize = getPulseSize(HitboxConfig.Size)
                    part.Size = Vector3.new(currentSize, currentSize, currentSize)
                    part.Transparency = HitboxConfig.Transparency
                    part.CanCollide = HitboxConfig.EnableCollision
                    part.Material = MaterialMap[HitboxConfig.Material] or Enum.Material.Neon
                    
                    local targetColor = HitboxConfig.RainbowColor and getRainbowColor() or (ColorMap[HitboxConfig.Color] or Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0))
                    part.Color = targetColor

                    if HitboxConfig.ShowOutline then
                        local highlight = part:FindFirstChild("HitboxHighlight") or Instance.new("Highlight")
                        highlight.Name = "HitboxHighlight"
                        highlight.FillTransparency = 1
                        highlight.OutlineColor = targetColor
                        highlight.OutlineTransparency = HitboxConfig.Transparency
                        highlight.Parent = part
                    else
                        local highlight = part:FindFirstChild("HitboxHighlight")
                        if highlight then highlight:Destroy() end
                    end

                    if HitboxConfig.GlowEffect then
                        local pointLight = part:FindFirstChild("HitboxLight") or Instance.new("PointLight")
                        pointLight.Name = "HitboxLight"
                        pointLight.Brightness = 5
                        pointLight.Range = 15
                        pointLight.Color = targetColor
                        pointLight.Parent = part
                    else
                        local pointLight = part:FindFirstChild("HitboxLight")
                        if pointLight then pointLight:Destroy() end
                    end
                else
                    part.Size = HitboxConfig.DefaultSizes[partName] or (partName == "Head" and Vector3.new(1.2, 1.2, 1.2) or Vector3.new(2, 2, 1))
                    part.Transparency = (partName == "HumanoidRootPart") and 1 or 0
                    part.Material = Enum.Material[HitboxConfig.DefaultMaterial] or Enum.Material.Plastic
                    part.CanCollide = true
                    
                    local highlight = part:FindFirstChild("HitboxHighlight")
                    if highlight then highlight:Destroy() end
                    local pointLight = part:FindFirstChild("HitboxLight")
                    if pointLight then pointLight:Destroy() end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- 5. 高性能环境扫描
local function scanForNPCs()
    -- 如果目标不包含NPC，彻底停止扫描并清理缓存
    if HitboxConfig.TargetEntity == "仅玩家" then 
        return 
    end
    
    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("Model") and obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
            if not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(obj) then
                npcCharacters[obj] = obj
            end
        end
    end
    for char, _ in pairs(npcCharacters) do
        if not char or not char.Parent or not char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
            npcCharacters[char] = nil
        end
    end
end

local function UpdateHitboxes(resetMode)
    -- 清算玩家
    for player, character in pairs(playerCharacters) do
        if player == Players.LocalPlayer then continue end
        
        -- 如果目标选了“仅NPC”，强行将所有玩家重置还原
        if resetMode or HitboxConfig.TargetEntity == "仅NPC" then
            modifyHitbox(character, true)
            continue
        end

        if HitboxConfig.TeamCheck and player.Team == Players.LocalPlayer.Team then
            modifyHitbox(character, true)
            continue
        end
        if HitboxConfig.CheckCorpses and isCorpse(character) then
            modifyHitbox(character, true)
            continue
        end

        modifyHitbox(character, false)
    end
    
    -- 清算NPC
    for character, _ in pairs(npcCharacters) do
        -- 如果目标选了“仅玩家”，强行将所有NPC重置还原
        if resetMode or HitboxConfig.TargetEntity == "仅玩家" then
            modifyHitbox(character, true)
            continue
        end
        if HitboxConfig.CheckCorpses and isCorpse(character) then
            modifyHitbox(character, true)
            continue
        end
        modifyHitbox(character, false)
    end
end

local function ResetAllHitboxes()
    UpdateHitboxes(true)
    for character, _ in pairs(npcCharacters) do
        modifyHitbox(character, true)
    end
end

-- 6. 生命周期钩子
local function onCharacterAdded(player, character)
    playerCharacters[player] = character
    if HitboxConfig.Active then
        modifyHitbox(character, false)
    end
end

local function onPlayerAdded(player)
    player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function(oldCharacter)
        pcall(function() modifyHitbox(oldCharacter, true) end)
    end)
    player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
        onCharacterAdded(player, character)
    end)
    if player.Character then
        task.spawn(onCharacterAdded, player, player.Character)
    end
end

for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    task.spawn(onPlayerAdded, player)
end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(onPlayerAdded)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
    if playerCharacters[player] then
        pcall(modifyHitbox, playerCharacters[player], true)
    end
    playerCharacters[player] = nil
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        if HitboxConfig.Active then
            scanForNPCs()
        end
        task.wait(2.5)
    end
end)

local function startHitboxLoop()
    if loopRunning then return end
    loopRunning = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while HitboxConfig.Active do
            UpdateHitboxes(false)
            task.wait(HitboxConfig.UpdateInterval or 0.1)
        end
        loopRunning = false
        ResetAllHitboxes()
    end)
end
