            setStatus("雷达寻路失败：找不到 HumanoidRootPart。")
            return false
        end

        local speed = getQuickMoveSpeed()
        local rayCount = normalizeRadarRayCount(RadarRayCount)
        local startTime = os.clock()

        local planner = createRadarPlanner(root.Position, target, rayCount)
        local route = nil
        local routeIndex = 1

        local hoverPosition = root.Position

        local lastStatusTime = 0
        local lastPartialRouteTime = 0
        local lastReplanTime = 0
        local lastClearCheckTime = 0
        local lastProgressTime = os.clock()
        local lastWaypointDistance = nil
        local lastProbeVisualTime = 0
        local lastProbeStatusTime = 0
        local lastTargetProgressTime = os.clock()
        local lastTargetDistance = (target - root.Position).Magnitude
        local plannerRouteApplied = false
        local lastPortalScanTime = 0
        local cachedPortalWaypoint = nil
        local cachedPortalOrigin = nil
        local cachedPortalConfidence = 0
        local cachedPortalSqueezeOnly = false
        local lastPhysicalPosition = root.Position
        local lastPhysicalMoveTime = os.clock()
        local lastWakeNudgeTime = 0

        local activeMoveDirection = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        local activeMoveTarget = nil
        local lastRenderMoveToTime = 0

        local committedPortalWaypoint = nil
        local committedPortalConfidence = 0
        local committedPortalStartedAt = 0
        local committedPortalUntil = 0
        local committedPortalStartPosition = nil

        local squeezeAttempt = nil
        local squeezeBlacklist = {}

        local expansionsPerFrame
        if rayCount >= 256 then
            expansionsPerFrame = 4
        elseif rayCount >= 128 then
            expansionsPerFrame = 7
        else
            expansionsPerFrame = 10
        end

        local function holdRootAtPosition(rootPart, position)
            activeMoveDirection = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            activeMoveTarget = nil
            setRadarSqueezeAutoRotateDisabled(false)
            applySmoothTeleportCFrame(rootPart, position)
        end

        local function resetProgressWatch()
            lastProgressTime = os.clock()
            lastWaypointDistance = nil
        end

        local function getPositionBucketKey(position)
            return string.format(
                "%d,%d,%d",
                roundToGrid(position.X / 5),
                roundToGrid(position.Y / 5),
                roundToGrid(position.Z / 5)
            )
        end

        local function blacklistSqueezePosition(position, duration)
            if typeof(position) ~= "Vector3" then
                return
            end

            squeezeBlacklist[getPositionBucketKey(position)] = os.clock() + (tonumber(duration) or 8)
        end

        local function isSqueezePositionBlacklisted(position)
            if typeof(position) ~= "Vector3" then
                return false
            end

            local key = getPositionBucketKey(position)
            local expiresAt = squeezeBlacklist[key]

            if expiresAt and expiresAt > os.clock() then
                return true
            end

            squeezeBlacklist[key] = nil
            return false
        end

        local function clearPortalCommitment()
            committedPortalWaypoint = nil
            committedPortalConfidence = 0
            committedPortalStartedAt = 0
            committedPortalUntil = 0
            committedPortalStartPosition = nil
        end

        local function commitPortalWaypoint(position, confidence, currentPosition)
            if typeof(position) ~= "Vector3" then
                return
            end

            local now = os.clock()
            committedPortalWaypoint = position
            committedPortalConfidence = tonumber(confidence) or 0
            committedPortalStartedAt = now
            committedPortalUntil = now + 7
            committedPortalStartPosition = currentPosition
        end

        local function isPortalCommitmentActive(currentPosition)
            if not committedPortalWaypoint then
                return false
            end

            local now = os.clock()

            if (committedPortalWaypoint - currentPosition).Magnitude <= 4.2 then
                clearPortalCommitment()
                return false
            end

            if now > committedPortalUntil then
                if committedPortalStartPosition
                    and (currentPosition - committedPortalStartPosition).Magnitude < 3
                then
                    blacklistSqueezePosition(committedPortalWaypoint, 10)
                end

                clearPortalCommitment()
                return false
            end

            return true
        end

        local function routesEquivalent(firstRoute, secondRoute)
            if type(firstRoute) ~= "table" or type(secondRoute) ~= "table" then
                return false
            end

            if #firstRoute == 0 or #secondRoute == 0 then
                return false
            end

            if #firstRoute <= 2 and #secondRoute <= 2 then
                return (firstRoute[#firstRoute] - secondRoute[#secondRoute]).Magnitude <= 1.25
            end

            if #firstRoute ~= #secondRoute then
                return false
            end

            for index = 1, #firstRoute do
                if (firstRoute[index] - secondRoute[index]).Magnitude > 1.5 then
                    return false
                end
            end

            return true
        end

        local function setRoute(newRoute, currentPosition, forceReplace)
            if type(newRoute) ~= "table" or #newRoute == 0 then
                return false
            end

            if forceReplace ~= true
                and route
                and routeIndex <= #route
                and routesEquivalent(route, newRoute)
            then
                return true
            end

            route = newRoute
            hoverPosition = nil
            squeezeAttempt = nil
            setRadarSqueezeAutoRotateDisabled(false)
            resetProgressWatch()

            local closestIndex = 1
            local closestDistance = math.huge

            for index, routePoint in ipairs(route) do
                local distance = (routePoint - currentPosition).Magnitude
                if distance < closestDistance then
                    closestDistance = distance
                    closestIndex = index
                end
            end

            local advanceTolerance = math.max(4, getRadarCellSize(rayCount) * 1.15)
            if closestIndex < #route and closestDistance <= advanceTolerance then
                routeIndex = closestIndex + 1
            else
                routeIndex = closestIndex
            end

            return true
        end

        local function applyRadarMovementStep(rootPart, desiredPosition, movementOffset, waypoint, aggressive, squeezeMode)
            local flatDirection = Vector3.new(movementOffset.X, 0, movementOffset.Z)
            if flatDirection.Magnitude > 0.05 then
                flatDirection = flatDirection.Unit
            else
                flatDirection = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end

            activeMoveDirection = flatDirection
            activeMoveTarget = waypoint
            setRadarSqueezeAutoRotateDisabled(squeezeMode == true)

            local lookVector = nil
            if squeezeMode and flatDirection.Magnitude > 0.05 then
                lookVector = Vector3.new(-flatDirection.Z, 0, flatDirection.X)
            end

            local finalPosition = applySmoothTeleportCFrame(rootPart, desiredPosition, {
                lookVector = lookVector,
            })

            wakeHumanoidMovement(movementOffset, false, waypoint)

            if aggressive and not squeezeMode and finalPosition then
                local character = LocalPlayer.Character

                pcall(function()
                    if character and character.Parent then
                        local facing = flatDirection.Magnitude > 0.05 and flatDirection or rootPart.CFrame.LookVector
                        character:PivotTo(CFrame.lookAt(finalPosition, finalPosition + facing))
                    end
                end)

                pcall(function()
                    if flatDirection.Magnitude > 0.05 then
                        rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = flatDirection * math.clamp(speed * 0.10, 2, 12)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end

        local function resetPlanner(currentPosition, keepRoute)
            if keepRoute ~= true then
                hoverPosition = currentPosition
                route = nil
                routeIndex = 1
                squeezeAttempt = nil
                setRadarSqueezeAutoRotateDisabled(false)
            end

            planner = createRadarPlanner(currentPosition, target, rayCount)
            plannerRouteApplied = false
            lastPartialRouteTime = os.clock()
            resetProgressWatch()
        end

        local radarRenderStepName = "WindUICoordTeleportRadarMove"
        Env.__WindUICoordTeleportRadarRenderStepName = radarRenderStepName

        pcall(function()
            RunService:BindToRenderStep(
                radarRenderStepName,
                Enum.RenderPriority.Character.Value + 2,
                function()
                    if Env.__WindUICoordTeleportRadarRenderStepName ~= radarRenderStepName then
                        return
                    end

                    local humanoid = getHumanoid(false)
                    if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                        return
                    end

                    pcall(function()
                        humanoid.Sit = false
                        humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                        humanoid:Move(activeMoveDirection, false)
                    end)

                    local now = os.clock()
                    if activeMoveTarget and now - lastRenderMoveToTime >= 0.12 then
                        lastRenderMoveToTime = now
                        pcall(function()
                            humanoid:MoveTo(activeMoveTarget)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            )
        end)

        local function markProbe(position, color, size, lifetime)
            pcall(function()
                local part = Instance.new("Part")
                part.Name = "CoordRadarProbe"
                part.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
                part.Size = Vector3.new(size, size, size)
                part.CFrame = CFrame.new(position)
                part.Anchored = true
                part.CanCollide = false
                part.CanTouch = false
                part.CanQuery = false
                part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                part.Color = color
                part.Transparency = 0.45
                part.Parent = workspace

                local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")
                Debris:AddItem(part, lifetime)
            end)
        end

        local function findSmartPortalWaypoint(currentPosition, forceWideProbe, makeVisuals)
            local now = os.clock()

            if cachedPortalWaypoint
                and cachedPortalOrigin
                and now - lastPortalScanTime < 0.7
                and (currentPosition - cachedPortalOrigin).Magnitude < 8
                and not isSqueezePositionBlacklisted(cachedPortalWaypoint)
            then
                return cachedPortalWaypoint, cachedPortalConfidence, cachedPortalSqueezeOnly
            end

            lastPortalScanTime = now
            cachedPortalOrigin = currentPosition
            cachedPortalWaypoint = nil
            cachedPortalConfidence = 0
            cachedPortalSqueezeOnly = false

            local raycastParams = makeRaycastParams()
            local baseDirection = getFlatDirection(currentPosition, target)
            local radius = getDynamicAgentDimensions()
            local laneOffset = math.clamp((tonumber(radius) or 2) * 1.35, 2.4, 5.5)
            local sampleCount = forceWideProbe and 24 or 16
            local travelDistances = forceWideProbe and { 8, 14, 22, 32, 44 } or { 8, 14, 22, 30 }
            local verticalOffsets = forceWideProbe and { 0, 5, -5 } or { 0 }
            local currentTargetDistance = (target - currentPosition).Magnitude
            local currentOpenCount, currentOpenTotal = measureOpenSpace(currentPosition, rayCount, raycastParams)
            local currentOpennessRatio = currentOpenTotal > 0 and (currentOpenCount / currentOpenTotal) or 0
            local currentSkyOpen = hasOpenSkyAbove(currentPosition, raycastParams)
            local likelyInside = not currentSkyOpen and currentOpennessRatio < 0.72
            local preliminary = {}

            -- 每个方位至少保留一个候选，背后的门不能在完整检查前就被目标距离淘汰。
            for i = 1, sampleCount do
                local angle = math.pi * 2 * ((i - 1) / sampleCount)
                local direction = rotateFlatDirection(baseDirection, angle)

                if direction.Magnitude > 0.05 then
                    direction = direction.Unit
                    local bestDirectionalItem = nil
                    local bestDirectionalScore = math.huge

                    for _, travelDistance in ipairs(travelDistances) do
                        for _, yOffset in ipairs(verticalOffsets) do
                            local candidate = currentPosition
                                + direction * travelDistance
                                + Vector3.new(0, yOffset, 0)

                            if not isSqueezePositionBlacklisted(candidate) then
                                local normalLightClear = isLightSegmentClearForBody(
                                    currentPosition,
                                    candidate,
                                    raycastParams
                                )

                                local squeezeLightClear = false
                                if not normalLightClear then
                                    squeezeLightClear = isLightSegmentClearForBody(
                                        currentPosition,
                                        candidate,
                                        raycastParams,
                                        RADAR_SQUEEZE_BODY_SCALE
                                    )
                                end

                                if normalLightClear or squeezeLightClear then
                                    local passThroughCount, namedPortalCount = getPassThroughSegmentInfo(
                                        currentPosition,
                                        candidate,
                                        raycastParams
                                    )

                                    local quickScore = -travelDistance * 0.42
                                        - math.min(passThroughCount, 3) * 22
                                        - namedPortalCount * 90
                                        + math.abs(yOffset) * 0.65
                                        + (squeezeLightClear and not normalLightClear and 15 or 0)

                                    if quickScore < bestDirectionalScore then
                                        bestDirectionalScore = quickScore
                                        bestDirectionalItem = {
                                            position = candidate,
                                            direction = direction,
                                            angle = angle,
                                            yOffset = yOffset,
                                            travelDistance = travelDistance,
                                            passThroughCount = passThroughCount,
                                            namedPortalCount = namedPortalCount,
                                            squeezeOnly = squeezeLightClear and not normalLightClear,
                                            score = quickScore,
                                        }
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    if bestDirectionalItem then
                        table.insert(preliminary, bestDirectionalItem)
                    end
                end
            end

            local bestPosition = nil
            local bestScore = math.huge
            local bestConfidence = 0
            local bestSqueezeOnly = false

            for _, item in ipairs(preliminary) do
                local candidate = item.position
                local normalClear = isSegmentClearForBody(
                    currentPosition,
                    candidate,
                    rayCount,
                    raycastParams
                )

                local squeezeClear = false
                if not normalClear and item.squeezeOnly then
                    squeezeClear = isSegmentClearForBody(
                        currentPosition,
                        candidate,
                        rayCount,
                        raycastParams,
                        RADAR_SQUEEZE_BODY_SCALE
                    )
                end

                if normalClear or squeezeClear then
                    local direction = item.direction
                    local rightDirection = Vector3.new(-direction.Z, 0, direction.X)
                    local openCount, openTotal = measureOpenSpace(candidate, rayCount, raycastParams)
                    local skyOpen = hasOpenSkyAbove(candidate, raycastParams)

                    local leftOrigin = currentPosition + rightDirection * laneOffset
                    local rightOrigin = currentPosition - rightDirection * laneOffset
                    local leftTarget = candidate + rightDirection * laneOffset
                    local rightTarget = candidate - rightDirection * laneOffset

                    local leftBlocked = not isLightSegmentClearForBody(leftOrigin, leftTarget, raycastParams)
                    local rightBlocked = not isLightSegmentClearForBody(rightOrigin, rightTarget, raycastParams)
                    local frameScore = (leftBlocked and 1 or 0) + (rightBlocked and 1 or 0)
                    local opennessRatio = openTotal > 0 and (openCount / openTotal) or 0
                    local opennessGain = opennessRatio - currentOpennessRatio
                    local candidateTargetDistance = (target - candidate).Magnitude
                    local targetDetour = math.max(0, candidateTargetDistance - currentTargetDistance)
                    local skyTransition = skyOpen and not currentSkyOpen
                    local doorwayEvidence = frameScore > 0
                        or item.passThroughCount > 0
                        or item.namedPortalCount > 0
                    local exitTransition = skyTransition or opennessGain >= 0.16

                    local confidence = openCount
                        + math.max(0, opennessGain) * 12
                        + (skyTransition and 7 or (skyOpen and 2 or 0))
                        + frameScore * 2.5
                        + math.min(item.passThroughCount, 2) * 2.5
                        + item.namedPortalCount * 5
                        - (squeezeClear and not normalClear and 1.5 or 0)

                    if confidence >= 4
                        and (doorwayEvidence or exitTransition or opennessRatio >= 0.68)
                    then
                        local detourWeight = likelyInside and 0.08 or 0.45
                        local score = item.travelDistance * 0.18
                            + targetDetour * detourWeight
                            - confidence * 18
                            - math.max(0, opennessGain) * 70
                            - (skyTransition and 110 or 0)
                            - frameScore * 24
                            - math.min(item.passThroughCount, 3) * 24
                            - item.namedPortalCount * 95
                            + math.abs(item.yOffset) * 0.55
                            + (squeezeClear and not normalClear and 22 or 0)

                        if score < bestScore then
                            bestScore = score
                            bestPosition = candidate
                            bestConfidence = confidence
                            bestSqueezeOnly = squeezeClear and not normalClear
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            if bestPosition then
                cachedPortalWaypoint = bestPosition
                cachedPortalConfidence = bestConfidence
                cachedPortalSqueezeOnly = bestSqueezeOnly

                if makeVisuals then
                    local color = bestSqueezeOnly
                        and Color3.fromRGB(255, 145, 70)
                        or Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 70)
                    markProbe(bestPosition, color, 5, 1.8)
                end

                if bestConfidence >= 8 and os.clock() - lastProbeStatusTime >= 0.9 then
                    lastProbeStatusTime = os.clock()
                    setStatus(
                        bestSqueezeOnly
                            and "雷达发现疑似窄门/出口，准备侧身尝试通过。"
                            or "雷达识别到高可信度门洞/建筑出口，正在优先穿过。"
                    )
                end
            end

            return bestPosition, bestConfidence, bestSqueezeOnly
        end

        local function chooseProbeWaypoint(currentPosition, blockedWaypoint, forceWideProbe, makeVisuals)
            local raycastParams = makeRaycastParams()
            local candidates = {}
            local baseDir = getFlatDirection(currentPosition, target)
            local rightDir = Vector3.new(-baseDir.Z, 0, baseDir.X)

            local smartPortalWaypoint = nil
            local smartPortalConfidence = 0
            local smartPortalSqueezeOnly = false

            if forceWideProbe or blockedWaypoint ~= nil then
                smartPortalWaypoint, smartPortalConfidence, smartPortalSqueezeOnly = findSmartPortalWaypoint(
                    currentPosition,
                    forceWideProbe,
                    makeVisuals
                )
            end

            local targetVerticalNeed = target.Y - currentPosition.Y
            local targetIsAbove = targetVerticalNeed > 14
            local upwardCheckHeight = math.clamp(targetVerticalNeed, 18, 90)
            local upwardBlocked = false

            if targetIsAbove then
                upwardBlocked = not isSegmentClearForBody(
                    currentPosition,
                    currentPosition + Vector3.new(0, upwardCheckHeight, 0),
                    rayCount,
                    raycastParams
                )
            end

            local shouldSearchLowerExit = forceWideProbe == true and targetIsAbove and upwardBlocked

            local function addCandidate(position, kind, squeezeOnly)
                if typeof(position) ~= "Vector3" then
                    return
                end

                if (position - currentPosition).Magnitude < 5 then
                    return
                end

                if isSqueezePositionBlacklisted(position) then
                    return
                end

                table.insert(candidates, {
                    position = position,
                    kind = kind or "probe",
                    squeezeOnly = squeezeOnly == true,
                })
            end

            if smartPortalWaypoint then
                addCandidate(smartPortalWaypoint, "portal", smartPortalSqueezeOnly)
            end

            local function addRing(center, radii, heights, stepCount)
                for _, radius in ipairs(radii) do
                    for i = 1, stepCount do
                        local angle = math.pi * 2 * ((i - 1) / stepCount)
                        local dir = rotateFlatDirection(Vector3.new(1, 0, 0), angle)

                        for _, yOffset in ipairs(heights) do
                            addCandidate(center + dir * radius + Vector3.new(0, yOffset, 0))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            local shortRadii = { 10, 16, 24, 36 }
            local shortHeights = { 0, 6, -6, 12, -12 }

            for _, radius in ipairs(shortRadii) do
                addCandidate(currentPosition + baseDir * radius)
                addCandidate(currentPosition + rightDir * radius)
                addCandidate(currentPosition - rightDir * radius)
                addCandidate(currentPosition - baseDir * radius)

                for _, height in ipairs(shortHeights) do
                    addCandidate(currentPosition + baseDir * radius + Vector3.new(0, height, 0))
                    addCandidate(currentPosition + rightDir * radius + Vector3.new(0, height, 0))
                    addCandidate(currentPosition - rightDir * radius + Vector3.new(0, height, 0))
                    addCandidate(currentPosition - baseDir * radius + Vector3.new(0, height, 0))
                end
            end

            if forceWideProbe then
                addCandidate(currentPosition + Vector3.new(0, 32, 0))
                addCandidate(currentPosition + Vector3.new(0, 56, 0))
                addCandidate(currentPosition + rightDir * 48 + Vector3.new(0, 24, 0))
                addCandidate(currentPosition - rightDir * 48 + Vector3.new(0, 24, 0))
                addCandidate(currentPosition - baseDir * 48 + Vector3.new(0, 24, 0))

                addRing(target, { 28, 44, 68, 96 }, { 0, 14, 28, 44, 68, -12, -28 }, 12)
                addRing(currentPosition, { 40, 64, 92 }, { 18, 36, 60, -18 }, 12)

                if shouldSearchLowerExit then
                    addCandidate(currentPosition + Vector3.new(0, -18, 0))
                    addCandidate(currentPosition + Vector3.new(0, -36, 0))
                    addCandidate(currentPosition + Vector3.new(0, -56, 0))

                    addCandidate(currentPosition + rightDir * 48 + Vector3.new(0, -18, 0))
                    addCandidate(currentPosition - rightDir * 48 + Vector3.new(0, -18, 0))
                    addCandidate(currentPosition - baseDir * 48 + Vector3.new(0, -18, 0))

                    addCandidate(currentPosition + rightDir * 72 + Vector3.new(0, -36, 0))
                    addCandidate(currentPosition - rightDir * 72 + Vector3.new(0, -36, 0))
                    addCandidate(currentPosition - baseDir * 72 + Vector3.new(0, -36, 0))

                    addRing(currentPosition, { 48, 76, 108, 144 }, { -56, -36, -18, 0, 18 }, 14)
                    addRing(target, { 52, 84, 120, 160 }, { -96, -72, -48, -24, 0, 24 }, 14)
                end
            end

            local bestPosition = nil
            local bestScore = math.huge
            local bestKind = nil
            local bestSqueezeOnly = false
            local visualCount = 0
            local canUseVisuals = makeVisuals == true and forceWideProbe == true

            for _, candidateData in ipairs(candidates) do
                local candidate = candidateData.position
                local normalClear = isPointOpenForBody(candidate)
                    and isSegmentClearForBody(currentPosition, candidate, rayCount, raycastParams)

                local squeezeClear = false
                if not normalClear then
                    squeezeClear = isPointOpenForBody(candidate, RADAR_SQUEEZE_BODY_SCALE)
                        and isSegmentClearForBody(
                            currentPosition,
                            candidate,
                            rayCount,
                            raycastParams,
                            RADAR_SQUEEZE_BODY_SCALE
                        )
                end

                if normalClear or squeezeClear then
                    local score = (candidate - target).Magnitude
                    local lateralMove = Vector3.new(
                        candidate.X - currentPosition.X,
                        0,
                        candidate.Z - currentPosition.Z
                    ).Magnitude
                    local verticalMove = math.abs(candidate.Y - currentPosition.Y)

                    score = score + lateralMove * 0.04 + verticalMove * 0.08

                    if candidateData.kind == "portal" then
                        score = score - 260 - smartPortalConfidence * 11
                    end

                    if squeezeClear and not normalClear then
                        score = score + 48
                    end

                    if isPointOpenForBody(target)
                        and isSegmentClearForBody(candidate, target, rayCount, raycastParams)
                    then
                        score = score - 260
                    end

                    if blockedWaypoint then
                        score = score + math.max(0, 26 - (candidate - blockedWaypoint).Magnitude) * 4.5
                    end

                    local candidateVertical = candidate.Y - currentPosition.Y

                    if shouldSearchLowerExit then
                        if candidateVertical > 12 then
                            score = score + 95
                        elseif candidateVertical < -8 then
                            score = score - 70
                        end

                        if lateralMove > 24 then
                            score = score - math.min(55, lateralMove * 0.22)
                        end
                    else
                        if math.abs(targetVerticalNeed) > 10 and candidateVertical * targetVerticalNeed > 0 then
                            score = score - 28
                        end

                        if forceWideProbe and candidate.Y > currentPosition.Y + 18 then
                            score = score - 18
                        end
                    end

                    if score < bestScore then
                        bestScore = score
                        bestPosition = candidate
                        bestKind = candidateData.kind
                        bestSqueezeOnly = squeezeClear and not normalClear
                    end

                    if canUseVisuals and visualCount < 8 then
                        visualCount = visualCount + 1
                        markProbe(
                            candidate,
                            squeezeClear and not normalClear
                                and Color3.fromRGB(255, 145, 70)
                                or Color3.fromRGB(80, 180, 255),
                            2.6,
                            1.2
                        )
                    end
                end
            end

            if bestPosition and canUseVisuals then
                local color = bestKind == "portal"
                    and Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 70)
                    or Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 120)
                markProbe(bestPosition, color, 4.2, 1.5)
            end

            return bestPosition, bestKind, bestSqueezeOnly, smartPortalConfidence
        end

        local function chooseEscapeWaypoint(currentPosition, blockedWaypoint, forceWideProbe)
            local probe, probeKind, probeSqueezeOnly, portalConfidence = chooseProbeWaypoint(
                currentPosition,
                blockedWaypoint,
                forceWideProbe,
                forceWideProbe
            )

            if probe then
                if probeKind == "portal" then
                    commitPortalWaypoint(probe, portalConfidence, currentPosition)
                end

                return probe, probeKind, probeSqueezeOnly
            end

            local raycastParams = makeRaycastParams()
            local baseDir = getFlatDirection(currentPosition, target)

            local angles = {
                math.rad(45), math.rad(-45),
                math.rad(75), math.rad(-75),
                math.rad(105), math.rad(-105),
                math.rad(135), math.rad(-135),
                math.rad(180),
            }

            local radii = forceWideProbe and { 20, 36, 56, 84 } or { 12, 20, 32, 48 }
            local heights = forceWideProbe and { 0, 10, -10, 24, -24, 42, 64 } or { 0, 6, -6, 12, -12, 20 }

            local bestPosition = nil
            local bestScore = math.huge
            local bestSqueezeOnly = false

            for _, radius in ipairs(radii) do
                for _, angle in ipairs(angles) do
                    local dir = rotateFlatDirection(baseDir, angle)

                    if dir.Magnitude > 0.05 then
                        dir = dir.Unit

                        for _, yOffset in ipairs(heights) do
                            local candidate = currentPosition + dir * radius + Vector3.new(0, yOffset, 0)

                            if not isSqueezePositionBlacklisted(candidate) then
                                local normalClear = isSegmentClearForBody(
                                    currentPosition,
                                    candidate,
                                    rayCount,
                                    raycastParams
                                )

                                local squeezeClear = false
                                if not normalClear then
                                    squeezeClear = isSegmentClearForBody(
                                        currentPosition,
                                        candidate,
                                        rayCount,
                                        raycastParams,
                                        RADAR_SQUEEZE_BODY_SCALE
                                    )
                                end

                                if normalClear or squeezeClear then
                                    local distanceScore = (candidate - target).Magnitude
                                    local heightScore = math.abs(yOffset) * 0.35
                                    local radiusScore = radius * 0.06
                                    local angleScore = math.abs(angle) * 1.1

                                    local blockedPenalty = 0
                                    if blockedWaypoint then
                                        blockedPenalty = math.max(0, 18 - (candidate - blockedWaypoint).Magnitude) * 3
                                    end

                                    local score = distanceScore
                                        + heightScore
                                        + radiusScore
                                        + angleScore
                                        + blockedPenalty
                                        + (squeezeClear and not normalClear and 45 or 0)

                                    if score < bestScore then
                                        bestScore = score
                                        bestPosition = candidate
                                        bestSqueezeOnly = squeezeClear and not normalClear
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            return bestPosition, "escape", bestSqueezeOnly
        end

        local function popupWithHover(currentRoot, title, desc)
            local popupHoverPosition = currentRoot and currentRoot.Position or hoverPosition or target

            stopQuickMove()

            showBlockPopup(
                target,
                pointName,
                finalPointData,
                title,
                desc
            )

            startPopupHover(popupHoverPosition, options.useNoclip == true)
        end

        local function setProbeRoute(currentPosition, blockedWaypoint, forceWideProbe, reason)
            local makeVisuals = forceWideProbe and os.clock() - lastProbeVisualTime >= 1.1
            if makeVisuals then
                lastProbeVisualTime = os.clock()
            end

            local probe, probeKind, probeSqueezeOnly, portalConfidence = chooseProbeWaypoint(
                currentPosition,
                blockedWaypoint,
                forceWideProbe,
                makeVisuals
            )

            if probe then
                if probeKind == "portal" then
                    commitPortalWaypoint(probe, portalConfidence, currentPosition)
                end

                setRoute({ currentPosition, probe }, currentPosition)

                if probeSqueezeOnly then
                    squeezeAttempt = {
                        waypoint = probe,
                        startedAt = os.clock(),
                        lastProgressAt = os.clock(),
                        bestDistance = (probe - currentPosition).Magnitude,
                    }
                end

                if os.clock() - lastProbeStatusTime >= 0.9 then
                    lastProbeStatusTime = os.clock()
                    setStatus(reason or "雷达探测到安全点，继续流畅移动。")
                end

                return true
            end

            return false
        end

        setStatus(string.format(
            "雷达寻路启动：%s | 射线：%d | 连续探测移动已启用",
            tostring(pointName),
            rayCount
        ))

        Env.__WindUICoordTeleportMoveConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
            local currentRoot = getRoot(false)
            if not currentRoot then
                stopQuickMove()
                setStatus("雷达寻路已停止：找不到 HumanoidRootPart。")
                return
            end

            if options.useNoclip == true then
                enableCharacterNoclip()
            end

            local now = os.clock()
            local currentPosition = currentRoot.Position
            local distanceToTarget = (target - currentPosition).Magnitude

            if (currentPosition - lastPhysicalPosition).Magnitude >= 0.12 then
                lastPhysicalPosition = currentPosition
                lastPhysicalMoveTime = now
            end

            if distanceToTarget < lastTargetDistance - 1.8 then
                lastTargetDistance = distanceToTarget
                lastTargetProgressTime = now
            end

            local previousCommittedPortal = committedPortalWaypoint
            local previousPortalDistance = previousCommittedPortal
                and (previousCommittedPortal - currentPosition).Magnitude
                or math.huge
            local portalCommitActive = isPortalCommitmentActive(currentPosition)

            if previousCommittedPortal and not portalCommitActive then
                cachedPortalWaypoint = nil
                cachedPortalOrigin = nil
                squeezeAttempt = nil
                lastTargetDistance = distanceToTarget
                lastTargetProgressTime = now
                resetPlanner(currentPosition, false)

                if previousPortalDistance <= 4.2 then
                    setStatus("雷达已穿过门洞/出口，正在从出口位置重新规划到目标。")
                end
            end

            local forceWideProbe = portalCommitActive
                or (now - startTime >= 5)
                or (now - lastTargetProgressTime >= 2.2)

            if distanceToTarget <= 3 then
                applySmoothTeleportCFrame(currentRoot, target)
                stopQuickMove()
                setStatus("雷达寻路完成：" .. tostring(pointName) .. " | " .. formatPoint(finalPointData))
                return
            end

            if now - startTime >= 40 then
                setStatus("雷达寻路超过 40 秒，已暂停并等待玩家选择。")
                popupWithHover(
                    currentRoot,
                    "⚠️ 雷达寻路超时",
                    "雷达已尝试连续探测超过 40 秒。可以选择穿墙强行前往，或者交由人工操作接管。"
                )
                return
            end

            local raycastParams = makeRaycastParams()

            if portalCommitActive and committedPortalWaypoint then
                if not route
                    or not route[#route]
                    or (route[#route] - committedPortalWaypoint).Magnitude > 1.5
                then
                    setRoute({ currentPosition, committedPortalWaypoint }, currentPosition, true)
                end
            elseif isPointOpenForBody(target)
                and isSegmentClearForBody(currentPosition, target, rayCount, raycastParams)
            then
                setRoute({ currentPosition, target }, currentPosition)
            end

            if planner and not planner.finished and not planner.failed then
                planner:step(expansionsPerFrame)
            end

            if not portalCommitActive
                and planner
                and planner.finished
                and planner.route
                and #planner.route > 0
            then
                if not plannerRouteApplied then
                    plannerRouteApplied = setRoute(planner.route, currentPosition)

                    if plannerRouteApplied then
                        setStatus(string.format("雷达完整路线已生成：%d 个路径点，继续移动。", #planner.route))
                    end
                end
            elseif not portalCommitActive
                and planner
                and not route
                and now - lastPartialRouteTime >= 0.18
            then
                lastPartialRouteTime = now

                local partialRoute = planner:getPartialRoute()
                if partialRoute then
                    setRoute(partialRoute, currentPosition)
                end
            end

            if route then
                local waypoint = route[routeIndex]

                if not waypoint then
                    route = nil
                    routeIndex = 1
                    resetPlanner(currentPosition, false)

                    if portalCommitActive and committedPortalWaypoint then
                        setRoute({ currentPosition, committedPortalWaypoint }, currentPosition, true)
                        return
                    end

                    if setProbeRoute(
                        currentPosition,
                        nil,
                        forceWideProbe,
                        forceWideProbe and "雷达正在进行全向出口搜索。" or "雷达继续选择下一段安全探测点。"
                    ) then
                        return
                    end

                    hoverPosition = currentPosition
                    holdRootAtPosition(currentRoot, hoverPosition)
                    return
                end

                if now - lastClearCheckTime >= 0.12 then
                    lastClearCheckTime = now

                    local normalClear = isSegmentClearForBody(
                        currentPosition,
                        waypoint,
                        rayCount,
                        raycastParams
                    )

                    if not normalClear then
                        local squeezeClear = not isSqueezePositionBlacklisted(waypoint)
                            and isSegmentClearForBody(
                                currentPosition,
                                waypoint,
                                rayCount,
                                raycastParams,
                                RADAR_SQUEEZE_BODY_SCALE
                            )

                        if squeezeClear then
                            if not squeezeAttempt
                                or (squeezeAttempt.waypoint - waypoint).Magnitude > 1
                            then
                                squeezeAttempt = {
                                    waypoint = waypoint,
                                    startedAt = now,
                                    lastProgressAt = now,
                                    bestDistance = (waypoint - currentPosition).Magnitude,
                                }

                                setStatus("雷达发现略窄通道，正在侧身减速尝试通过；失败会自动换路。")
                            end
                        else
                            if squeezeAttempt then
                                blacklistSqueezePosition(squeezeAttempt.waypoint, 9)
                                squeezeAttempt = nil
                            end

                            local escapeWaypoint = chooseEscapeWaypoint(
                                currentPosition,
                                waypoint,
                                forceWideProbe
                            )

                            if escapeWaypoint then
                                setRoute({ currentPosition, escapeWaypoint }, currentPosition, true)
                                if forceWideProbe then
                                    setStatus("雷达全向探测发现新的绕行点，正在避开当前结构。")
                                else
                                    setStatus("雷达检测到前方结构，正在侧向绕开。")
                                end
                            else
                                resetPlanner(currentPosition, false)
                                if not setProbeRoute(
                                    currentPosition,
                                    waypoint,
                                    true,
                                    "雷达前方不可通行，正在全向搜索门洞和替代路线。"
                                ) then
                                    hoverPosition = currentPosition
                                    holdRootAtPosition(currentRoot, hoverPosition)
                                end
                            end

                            return
                        end
                    elseif squeezeAttempt
                        and (squeezeAttempt.waypoint - waypoint).Magnitude <= 1
                    then
                        squeezeAttempt = nil
                    end
                end

                local offset = waypoint - currentPosition
                local distance = offset.Magnitude
                local squeezeMode = squeezeAttempt ~= nil
                    and squeezeAttempt.waypoint
                    and (squeezeAttempt.waypoint - waypoint).Magnitude <= 1

                if lastWaypointDistance == nil or distance < lastWaypointDistance - 0.22 then
                    lastWaypointDistance = distance
                    lastProgressTime = now
                end

                if squeezeMode then
                    if distance < squeezeAttempt.bestDistance - 0.16 then
                        squeezeAttempt.bestDistance = distance
                        squeezeAttempt.lastProgressAt = now
                    elseif now - squeezeAttempt.lastProgressAt >= 0.85
                        or now - squeezeAttempt.startedAt >= 2.6
                        or (
                            now - squeezeAttempt.startedAt >= 0.75
                            and now - lastPhysicalMoveTime >= 0.75
                        )
                    then
                        blacklistSqueezePosition(waypoint, 10)

                        if committedPortalWaypoint
                            and (committedPortalWaypoint - waypoint).Magnitude <= 1.5
                        then
                            clearPortalCommitment()
                        end

                        squeezeAttempt = nil
                        route = nil
                        routeIndex = 1
                        resetPlanner(currentPosition, false)

                        if not setProbeRoute(
                            currentPosition,
                            waypoint,
                            true,
                            "窄缝尝试失败，雷达已记住该位置并改找其他门或通道。"
                        ) then
                            hoverPosition = currentPosition
                            holdRootAtPosition(currentRoot, hoverPosition)
                        end

                        return
                    end
                elseif now - lastProgressTime >= 0.85 then
                    local escapeWaypoint = chooseEscapeWaypoint(currentPosition, waypoint, true)

                    if escapeWaypoint then
                        setRoute({ currentPosition, escapeWaypoint }, currentPosition, true)
                        setStatus("雷达检测到停滞，已自动唤醒移动并切换全向脱困路线。")
                    else
                        resetPlanner(currentPosition, false)
                        if not setProbeRoute(
                            currentPosition,
                            waypoint,
                            true,
                            "雷达卡住，正在全向搜索新路线。"
                        ) then
                            hoverPosition = currentPosition
                            holdRootAtPosition(currentRoot, hoverPosition)
                        end
                    end

                    return
                end

                if distance <= 3 then
                    squeezeAttempt = nil
                    squeezeMode = false
                    routeIndex = routeIndex + 1
                    resetProgressWatch()

                    if routeIndex > #route then
                        route = nil
                        routeIndex = 1

                        if committedPortalWaypoint
                            and (committedPortalWaypoint - currentPosition).Magnitude <= 5
                        then
                            clearPortalCommitment()
                            lastTargetDistance = distanceToTarget
                            lastTargetProgressTime = now
                            resetPlanner(currentPosition, false)
                            setStatus("雷达已通过出口，开始计算出口到目标的后半段路线。")
                            return
                        end

                        resetPlanner(currentPosition, false)

                        if setProbeRoute(
                            currentPosition,
                            nil,
                            forceWideProbe,
                            forceWideProbe and "雷达继续全向扩散寻找入口/出口。" or "雷达继续衔接下一段路线。"
                        ) then
                            return
                        end

                        hoverPosition = currentPosition
                        holdRootAtPosition(currentRoot, hoverPosition)
                        return
                    end

                    waypoint = route[routeIndex]
                    offset = waypoint - currentPosition
                    distance = offset.Magnitude
                end

                if distance > 0.05 then
                    local movementSpeed = squeezeMode and speed * 0.38 or speed
                    local stepDistance = movementSpeed * getTeleportMoveDelta(deltaTime)
                    local nextPosition = stepDistance >= distance
                        and waypoint
                        or (currentPosition + offset.Unit * stepDistance)

                    local aggressiveWake = not squeezeMode
                        and now - lastPhysicalMoveTime >= 0.30
                        and now - lastWakeNudgeTime >= 0.20

                    if aggressiveWake then
                        -- 旧版在发出唤醒后直接伪造“已经移动”，会掩盖真实停滞。
                        lastWakeNudgeTime = now
                    end

                    applyRadarMovementStep(
                        currentRoot,
                        nextPosition,
                        offset,
                        waypoint,
                        aggressiveWake,
                        squeezeMode
                    )
                end

                return
            end

            if setProbeRoute(
                currentPosition,
                nil,
                forceWideProbe,
                forceWideProbe and "雷达全向搜索已启动：正在寻找门洞、出口和绕行点。" or "雷达正在连续探测下一段安全路线。"
            ) then
                return
            end

            if planner and planner.failed then
                setStatus("雷达寻路失败：搜索空间内没有找到可通行路线。")
                popupWithHover(
                    currentRoot,
                    "⚠️ 雷达寻路失败",
                    "雷达没有找到可通行路线。可以选择穿墙强行前往，或者交由人工操作接管。"
                )
                return
            end

            if not hoverPosition then
                hoverPosition = currentPosition
            end

            holdRootAtPosition(currentRoot, hoverPosition)
            wakeHumanoidMovement(target - currentPosition, false, target)

            if now - lastStatusTime >= 0.8 then
                lastStatusTime = now

                setStatus(string.format(
                    "雷达连续探测中：已展开 %d 个节点，当前最接近 %.1f studs%s",
                    planner and planner.expansions or 0,
                    planner and planner.bestNode and planner.bestNode.h or distanceToTarget,
                    forceWideProbe and "，全向出口搜索已介入" or ""
                ))
            end
        end)

        return true
    end

    --====================================================
    -- 传送模式分配
    --====================================================

    local function computeRobloxPath(start, target)
        local currentRadius, currentHeight = getDynamicAgentDimensions()

        local path = PathfindingService:CreatePath({
            AgentRadius = currentRadius,
            AgentHeight = currentHeight,
            AgentCanJump = true,
        })

        local ok = pcall(function()
            path:ComputeAsync(start, target)
        end)

        if not ok or path.Status ~= Enum.PathStatus.Success then
            return nil
        end

        local waypoints = {}

        for _, wp in ipairs(path:GetWaypoints()) do
            table.insert(waypoints, {
                position = wp.Position,
                action = wp.Action,
            })
        end

        if #waypoints == 0 then
            return nil
        end

        return waypoints
    end

    local function quickMoveToSelected()
        local pointer = getSelectedTeleportPointer()

        if not pointer or not pointer.point then
            setStatus("没有选中的坐标。")
            return false
        end

        local root = getRoot(true)
        if not root then
            setStatus("找不到 HumanoidRootPart，无法移动。")
            return false
        end

        stopQuickMove()
        stopPopupHover()

        local target = pointToVector3(pointer.point)
        local start = root.Position
        local waypoints = {}

        if TeleportMode == "快速位移" then
            table.insert(waypoints, target)
            return executeMovementPath(waypoints, pointer.point.name, pointer.point, { useNoclip = true })

        elseif TeleportMode == "无人机返航" then
            local cruiseY = math.max(tonumber(DroneHeight) or 240, start.Y, target.Y)

            table.insert(waypoints, Vector3.new(start.X, cruiseY, start.Z))
            table.insert(waypoints, Vector3.new(target.X, cruiseY, target.Z))
            table.insert(waypoints, target)

            return executeMovementPath(waypoints, pointer.point.name, pointer.point, {
                useNoclip = true,
                skipGroundClearance = true,
            })

        elseif TeleportMode == "最后上升" then
            table.insert(waypoints, Vector3.new(target.X, start.Y, target.Z))
            table.insert(waypoints, target)

            return executeMovementPath(waypoints, pointer.point.name, pointer.point, { useNoclip = true })

        elseif TeleportMode == "不穿墙模式" then
            NoWallPathMode = normalizeNoWallPathMode(NoWallPathMode)

            if NoWallPathMode == "原生寻路" then
                local currentRadius, currentHeight = getDynamicAgentDimensions()

                setStatus(string.format(
                    "不穿墙模式：使用 Roblox 原生寻路。体型半径 %.1f，高度 %.1f。",
                    currentRadius,
                    currentHeight
                ))

                waypoints = computeRobloxPath(start, target)

                if waypoints then
                    return executeMovementPath(waypoints, pointer.point.name, pointer.point, {
                        useNoclip = false,
                        nativePath = true,
                    })
                end

                showBlockPopup(
                    target,
                    pointer.point.name,
                    pointer.point,
                    "⚠️ Roblox 寻路失败",
                    "Roblox 原生寻路无法生成可行路线。可以选择穿墙强行前往，或者交由人工操作接管。"
                )
                startPopupHover(root.Position, false)
                return false

            elseif NoWallPathMode == "雷达寻路" then
                setStatus("不穿墙模式：使用雷达寻路，不依赖 Roblox 原生寻路。")
                return executeRadarMovement(target, pointer.point.name, pointer.point, { useNoclip = false })

            elseif NoWallPathMode == "融合模式" then
                setStatus("不穿墙融合模式：正在优先尝试 Roblox 原生寻路。")

                waypoints = computeRobloxPath(start, target)

                if waypoints then
                    setStatus("融合模式：Roblox 原生寻路成功，使用原生路线。")
                    return executeMovementPath(waypoints, pointer.point.name, pointer.point, {
                        useNoclip = false,
                        nativePath = true,
                    })
                end

                setStatus("融合模式：Roblox 原生寻路失败，自动切换到雷达寻路。")
                return executeRadarMovement(target, pointer.point.name, pointer.point, { useNoclip = false })
            end
        end

        setStatus("移动失败：未知传送方式 " .. tostring(TeleportMode))
        return false
    end

    local function teleportToSelected()
        TeleportMode = normalizeTeleportMode(TeleportMode)
        NoWallPathMode = normalizeNoWallPathMode(NoWallPathMode)

        if TeleportMode == "直接瞬移" then
            return teleportToSelectedInstant()
        end

        return quickMoveToSelected()
    end

    local function renameManagedPoint()
        local pointer = getSelectedManagePointer()

        if not pointer or not pointer.point then
            setStatus("重命名失败：没有选中的坐标。")
            return
        end

        local newName = trim(ManageRenameText)

        if newName == "" then
            setStatus("重命名失败：新名称不能为空。")
            return
        end

        pointer.point.name = newName
        saveData()

        refreshAllDropdowns(pointer.key)
        setStatus("已重命名为：" .. newName)
    end

    local function deleteManagedPoint()
        local pointer = getSelectedManagePointer()

        if not pointer or not pointer.list or not pointer.index then
            setStatus("删除失败：没有选中的坐标。")
            return
        end

        local deletedName = pointer.point and pointer.point.name or "坐标"

        table.remove(pointer.list, pointer.index)
        saveData()

        SelectedDisplay = nil
        ManageSelectedDisplay = nil

        refreshAllDropdowns(nil)
        setStatus("已删除：" .. tostring(deletedName))
    end

    --====================================================
    -- 快捷悬浮按钮系统
    --====================================================

    local QuickTeleportButtonTextDefault = "快捷传送"
    local QuickTeleportHolder = nil
    local QuickTeleportButton = nil
    local QuickTeleportText = nil
    local QuickTeleportGradient = nil
    local QuickTeleportTextToken = 0

    local function getViewportSize()
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if camera then
            return camera.ViewportSize
        end

        return Vector2.new(1920, 1080)
    end

    local function clampQuickTeleportHolderPosition(pos)
        local viewport = getViewportSize()
        local size = QuickTeleportHolder and QuickTeleportHolder.AbsoluteSize or Vector2.new(94, 38)
        local padding = 8

        local x = math.clamp(pos.X, padding, math.max(padding, viewport.X - size.X - padding))
        local y = math.clamp(pos.Y, padding, math.max(padding, viewport.Y - size.Y - padding))

        return Vector2.new(x, y)
    end

    QuickTeleportHolder = Instance.new("Frame")
    QuickTeleportHolder.Name = "CoordQuickTeleportHolder"
    QuickTeleportHolder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(94, 38)
    QuickTeleportHolder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -118, 0.52, 0)
    QuickTeleportHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    QuickTeleportHolder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    QuickTeleportHolder.Active = true
    QuickTeleportHolder.Visible = false
    QuickTeleportHolder.ZIndex = 999
    QuickTeleportHolder.Parent = OverlayGui

    pcall(function()
        if type(isfile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function" and isfile(QuickTeleportPosFile) then
            local data = readfile(QuickTeleportPosFile)
            local x, y = string.match(tostring(data or ""), "([^,]+),([^,]+)")

            x = tonumber(x)
            y = tonumber(y)

            if x and y then
                local savedPos = clampQuickTeleportHolderPosition(Vector2.new(x, y))
                QuickTeleportHolder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(savedPos.X, savedPos.Y)
            end
        end
    end)

    QuickTeleportButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
    QuickTeleportButton.Name = "Button"
    QuickTeleportButton.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    QuickTeleportButton.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    QuickTeleportButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 24)
    QuickTeleportButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.06
    QuickTeleportButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
    QuickTeleportButton.AutoButtonColor = false
    QuickTeleportButton.Text = ""
    QuickTeleportButton.Active = true
    QuickTeleportButton.ZIndex = 1000
    QuickTeleportButton.Parent = QuickTeleportHolder

    local QuickTeleportButtonCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    QuickTeleportButtonCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    QuickTeleportButtonCorner.Parent = QuickTeleportButton

    local QuickTeleportRainbowStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    QuickTeleportRainbowStroke.Thickness = 2.4
    QuickTeleportRainbowStroke.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    QuickTeleportRainbowStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    QuickTeleportRainbowStroke.Parent = QuickTeleportButton

    QuickTeleportGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    QuickTeleportGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.16, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.33, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.50, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.66, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.83, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
    })
    QuickTeleportGradient.Parent = QuickTeleportRainbowStroke

    local QuickTeleportInnerStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    QuickTeleportInnerStroke.Thickness = 1
    QuickTeleportInnerStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    QuickTeleportInnerStroke.Transparency = 0.86
    QuickTeleportInnerStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    QuickTeleportInnerStroke.Parent = QuickTeleportButton

    QuickTeleportText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    QuickTeleportText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    QuickTeleportText.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    QuickTeleportText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    QuickTeleportText.TextSize = 12
    QuickTeleportText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 245)
    QuickTeleportText.Text = QuickTeleportButtonTextDefault
    QuickTeleportText.ZIndex = 1001
    QuickTeleportText.Parent = QuickTeleportButton

    addFloatConnection(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        if QuickTeleportGradient and QuickTeleportGradient.Parent and QuickTeleportHolder and QuickTeleportHolder.Visible then
            QuickTeleportGradient.Rotation = (QuickTeleportGradient.Rotation + dt * 160) % 360
        end
    end))

    local QuickTeleportIsDragging = false
    local QuickTeleportDragStart = nil
    local QuickTeleportStartAbsolutePos = nil
    local QuickTeleportActiveTouch = nil
    local QuickTeleportHasMoved = false
    local QUICK_TELEPORT_DRAG_DISTANCE = 6

    local function saveQuickTeleportPosition()
        if not QuickTeleportHolder then return end

        pcall(function()
            if type(writefile) == "function" then
                writefile(
                    QuickTeleportPosFile,
                    tostring(math.floor(QuickTeleportHolder.AbsolutePosition.X)) .. "," .. tostring(math.floor(QuickTeleportHolder.AbsolutePosition.Y))
                )
            end
        end)
    end

    local function applyQuickTeleportDragPosition(input)
        if not QuickTeleportIsDragging or not QuickTeleportDragStart or not QuickTeleportStartAbsolutePos or not QuickTeleportHolder then
            return
        end

        local delta = input.Position - QuickTeleportDragStart

        if math.abs(delta.X) > QUICK_TELEPORT_DRAG_DISTANCE or math.abs(delta.Y) > QUICK_TELEPORT_DRAG_DISTANCE then
            QuickTeleportHasMoved = true
        end

        local rawPos = QuickTeleportStartAbsolutePos + Vector2.new(delta.X, delta.Y)
        rawPos = clampQuickTeleportHolderPosition(rawPos)
        QuickTeleportHolder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(rawPos.X, rawPos.Y)
    end

    local function playQuickTeleportPressAnim()
        pcall(function()
            TweenService:Create(
                QuickTeleportHolder,
                TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(100, 42) }
            ):Play()
        end)
    end

    local function playQuickTeleportReleaseAnim()
        pcall(function()
            TweenService:Create(
                QuickTeleportHolder,
                TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(94, 38) }
            ):Play()
        end)
    end

    local function setQuickTeleportTemporaryText(text)
        QuickTeleportTextToken = QuickTeleportTextToken + 1
        local token = QuickTeleportTextToken

        QuickTeleportText.Text = tostring(text or QuickTeleportButtonTextDefault)

        local delayFunction = (type(task) == "table" and type(task.delay) == "function") and task.delay or delay
        delayFunction(1.4, function()
            if token ~= QuickTeleportTextToken then return end

            if QuickTeleportText and QuickTeleportText.Parent then
                QuickTeleportText.Text = QuickTeleportButtonTextDefault
            end
        end)
    end

    addFloatConnection(QuickTeleportButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if not QuickTeleportHolder.Visible then return end

        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and not QuickTeleportActiveTouch)
        then
            QuickTeleportIsDragging = true
            QuickTeleportDragStart = input.Position
            QuickTeleportStartAbsolutePos = QuickTeleportHolder.AbsolutePosition
            QuickTeleportHasMoved = false

            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                QuickTeleportActiveTouch = input
            end

            playQuickTeleportPressAnim()

            local changedConnection
            changedConnection = input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    if changedConnection then
                        changedConnection:Disconnect()
                        changedConnection = nil
                    end

                    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                        if input == QuickTeleportActiveTouch then
                            QuickTeleportActiveTouch = nil
                            QuickTeleportIsDragging = false

                            if QuickTeleportHasMoved then
                                saveQuickTeleportPosition()
                            end

                            playQuickTeleportReleaseAnim()
                        end
                    else
                        QuickTeleportIsDragging = false

                        if QuickTeleportHasMoved then
                            saveQuickTeleportPosition()
                        end

                        playQuickTeleportReleaseAnim()
                    end
                end
            end)

            addFloatConnection(changedConnection)
        end
    end))

    addFloatConnection(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not QuickTeleportIsDragging then return end

        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
            or (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input == QuickTeleportActiveTouch)
        then
            applyQuickTeleportDragPosition(input)
        end
    end))

    addFloatConnection(QuickTeleportButton.Activated:Connect(function()
        if QuickTeleportHasMoved then
            QuickTeleportHasMoved = false
            return
        end

        local ok = teleportToSelected()

        if ok then
            setQuickTeleportTemporaryText("成功")
        else
            setQuickTeleportTemporaryText("失败")
        end
    end))

    local function setQuickTeleportButtonVisible(state)
        QuickTeleportButtonEnabled = state == true
        QuickTeleportHolder.Visible = QuickTeleportButtonEnabled

        if not QuickTeleportButtonEnabled then
            QuickTeleportIsDragging = false
            QuickTeleportActiveTouch = nil
            QuickTeleportHasMoved = false
            QuickTeleportTextToken = QuickTeleportTextToken + 1
            QuickTeleportText.Text = QuickTeleportButtonTextDefault
            playQuickTeleportReleaseAnim()
        end
    end

    local function applyOverlayCorner()
        OverlayCorner = normalizeOverlayCorner(OverlayCorner)

        if OverlayCorner == "左上角" then
            CoordLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
            CoordLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12)
        elseif OverlayCorner == "右上角" then
            CoordLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
            CoordLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 12)
        elseif OverlayCorner == "右下角" then
            CoordLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
            CoordLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -12)
        else
            CoordLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
            CoordLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, -12)
        end
    end

    --====================================================
    -- 加载本地数据
    --====================================================

    loadData()
    applyOverlayCorner()

    local lastOverlayUpdate = 0

    Env.__WindUICoordTeleportConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not OverlayEnabled then
            CoordLabel.Visible = false
            return
        end

        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastOverlayUpdate < 0.1 then return end
        lastOverlayUpdate = now

        local root = getRoot(false)

        if not root then
            CoordLabel.Text = "坐标：未找到角色"
            CoordLabel.Visible = true
            return
        end

        local p = root.Position
        CoordLabel.Text = string.format("X: %.2f   Y: %.2f   Z: %.2f", p.X, p.Y, p.Z)
        CoordLabel.Visible = true
    end)

    --====================================================
    -- Tab 1：坐标传送
    --====================================================

    local CoordTab = Window:Tab({
        Title = "坐标传送",
        Icon = "lucide:map-pin",
        Locked = false,
    })

    StatusParagraph = CoordTab:Paragraph({
        Title = "状态",
        Desc = "坐标传送已加载。",
    })

    CoordTab:Section({
        Title = "选择并传送",
        Box = false,
        Opened = true,
    })

    SavedDropdown = CoordTab:Dropdown({
        Title = "选择坐标",

        Values = { "暂无坐标" },
        Value = "暂无坐标",
        Multi = false,
        AllowNone = false,
        Callback = function(option)
            if option == "暂无坐标" then
                SelectedDisplay = nil
                return
            end

            SelectedDisplay = option

            local pointer = getSelectedTeleportPointer()

            if pointer and pointer.point then
                setStatus("当前选择：" .. tostring(pointer.point.name) .. " | " .. formatPoint(pointer.point))
            end
        end,
    })

    CoordTab:Button({
        Title = "传送",
        Desc = "按当前选择的传送方式执行。",
        Locked = false,
        Callback = function()
            teleportToSelected()
        end,
    })

    CoordTab:Button({
        Title = "停止传送",
        Desc = "立即停止当前正在执行的平滑移动、雷达寻路或融合寻路，并恢复碰撞。",
        Locked = false,
        Callback = function()
            stopTeleportByUser()
        end,
    })

    CoordTab:Button({
        Title = "直接瞬移",
        Desc = "无视传送方式选择，直接瞬移到当前选中的坐标。",
        Locked = false,
        Callback = function()
            teleportToSelectedInstant()
        end,
    })

    CoordTab:Dropdown({
        Title = "传送方式",
        Desc = "选择主要传送方式。除“不穿墙模式”外，移动时会自动临时关闭角色碰撞并在结束后恢复。",
        Values = {
            "快速位移",
            "直接瞬移",

            "最后上升",
            "不穿墙模式",
        },
        Value = TeleportMode,

            TeleportMode = normalizeTeleportMode(option)

    CoordTab:Dropdown({
        Title = "不穿墙寻路算法",
        Desc = "仅在“传送方式 = 不穿墙模式”时生效。原生寻路用 Roblox Pathfinding；雷达寻路用射线采样自建路线；融合模式会先原生，失败后雷达。",
        Values = {
            "原生寻路",
            "雷达寻路",
            "融合模式",
        },
        Value = NoWallPathMode,
        Multi = false,
        AllowNone = false,

            NoWallPathMode = normalizeNoWallPathMode(option)
            saveData()
            setStatus("不穿墙寻路算法已切换为：" .. tostring(NoWallPathMode))
        end,
    })

    CoordTab:Dropdown({
        Title = "雷达射线数量",
        Desc = "仅影响不穿墙模式下的雷达寻路与融合模式。64 性能好，128 均衡，256 更细但更吃性能。",
        Values = { "64", "128", "256" },
        Value = tostring(RadarRayCount),
        Multi = false,
        AllowNone = false,
        Callback = function(option)
            RadarRayCount = normalizeRadarRayCount(option)
            saveData()
            setStatus("雷达射线数量已设置为：" .. tostring(RadarRayCount))
        end,
    })

    CoordTab:Input({
        Title = "无人机返航预设高度",
        Desc = "仅在“无人机返航”模式下生效。实际巡航高度会取当前高度、目标高度和预设高度中的最大值。",
        Value = DroneHeightText,
        Type = "Input",
        Placeholder = "例如：240",
        Callback = function(input)
            DroneHeightText = input

            local height = tonumber(trim(input))

            if not height then
                setStatus("预设高度无效，请输入有效的数字。")
                return
            end

            DroneHeight = height
            DroneHeightText = tostring(DroneHeight)
            saveData()
            setStatus("无人机返航预设高度已设置为：" .. tostring(DroneHeight))
        end,
    })

    CoordTab:Input({
        Title = "平滑移动速度",
        Desc = "单位：studs/s，适用于非直接瞬移的所有轨迹。范围：1 - 10000。",
        Value = QuickMoveSpeedText,
        Type = "Input",
        Placeholder = "例如：300",
        Callback = function(input)
            QuickMoveSpeedText = input

            local speed = tonumber(trim(input))

            if not speed or speed <= 0 then
                setStatus("移动速度无效，请输入大于 0 的数字。")
                return
            end

            QuickMoveSpeed = clampNumber(speed, 1, 10000)
            QuickMoveSpeedText = tostring(QuickMoveSpeed)
            saveData()
            setStatus("平滑移动速度已设置为：" .. tostring(QuickMoveSpeed) .. " studs/s")
        end,
    })

    CoordTab:Toggle({
        Title = "快捷传送悬浮按钮",
        Desc = "开启后显示可拖动按钮；开关本次运行有效，按钮位置会记忆。",
        Value = false,
        Callback = function(state)
            setQuickTeleportButtonVisible(state == true)

            if QuickTeleportButtonEnabled then
                setStatus("已开启快捷传送悬浮按钮。")
            else
                setStatus("已关闭快捷传送悬浮按钮。")
            end
        end,
    })

    CoordTab:Section({
        Title = "保存当前坐标",
        Box = false,
        Opened = true,
    })

    CoordTab:Input({
        Title = "当前坐标名称",
        Desc = "保存当前位置时使用这个名称，不填则自动生成。",
        Value = "",
        Type = "Input",
        Placeholder = "例如：基地 / 高楼 / 安全点",
        Callback = function(input)
            CurrentSaveNameText = input
        end,
    })

    CoordTab:Button({
        Title = "保存当前坐标",
        Desc = "弹窗选择保存为全局或绑定当前游戏 ID。",
        Locked = false,
        Callback = function()
            local SaveCurrentDialog

            SaveCurrentDialog = Window:Dialog({
                Title = "保存当前坐标",
                Content = "是否保存当前坐标？\n\n名称：" .. (trim(CurrentSaveNameText) ~= "" and trim(CurrentSaveNameText) or "自动生成") .. "\n当前 GameId: " .. CurrentGameId,
                Buttons = {
                    {
                        Title = "取消",
                        Callback = function()
                            if SaveCurrentDialog then
                                SaveCurrentDialog:Close()
                            end
                        end,
                    },
                    {
                        Title = "保存",
                        Callback = function()
                            local pos = getCurrentPosition()

                            if not pos then
                                setStatus("保存失败：找不到当前位置。")
                                return
                            end

                            savePoint(pos, CurrentSaveNameText, false)

                            if SaveCurrentDialog then
                                SaveCurrentDialog:Close()
                            end
                        end,
                    },
                    {
                        Title = "关联游戏ID并保存",
                        Callback = function()
                            local pos = getCurrentPosition()

                            if not pos then
                                setStatus("保存失败：找不到当前位置。")
                                return
                            end

                            savePoint(pos, CurrentSaveNameText, true)

                            if SaveCurrentDialog then
                                SaveCurrentDialog:Close()
                            end
                        end,
                    },
                },
            })
        end,
    })

    CoordTab:Section({
        Title = "手动保存坐标",
        Box = false,
        Opened = true,
    })

    CoordTab:Input({
        Title = "手动坐标",
        Desc = "支持：12 34 56 / 12,34,56 / 123456",
        Value = "",
        Type = "Input",
        Placeholder = "例如：12 34 56 或 123456",
        Callback = function(input)
            ManualCoordText = input
        end,

    CoordTab:Input({
        Title = "手动坐标名称",
        Desc = "不填会自动生成名字。",

        Type = "Input",

            ManualNameText = input

            if not pos then
                setStatus("手动保存失败：" .. tostring(err))
                return
            end

            savePoint(pos, ManualNameText, false)
        end,
    })

    CoordTab:Button({
        Title = "手动关联游戏ID并保存",

                return

            savePoint(pos, ManualNameText, true)
        end,

    CoordTab:Toggle({
        Title = "显示当前坐标",

            OverlayEnabled = state == true
            CoordLabel.Visible = OverlayEnabled

            if OverlayEnabled then
                setStatus("已开启当前坐标显示。")

                setStatus("已关闭当前坐标显示。")
